<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="de">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover">
<title>Malte – Minecraft-Entwickler</title>
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Bricolage+Grotesque:wght@500;700;800&family=Figtree:wght@400;500;600&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<style>
:root{
  --bg:#f3f1ec; --bg2:#e9e6de; --ink:#1c2233; --muted:#5b6377; --line:#d5d1c6;
  --accent:#d9412f; --accent2:#2f5bd8; --gold:#e0a91f; --card:#faf9f6;
  --head:"Bricolage Grotesque","Trebuchet MS",system-ui,sans-serif;
  --body:"Figtree",system-ui,-apple-system,"Segoe UI",sans-serif;
  box-sizing:border-box; padding-top:env(safe-area-inset-top,0px); padding-bottom:env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px);
}
@media (prefers-color-scheme:dark){
  :root:not([data-theme="light"]){--bg:#171b28;--bg2:#1f2434;--ink:#eceef4;--muted:#9aa2b8;--line:#2e3549;--accent:#ff6a55;--accent2:#7d9dff;--gold:#f0bf45;--card:#1c2131}
}
:root[data-theme="dark"]{--bg:#171b28;--bg2:#1f2434;--ink:#eceef4;--muted:#9aa2b8;--line:#2e3549;--accent:#ff6a55;--accent2:#7d9dff;--gold:#f0bf45;--card:#1c2131}
html{scroll-padding-top:env(safe-area-inset-top,0px);scroll-behavior:smooth}
*{box-sizing:border-box}
body{margin:0;background:var(--bg);color:var(--ink);font:400 1.0625rem/1.65 var(--body);
  -webkit-user-select:none;user-select:none;-webkit-touch-callout:none}
.allow{-webkit-user-select:text;user-select:text}
a{color:inherit}
:focus-visible{outline:3px solid var(--accent2);outline-offset:3px;border-radius:4px}
.wrap{max-width:1080px;margin:0 auto;padding:0 24px}
header.top{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:22px 0;position:relative;z-index:2}
.logo{font:800 1.25rem var(--head);text-decoration:none;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px}
.logo i{width:22px;height:22px;background:var(--accent);display:block;box-shadow:inset -5px -5px 0 rgba(0,0,0,.22),inset 5px 5px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.25)}
nav a{margin-left:22px;text-decoration:none;color:var(--muted);font-weight:500}
nav a:hover{color:var(--ink)}
.hero{position:relative;overflow:hidden;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
#grid{position:absolute;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%;z-index:0}
.hero .wrap{position:relative;z-index:1;padding-top:64px;padding-bottom:96px;pointer-events:none}
.hero .wrap a,.hero .wrap button{pointer-events:auto}
h1{font:800 clamp(2.8rem,8vw,6rem)/1 var(--head);letter-spacing:-.03em;margin:0 0 24px;max-width:12ch}
.lead{font-size:1.25rem;max-width:36rem;color:var(--muted);margin:0 0 32px}
.btns{display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap}
.btn{display:inline-block;padding:13px 22px;font:600 1rem var(--body);text-decoration:none;border:2px solid var(--ink);background:var(--ink);color:var(--bg);cursor:pointer;border-radius:3px;box-shadow:4px 4px 0 var(--accent)}
.btn.alt{background:transparent;color:var(--ink);box-shadow:4px 4px 0 var(--line)}
.btn:active{transform:translate(2px,2px);box-shadow:2px 2px 0 var(--accent)}
section{padding:88px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
h2{font:800 clamp(1.9rem,4vw,2.8rem)/1.1 var(--head);letter-spacing:-.02em;margin:0 0 12px}
.sub{color:var(--muted);max-width:38rem;margin:0 0 44px}
.about{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.4fr 1fr;gap:56px}
.about p{margin:0 0 18px}
.facts{background:var(--card);border:2px solid var(--ink);padding:26px;align-self:start;box-shadow:6px 6px 0 var(--accent2)}
.facts dl{margin:0}.facts dt{font-weight:600;color:var(--muted);font-size:.95rem}
.facts dd{margin:0 0 16px;font:700 1.35rem var(--head)}.facts dd:last-child{margin:0}
.skills{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:0;border:2px solid var(--ink)}
.skill{padding:28px;background:var(--card);border-right:2px solid var(--ink)}
.skill:last-child{border-right:0}
.skill h3{font:700 1.3rem var(--head);margin:0 0 8px}
.skill p{margin:0;color:var(--muted)}
.cl{font:700 1.1rem var(--head);margin:32px 0 0}
.chips{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;margin-top:28px}
.chip{padding:6px 14px;border:1.5px solid var(--line);border-radius:99px;font-weight:500;background:var(--card)}
.list{border-top:2px solid var(--ink)}
.row{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(150px,220px) 1fr auto;gap:24px;align-items:center;padding:22px 8px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);text-decoration:none;transition:background .15s,padding .15s}
.row:hover{background:var(--bg2);padding-left:20px}
.row b{font:700 1.25rem var(--head)}
.row span{color:var(--muted)}
.row em{font-style:normal;font-weight:600;color:var(--accent2);white-space:nowrap}
.sys{border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
.sys .row{border-bottom:0}
.sys details{padding:0 8px 22px}
.sys summary{cursor:pointer;font-weight:600;color:var(--accent2);width:max-content;padding:4px 0;list-style:none}
.sys summary::-webkit-details-marker{display:none}
.sys summary::before{content:"+";display:inline-block;width:1.2em;font-weight:800}
.sys details[open] summary::before{content:"\2212"}
.code{margin:12px 0 0;padding:18px;max-height:480px;overflow:auto;background:var(--bg2);border:2px solid var(--ink);font:400 .82rem/1.55 ui-monospace,SFMono-Regular,Menlo,Consolas,monospace;tab-size:4;white-space:pre;color:var(--ink)}
.code .c{color:var(--muted);font-style:italic}.code .s{color:var(--accent)}.code .k{color:var(--accent2);font-weight:600}
.note{margin-top:24px;font-size:.95rem;color:var(--muted)}
.proj{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:24px}
.pcard{background:var(--card);border:2px solid var(--ink);padding:28px}
.pcard:first-child{box-shadow:6px 6px 0 var(--gold)}
.pcard h3{font:700 1.4rem var(--head);margin:0 0 6px}
.pcard p{margin:0 0 14px;color:var(--muted)}
.tag{display:inline-block;font-size:.85rem;font-weight:600;padding:3px 10px;background:var(--bg2);border-radius:3px;margin-right:6px}
.contact{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:24px}
.cbox{border:2px solid var(--ink);padding:26px;background:var(--card)}
.cbox small{color:var(--muted);font-weight:600}
.cbox strong{display:block;font:700 1.4rem var(--head);margin:4px 0 16px;word-break:break-all}
.cbox .btn{padding:9px 16px;font-size:.95rem;box-shadow:3px 3px 0 var(--accent)}
footer{padding:36px 0;color:var(--muted);font-size:.95rem}
#toast{position:fixed;left:50%;bottom:calc(24px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px));transform:translate(-50%,30px);background:var(--ink);color:var(--bg);padding:11px 20px;border-radius:4px;opacity:0;transition:.25s;pointer-events:none;z-index:9;font-weight:500}
#toast.on{opacity:1;transform:translate(-50%,0)}
@media (max-width:820px){
  .about,.proj,.contact,.skills{grid-template-columns:1fr}
  .skill{border-right:0;border-bottom:2px solid var(--ink)}.skill:last-child{border-bottom:0}
  .row{grid-template-columns:1fr;gap:4px}
  nav a:nth-child(n+3){display:none}
  section{padding:64px 0}
}
@media print{body{display:none!important}}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){*{transition:none!important;scroll-behavior:auto!important}}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div class="hero">
  <canvas id="grid" aria-hidden="true"></canvas>
  <div class="wrap">
    <header class="top">
      <a class="logo" href="#top"><i></i>Malte</a>
      <nav><a href="#about">Über mich</a><a href="#systems">Systeme</a><a href="#projects">Projekte</a><a href="#contact">Kontakt</a></nav>
    </header>
    <div id="top" style="padding-top:56px">
      <h1>Ich baue Minecraft-Plugins.</h1>
      <p class="lead">Ich bin Malte, 23, und schreibe seit 7 Jahren Code. Am liebsten Java-Plugins für Server und Addons für LabyMod.</p>
      <div class="btns"><a class="btn" href="#systems">Meine Systeme ansehen</a><a class="btn alt" href="#contact">Kontakt aufnehmen</a></div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<main>
<section id="about"><div class="wrap about">
  <div>
    <h2>Über mich</h2>
    <p>Ich programmiere seit 7 Jahren und habe mit Minecraft angefangen, weil ich Dinge bauen wollte, die andere Spieler direkt benutzen. Daraus sind Server-Plugins und Client-Addons geworden.</p>
    <p>Unter dem Namen cMalte entwickle ich Addons für LabyMod 3 und LabyMod 4 sowie Plugins für Paper und Spigot. Ich portiere auch ältere Addons auf neue Versionen, damit sie weiterlaufen.</p>
    <p>Mein Code ist in Systeme aufgeteilt. Jedes System übernimmt genau eine Aufgabe auf dem Server und lässt sich einzeln pflegen.</p>
  </div>
  <div class="facts"><dl>
    <dt>Alter</dt><dd>23 Jahre</dd>
    <dt>Erfahrung</dt><dd>7 Jahre Programmieren</dd>
    <dt>Entwicklername</dt><dd>cMalte</dd>
    <dt>Sprache</dt><dd>Java</dd>
  </dl></div>
</div></section>

<section id="skills"><div class="wrap">
  <h2>Was ich mache</h2>
  <p class="sub">Drei Bereiche, in denen ich täglich arbeite.</p>
  <div class="skills">
    <div class="skill"><h3>Server-Plugins</h3><p>Plugins für Paper und Spigot, zuletzt für Paper 1.21, mit klar getrennten Systemen.</p></div>
    <div class="skill"><h3>LabyMod-Addons</h3><p>Addons für LabyMod 3 und LabyMod 4 mit eigenen Oberflächen im Spiel.</p></div>
    <div class="skill"><h3>Portierungen</h3><p>Bestehende Addons auf neue API-Versionen heben, zum Beispiel von LabyMod 3 auf 4.</p></div>
  </div>
  <h3 class="cl">Sprachen</h3>
  <div class="chips" id="langs"></div>
  <h3 class="cl">Plattformen</h3>
  <div class="chips"><span class="chip">Paper API</span><span class="chip">Spigot API</span><span class="chip">LabyMod 3</span><span class="chip">LabyMod 4</span><span class="chip">Minecraft 1.21</span></div>
</div></section>

<section id="experience"><div class="wrap">
  <h2>Erfahrung</h2>
  <p class="sub">Wo ich entwickelt habe.</p>
  <div class="list">
    <div class="row"><b>ChunkBuild</b><span>Mein eigenes Minecraft-Netzwerk, das ich selbst aufgebaut und betrieben habe.</span><em>2020 bis 2021</em></div>
    <div class="row"><b>Diverse Netzwerke</b><span>Entwicklung von Plugins und Systemen für verschiedene Minecraft-Netzwerke.</span><em></em></div>
  </div>
</div></section>

<section id="systems"><div class="wrap">
  <h2>Meine Systeme</h2>
  <p class="sub">Die einzelnen Bausteine meines Plugins. Ein Klick öffnet das jeweilige System.</p>
  <div class="list" id="systemList"></div>

</div></section>

<section id="projects"><div class="wrap">
  <h2>Projekte</h2>
  <p class="sub">Woran ich zuletzt gearbeitet habe.</p>
  <div class="proj" style="grid-template-columns:1fr">
    <div class="pcard"><h3>SMPAddonSystem</h3><p>Ein Plugin für das ChaosSMP-Netzwerk auf Paper 1.21. Alle Funktionen sind in einzelne Systeme getrennt, die oben aufgelistet sind.</p><span class="tag">Java</span><span class="tag">Paper 1.21</span></div>
  </div>
</div></section>

<section id="contact"><div class="wrap">
  <h2>Kontakt</h2>
  <p class="sub">Schreib mir für Anfragen, Ideen oder Zusammenarbeit.</p>
  <div class="contact">
    <div class="cbox"><small>Discord</small><strong class="allow" id="dc">hakubaa_</strong><button class="btn" data-copy="hakubaa_">Name kopieren</button></div>
    <div class="cbox"><small>E-Mail</small><strong class="allow" id="ml">bstobst@atomicmail.io</strong><a class="btn" href="mailto:bstobst@atomicmail.io" style="margin-right:8px">E-Mail schreiben</a><button class="btn alt" data-copy="bstobst@atomicmail.io">Adresse kopieren</button></div>
  </div>
</div></section>
</main>

<footer><div class="wrap">© <span id="yr"></span> Malte (cMalte). Inhalte und Code dieser Seite sind geschützt.</div></footer>
<div id="toast" role="status"></div>

org.bukkit.scheduler.BukkitTask;\nimport org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;\nimport org.bukkit.util.Vector;\n\n/**\n * Server-side heuristic anti-cheat layer for ChaosSMP.\n *\n * <p>ChaosGuard deliberately combines several weak signals before punishment. A\n * single lag spike, unusual hit or lucky ore vein is logged, but cannot normally\n * ban a player. Structurally invalid payloads and packet floods use the separate\n * CRASH check and are banned immediately.<\/p>\n */\npublic final class ChaosGuardPlugin extends JavaPlugin implements Listener, CommandExecutor, TabCompleter, PluginMessageListener {\n    private static final String ROOT = \"\";\n    private static final String PREFIX = \"&8[&c&lChaosGuard&8] &r\";\n    private static final BlockFace[] FACES = {\n        BlockFace.UP, BlockFace.DOWN, BlockFace.NORTH, BlockFace.SOUTH, BlockFace.EAST, BlockFace.WEST\n    };\n    private static final Set<Material> MINING_BLOCKS = Material.DIORITE, Material.ANDESITE, Material.CALCITE\n    )));\n    private static final Set<Material> ICE = Collections.unmodifiableSet(new HashSet<>(Arrays.asList(\n        Material.ICE, Material.PACKED_ICE, Material.BLUE_ICE, Material.FROSTED_ICE\n    )));\n    private static final Set<Material> FALL_EXEMPT = Collections.unmodifiableSet(new HashSet<>(Arrays.asList(\n        Material.WATER, Material.LAVA, Material.SLIME_BLOCK, Material.HAY_BLOCK, Material.HONEY_BLOCK,\n        Material.COBWEB, Material.POWDER_SNOW, Material.SCAFFOLDING, Material.LADDER, Material.VINE,\n        Material.TWISTING_VINES, Material.WEEPING_VINES\n    )));\n    private static final Map<Material, Double> ORE_WEIGHTS = createOreWeights();\n    private static final List<String> SUSPICIOUS_BRANDS = Arrays.asList(\n        \"wurst\", \"liquidbounce\", \"meteor\", \"aristois\", \"impact\", \"inertia\", \"bleachhack\",\n        \"rusherhack\", \"futureclient\", \"sigma\", \"vape\", \"horion\"\n    );\n\n    private final Map<UUID, Profile> profiles = new HashMap<>();\n    private final Map<UUID, BanRecord> activeBans = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();\n    private final Set<UUID> alertToggles = new HashSet<>();\n    private final ConcurrentLinkedQueue<String> pendingLogLines = new ConcurrentLinkedQueue<>();\n    private BukkitTask decayTask;\n    private BukkitTask logTask;\n    private boolean brandChannelRegistered;\n    private File banFile;\n    private FileConfiguration banConfig;\n    private File stateFile;\n    private SqlDocumentStore database;\n\n    @Override\n    public void onEnable() {\n        saveDefaultConfig();\n        getConfig().options().copyDefaults(true);\n        saveConfig();\n        database = new SqlDocumentStore(this);\n        database.initialize();\n        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimerAsynchronously(this, database::reconnectIfNeeded, 1200L, 1200L);\n        File databaseBanFile = new File(getDataFolder(), \"bans.yml\");\n        File databaseStateFile = new File(getDataFolder(), \"violations.yml\");\n        database.restoreIfNewer(\"guard:bans\", databaseBanFile);\n        database.restoreIfNewer(\"guard:violations\", databaseStateFile);\n        loadBans();\n        loadViolationState();\n        Bukkit.getPluginManager().registerEvents(this, this);\n        if (getCommand(\"chaosguard\") != null) {\n            getCommand(\"chaosguard\").setExecutor(this);\n            getCommand(\"chaosguard\").setTabCompleter(this);\n        }\n        try {\n            getServer().getMessenger().registerIncomingPluginChannel(this, \"minecraft:brand\", this);\n            brandChannelRegistered = true;\n        } catch (IllegalArgumentException exception) {\n            getLogger().warning(\"Could not monitor minecraft:brand: \" + exception.getMessage());\n        }\n        decayTask = Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimer(this, this::decayProfiles, 1200L, 1200L);\n        logTask = Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimerAsynchronously(this, this::flushLogs, 40L, 40L);\n        queueSystemLog(\"START\", \"ChaosGuard enabled; checks=\" + Check.enabledNames());\n        reportLaunchDiagnostics();\n        getLogger().info(\"Combat, movement, mining, automation and crash protection active.\");\n    }\n\n    private void reportLaunchDiagnostics() {\n        boolean commandReady = getCommand(\"chaosguard\") != null;\n        String databaseState = getConfig().getBoolean(\"database.enabled\", false) ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\";\n        getLogger().info(\"Launch diagnostics: command=\" + (commandReady ? \"bound\" : \"missing\")\n            + \", checks=\" + Check.enabledNames()\n            + \", bans=\" + activeBans.size()\n            + \", database=\" + databaseState + \".\");\n        if (!commandReady) {\n            getLogger().severe(\"Launch diagnostics: chaosguard command is missing from plugin.yml.\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void reloadRuntime() {\n        for (Profile profile : profiles.values()) {\n            profile.resetTransient();\n        }\n        queueSystemLog(\"RELOAD\", \"Configuration reloaded\");\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void onDisable() {\n        if (decayTask != null) {\n            decayTask.cancel();\n        }\n        if (logTask != null) {\n            logTask.cancel();\n        }\n        if (brandChannelRegistered) {\n            getServer().getMessenger().unregisterIncomingPluginChannel(this, \"minecraft:brand\", this);\n        }\n        queueSystemLog(\"STOP\", \"ChaosGuard disabled\");\n        saveViolationState();\n        flushLogs();\n        if (database != null) {\n            database.close();\n        }\n        profiles.clear();\n        alertToggles.clear();\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)\n    public void onJoin(PlayerJoinEvent event) {\n        Profile profile = profile(event.getPlayer());\n        profile.playerName = event.getPlayer().getName();\n        profile.lastSeenAt = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        profile.teleportGraceUntil = System.currentTimeMillis() + 4000L;\n        queuePlayerLog(event.getPlayer(), \"SESSION\", \"JOIN\", 0.0, 0.0,\n            \"ping=\" + event.getPlayer().getPing());\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)\n    public void onQuit(PlayerQuitEvent event) {\n        Profile profile = profiles.get(event.getPlayer().getUniqueId());\n        if (profile != null) {\n            profile.lastSeenAt = System.currentTimeMillis();\n            profile.resetTransient();\n            queuePlayerLog(event.getPlayer(), \"SESSION\", \"QUIT\", profile.totalVl(), 0.0,\n                \"checks=\" + profile.compactViolations());\n        }\n        alertToggles.remove(event.getPlayer().getUniqueId());\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST)\n    public void onPreLogin(AsyncPlayerPreLoginEvent event) {\n        BanRecord ban = activeBans.get(event.getUniqueId());\n        if (ban == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        event.disallow(AsyncPlayerPreLoginEvent.Result.KICK_BANNED,\n            color(\"&c&lChaosGuard\\n&7Du bist permanent gebannt.\\n&7Grund: &f\" + ban.reason));\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onTeleport(PlayerTeleportEvent event) {\n        Profile profile = profile(event.getPlayer());\n        profile.teleportGraceUntil = System.currentTimeMillis() + 2500L;\n        profile.lastLocation = event.getTo() == null ? null : event.getTo().clone();\n        profile.airTicks = 0;\n        profile.airbornePeakY = event.getTo() == null ? event.getFrom().getY() : event.getTo().getY();\n        profile.lastSolidSupport = true;\n        profile.velocityCheckSequence++;\n        profile.phaseBuffer = 0;\n        profile.elytraBuffer = 0;\n        profile.vehicleBuffer = 0;\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onCombat(EntityDamageByEntityEvent event) {\n        if (event.getEntity() instanceof Player victim && active(victim)) {\n            profile(victim).velocityGraceUntil = System.currentTimeMillis() + 1250L;\n        }\n        if (!(event.getDamager() instanceof Player attacker) || attacker.equals(event.getEntity())) {\n            return;\n        }\n        if (!active(attacker) || attacker.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE || attacker.getGameMode() == GameMode.SPECTATOR) {\n            return;\n        }\n\n        Profile profile = profile(attacker);\n        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        if (profile.inventoryOpenAt > 0L && now - profile.inventoryOpenAt > 250L) {\n            double vl = flag(attacker, Check.BAD_ACTIONS, 2.4,\n                \"attack-with-open-inventory open-ms=\" + (now - profile.inventoryOpenAt));\n            if (vl >= cancelVl()) {\n                event.setCancelled(true);\n            }\n        }\n        profile.attackTimes.addLast(now);\n        trim(profile.attackTimes, now - 1000L);\n        profile.recentTargets.put(event.getEntity().getUniqueId(), now);\n        profile.recentTargets.entrySet().removeIf(entry -> entry.getValue() < now - 350L);\n\n        double distance = distanceToBox(attacker.getEyeLocation(), event.getEntity());\n        double allowedReach = configDouble(\"checks.combat.max-reach\", 3.15)\n            + Math.min(0.35, Math.max(0, attacker.getPing()) / 1000.0);\n        if (distance > allowedReach) {\n            double vl = flag(attacker, Check.REACH, 2.0 + (distance - allowedReach) * 5.0,\n                \"distance=\" + fmt(distance) + \" allowed=\" + fmt(allowedReach) + \" ping=\" + attacker.getPing());\n            if (distance > allowedReach + 0.35 || vl >= cancelVl()) {\n                event.setCancelled(true);\n            }\n        }\n\n        Vector towardTarget = event.getEntity().getBoundingBox().getCenter().subtract(attacker.getEyeLocation().toVector());\n        if (towardTarget.lengthSquared() > 0.0001) {\n            double dot = clamp(attacker.getEyeLocation().getDirection().normalize().dot(towardTarget.normalize()), -1.0, 1.0);\n            double angle = Math.toDegrees(Math.acos(dot));\n            double maxAngle = configDouble(\"checks.combat.max-angle\", 72.0);\n            if (distance > 1.35 && angle > maxAngle) {\n                double vl = flag(attacker, Check.KILLAURA, 1.5 + (angle - maxAngle) / 20.0,\n                    \"angle=\" + fmt(angle) + \" distance=\" + fmt(distance));\n                if (angle > 105.0 || vl >= cancelVl()) {\n                    event.setCancelled(true);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (!attacker.hasLineOfSight(event.getEntity()) && distance > 1.6) {\n            double vl = flag(attacker, Check.KILLAURA, 1.4, \"hit-through-block distance=\" + fmt(distance));\n            if (vl >= cancelVl()) {\n                event.setCancelled(true);\n            }\n        }\n        if (event.isCritical() && attacker.getInventory().getItemInMainHand().getType() != Material.MACE) {\n            boolean physicallyGrounded = hasSolidSupport(attacker.getLocation());\n            boolean microFall = attacker.getFallDistance() < 0.025F\n                && Math.abs(profile.lastVerticalDelta) < 0.015;\n            if (physicallyGrounded || microFall) {\n                profile.criticalBuffer++;\n            } else {\n                profile.criticalBuffer = Math.max(0, profile.criticalBuffer - 1);\n            }\n            if (profile.criticalBuffer >= configInt(\"checks.combat.criticals-required-samples\", 3)) {\n                profile.criticalBuffer = 1;\n                flag(attacker, Check.CRITICALS, 1.6,\n                    \"critical=true support=\" + physicallyGrounded + \" fall=\" + fmt(attacker.getFallDistance())\n                        + \" dy=\" + fmt(profile.lastVerticalDelta));\n            }\n        }\n        if (profile.recentTargets.size() >= 3) {\n            double vl = flag(attacker, Check.KILLAURA, 2.2,\n                \"multi-target=\" + profile.recentTargets.size() + \"/350ms\");\n            if (vl >= cancelVl()) {\n                event.setCancelled(true);\n            }\n        }\n        int maxCps = configInt(\"checks.combat.max-cps\", 18);\n        if (profile.attackTimes.size() > maxCps) {\n            flag(attacker, Check.AUTOCLICKER, 1.0 + (profile.attackTimes.size() - maxCps) * 0.25,\n                \"successful-attacks=\" + profile.attackTimes.size() + \"/s\");\n        }\n        if (!event.isCancelled() && event.getEntity() instanceof Player victim && active(victim)) {\n            scheduleVelocityCheck(victim);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)\n    public void onDamage(EntityDamageEvent event) {\n        if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof Player player) || !active(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Profile profile = profile(player);\n        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        if (event.getCause() == EntityDamageEvent.DamageCause.FALL) {\n            profile.lastFallDamageAt = now;\n            profile.missedFallDamage = 0;\n        } else if (event.getCause() == EntityDamageEvent.DamageCause.ENTITY_ATTACK\n            || event.getCause() == EntityDamageEvent.DamageCause.ENTITY_EXPLOSION\n            || event.getCause() == EntityDamageEvent.DamageCause.BLOCK_EXPLOSION\n            || event.getCause() == EntityDamageEvent.DamageCause.PROJECTILE) {\n            profile.velocityGraceUntil = now + 1250L;\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onArmAnimation(PlayerAnimationEvent event) {\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n        if (!active(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Profile profile = profile(player);\n        int swings = rate(profile.swingTimes, 1000L);\n        int maximum = configInt(\"checks.combat.max-swing-cps\", 24);\n        if (swings > maximum && System.currentTimeMillis() - profile.lastSwingFlag > 1000L) {\n            profile.lastSwingFlag = System.currentTimeMillis();\n            flag(player, Check.AUTOCLICKER, 1.0 + (swings - maximum) * 0.15,\n                \"arm-swings=\" + swings + \"/s\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {\n        if (event.getTo() == null || !active(event.getPlayer())) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n        Profile profile = profile(player);\n        Location from = event.getFrom();\n        Location to = event.getTo();\n        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        if (!Double.isFinite(to.getX()) || !Double.isFinite(to.getY()) || !Double.isFinite(to.getZ())\n            || !Float.isFinite(to.getYaw()) || !Float.isFinite(to.getPitch())\n            || Math.abs(to.getX()) > 30_100_000.0\n            || Math.abs(to.getZ()) > 30_100_000.0) {\n            event.setTo(from);\n            crashBan(player, \"Ungueltige Bewegungsdaten\",\n                \"x=\" + to.getX() + \" y=\" + to.getY() + \" z=\" + to.getZ()\n                    + \" yaw=\" + to.getYaw() + \" pitch=\" + to.getPitch());\n            return;\n        }\n        if (Math.abs(to.getPitch()) > 90.01F) {\n            event.setTo(from);\n            flag(player, Check.INVALID_PAYLOAD, 4.0, \"invalid-pitch=\" + to.getPitch());\n            return;\n        }\n        if (!from.getWorld().equals(to.getWorld()) || now < profile.teleportGraceUntil) {\n            profile.lastLocation = to.clone();\n            profile.lastMoveAt = now;\n            return;\n        }\n        int movementRate = rate(profile.moveTimes, 1000L);\n        sampleTimer(player, profile, movementRate, now);\n        double dx = to.getX() - from.getX();\n        double dz = to.getZ() - from.getZ();\n        double horizontal = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz);\n        double vertical = to.getY() - from.getY();\n        long elapsed = profile.lastMoveAt == 0L ? 50L : Math.max(25L, Math.min(250L, now - profile.lastMoveAt));\n        if (horizontal > 12.0 || Math.abs(vertical) > 8.0) {\n            profile.teleportGraceUntil = now + 1500L;\n            return;\n        }\n\n        if (player.isGliding() && now >= profile.velocityGraceUntil) {\n            double allowedElytra = configDouble(\"checks.movement.elytra-max-blocks-per-tick\", 4.25)\n                * (elapsed / 50.0);\n            if (horizontal > allowedElytra || Math.abs(vertical) > allowedElytra) {\n                profile.elytraBuffer++;\n            } else {\n                profile.elytraBuffer = Math.max(0, profile.elytraBuffer - 1);\n            }\n            if (profile.elytraBuffer >= configInt(\"checks.movement.elytra-required-samples\", 3)) {\n                profile.elytraBuffer = 1;\n                flag(player, Check.ELYTRA, 1.5,\n                    \"horizontal=\" + fmt(horizontal) + \" vertical=\" + fmt(vertical)\n                        + \" allowed=\" + fmt(allowedElytra));\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (player.isInsideVehicle() && player.getVehicle() != null) {\n            double allowedVehicle = configDouble(\"checks.movement.vehicle-max-blocks-per-tick\", 4.0)\n                * (elapsed / 50.0);\n            if (horizontal > allowedVehicle || Math.abs(vertical) > allowedVehicle) {\n                profile.vehicleBuffer++;\n            } else {\n                profile.vehicleBuffer = Math.max(0, profile.vehicleBuffer - 1);\n            }\n            if (profile.vehicleBuffer >= configInt(\"checks.movement.vehicle-required-samples\", 3)) {\n                profile.vehicleBuffer = 1;\n                flag(player, Check.VEHICLE, 1.6,\n                    \"type=\" + player.getVehicle().getType() + \" horizontal=\" + fmt(horizontal)\n                        + \" vertical=\" + fmt(vertical) + \" allowed=\" + fmt(allowedVehicle));\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (now >= profile.velocityGraceUntil && !player.isInsideVehicle()\n            && player.getGameMode() != GameMode.SPECTATOR && horizontal + Math.abs(vertical) > 0.04) {\n            boolean fromCollision = collidesWithSolid(player, from);\n            boolean toCollision = collidesWithSolid(player, to);\n            if (toCollision && !fromCollision) {\n                profile.phaseBuffer++;\n            } else if (!toCollision) {\n                profile.phaseBuffer = Math.max(0, profile.phaseBuffer - 1);\n            }\n            if (profile.phaseBuffer >= configInt(\"checks.movement.phase-required-samples\", 2)) {\n                profile.phaseBuffer = 0;\n                double vl = flag(player, Check.PHASE, 2.2,\n                    \"entered-solid at=\" + to.getBlockX() + \",\" + to.getBlockY() + \",\" + to.getBlockZ());\n                if (vl >= cancelVl() && profile.lastGroundLocation != null) {\n                    event.setTo(profile.lastGroundLocation);\n                    profile.teleportGraceUntil = now + 1000L;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (profile.inventoryOpenAt > 0L && now - profile.inventoryOpenAt > 500L\n            && now >= profile.velocityGraceUntil && horizontal > configDouble(\"checks.movement.inventory-max-speed\", 0.13)\n            && !player.isInsideVehicle() && !onIce(player)) {\n            profile.inventoryMoveTicks++;\n        } else {\n            profile.inventoryMoveTicks = Math.max(0, profile.inventoryMoveTicks - 2);\n        }\n        if (profile.inventoryMoveTicks >= configInt(\"checks.movement.inventory-required-samples\", 8)\n            && now - profile.lastInventoryMoveFlag > 1200L) {\n            profile.lastInventoryMoveFlag = now;\n            profile.inventoryMoveTicks = 3;\n            flag(player, Check.INVENTORY_MOVE, 1.4,\n                \"horizontal=\" + fmt(horizontal) + \" open-ms=\" + (now - profile.inventoryOpenAt));\n        }\n\n        boolean movementExempt = player.getAllowFlight() || player.isFlying() || player.isGliding()\n            || player.isSwimming() || player.isRiptiding() || player.isInsideVehicle()\n            || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.SPECTATOR;\n        if (!movementExempt) {\n            double speedAmplifier = potionMultiplier(player, PotionEffectType.SPEED, 0.20);\n            double base = hasSolidSupport(to) ? 0.78 : 0.92;\n            double allowed = base * speedAmplifier * Math.max(0.5, player.getWalkSpeed() / 0.2) * (elapsed / 50.0);\n            if (onIce(player) || Math.abs(player.getVelocity().getX()) + Math.abs(player.getVelocity().getZ()) > 1.15) {\n                allowed *= 2.4;\n            }\n            if (horizontal > allowed && horizontal > 0.82 && now >= profile.velocityGraceUntil) {\n                double vl = flag(player, Check.SPEED, 0.8 + (horizontal - allowed) * 2.0,\n                    \"horizontal=\" + fmt(horizontal) + \" allowed=\" + fmt(allowed) + \" dt=\" + elapsed);\n                if (vl >= cancelVl() + 4.0) {\n                    event.setTo(from);\n                }\n            }\n\n            double maxStep = configDouble(\"checks.movement.max-step-height\", 0.85);\n            if (vertical > maxStep && vertical < 2.5 && now >= profile.velocityGraceUntil\n                && !hasEffect(player, PotionEffectType.JUMP_BOOST) && !naturalVerticalBoost(from, to)) {\n                double vl = flag(player, Check.STEP, 1.4 + (vertical - maxStep),\n                    \"vertical=\" + fmt(vertical) + \" max=\" + fmt(maxStep));\n                if (vl >= cancelVl() && profile.lastGroundLocation != null) {\n                    event.setTo(profile.lastGroundLocation);\n                    profile.teleportGraceUntil = now + 750L;\n                }\n            }\n\n            boolean liquidSurface = isOnLiquidSurface(to);\n            if (liquidSurface && horizontal > 0.04 && Math.abs(vertical) < 0.025) {\n                profile.liquidSurfaceTicks++;\n            } else {\n                profile.liquidSurfaceTicks = Math.max(0, profile.liquidSurfaceTicks - 2);\n            }\n            if (profile.liquidSurfaceTicks > configInt(\"checks.movement.max-liquid-surface-ticks\", 10)\n                && now - profile.lastJesusFlag > 1200L) {\n                profile.lastJesusFlag = now;\n                double vl = flag(player, Check.JESUS, 1.5,\n                    \"surface-ticks=\" + profile.liquidSurfaceTicks + \" horizontal=\" + fmt(horizontal));\n                if (vl >= cancelVl()) {\n                    event.setTo(from);\n                }\n            }\n\n            if (player.isBlocking() && horizontal > configDouble(\"checks.movement.blocking-max-speed\", 0.22)\n                && now >= profile.velocityGraceUntil && !onIce(player)) {\n                profile.noSlowTicks++;\n            } else {\n                profile.noSlowTicks = Math.max(0, profile.noSlowTicks - 2);\n            }\n            if (profile.noSlowTicks > 8 && now - profile.lastNoSlowFlag > 1200L) {\n                profile.lastNoSlowFlag = now;\n                flag(player, Check.NOSLOW, 1.2,\n                    \"blocking-speed=\" + fmt(horizontal) + \" ticks=\" + profile.noSlowTicks);\n            }\n\n            boolean support = hasSupport(to);\n            if (!support && vertical > -0.025\n                && !hasEffect(player, PotionEffectType.LEVITATION)\n                && !hasEffect(player, PotionEffectType.SLOW_FALLING)) {\n                profile.airTicks++;\n            } else if (support || vertical < -0.08) {\n                profile.airTicks = 0;\n            }\n            int maxAirTicks = configInt(\"checks.movement.max-hover-ticks\", 16);\n            if (profile.airTicks > maxAirTicks) {\n                double vl = flag(player, Check.FLIGHT, 1.25,\n                    \"hover-ticks=\" + profile.airTicks + \" vertical=\" + fmt(vertical));\n                if (vl >= cancelVl() + 2.0 && profile.lastGroundLocation != null) {\n                    event.setTo(profile.lastGroundLocation);\n                    profile.teleportGraceUntil = now + 1000L;\n                }\n            }\n        } else {\n            profile.airTicks = 0;\n            profile.liquidSurfaceTicks = 0;\n            profile.noSlowTicks = 0;\n        }\n        boolean solidSupport = hasSolidSupport(to);\n        trackExpectedFall(player, profile, to, solidSupport, movementExempt, now);\n        if (solidSupport) {\n            profile.lastGroundLocation = to.clone();\n        }\n        profile.lastLocation = to.clone();\n        profile.lastMoveAt = now;\n        profile.lastYaw = to.getYaw();\n        profile.lastPitch = to.getPitch();\n        profile.lastVerticalDelta = vertical;\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onVelocity(PlayerVelocityEvent event) {\n        if (active(event.getPlayer())) {\n            profile(event.getPlayer()).velocityGraceUntil = System.currentTimeMillis() + 1250L;\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onBlockBreak(BlockBreakEvent event) {\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n        if (!active(player) || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Profile profile = profile(player);\n        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        double breakDistance = distanceToBlock(player.getEyeLocation(), event.getBlock());\n        double allowedBreakReach = configDouble(\"checks.fastbreak.max-break-reach\", 5.15)\n            + Math.min(0.30, Math.max(0, player.getPing()) / 1200.0);\n        if (breakDistance > allowedBreakReach) {\n            double vl = flag(player, Check.BREAK_REACH, 2.0 + (breakDistance - allowedBreakReach) * 4.0,\n                \"distance=\" + fmt(breakDistance) + \" allowed=\" + fmt(allowedBreakReach));\n            if (breakDistance > allowedBreakReach + 0.35 || vl >= cancelVl()) {\n                event.setCancelled(true);\n                return;\n            }\n        }\n\n        float progressPerTick = event.getBlock().getBreakSpeed(player);\n        if (progressPerTick > 0.0F && progressPerTick < 1.0F && profile.lastBreakAt > 0L) {\n            long expectedMillis = Math.min(5000L, (long)Math.ceil(1.0 / progressPerTick) * 50L);\n            long actualMillis = now - profile.lastBreakAt;\n            if (expectedMillis >= 150L\n                && actualMillis < expectedMillis * configDouble(\"checks.fastbreak.minimum-time-ratio\", 0.32)) {\n                profile.fastBreakBuffer++;\n            } else {\n                profile.fastBreakBuffer = Math.max(0, profile.fastBreakBuffer - 1);\n            }\n            if (profile.fastBreakBuffer >= configInt(\"checks.fastbreak.required-samples\", 4)) {\n                profile.fastBreakBuffer = 1;\n                double vl = flag(player, Check.FASTBREAK, 1.7,\n                    \"actual-ms=\" + actualMillis + \" expected-ms=\" + expectedMillis\n                        + \" progress=\" + fmt(progressPerTick));\n                if (vl >= cancelVl()) {\n                    event.setCancelled(true);\n                    return;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        profile.lastBreakAt = now;\n        profile.breakTimes.addLast(now);\n        trim(profile.breakTimes, now - 1000L);\n        int maxBlocks = configInt(\"checks.fastbreak.max-blocks-per-second\", 14);\n        if (profile.breakTimes.size() > maxBlocks && MINING_BLOCKS.contains(event.getBlock().getType())) {\n            double vl = flag(player, Check.FASTBREAK, 1.0 + (profile.breakTimes.size() - maxBlocks) * 0.20,\n                \"blocks=\" + profile.breakTimes.size() + \"/s tool=\" + player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand().getType());\n            if (vl >= cancelVl()) {\n                event.setCancelled(true);\n                return;\n            }\n        }\n\n        Material material = event.getBlock().getType();\n        if (MINING_BLOCKS.contains(material)) {\n            profile.minedBaseBlocks++;\n        }\n        Double oreWeight = ORE_WEIGHTS.get(material);\n        if (oreWeight == null || alreadyCountedVein(profile, event.getBlock())) {\n            return;\n        }\n        rememberOre(profile, event.getBlock());\n        boolean exposed = isExposed(event.getBlock());\n        double weight = exposed ? oreWeight * 0.15 : oreWeight;\n        profile.rareOreVeins++;\n        profile.xrayScore += weight;\n        int minBlocks = configInt(\"checks.xray.minimum-mined-blocks\", 48);\n        int minVeins = configInt(\"checks.xray.minimum-rare-veins\", 5);\n        double threshold = configDouble(\"checks.xray.score-threshold\", 8.5);\n        double normalized = profile.xrayScore / Math.max(1.0, profile.minedBaseBlocks / 48.0);\n        if (!exposed && profile.minedBaseBlocks >= minBlocks && profile.rareOreVeins >= minVeins\n            && normalized > threshold && now - profile.lastXrayFlag > 3000L) {\n            profile.lastXrayFlag = now;\n            flag(player, Check.XRAY, Math.min(4.0, 1.0 + (normalized - threshold) / 2.0),\n                \"ore=\" + material + \" hidden=true veins=\" + profile.rareOreVeins\n                    + \" mined=\" + profile.minedBaseBlocks + \" score=\" + fmt(normalized));\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onBlockPlace(BlockPlaceEvent event) {\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n        if (!active(player) || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Profile profile = profile(player);\n        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        profile.placeTimes.addLast(now);\n        trim(profile.placeTimes, now - 1000L);\n        int fastPlaceLimit = configInt(\"checks.fastplace.max-blocks-per-second\", 16);\n        if (profile.placeTimes.size() > fastPlaceLimit) {\n            double vl = flag(player, Check.FASTPLACE,\n                1.0 + (profile.placeTimes.size() - fastPlaceLimit) * 0.20,\n                \"places=\" + profile.placeTimes.size() + \"/s block=\" + event.getBlockPlaced().getType());\n            if (vl >= cancelVl()) {\n                event.setCancelled(true);\n                return;\n            }\n        }\n        Location location = event.getBlockPlaced().getLocation().add(0.5, 0.5, 0.5);\n        boolean underPlayer = location.getY() <= player.getLocation().getY()\n            && horizontalDistanceSquared(location, player.getLocation()) < 2.4;\n        int maxPlaces = configInt(\"checks.scaffold.max-places-per-second\", 9);\n        if (underPlayer && profile.placeTimes.size() > maxPlaces && player.getLocation().getPitch() < 45.0F) {\n            double vl = flag(player, Check.SCAFFOLD, 1.4,\n                \"places=\" + profile.placeTimes.size() + \"/s pitch=\" + fmt(player.getLocation().getPitch()));\n            if (vl >= cancelVl()) {\n                event.setCancelled(true);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onInteract(PlayerInteractEvent event) {\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n        if (!active(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Profile profile = profile(player);\n        int interactions = rate(profile.interactTimes, 1000L);\n        int cancelRate = configInt(\"checks.crash.interactions-per-second\", 85);\n        int banRate = configInt(\"checks.crash.interaction-ban-rate\", 240);\n        if (interactions > cancelRate) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            flag(player, Check.PACKET_SPAM, 1.5,\n                \"interactions=\" + interactions + \"/s action=\" + event.getAction());\n        }\n        if (interactions > banRate) {\n            crashBan(player, \"Interact-Paketflut\", \"interactions=\" + interactions + \"/s\");\n            return;\n        }\n        if ((event.getAction() == Action.RIGHT_CLICK_AIR || event.getAction() == Action.RIGHT_CLICK_BLOCK)\n            && event.getItem() != null && isTrackedUseItem(event.getItem().getType())) {\n            profile.useStartedAt = System.currentTimeMillis();\n            profile.useMaterial = event.getItem().getType();\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onConsume(PlayerItemConsumeEvent event) {\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n        if (!active(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Profile profile = profile(player);\n        Material material = event.getItem().getType();\n        if (profile.useStartedAt <= 0L || profile.useMaterial != material) {\n            return;\n        }\n        long elapsed = System.currentTimeMillis() - profile.useStartedAt;\n        long minimum = material == Material.DRIED_KELP\n            ? configInt(\"checks.fastuse.dried-kelp-minimum-ms\", 650)\n            : configInt(\"checks.fastuse.minimum-consume-ms\", 1150);\n        profile.useStartedAt = 0L;\n        if (elapsed < minimum) {\n            double vl = flag(player, Check.FASTUSE, 2.0,\n                \"item=\" + material + \" elapsed-ms=\" + elapsed + \" minimum-ms=\" + minimum);\n            if (vl >= cancelVl()) {\n                event.setCancelled(true);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onBowShoot(EntityShootBowEvent event) {\n        if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof Player player) || !active(player) || event.getBow() == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Profile profile = profile(player);\n        Material type = event.getBow().getType();\n        if (type != Material.BOW || profile.useMaterial != Material.BOW || profile.useStartedAt <= 0L) {\n            return;\n        }\n        long elapsed = System.currentTimeMillis() -
