true)\n    public void onSignChange(SignChangeEvent event) {\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n        if (!active(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        int total = 0;\n        int longest = 0;\n        for (String line : event.getLines()) {\n            if (line != null) {\n                total += line.length();\n                longest = Math.max(longest, line.length());\n            }\n        }\n        if (total > configInt(\"checks.crash.sign-hard-limit\", 8192) || longest > 2048) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            crashBan(player, \"Ungueltige Schilddaten\", \"total=\" + total + \" longest=\" + longest);\n        } else if (total > configInt(\"checks.crash.sign-soft-limit\", 1536)) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            flag(player, Check.INVALID_PAYLOAD, 4.0,\n                \"large-sign total=\" + total + \" longest=\" + longest);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onTabComplete(TabCompleteEvent event) {\n        if (!(event.getSender() instanceof Player player) || !active(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Profile profile = profile(player);\n        int requests = rate(profile.tabTimes, 1000L);\n        int maximum = configInt(\"checks.crash.tab-completes-per-second\", 24);\n        if (requests > maximum || event.getBuffer().length() > configInt(\"checks.crash.tab-buffer-limit\", 1024)) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            flag(player, Check.PACKET_SPAM, 2.0,\n                \"tab-requests=\" + requests + \"/s buffer=\" + event.getBuffer().length());\n        }\n        if (requests > configInt(\"checks.crash.tab-ban-rate\", 140)) {\n            crashBan(player, \"TabComplete-Paketflut\", \"requests=\" + requests + \"/s\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onInventoryClick(InventoryClickEvent event) {\n        if (!(event.getWhoClicked() instanceof Player player) || !active(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Profile profile = profile(player);\n        int count = rate(profile.inventoryTimes, 1000L);\n        int cancelRate = configInt(\"checks.crash.inventory-clicks-per-second\", 65);\n        int banRate = configInt(\"checks.crash.inventory-ban-rate\", 180);\n        if (count > cancelRate) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            flag(player, Check.PACKET_SPAM, Math.min(5.0, 1.0 + (count - cancelRate) / 15.0),\n                \"inventory-clicks=\" + count + \"/s\");\n        }\n        if (count > banRate) {\n            crashBan(player, \"Inventory-Paketflut\", \"inventory-clicks=\" + count + \"/s\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onDrop(PlayerDropItemEvent event) {\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n        if (!active(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Profile profile = profile(player);\n        int count = rate(profile.dropTimes, 1000L);\n        int cancelRate = configInt(\"checks.crash.drops-per-second\", 70);\n        int banRate = configInt(\"checks.crash.drop-ban-rate\", 220);\n        if (count > cancelRate) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            flag(player, Check.PACKET_SPAM, 1.5, \"drops=\" + count + \"/s\");\n        }\n        if (count > banRate) {\n            crashBan(player, \"Drop-Paketflut\", \"drops=\" + count + \"/s\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onCommand(PlayerCommandPreprocessEvent event) {\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n        if (!active(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        String message = event.getMessage();\n        int hardLimit = configInt(\"checks.crash.command-hard-limit\", 8192);\n        int softLimit = configInt(\"checks.crash.command-soft-limit\", 1024);\n        if (message.length() > hardLimit || containsControlCharacters(message)) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            crashBan(player, \"Manipuliertes Command-Paket\", \"length=\" + message.length());\n            return;\n        }\n        Profile profile = profile(player);\n        int count = rate(profile.commandTimes, 1000L);\n        if (message.length() > softLimit || count > configInt(\"checks.crash.commands-per-second\", 35)) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            flag(player, Check.PACKET_SPAM, 2.5,\n                \"commands=\" + count + \"/s length=\" + message.length());\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onBookEdit(PlayerEditBookEvent event) {\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n        if (!active(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        List<String> pages = event.getNewBookMeta().getPages();\n        int total = 0;\n        int longest = 0;\n        for (String page : pages) {\n            total += page.length();\n            longest = Math.max(longest, page.length());\n        }\n        if (pages.size() > 100 || longest > 1024) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            crashBan(player, \"Ungueltige Buchdaten\",\n                \"pages=\" + pages.size() + \" longest=\" + longest + \" total=\" + total);\n        } else if (total > configInt(\"checks.crash.book-soft-limit\", 50000)) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            flag(player, Check.INVALID_PAYLOAD, 4.0,\n                \"large-book pages=\" + pages.size() + \" total=\" + total);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.LOWEST)\n    public void onPaperKick(PlayerKickEvent event) {\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n        if (!active(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        PlayerKickEvent.Cause cause = event.getCause();\n        String reason = safe(event.getReason(), 240);\n        boolean invalidExploit = cause == PlayerKickEvent.Cause.INVALID_PAYLOAD\n            || cause == PlayerKickEvent.Cause.INVALID_ENTITY_ATTACKED\n            || cause == PlayerKickEvent.Cause.SELF_INTERACTION;\n        boolean packetLimiter = cause == PlayerKickEvent.Cause.SPAM\n            && (reason.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).contains(\"packet\")\n                || reason.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).contains(\"exceeded\"));\n        if (invalidExploit && configBoolean(\"checks.crash.ban-on-paper-invalid-payload\", true)) {\n            crashBan(player, \"Paper blockierte ein ungueltiges Paket\",\n                \"cause=\" + cause + \" reason=\" + reason);\n        } else if (packetLimiter && configBoolean(\"checks.crash.ban-on-packet-limiter\", true)) {\n            crashBan(player, \"Paper Packet-Limiter ausgeloest\",\n                \"cause=\" + cause + \" reason=\" + reason);\n        } else if (cause == PlayerKickEvent.Cause.INVALID_PLAYER_MOVEMENT\n            || cause == PlayerKickEvent.Cause.INVALID_VEHICLE_MOVEMENT) {\n            flag(player, Check.INVALID_PAYLOAD, 4.0,\n                \"paper-kick=\" + cause + \" reason=\" + reason);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)\n    public void onChannelRegister(PlayerRegisterChannelEvent event) {\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n        if (!active(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        String channel = event.getChannel().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n        Profile profile = profile(player);\n        int registrations = rate(profile.channelTimes, 1000L);\n        profile.registeredChannels.add(channel);\n        if (channel.length() > 128 || registrations > configInt(\"checks.crash.channel-ban-rate\", 140)) {\n            crashBan(player, \"Ungueltige Plugin-Channel-Daten\",\n                \"length=\" + channel.length() + \" registrations=\" + registrations + \"/s\");\n            return;\n        }\n        if (registrations > configInt(\"checks.crash.channels-per-second\", 35)\n            || profile.registeredChannels.size() > configInt(\"checks.crash.maximum-registered-channels\", 256)) {\n            flag(player, Check.PACKET_SPAM, 2.5,\n                \"channel-registrations=\" + registrations + \"/s unique=\" + profile.registeredChannels.size());\n        }\n        if (SUSPICIOUS_BRANDS.stream().anyMatch(channel::contains)) {\n            flag(player, Check.CLIENT, 1.0, \"suspicious-channel=\" + safe(channel, 120));\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void onPluginMessageReceived(String channel, Player player, byte[] message) {\n        if (!\"minecraft:brand\".equals(channel) || !active(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        if (message.length > 1024) {\n            crashBan(player, \"Ungueltige Client-Brand\", \"payload-bytes=\" + message.length);\n            return;\n        }\n        String brand = decodeBrand(message).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n        profile(player).clientBrand = safe(brand, 80);\n        queuePlayerLog(player, \"CLIENT\", \"BRAND\", 0.0, 0.0, \"brand=\" + safe(brand, 120));\n        if (SUSPICIOUS_BRANDS.stream().anyMatch(brand::contains)) {\n            flag(player, Check.CLIENT, 2.0, \"suspicious-brand=\" + safe(brand, 120));\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean onCommand(CommandSender sender, Command command, String label, String[] args) {\n        if (!sender.hasPermission(\"chaosguard.admin\")) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(PREFIX + \"&cKeine Berechtigung.\"));\n            return true;\n        }\n        if (args.length == 0 || \"status\".equalsIgnoreCase(args[0])) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(\"&8&m--------------------------------------------------\"));\n            sender.sendMessage(color(\"&c&lChaosGuard &7- Status\"));\n            sender.sendMessage(color(\"&7Aktiv: \" + (enabled() ? \"&aJa\" : \"&cNein\")\n                + \" &8| &7Profile: &f\" + profiles.size() + \" &8| &7Banns: &f\" + activeBans.size()\n                + \" &8| &7Logqueue: &f\" + pendingLogLines.size()));\n            sender.sendMessage(color(\"&7Checks: &f\" + Check.values().length + \" registriert &8| &7TPS: &f\"\n                + fmt(Bukkit.getTPS()[0])));\n            sender.sendMessage(color(\"&7Log: &fplugins/ChaosGuard/logs/\"));\n            sender.sendMessage(color(\"&8&m--------------------------------------------------\"));\n            return true;\n        }\n        if (\"alerts\".equalsIgnoreCase(args[0]) && sender instanceof Player player) {\n            boolean alwaysOn = configBoolean(\"alerts-always-on\", true);\n            boolean enabledNow;\n            if (!alertToggles.add(player.getUniqueId())) {\n                alertToggles.remove(player.getUniqueId());\n                enabledNow = alwaysOn;\n            } else {\n                enabledNow = !alwaysOn;\n            }\n            sender.sendMessage(color(PREFIX + (enabledNow ? \"&aAlerts aktiviert.\" : \"&7Alerts deaktiviert.\")));\n            return true;\n        }\n        if (\"checks\".equalsIgnoreCase(args[0])) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(PREFIX + \"&fCheck-Status\"));\n            StringBuilder line = new StringBuilder();\n            for (Check check : Check.values()) {\n                String entry = (isCheckEnabled(check) ? \"&a\" : \"&c\") + check.display + \" &8| \";\n                if (line.length() + entry.length() > 95) {\n                    sender.sendMessage(color(line.toString()));\n                    line.setLength(0);\n                }\n                line.append(entry);\n            }\n            if (!line.isEmpty()) {\n                sender.sendMessage(color(line.toString()));\n            }\n            return true;\n        }\n        if (\"top\".equalsIgnoreCase(args[0])) {\n            List<Map.Entry<UUID, Profile>> entries = new ArrayList<>(profiles.entrySet());\n            entries.sort((first, second) -> Double.compare(second.getValue().totalVl(), first.getValue().totalVl()));\n            sender.sendMessage(color(PREFIX + \"&cTop Verdächtige &7(aktuelle Session)\"));\n            int shown = 0;\n            for (Map.Entry<UUID, Profile> entry : entries) {\n                if (entry.getValue().totalVl() <= 0.01 || shown >= 8) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                Player target = Bukkit.getPlayer(entry.getKey());\n                String name = target == null ? entry.getValue().playerName : target.getName();\n                sender.sendMessage(color(\"&8\" + (++shown) + \". &f\" + name + \" &8- &e\"\n                    + fmt(entry.getValue().totalVl()) + \" VL &7\" + entry.getValue().compactViolations()));\n            }\n            if (shown == 0) {\n                sender.sendMessage(color(\"&7Keine aktiven Verstöße.\"));\n            }\n            return true;\n        }\n        if (\"inspect\".equalsIgnoreCase(args[0]) && args.length >= 2) {\n            Player target = Bukkit.getPlayerExact(args[1]);\n            Profile profile = target == null ? findProfile(args[1]) : profile(target);\n            if (profile == null) {\n                sender.sendMessage(color(PREFIX + \"&cKein aktives oder aufbewahrtes Profil gefunden.\"));\n                return true;\n            }\n            sender.sendMessage(color(PREFIX + \"&f\" + profile.playerName + \" &7Status=&f\"\n                + (target == null ? \"offline/retained\" : \"online\") + \" &7Ping=&f\" + (target == null ? \"-\" : target.getPing())\n                + \" &7Brand=&f\" + profile.clientBrand + \" &7VL=&e\" + fmt(profile.totalVl())));\n            sender.sendMessage(color(\"&7Checks: &f\" + profile.compactViolations()));\n            sender.sendMessage(color(\"&7Mining: &f\" + profile.minedBaseBlocks + \" Blöcke, \"\n                + profile.rareOreVeins + \" seltene Adern, Score \" + fmt(profile.xrayScore)));\n            for (String recent : profile.recentFlags) {\n                sender.sendMessage(color(\"&8- &7\" + recent));\n            }\n            return true;\n        }\n        if (\"reset\".equalsIgnoreCase(args[0]) && args.length >= 2) {\n            Player target = Bukkit.getPlayerExact(args[1]);\n            Profile profile = target == null ? findProfile(args[1]) : profile(target);\n            if (profile == null) {\n                sender.sendMessage(color(PREFIX + \"&cKein Profil gefunden.\"));\n                return true;\n            }\n            profile.clearAll();\n            queueSystemLog(\"RESET\", \"actor=\" + sender.getName() + \" player=\" + profile.playerName);\n            sender.sendMessage(color(PREFIX + \"&aProfil von &e\" + profile.playerName + \" &azurückgesetzt.\"));\n            return true;\n        }\n        if (\"unban\".equalsIgnoreCase(args[0]) && args.length >= 2) {\n            BanRecord removed = removeBan(args[1]);\n            if (removed == null) {\n                sender.sendMessage(color(PREFIX + \"&cKein ChaosGuard-Bann für &f\" + args[1] + \" &cgefunden.\"));\n            } else {\n                sender.sendMessage(color(PREFIX + \"&aBann von &e\" + removed.name + \" &aaufgehoben.\"));\n                queueSystemLog(\"UNBAN\", \"actor=\" + sender.getName() + \" player=\" + removed.name);\n            }\n            return true;\n        }\n        if (\"reload\".equalsIgnoreCase(args[0])) {\n            reloadConfig();\n            getConfig().options().copyDefaults(true);\n            saveConfig();\n            reloadRuntime();\n            sender.sendMessage(color(PREFIX + \"&aKonfiguration neu geladen.\"));\n            return true;\n        }\n        sender.sendMessage(color(PREFIX + \"&e/\" + label + \" <status|checks|alerts|top|inspect Spieler|reset Spieler|unban Spieler|reload>\"));\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public List<String> onTabComplete(CommandSender sender, Command command, String alias, String[] args) {\n        if (!sender.hasPermission(\"chaosguard.admin\")) {\n            return Collections.emptyList();\n        }\n        if (args.length == 1) {\n            return filter(Arrays.asList(\"status\", \"checks\", \"alerts\", \"top\", \"inspect\", \"reset\", \"unban\", \"reload\"), args[0]);\n        }\n        if (args.length == 2 && (\"inspect\".equalsIgnoreCase(args[0]) || \"reset\".equalsIgnoreCase(args[0]))) {\n            List<String> names = new ArrayList<>();\n            for (Player player : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {\n                names.add(player.getName());\n            }\n            for (Profile profile : profiles.values()) {\n                if (!names.contains(profile.playerName)) {\n                    names.add(profile.playerName);\n                }\n            }\n            return filter(names, args[1]);\n        }\n        if (args.length == 2 && \"unban\".equalsIgnoreCase(args[0])) {\n            List<String> names = new ArrayList<>();\n            for (BanRecord ban : activeBans.values()) {\n                names.add(ban.name);\n            }\n            return filter(names, args[1]);\n        }\n        return Collections.emptyList();\n    }\n\n    private double flag(Player player, Check check, double amount, String evidence) {\n        if (!active(player) || amount <= 0.0 || !isCheckEnabled(check)) {\n            return 0.0;\n        }\n        if (check.lagSensitive && Bukkit.getTPS()[0] < configDouble(\"minimum-tps-for-realtime-checks\", 17.5)) {\n            return 0.0;\n        }\n        Profile profile = profile(player);\n        double vl = profile.violations.merge(check, amount, Double::sum);\n        profile.recentFlags.addFirst(check.display + \" (\" + fmt(vl) + \" VL): \" + safe(evidence, 140));\n        while (profile.recentFlags.size() > 8) {\n            profile.recentFlags.removeLast();\n        }\n        queuePlayerLog(player, check.name(), \"FLAG\", vl, amount, evidence);\n        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        long lastAlert = profile.lastAlerts.getOrDefault(check, 0L);\n        if (vl >= configDouble(\"punishments.alert-vl\", 2.0) && now - lastAlert >= 1000L) {\n            profile.lastAlerts.put(check, now);\n            String alert = color(PREFIX + \"&f\" + player.getName() + \" &7failed &c\" + check.display\n                + \" &8[&e\" + fmt(vl) + \" VL&8] &7\" + safe(evidence, 180));\n            getLogger().warning(ChatColor.stripColor(alert));\n            for (Player staff : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {\n                if (staff.hasPermission(\"chaosguard.alerts\")\n                    && receivesAlerts(staff)) {\n                    staff.sendMessage(alert);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        maybePunish(player, check, vl, evidence);\n        return vl;\n    }\n\n    private boolean isCheckEnabled(Check check) {\n        return configBoolean(\"check-enabled.\" + check.name().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT), true);\n    }\n\n    private void maybePunish(Player player, Check check, double vl, String evidence) {\n        Profile profile = profile(player);\n        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        if (now - profile.lastPunishment < 5000L || check == Check.CLIENT || check == Check.XRAY) {\n            return;\n        }\n        double banVl = configDouble(\"punishments.ban-vl\", 60.0);\n        if (configBoolean(\"punishments.auto-ban-cheats\", true) && vl >= banVl) {\n            profile.lastPunishment = now;\n            queuePlayerLog(player, check.name(), \"BAN\", vl, 0.0, evidence);\n            banPlayer(player, \"ChaosGuard: \" + check.display + \" (VL \" + fmt(vl) + \")\");\n            return;\n        }\n        double kickVl = configDouble(\"punishments.kick-vl\", 28.0);\n        if (vl >= kickVl) {\n            profile.lastPunishment = now;\n            queuePlayerLog(player, check.name(), \"KICK\", vl, 0.0, evidence);\n            player.kickPlayer(color(\"&cChaosGuard\\n&7Unzulässiges Spielverhalten erkannt: &f\" + check.display));\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void crashBan(Player player, String reason, String evidence) {\n        if (!active(player) || !isCheckEnabled(Check.CRASH) || profile(player).crashBanned) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Profile profile = profile(player);\n        profile.crashBanned = true;\n        profile.violations.put(Check.CRASH, 100.0);\n        queuePlayerLog(player, Check.CRASH.name(), \"DIRECT_BAN\", 100.0, 100.0,\n            reason + \" \" + evidence);\n        String alert = color(PREFIX + \"&4&lCRASH-VERSUCH &8| &f\" + player.getName()\n            + \" &7wurde direkt gebannt. &c\" + reason + \" &8(\" + safe(evidence, 160) + \")\");\n        getLogger().severe(ChatColor.stripColor(alert));\n        for (Player staff : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {\n            if (staff.hasPermission(\"chaosguard.alerts\")) {\n                staff.sendMessage(alert);\n            }\n        }\n        banPlayer(player, \"ChaosGuard: Crash-Versuch - \" + reason);\n    }\n\n    private void loadBans() {\n        if (!getDataFolder().exists() && !getDataFolder().mkdirs()) {\n            getLogger().warning(\"Plugin-Datenordner konnte nicht erstellt werden.\");\n        }\n        banFile = new File(getDataFolder(), \"bans.yml\");\n        banConfig = YamlConfiguration.loadConfiguration(banFile);\n        if (!banFile.exists()) {\n            saveBans();\n        }\n        activeBans.clear();\n        ConfigurationSection section = banConfig.getConfigurationSection(\"bans\");\n        if (section == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        for (String key : section.getKeys(false)) {\n            try {\n                UUID uuid = UUID.fromString(key);\n                String path = \"bans.\" + key;\n                activeBans.put(uuid, new BanRecord(uuid,\n                    banConfig.getString(path + \".name\", key),\n                    banConfig.getString(path + \".reason\", \"ChaosGuard-Verstoss\"),\n                    banConfig.getLong(path + \".created-at\", 0L)));\n            } catch (IllegalArgumentException exception) {\n                getLogger().warning(\"Ungueltige UUID in bans.yml: \" + key);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void banPlayer(Player player, String reason) {\n        long createdAt = Instant.now().getEpochSecond();\n        BanRecord ban = new BanRecord(player.getUniqueId(), player.getName(), reason, createdAt);\n        activeBans.put(player.getUniqueId(), ban);\n        String path = \"bans.\" + player.getUniqueId();\n        banConfig.set(path + \".name\", player.getName());\n        banConfig.set(path + \".reason\", reason);\n        banConfig.set(path + \".created-at\", createdAt);\n        saveBans();\n        Bukkit.broadcastMessage(color(PREFIX + \"&c\" + player.getName()\n            + \" wurde permanent gebannt. &7Grund: &f\" + reason));\n        player.kickPlayer(color(\"&c&lChaosGuard\\n&7Du wurdest permanent gebannt.\\n&7Grund: &f\" + reason));\n    }\n\n    private BanRecord removeBan(String identifier) {\n        BanRecord match = null;\n        try {\n            match = activeBans.get(UUID.fromString(identifier));\n        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) {\n            // Player names are resolved from the local ban database below.\n        }\n        if (match == null) {\n            for (BanRecord ban : activeBans.values()) {\n                if (ban.name.equalsIgnoreCase(identifier)) {\n                    match = ban;\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        if (match == null) {\n            return null;\n        }\n        activeBans.remove(match.uuid);\n        Profile profile = profiles.get(match.uuid);\n        if (profile != null) {\n            profile.crashBanned = false;\n            profile.violations.remove(Check.CRASH);\n        }\n        banConfig.set(\"bans.\" + match.uuid, null);\n        saveBans();\n        return match;\n    }\n\n    private void saveBans() {\n        try {\n            banConfig.save(banFile);\n            if (database != null) {\n                database.queueSnapshot(\"guard:bans\", banFile);\n            }\n        } catch (IOException exception) {\n            getLogger().severe(\"bans.yml konnte nicht gespeichert werden: \" + exception.getMessage());\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void loadViolationState() {\n        stateFile = new File(getDataFolder(), \"violations.yml\");\n        FileConfiguration state = YamlConfiguration.loadConfiguration(stateFile);\n        ConfigurationSection section = state.getConfigurationSection(\"profiles\");\n        if (section == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        long cutoff = System.currentTimeMillis() - configInt(\"profile-retention-minutes\", 30) * 60_000L;\n        for (String key : section.getKeys(false)) {\n            try {\n                UUID uuid = UUID.fromString(key);\n                String path = \"profiles.\" + key;\n                long lastSeen = state.getLong(path + \".last-seen\", 0L);\n                if (lastSeen < cutoff) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                Profile profile = new Profile(null);\n                profile.playerName = state.getString(path + \".name\", key);\n                profile.clientBrand = state.getString(path + \".client-brand\", \"unknown\");\n                profile.lastSeenAt = lastSeen;\n                profile.minedBaseBlocks = state.getInt(path + \".mined-blocks\", 0);\n                profile.rareOreVeins = state.getInt(path + \".rare-ore-veins\", 0);\n                profile.xrayScore = state.getDouble(path + \".xray-score\", 0.0);\n                profile.recentFlags.addAll(state.getStringList(path + \".recent-flags\"));\n                ConfigurationSection violations = state.getConfigurationSection(path + \".violations\");\n                if (violations != null) {\n                    for (String checkName : violations.getKeys(false)) {\n                        try {\n                            Check check = Check.valueOf(checkName);\n                            double value = violations.getDouble(checkName, 0.0);\n                            if (value > 0.0) {\n                                profile.violations.put(check, value);\n                            }\n                        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) {\n                            getLogger().warning(\"Unbekannter Check in violations.yml: \" + checkName);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n                profiles.put(uuid, profile);\n            } catch (IllegalArgumentException exception) {\n                getLogger().warning(\"Ungueltige Profil-UUID in violations.yml: \" + key);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void saveViolationState() {\n        if (stateFile == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        YamlConfiguration state = new YamlConfiguration();\n        for (Map.Entry<UUID, Profile> entry : profiles.entrySet()) {\n            Profile profile = entry.getValue();\n            if (profile.totalVl() <= 0.01 && profile.recentFlags.isEmpty()) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            String path = \"profiles.\" + entry.getKey();\n            state.set(path + \".name\", profile.playerName);\n            state.set(path + \".client-brand\", profile.clientBrand);\n            state.set(path + \".last-seen\", profile.lastSeenAt);\n            state.set(path + \".mined-blocks\", profile.minedBaseBlocks);\n            state.set(path + \".rare-ore-veins\", profile.rareOreVeins);\n            state.set(path + \".xray-score\", profile.xrayScore);\n            state.set(path + \".recent-flags\", new ArrayList<>(profile.recentFlags));\n            for (Map.Entry<Check, Double> violation : profile.violations.entrySet()) {\n                if (violation.getValue() > 0.01) {\n                    state.set(path + \".violations.\" + violation.getKey().name(), violation.getValue());\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        try {\n            state.save(stateFile);\n            if (database != null) {\n                database.queueSnapshot(\"guard:violations\", stateFile);\n            }\n        } catch (IOException exception) {\n            getLogger().warning(\"violations.yml konnte nicht gespeichert werden: \" + exception.getMessage());\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void decayProfiles() {\n        double decay = configDouble(\"violation-decay-per-minute\", 1.25);\n        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        long retention = configInt(\"profile-retention-minutes\", 30) * 60_000L;\n        profiles.entrySet().removeIf(entry -> Bukkit.getPlayer(entry.getKey()) == null\n            && now - entry.getValue().lastSeenAt > retention);\n        for (Profile profile : profiles.values()) {\n            for (Check check : Check.values()) {\n                double current = profile.violations.getOrDefault(check, 0.0);\n                if (current <= 0.0 || check == Check.CRASH) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                double multiplier = check == Check.XRAY ? 0.25 : 1.0;\n                profile.violations.put(check, Math.max(0.0, current - decay * multiplier));\n            }\n        }\n        saveViolationState();\n    }\n\n    private void flushLogs() {\n        if (pendingLogLines.isEmpty()) {\n            return;\n        }\n        File folder = new File(getDataFolder(), \"logs\");\n        if (!folder.exists() && !folder.mkdirs()) {\n            return;\n        }\n        File file = new File(folder, \"violations-\" + LocalDate.now() + \".jsonl\");\n        try (BufferedWriter writer = Files.newBufferedWriter(file.toPath(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8,\n            StandardOpenOption.CREATE, StandardOpenOption.APPEND)) {\n            String line;\n            while ((line = pendingLogLines.poll()) != null) {\n                writer.write(line);\n                writer.newLine();\n            }\n        } catch (IOException exception) {\n            getLogger().warning(\"Log write failed: \" + exception.getMessage());\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void queueSystemLog(String action, String evidence) {\n        pendingLogLines.add(\"{\\\"time\\\":\\\"\" + Instant.now() + \"\\\",\\\"player\\\":null,\\\"check\\\":\\\"SYSTEM\\\",\"\n            + \"\\\"action\\\":\\\"\" + json(action) + \"\\\",\\\"evidence\\\":\\\"\" + json(evidence) + \"\\\"}\");\n    }\n\n    private void queuePlayerLog(Player player, String check, String action, double vl, double added, String evidence) {\n        Location location = player.getLocation();\n        pendingLogLines.add(\"{\\\"time\\\":\\\"\" + Instant.now() + \"\\\",\\\"uuid\\\":\\\"\" + player.getUniqueId()\n            + \"\\\",\\\"player\\\":\\\"\" + json(player.getName()) + \"\\\",\\\"check\\\":\\\"\" + json(check)\n            + \"\\\",\\\"action\\\":\\\"\" + json(action) + \"\\\",\\\"vl\\\":\" + fmt(vl) + \",\\\"added\\\":\" + fmt(added)\n            + \",\\\"ping\\\":\" + player.getPing() + \",\\\"world\\\":\\\"\" + json(location.getWorld().getName())\n            + \"\\\",\\\"x\\\":\" + fmt(location.getX()) + \",\\\"y\\\":\" + fmt(location.getY()) + \",\\\"z\\\":\"\n            + fmt(location.getZ()) + \",\\\"evidence\\\":\\\"\" + json(evidence) + \"\\\"}\");\n    }\n\n    private boolean enabled() {\n        return configBoolean(\"enabled\", true);\n    }\n\n    private boolean active(Player player) {\n        return enabled() && player != null && !player.hasPermission(configString(\"bypass-permission\", \"chaosguard.bypass\"));\n    }\n\n    private boolean receivesAlerts(Player player) {\n        boolean toggled = alertToggles.contains(player.getUniqueId());\n        return configBoolean(\"alerts-always-on\", true) ? !toggled : toggled;\n    }\n\n    private double cancelVl() {\n        return configDouble(\"punishments.cancel-vl\", 6.0);\n    }\n\n    private Profile profile(Player player) {\n        Profile profile = profiles.computeIfAbsent(player.getUniqueId(), ignored -> new Profile(player.getLocation()));\n        if (profile.lastLocation == null) {\n            profile.lastLocation = player.getLocation().clone();\n            profile.lastGroundLocation = player.getLocation().clone();\n            profile.airbornePeakY = player.getLocation().getY();\n            profile.teleportGraceUntil = System.currentTimeMillis() + 3000L;\n        }\n        profile.playerName = player.getName();\n        profile.lastSeenAt = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        return profile;\n    }\n\n    private Profile findProfile(String nameOrUuid) {\n        try {\n            Profile byUuid = profiles.get(UUID.fromString(nameOrUuid));\n            if (byUuid != null) {\n                return byUuid;\n            }\n        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) {\n            // Continue with the cached player name.\n        }\n        for (Profile profile : profiles.values()) {\n            if (profile.playerName.equalsIgnoreCase(nameOrUuid)) {\n                return profile;\n            }\n        }\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    private int rate(Deque<Long> deque, long windowMillis) {\n        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        deque.addLast(now);\n        trim(deque, now - windowMillis);\n        return deque.size();\n    }\n\n    private static void trim(Deque<Long> deque, long cutoff) {\n        while (!deque.isEmpty() && deque.peekFirst() < cutoff) {\n            deque.removeFirst();\n        }\n    }\n\n    private boolean alreadyCountedVein(Profile profile, Block block) {\n        UUID world = block.getWorld().getUID();\n        for (int x = -2; x <= 2; x++) {\n            for (int y = -2; y <= 2; y++) {\n                for (int z = -2; z <= 2; z++) {\n                    if (profile.seenOres.contains(blockKey(world, block.getX() + x, block.getY() + y, block.getZ() + z))) {\n                        return true;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    private void rememberOre(Profile profile, Block block) {\n        profile.seenOres.add(blockKey(block.getWorld().getUID(), block.getX(), block.getY(), block.getZ()));\n        if (profile.seenOres.size() > 2048) {\n            profile.seenOres.clear();\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static String blockKey(UUID world, int x, int y, int z) {\n        return world + \":\" + x + \":\" + y + \":\" + z;\n    }\n\n    private static boolean isExposed(Block block) {\n        for (BlockFace face : FACES) {\n            Material neighbor = block.getRelative(face).getType();\n            if (neighbor.isAir() || neighbor == Material.WATER || neighbor == Material.LAVA) {\n                return true;\n            }\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    private static boolean hasSupport(Location location) {\n        Block below = location.clone().subtract(0.0, 0.35, 0.0).getBlock();\n        return below.getType().isSolid() || below.isLiquid();\n    }\n\n    private static boolean hasSolidSupport(Location location) {\n        return location.clone().subtract(0.0, 0.35, 0.0).getBlock().getType().isSolid();\n    }\n\n    private void sampleTimer(Player player, Profile profile, int movementRate, long now) {\n        if (now - profile.lastTimerSample < 500L) {\n            return;\n        }\n        profile.lastTimerSample = now;\n        int maximum = configInt(\"checks.movement.max-move-packets-per-second\", 27);\n        if (movementRate > maximum && player.getPing() < 350 && Bukkit.getTPS()[0] >= 18.0) {\n            profile.timerSamples++;\n        } else {\n            profile.timerSamples = Math.max(0, profile.timerSamples - 1);\n        }\n        if (profile.timerSamples >= 3 && now - profile.lastTimerFlag > 1500L) {\n            profile.lastTimerFlag = now;\n            profile.timerSamples = 1;\n            flag(player, Check.TIMER, 1.2 + Math.min(2.5, (movementRate - maximum) * 0.12),\n                \"move-packets=\" + movementRate + \"/s ping=\" + player.getPing()\n                    + \" tps=\" + fmt(Bukkit.getTPS()[0]));\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static boolean naturalVerticalBoost(Location from, Location to) {\n        for (Location location : Arrays.asList(from, to, from.clone().subtract(0.0, 1.0, 0.0))) {\n            Material type = location.getBlock().getType();\n            String name = type.name();\n            if (type == Material.SLIME_BLOCK || type == Material.HONEY_BLOCK || type == Material.PISTON_HEAD\n                || type == Material.MOVING_PISTON || type == Material.SCAFFOLDING || type == Material.LADDER\n                || type == Material.VINE || type == Material.WATER || type == Material.BUBBLE_COLUMN\n                || type == Material.POWDER_SNOW || name.endsWith(\"_VINES\") || name.endsWith(\"_BED\")) {\n                return true;\n            }\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    private static boolean isOnLiquidSurface(Location location) {\n        Material feet = location.getBlock().getType();\n        Material below = location.clone().subtract(0.0, 0.08, 0.0).getBlock().getType();\n        return feet.isAir() && (below == Material.WATER || below == Material.LAVA);\n    }\n\n    private static boolean collidesWithSolid(Player player, Location location) {\n        BoundingBox current = player.getBoundingBox();\n        double halfWidth = Math.max(0.15, (current.getWidthX() / 2.0) - 0.045);\n        double height = Math.max(0.8, current.getHeight() - 0.10);\n        BoundingBox predicted = new BoundingBox(\n            location.getX() - halfWidth, location.getY() + 0.05, location.getZ() - halfWidth,\n            location.getX() + halfWidth, location.getY() + height, location.getZ() + halfWidth);\n        int minX = (int)Math.floor(predicted.getMinX());\n        int maxX = (int)Math.floor(predicted.getMaxX());\n        int minY = (int)Math.floor(predicted.getMinY());\n        int maxY = (int)Math.floor(predicted.getMaxY());\n        int minZ = (int)Math.floor(predicted.getMinZ());\n        int maxZ = (int)Math.floor(predicted.getMaxZ());\n        for (int x = minX; x <= maxX; x++) {\n            for (int y = minY; y <= maxY; y++) {\n                for (int z = minZ; z <= maxZ; z++) {\n                    Block block = location.getWorld().getBlockAt(x, y, z);\n                    if (!block.isPassable() && block.getCollisionShape().overlaps(predicted)) {\n                        return true;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    private void trackExpectedFall(Player player, Profile profile, Location to, boolean supported,\n                                   boolean movementExempt, long now) {\n        if (movementExempt || now < profile.teleportGraceUntil\n            || hasEffect(player, PotionEffectType.SLOW_FALLING)\n            || hasEffect(player, PotionEffectType.LEVITATION)) {\n            profile.lastSolidSupport = supported;\n            profile.airbornePeakY = to.getY();\n            return;\n        }\n        if (!supported) {\n            if (profile.lastSolidSupport) {\n                profile.airbornePeakY = to.getY();\n            } else {\n                profile.airbornePeakY = Math.max(profile.airbornePeakY, to.getY());\n            }\n            profile.lastSolidSupport = false;\n            return;\n        }\n        if (!profile.lastSolidSupport) {\n            double physicalDrop = profile.airbornePeakY - to.getY();\n            double minimum = configDouble(\"checks.movement.nofall-minimum-drop\", 4.25);\n            if (physicalDrop > minimum && !fallExemptLanding(player, to)) {\n                long landingAt = now;\n                int landingSequence = ++profile.landingSequence;\n                UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();\n                Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskLater(this, () -> {\n                    Profile current = profiles.get(uuid);\n                    Player online = Bukkit.getPlayer(uuid);\n                    if (current == null || online == null || current.landingSequence != landingSequence) {\n                        return;\n                    }\n                    if (current.lastFallDamageAt < landingAt - 250L) {\n                        current.missedFallDamage++;\n                        if (current.missedFallDamage >= configInt(\"checks.movement.nofall-required-misses\", 2)) {\n                            current.missedFallDamage = 0;\n                            flag(online, Check.NOFALL, 2.0,\n                                \"physical-drop=\" + fmt(physicalDrop) + \" expected-fall-damage=missing\");\n                        }\n                    } else {\n                        current.missedFallDamage = 0;\n                    }\n                }, 4L);\n            }\n        }\n        profile.lastSolidSupport = true;\n        profile.airbornePeakY = to.getY();\n    }\n\n    private static boolean fallExemptLanding(Player player, Location location) {\n        Material feet = location.getBlock().getType();\n        Material below = location.clone().subtract(0.0, 0.35, 0.0).getBlock().getType();\n        return FALL_EXEMPT.contains(feet) || FALL_EXEMPT.contains(below)\n            || feet.name().endsWith(\"_BED\") || below.name().endsWith(\"_BED\")\n            || player.isGliding() || player.isRiptiding() || player.isInsideVehicle();\n    }\n\n    private static boolean onIce(Player player) {\n        Material below = player.getLocation().clone().subtract(0.0, 0.2, 0.0).getBlock().getType();\n        return ICE.contains(below);\n    }\n\n    private static boolean hasEffect(Player player, PotionEffectType type) {\n        return player.hasPotionEffect(type);\n    }\n\n    private static boolean isTrackedUseItem(Material material) {\n        return material.isEdible() || material == Material.POTION || material == Material.MILK_BUCKET\n            || material == Material.HONEY_BOTTLE || material == Material.BOW || material == Material.CROSSBOW\n            || material == Material.TRIDENT || material == Material.SHIELD || material == Material.SPYGLASS\n            || material == Material.GOAT_HORN;\n    }\n\n    private static double potionMultiplier(Player player, PotionEffectType type, double perLevel) {\n        PotionEffect effect = player.getPotionEffect(type);\n        return effect == null ? 1.0 : 1.0 + (effect.getAmplifier() + 1) * perLevel;\n    }\n\n    private static double distanceToBox(Location eye, Entity entity) {\n        BoundingBox box = entity.getBoundingBox();\n        double x = clamp(eye.getX(), box.getMinX(), box.getMaxX());\n        double y = clamp(eye.getY(), box.getMinY(), box.getMaxY());\n        double z = clamp(eye.getZ(), box.getMinZ(), box.getMaxZ());\n        double dx = eye.getX() - x;\n        double dy = eye.getY() - y;\n        double dz = eye.getZ() - z;\n        return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz);\n    }\n\n    private static double distanceToBlock(Location eye, Block block) {\n        BoundingBox box = block.getBoundingBox();\n        double x = clamp(eye.getX(), box.getMinX(), box.getMaxX());\n        double y = clamp(eye.getY(), box.getMinY(), box.getMaxY());\n        double z = clamp(eye.getZ(), box.getMinZ(), box.getMaxZ());\n        double dx = eye.getX() - x;\n        double dy = eye.getY() - y;\n        double dz = eye.getZ() - z;\n        return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz);\n    }\n\n    private void scheduleVelocityCheck(Player player) {\n        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();\n        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskLater(this, () -> {\n            Player target = Bukkit.getPlayer(uuid);\n            Profile profile = profiles.get(uuid);\n            if (target == null || profile == null || !active(target) || target.isInsideVehicle()) {\n                return;\n            }\n            Vector velocity = target.getVelocity().clone();\n            double horizontalVelocity = Math.sqrt(velocity.getX() * velocity.getX() + velocity.getZ() * velocity.getZ());\n            if (horizontalVelocity < configDouble(\"checks.combat.velocity-minimum\", 0.18)\n                || !hasHorizontalEscape(target, velocity)) {\n                return;\n            }\n            Location start = target.getLocation().clone();\n            int sequence = ++profile.velocityCheckSequence;\n            Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskLater(this, () -> {\n                Player currentPlayer = Bukkit.getPlayer(uuid);\n                Profile current = profiles.get(uuid);\n                if (currentPlayer == null || current == null || current.velocityCheckSequence != sequence\n                    || currentPlayer.isInsideVehicle() || currentPlayer.isGliding()) {\n                    return;\n                }\n                double travelled = Math.sqrt(horizontalDistanceSquared(start, currentPlayer.getLocation()));\n                double required = Math.max(0.11, horizontalVelocity\n                    * configDouble(\"checks.combat.velocity-required-ratio\", 0.45));\n                if (travelled < required) {\n                    current.velocityMisses++;\n                    if (current.velocityMisses >= configInt(\"checks.combat.velocity-required-misses\", 3)) {\n                        current.velocityMisses = 0;\n                        flag(currentPlayer, Check.VELOCITY, 1.8,\n                            \"received=\" + fmt(horizontalVelocity) + \" travelled=\" + fmt(travelled)\n                                + \" required=\" + fmt(required));\n                    }\n                } else {\n                    current.velocityMisses = Math.max(0, current.velocityMisses - 1);\n                }\n            }, 10L);\n        }, 1L);\n    }\n\n    private static boolean hasHorizontalEscape(Player player, Vector velocity) {\n        Vector horizontal = new Vector(velocity.getX(), 0.0, velocity.getZ());\n        if (horizontal.lengthSquared() < 0.0001) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        horizontal.normalize().multiply(0.65);\n        Location feet = player.getLocation().clone().add(horizontal);\n        Location body = feet.clone().add(0.0, 1.0, 0.0);\n        return feet.getBlock().isPassable() && body.getBlock().isPassable();\n    }\n\n    private static double horizontalDistanceSquared(Location first, Location second) {\n        double dx = first.getX() - second.getX();\n        double dz = first.getZ() - second.getZ();\n        return dx * dx + dz * dz;\n    }\n\n    private static boolean containsControlCharacters(String value) {\n        for (int i = 0; i < value.length(); i++) {\n            char character = value.charAt(i);\n            if (character == '\\u0000' || character == '\\r' || character == '\\n') {\n                return true;\n            }\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    private static String decodeBrand(byte[] data) {\n        if (data.length == 0) {\n            return \"unknown\";\n        }\n        int index = 0;\n        int length = 0;\n        int shift = 0;\n        while (index < data.length && shift < 35) {\n            int current = data[index++] & 0xFF;\n            length |= (current & 0x7F) << shift;\n            if ((current & 0x80) == 0) {\n                break;\n            }\n            shift += 7;\n        }\n        if (length < 0 || index + length > data.length) {\n            length = data.length - index;\n        }\n        return new String(data, index, Math.max(0, length), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n    }\n\n    private boolean configBoolean(String path, boolean fallback) {\n        return getConfig().getBoolean(ROOT + path, fallback);\n    }\n\n    private int configInt(String path, int fallback) {\n        return getConfig().getInt(ROOT + path, fallback);\n    }\n\n    private double configDouble(String path, double fallback) {\n        return getConfig().getDouble(ROOT + path, fallback);\n    }\n\n    private String configString(String path, String fallback) {\n        return getConfig().getString(ROOT + path, fallback);\n    }\n\n    private static String fmt(double value) {\n        return String.format(Locale.US, \"%.2f\", value);\n    }\n\n    private static double clamp(double value, double minimum, double maximum) {\n        return Math.max(minimum, Math.min(maximum, value));\n    }\n\n    private static String safe(String value, int maxLength) {\n        if (value == null) {\n            return \"\";\n        }\n        String clean = value.replace('\\n', ' ').replace('\\r', ' ').replace('\\u0000', '?');\n        return clean.length() <= maxLength ? clean : clean.substring(0, maxLength) + \"...\";\n    }\n\n    private static String json(String value) {\n        return safe(value, 1000).replace(\"\\\\\", \"\\\\\\\\\").replace(\"\\\"\", \"\\\\\\\"\");\n    }\n\n    private static String color(String value) {\n        return ChatColor.translateAlternateColorCodes('&', normalizeGermanTypography(value));\n    }\n\n    private static String normalizeGermanTypography(String text) {\n        if (text == null) {\n            return \"\";\n        }\n        return text\n            .replace(\"Gruende\", \"Gründe\").replace(\"gruende\", \"gründe\")\n            .replace(\"Pruef\", \"Prüf\").replace(\"pruef\", \"prüf\")\n            .replace(\"Ueber\", \"Über\").replace(\"ueber\", \"über\")\n            .replace(\"Für\", \"Für\").replace(\"für\", \"für\");\n    }\n\n    private static List<String> filter(List<String> values, String prefix) {\n        List<String> result = new ArrayList<>();\n        String lower = prefix.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n        for (String value : values) {\n            if (value.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).startsWith(lower)) {\n                result.add(value);\n            }\n        }\n        return result;\n    }\n\n    private static Map<Material, Double> createOreWeights() {\n        Map<Material, Double> weights = new EnumMap<>(Material.class);\n        weights.put(Material.DIAMOND_ORE, 3.0);\n        weights.put(Material.DEEPSLATE_DIAMOND_ORE, 3.2);\n        weights.put(Material.EMERALD_ORE, 3.0);\n        weights.put(Material.DEEPSLATE_EMERALD_ORE, 3.2);\n        weights.put(Material.ANCIENT_DEBRIS, 4.0);\n        weights.put(Material.GOLD_ORE, 1.1);\n        weights.put(Material.DEEPSLATE_GOLD_ORE, 1.2);\n        weights.put(Material.NETHER_GOLD_ORE, 0.6);\n        weights.put(Material.LAPIS_ORE, 1.0);\n        weights.put(Material.DEEPSLATE_LAPIS_ORE, 1.1);\n        return Collections.unmodifiableMap(weights);\n    }\n\n    private enum Check {\n        KILLAURA(\"KillAura\"),\n        REACH(\"Reach\"),\n        AUTOCLICKER(\"AutoClicker\"),\n        CRITICALS(\"Criticals\"),\n        BAD_ACTIONS(\"BadActions\"),\n        VELOCITY(\"AntiKnockback\"),\n        TIMER(\"Timer\"),\n        SPEED(\"Speed\"),\n        FLIGHT(\"Flight\"),\n        STEP(\"Step\"),\n        JESUS(\"Jesus\"),\n        NOFALL(\"NoFall\"),\n        NOSLOW(\"NoSlow\"),\n        ELYTRA(\"ElytraSpeed\"),\n        VEHICLE(\"VehicleSpeed\"),\n        PHASE(\"Phase\"),\n        INVENTORY_MOVE(\"InventoryMove\"),\n        XRAY(\"XRay\", false),\n        BREAK_REACH(\"BreakReach\"),\n        FASTBREAK(\"FastBreak/Nuker\"),\n        FASTPLACE(\"FastPlace\"),\n        SCAFFOLD(\"Scaffold\"),\n        FASTUSE(\"FastUse\"),\n        FASTBOW(\"FastBow\"),\n        PACKET_SPAM(\"PacketSpam\", false),\n        INVALID_PAYLOAD(\"InvalidPayload\", false),\n        CLIENT(\"HackClient\", false),\n        CRASH(\"CrashExploit\", false);\n\n        private final String display;\n        private final boolean lagSensitive;\n\n        Check(String display) {\n            this(display, true);\n        }\n\n        Check(String display, boolean lagSensitive) {\n            this.display = display;\n            this.lagSensitive = lagSensitive;\n        }\n\n        private static String enabledNames() {\n            return Arrays.toString(values());\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static final class Profile {\n        private final EnumMap<Check, Double> violations = new EnumMap<>(Check.class);\n        private final EnumMap<Check, Long> lastAlerts = new EnumMap<>(Check.class);\n        private final Deque<Long> attackTimes = new ArrayDeque<>();\n        private final Deque<Long> swingTimes = new ArrayDeque<>();\n        private final Deque<Long> private final Deque<Long> interactTimes = new ArrayDeque<>();\n        private final Deque<Long> tabTimes = new ArrayDeque<>();\n        private final Deque<Long> channelTimes = new ArrayDeque<>();\n inventoryOpenAt;\n        private long useStartedAt;\n        private long lastBreakAt;\n        private long lastSeenAt;\n        private long lastXrayFlag;\n        private long lastPunishment;\n        private int airTicks;\n        private int timerSamples;\n        private int liquidSurfaceTicks;\n        private int noSlowTicks;\n        private int missedFallDamage;\n        private int landingSequence;\n        private int velocityCheckSequence;\n        private int velocityMisses;\n        private int criticalBuffer;\n        private int elytraBuffer;\n        private int rareOreVeins;\n        private double xrayScore;\n        private double airbornePeakY;\n        private double lastVerticalDelta;\n        private float lastYaw;\n        private float lastPitch;\n        private String clientBrand = \"unknown\";\n        private String playerName = \"unknown\";\n        private Material useMaterial = Material.AIR;\n        private boolean crashBanned;\n        private boolean lastSolidSupport = true;\n\n        private Profile(Location location) {\n            this.lastLocation = location == null ? null : location.clone();\n            this.lastGroundLocation = location == null ? null : location.clone();\n            this.airbornePeakY = location == null ? 0.0 : location.getY();\n            this.lastSeenAt = System.currentTimeMillis();\n            this.teleportGraceUntil = System.currentTimeMillis() + 3000L;\n        }\n\n        private double totalVl() {\n            double total = 0.0;\n            for (double value : violations.values()) {\n                total += value;\n            }\n            return total;\n        }\n\n        private String compactViolations() {\n            List<String> values = new ArrayList<>();\n            for (Map.Entry<Check, Double> entry : violations.entrySet()) {\n                if (entry.getValue() > 0.01) {\n                    values.add(entry.getKey().display + \"=\" + fmt(entry.getValue()));\n                }\n            }\n            return values.isEmpty() ? \"keine\" : String.join(\", \", values);\n        }\n\n        private void resetTransient() {\n            attackTimes.clear();\n            swingTimes.clear();\n            moveTimes.clear();\n            breakTimes.clear();\n            placeTimes.clear();\n            inventoryTimes.clear();\n            dropTimes.clear();\n            commandTimes.clear();\n            interactTimes.clear();\n            tabTimes.clear();\n          noSlowTicks = 0;\n            criticalBuffer = 0;\n            elytraBuffer = 0;\n            vehicleBuffer = 0;\n            phaseBuffer = 0;\n            inventoryMoveTicks = 0;\n            fastBreakBuffer = 0;\n            inventoryOpenAt = 0L;\n            useStartedAt = 0L;\n            useMaterial = Material.AIR;\n            velocityCheckSequence++;\n            velocityMisses = 0;\n        }\n\n        private void clearAll() {\n            violations.clear();\n            lastAlerts.clear();\n            recentFlags.clear();\n            seenOres.clear();\n            registeredChannels.clear();\n            minedBaseBlocks = 0;\n            rareOreVeins = 0;\n            xrayScore = 0.0;\n            crashBanned = false;\n            resetTransient();\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static final class BanRecord {\n        private final UUID uuid;\n        private final String name;\n        private final String reason;\n        private final long createdAt;\n\n        private BanRecord(UUID uuid, String name, String reason, long createdAt) {\n            this.uuid = uuid;\n            this.name = name;\n            this.reason = reason;\n
