private final Set<UUID> woodcutterBreaking = new HashSet<>();\n    private final Map<UUID, Selection> selections = new HashMap<>();\n    private final Map<UUID, Clipboard> clipboards = new HashMap<>();\n    private static final String RECIPE_GUI_TITLE = \"&6&lAltar Rezepte\";\n    private NamespacedKey customItemKey;\n\n    @Override\n    public void onEnable() {\n        saveDefaultConfig();\n        this.customItemKey = new NamespacedKey(this, \"custom_item\");\n        Bukkit.getPluginManager().registerEvents(this, this);\n        if (getCommand(\"colorfloor\") != null) {\n            getCommand(\"colorfloor\").setExecutor(this);\n        }\n        if (getCommand(\"altarcrafting\") != null) {\n            getCommand(\"altarcrafting\").setExecutor(this);\n        }\n        if (getCommand(\"altaritem\") != null) {\n            getCommand(\"altaritem\").setExecutor(this);\n        }\n        getLogger().info(\"CustomCraftingAltar aktiviert.\");\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean onCommand(CommandSender sender, Command command, String label, String[] args) {\n        if (\"altarcrafting\".equalsIgnoreCase(command.getName())) {\n            if (args.length > 0 && (\"givealtar\".equalsIgnoreCase(args[0]) || \"altaritem\".equalsIgnoreCase(args[0]))) {\n                return giveAltarItemCommand(sender, args, 1);\n            }\n            if (!(sender instanceof Player player)) {\n                sender.sendMessage(color(\"&cNur Spieler koennen die Rezept-GUI oeffnen.\"));\n                return true;\n            }\n            openRecipeGui(player);\n            return true;\n        }\n        if (\"altaritem\".equalsIgnoreCase(command.getName())) {\n            return giveAltarItemCommand(sender, args, 0);\n        }\n        if (\"colorfloor\".equalsIgnoreCase(command.getName())) {\n            if (!(sender instanceof Player player)) {\n                sender.sendMessage(color(\"&cNur Spieler koennen /colorfloor benutzen.\"));\n                return true;\n            }\n            if (!isCustomItem(player.getInventory().getBoots(), \"walkblocking_boots\")) {\n                player.sendMessage(color(\"&cDu musst die Walkblocking Boots tragen.\"));\n                return true;\n            }\n            player.sendMessage(color(\"&aColorfloor ist mit den Walkblocking Boots automatisch aktiv.\"));\n            return true;\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    private boolean giveAltarItemCommand(CommandSender sender, String[] args, int startIndex) {\n        if (!sender.hasPermission(\"customcraftingaltar.admin\")) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(\"&cDafuer hast du keine Rechte.\"));\n            return true;\n        }\n        Player target;\n        int amountIndex;\n        if (args.length > startIndex && isInteger(args[startIndex]) && sender instanceof Player player) {\n            target = player;\n            amountIndex = startIndex;\n        } else if (args.length > startIndex) {\n            target = Bukkit.getPlayerExact(args[startIndex]);\n            amountIndex = startIndex + 1;\n            if (target == null) {\n                sender.sendMessage(color(\"&cSpieler nicht gefunden.\"));\n                return true;\n            }\n        } else if (sender instanceof Player player) {\n            target = player;\n            amountIndex = startIndex;\n        } else {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(\"&cNutze: /altaritem <spieler> [anzahl]\"));\n            return true;\n        }\n        int amount = 1;\n        if (args.length > amountIndex) {\n            try {\n                amount = Math.max(1, Math.min(64, Integer.parseInt(args[amountIndex])));\n            } catch (NumberFormatException ex) {\n                sender.sendMessage(color(\"&cDie Anzahl muss eine Zahl sein.\"));\n                return true;\n            }\n        }\n        ItemStack item = createAltarItem();\n        item.setAmount(amount);\n        giveItem(target, item);\n        target.sendMessage(color(\"&6Altar &8| &aDu hast \" + amount + \"x Crafting-Altar Item erhalten.\"));\n        if (!target.equals(sender)) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(\"&6Altar &8| &a\" + target.getName() + \" hat \" + amount + \"x Crafting-Altar erhalten.\"));\n        }\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler\n    public void onItemSpawn(ItemSpawnEvent event) {\n        int delay = getConfig().getInt(\"altar.recipe-check-delay-ticks\", 10);\n        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskLater(this, () -> checkAltarCraft(event.getEntity().getLocation()), delay);\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler\n    public void onBlockBreak(BlockBreakEvent event) {\n        if (event.isCancelled()) {\n            return;\n        }\n        ItemStack tool = event.getPlayer().getInventory().getItemInMainHand();\n        if (this.woodcutterBreaking.contains(event.getPlayer().getUniqueId())) {\n            return;\n        }\n        if (isCustomItem(tool, \"smelter_pickaxe\")) {\n            handleSmelterPickaxe(event, tool);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (isCustomItem(tool, \"vain_pickaxe\")) {\n            breakVainArea(event.getPlayer(), event.getBlock(), tool);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (isCustomItem(tool, \"woodcutter\")) {\n            fellTree(event.getPlayer(), event.getBlock(), tool);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler\n    public void onBlockPlace(BlockPlaceEvent event) {\n        if (!isCustomItem(event.getItemInHand(), \"crafting_altar\")) {\n            return;\n        }\n        event.getPlayer().sendMessage(color(\"&6Altar &8| &aCrafting Altar platziert. Droppe Rezept-Items oben drauf.\"));\n        event.getPlayer().playSound(event.getBlockPlaced().getLocation(), Sound.BLOCK_BEACON_POWER_SELECT, 0.8f, 1.2f);\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler\n    public void onPlayerInteract(PlayerInteractEvent event) {\n        if (event.getHand() == EquipmentSlot.OFF_HAND && isCustomItem(event.getPlayer().getInventory().getItemInMainHand(), \"we_axe\")) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (event.getHand() != EquipmentSlot.HAND) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n        ItemStack hand = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();\n        if (isCustomItem(hand, \"magnet_hoe\")) {\n            handleMagnetHoe(event, player);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (event.getClickedBlock() == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        if (isCustomItem(hand, \"we_axe\")) {\n            handleWorldEditAxe(event, player);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (isCustomItem(hand, \"worldedit_shears\")) {\n            handleWorldEditShears(event, player);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler\n    public void onPlayerMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {\n        if (!isCustomItem(event.getPlayer().getInventory().getBoots(), \"walkblocking_boots\")) {\n            return;\n        }\n        if (event.getTo() == null || sameBlock(event.getFrom(), event.getTo())) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Block block = event.getTo().getBlock().getRelative(BlockFace.DOWN);\n        if (block.getType() != Material.AIR && block.getType() != Material.CAVE_AIR && block.getType() != Material.VOID_AIR) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Material[] colors = {\n                Material.RED_STAINED_GLASS, Material.ORANGE_STAINED_GLASS, Material.YELLOW_STAINED_GLASS,\n                Material.LIME_STAINED_GLASS, Material.LIGHT_BLUE_STAINED_GLASS, Material.PURPLE_STAINED_GLASS\n        };\n        Material material = colors[Math.floorMod(event.getPlayer().getTicksLived(), colors.length)];\n        block.setType(material, false);\n        int lifetime = getConfig().getInt(\"tools.walkblocking-boots.bridge-block-lifetime-ticks\", 120);\n        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskLater(this, () -> {\n            if (block.getType() == material) {\n                block.setType(Material.AIR, false);\n            }\n        }, lifetime);\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler\n    public void onInventoryClick(InventoryClickEvent event) {\n        if (!event.getView().getTitle().equals(color(RECIPE_GUI_TITLE))) {\n            return;\n        }\n        event.setCancelled(true);\n    }\n\n    private void openRecipeGui(Player player) {\n        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(null, 54, color(RECIPE_GUI_TITLE));\n        int[] slots = {10, 12, 14, 16, 28, 30, 32};\n        List<Recipe> recipeList = recipes();\n        for (int i = 0; i < recipeList.size() && i < slots.length; i++) {\n            Recipe recipe = recipeList.get(i);\n            ItemStack display = recipe.createResult(this);\n            ItemMeta meta = display.getItemMeta();\n            if (meta != null) {\n                List<String> lore = new ArrayList<>();\n                lore.add(color(\"&8Altar-Rezept\"));\n                lore.add(color(\"&7Droppe diese Items auf einen &fBeacon&7:\"));\n                for (Ingredient ingredient : recipe.ingredients()) {\n                    lore.add(color(\"&8- &f\" + ingredient.displayName(this)));\n                }\n                lore.add(color(\"&7\"));\n                lore.add(color(\"&eFertiges Item droppt nach der Animation.\"));\n                meta.setLore(lore);\n                display.setItemMeta(meta);\n            }\n            inventory.setItem(slots[i], display);\n        }\n        inventory.setItem(49, createAltarItem());\n        player.openInventory(inventory);\n    }\n\n    private void checkAltarCraft(Location location) {\n        Block altar = findAltar(location);\n        if (altar == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        String key = altarKey(altar);\n        if (this.activeAltars.contains(key)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        List<Item> nearbyItems = nearbyItems(altar.getLocation().add(0.5, 1.1, 0.5));\n        for (Recipe recipe : recipes()) {\n            if (!recipe.matches(nearbyItems, this)) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            this.activeAltars.add(key);\n            removeAltarDrops(nearbyItems);\n            animateCraft(altar, recipe.createResult(this), key);\n            return;\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void removeAltarDrops(List<Item> items) {\n        for (Item item : items) {\n            if (!item.isDead()) {\n                item.remove();\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private Block findAltar(Location location) {\n        World world = location.getWorld();\n        if (world == null) {\n            return null;\n        }\n        Material core = materialFromConfig(\"altar.core-block\", Material.BEACON);\n        int radius = (int)Math.ceil(getConfig().getDouble(\"altar.scan-radius\", 2.6));\n        Block base = location.getBlock();\n        Block best = null;\n        double bestDistance = Double.MAX_VALUE;\n        for (int x = -radius; x <= radius; x++) {\n            for (int y = -2; y <= 1; y++) {\n                for (int z = -radius; z <= radius; z++) {\n                    Block block = base.getRelative(x, y, z);\n                    if (block.getType() != core) {\n                        continue;\n                    }\n                    double distance = block.getLocation().add(0.5, 1.0, 0.5).distanceSquared(location);\n                    if (distance < bestDistance) {\n                        bestDistance = distance;\n                        best = block;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        return best;\n    }\n\n    private List<Item> nearbyItems(Location center) {\n        double radius = getConfig().getDouble(\"altar.scan-radius\", 2.6);\n        List<Item> items = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (Entity entity : center.getWorld().getNearbyEntities(center, radius, radius, radius)) {\n            if (entity instanceof Item item && !item.isDead()) {\n                items.add(item);\n            }\n        }\n        return items;\n    }\n\n    private void animateCraft(Block altar, ItemStack result, String key) {\n        Location center = altar.getLocation().add(0.5, 1.2, 0.5);\n        int ticks = getConfig().getInt(\"altar.animation-ticks\", 60);\n        World world = altar.getWorld();\n        world.playSound(center, Sound.BLOCK_BEACON_ACTIVATE, 1.0f, 1.2f);\n        for (int i = 0; i <= ticks; i += 5) {\n            final int step = i;\n            Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskLater(this, () -> {\n                double angle = step / 5.0;\n                for (int p = 0; p < 8; p++) {\n                    double point = angle + p * Math.PI / 4.0;\n                    Location particle = center.clone().add(Math.cos(point) * 0.9, 0.15 + step / 45.0, Math.sin(point) * 0.9);\n                    world.spawnParticle(Particle.ENCHANT, particle, 8, 0.05, 0.05, 0.05, 0.02);\n                }\n                world.spawnParticle(Particle.END_ROD, center, 12, 0.45, 0.45, 0.45, 0.01);\n            }, i);\n        }\n        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskLater(this, () -> {\n            world.dropItemNaturally(center, result);\n            world.spawnParticle(Particle.FIREWORK, center, 35, 0.4, 0.4, 0.4, 0.05);\n            world.playSound(center, Sound.ENTITY_PLAYER_LEVELUP, 1.0f, 1.0f);\n            this.activeAltars.remove(key);\n        }, ticks);\n    }\n\n    private List<Recipe> recipes() {\n        return Arrays.asList(\n                new Recipe(\"vain_pickaxe\", Arrays.asList(\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.DIAMOND_BLOCK, 4),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.EMERALD_BLOCK, 4),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.NETHERITE_PICKAXE, 1),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.BEACON, 1)\n                )),\n                new Recipe(\"we_axe\", Arrays.asList(\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.GOLDEN_AXE, 1),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.NETHER_STAR, 2),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.NETHERITE_BLOCK, 2),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.GOLD_BLOCK, 2),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.ANVIL, 1),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.BLAZE_POWDER, 1)\n                )),\n                new Recipe(\"walkblocking_boots\", Arrays.asList(\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.DIAMOND_HORSE_ARMOR, 2),\n                        Ingredient.anyMaterial(Arrays.asList(Material.PUMPKIN, Material.CARVED_PUMPKIN), 1),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.ENDER_CHEST, 2),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.GLOWSTONE, 2),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.NETHER_STAR, 1),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.NETHERITE_BOOTS, 1)\n                )),\n                new Recipe(\"woodcutter\", Arrays.asList(\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.OAK_LOG, 64),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.SPRUCE_LOG, 64),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.ACACIA_LOG, 64),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.BEACON, 2),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.NETHERITE_AXE, 1),\n                        Ingredient.custom(\"we_axe\", 1)\n                )),\n                new Recipe(\"worldedit_shears\", Arrays.asList(\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.SPAWNER, 1),\n                        Ingredient.custom(\"we_axe\", 1),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.SHEARS, 1)\n                )),\n                new Recipe(\"smelter_pickaxe\", Arrays.asList(\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.NETHERITE_PICKAXE, 1),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.BLAST_FURNACE, 2),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.LAVA_BUCKET, 1),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.COAL_BLOCK, 4),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.NETHER_STAR, 1),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.ANCIENT_DEBRIS, 2)\n                )),\n                new Recipe(\"magnet_hoe\", Arrays.asList(\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.NETHERITE_HOE, 1),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.HOPPER, 4),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.ENDER_PEARL, 8),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.IRON_BLOCK, 2),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.ECHO_SHARD, 1),\n                        Ingredient.material(Material.NETHER_STAR, 1)\n                ))\n        );\n    }\n\n    private ItemStack createCustomItem(String id) {\n        ItemStack item;\n        String name;\n        List<String> lore = new ArrayList<>();\n        if (\"vain_pickaxe\".equals(id)) {\n            item = new ItemStack(Material.NETHERITE_PICKAXE);\n            name = \"&5&lVainPickaxe\";\n            lore.add(\"&7Baut eine 3x3 Flaeche ab.\");\n            lore.add(\"&7Perfekt fuer schnelles Mining.\");\n        } else if (\"we_axe\".equals(id)) {\n            item = new ItemStack(Material.GOLDEN_AXE);\n            name = \"&6&lWE-Axt\";\n            lore.add(\"&7Links-/Rechtsklick setzt Position 1/2.\");\n            lore.add(\"&7Sneak-Rechtsklick fuellt mit deinem Offhand-Block.\");\n            lore.add(\"&7In Survival werden Offhand-Bloecke verbraucht.\");\n        } else if (\"walkblocking_boots\".equals(id)) {\n            item = new ItemStack(Material.NETHERITE_BOOTS);\n            name = \"&b&lWalkblocking Boots\";\n            lore.add(\"&7Colorfloor ist beim Tragen automatisch aktiv.\");\n            lore.add(\"&7Hilft beim Laufen ueber Luecken.\");\n        } else if (\"woodcutter\".equals(id)) {\n            item = new ItemStack(Material.NETHERITE_AXE);\n            name = \"&2&lWoodcutter\";\n            lore.add(\"&7Faellt verbundene Baumstaemme auf einmal.\");\n            lore.add(\"&7Nutzt Haltbarkeit wie ein normales Tool.\");\n        } else if (\"worldedit_shears\".equals(id)) {\n            item = new ItemStack(Material.SHEARS);\n            name = \"&d&lWorldEdit Schere\";\n            lore.add(\"&7Sneak-Rechtsklick kopiert deine WE-Auswahl.\");\n            lore.add(\"&7Rechtsklick fuegt die Struktur am Ziel ein.\");\n        } else if (\"smelter_pickaxe\".equals(id)) {\n            item = new ItemStack(Material.NETHERITE_PICKAXE);\n            name = \"&c&lSmelterPickaxe\";\n            lore.add(\"&7Schmilzt Erze direkt beim Abbauen.\");\n            lore.add(\"&7Perfekt fuer Mining ohne Ofen-Pausen.\");\n        } else {\n            item = new ItemStack(Material.NETHERITE_HOE);\n            name = \"&b&lMagnetHoe\";\n            lore.add(\"&7Rechtsklick zieht Drops in deiner Naehe an.\");\n            lore.add(\"&7Items landen direkt in deinem Inventar.\");\n        }\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta != null) {\n            meta.setDisplayName(color(name));\n            List<String> coloredLore = new ArrayList<>();\n            coloredLore.add(color(\"&8Custom Altar Item\"));\n            coloredLore.addAll(lore.stream().map(this::color).toList());\n            meta.setLore(coloredLore);\n            meta.addItemFlags(ItemFlag.HIDE_ENCHANTS);\n            meta.getPersistentDataContainer().set(this.customItemKey, PersistentDataType.STRING, id);\n            item.setItemMeta(meta);\n        }\n        item.addUnsafeEnchantment(Enchantment.UNBREAKING, 1);\n        return item;\n    }\n\n    private ItemStack createAltarItem() {\n        Material material = materialFromConfig(\"altar.core-block\", Material.BEACON);\n        ItemStack item = new ItemStack(material);\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta != null) {\n            meta.setDisplayName(color(\"&6&lCrafting Altar\"));\n            meta.setLore(Arrays.asList(\n                    color(\"&8Admin Item\"),\n                    color(\"&7Platziere diesen Beacon als Altar.\"),\n                    color(\"&7Droppe Rezept-Items oben drauf.\"),\n                    color(\"&7Nach der Animation droppt das Custom-Item.\"),\n                    color(\"&8/custom: crafting_altar\")\n            ));\n            meta.addItemFlags(ItemFlag.HIDE_ENCHANTS);\n            meta.getPersistentDataContainer().set(this.customItemKey, PersistentDataType.STRING, \"crafting_altar\");\n            item.setItemMeta(meta);\n        }\n        item.addUnsafeEnchantment(Enchantment.UNBREAKING, 1);\n        return item;\n    }\n\n    private void handleWorldEditAxe(PlayerInteractEvent event, Player player) {\n        event.setCancelled(true);\n        Selection selection = this.selections.computeIfAbsent(player.getUniqueId(), uuid -> new Selection());\n        if (event.getAction() == Action.LEFT_CLICK_BLOCK) {\n            selection.first = event.getClickedBlock().getLocation();\n            player.sendMessage(color(\"&6WE-Axt &8| &aPosition 1 gesetzt.\"));\n        } else if (event.getAction() == Action.RIGHT_CLICK_BLOCK && player.isSneaking()) {\n            fillSelectionWithOffhand(player);\n        } else if (event.getAction() == Action.RIGHT_CLICK_BLOCK) {\n            selection.second = event.getClickedBlock().getLocation();\n            player.sendMessage(color(\"&6WE-Axt &8| &aPosition 2 gesetzt.\"));\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void fillSelectionWithOffhand(Player player) {\n        Selection selection = this.selections.get(player.getUniqueId());\n        if (!hasCompleteSelection(selection)) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(\"&cSetze erst zwei Positionen mit der WE-Axt.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        ItemStack offhand = player.getInventory().getItemInOffHand();\n        if (offhand == null || offhand.getType() == Material.AIR || !offhand.getType().isBlock()) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(\"&cLege den Block, den du setzen willst, in deine Offhand.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        int minX = Math.min(selection.first.getBlockX(), selection.second.getBlockX());\n        int minY = Math.min(selection.first.getBlockY(), selection.second.getBlockY());\n        int minZ = Math.min(selection.first.getBlockZ(), selection.second.getBlockZ());\n        int maxX = Math.max(selection.first.getBlockX(), selection.second.getBlockX());\n        int maxY = Math.max(selection.first.getBlockY(), selection.second.getBlockY());\n        int maxZ = Math.max(selection.first.getBlockZ(), selection.second.getBlockZ());\n        int total = (maxX - minX + 1) * (maxY - minY + 1) * (maxZ - minZ + 1);\n        int maxBlocks = getConfig().getInt(\"tools.we-axe.max-fill-blocks\", 2048);\n        if (total > maxBlocks) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(\"&cAuswahl zu gross. Maximal \" + maxBlocks + \" Bloecke.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        boolean creative = player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE;\n        if (!creative && offhand.getAmount() < total) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(\"&cDu brauchst \" + total + \"x \" + prettyMaterial(offhand.getType()) + \" in der Offhand.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        World world = selection.first.getWorld();\n        BlockData data = offhand.getType().createBlockData();\n        int placed = 0;\n        for (int x = minX; x <= maxX; x++) {\n            for (int y = minY; y <= maxY; y++) {\n                for (int z = minZ; z <= maxZ; z++) {\n                    Block block = world.getBlockAt(x, y, z);\n                    if (block.getType().getHardness() < 0.0f) {\n                        continue;\n                    }\n                    block.setType(offhand.getType(), false);\n                    placed++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        if (!creative) {\n            offhand.setAmount(Math.max(0, offhand.getAmount() - placed));\n            player.getInventory().setItemInOffHand(offhand.getAmount() <= 0 ? new ItemStack(Material.AIR) : offhand);\n        }\n        player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.BLOCK_AMETHYST_BLOCK_PLACE, 0.8f, 1.2f);\n        player.sendMessage(color(\"&6WE-Axt &8| &a\" + placed + \" Bloecke mit &e\" + prettyMaterial(offhand.getType()) + \" &agefuellt.\"));\n    }\n\n    private void handleWorldEditShears(PlayerInteractEvent event, Player player) {\n        if (event.getAction() != Action.RIGHT_CLICK_BLOCK) {\n            return;\n        }\n        event.setCancelled(true);\n        if (player.isSneaking()) {\n            copySelection(player);\n        } else {\n            pasteClipboard(player, event.getClickedBlock().getRelative(event.getBlockFace()).getLocation());\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void copySelection(Player player) {\n        Selection selection = this.selections.get(player.getUniqueId());\n        if (!hasCompleteSelection(selection)) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(\"&cSetze erst zwei Positionen mit der WE-Axt.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        int maxBlocks = getConfig().getInt(\"tools.worldedit-shears.max-copy-blocks\", 4096);\n        int minX = Math.min(selection.first.getBlockX(), selection.second.getBlockX());\n        int minY = Math.min(selection.first.getBlockY(), selection.second.getBlockY());\n        int minZ = Math.min(selection.first.getBlockZ(), selection.second.getBlockZ());\n        int maxX = Math.max(selection.first.getBlockX(), selection.second.getBlockX());\n        int maxY = Math.max(selection.first.getBlockY(), selection.second.getBlockY());\n        int maxZ = Math.max(selection.first.getBlockZ(), selection.second.getBlockZ());\n        List<CopiedBlock> blocks = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (int x = minX; x <= maxX; x++) {\n            for (int y = minY; y <= maxY; y++) {\n                for (int z = minZ; z <= maxZ; z++) {\n                    if (blocks.size() >= maxBlocks) {\n                        player.sendMessage(color(\"&cAuswahl zu gross. Maximal \" + maxBlocks + \" Bloecke.\"));\n                        return;\n                    }\n                    Block block = selection.first.getWorld().getBlockAt(x, y, z);\n                    if (!block.getType().isAir()) {\n                        blocks.add(new CopiedBlock(x - minX, y - minY, z - minZ, block.getBlockData()));\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        this.clipboards.put(player.getUniqueId(), new Clipboard(blocks));\n        player.sendMessage(color(\"&dWorldEdit Schere &8| &a\" + blocks.size() + \" Bloecke kopiert.\"));\n    }\n\n    private void pasteClipboard(Player player, Location origin) {\n        Clipboard clipboard = this.clipboards.get(player.getUniqueId());\n        if (clipboard == null || clipboard.blocks.isEmpty() || origin.getWorld() == null) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(\"&cDu hast noch keine Struktur kopiert.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        for (CopiedBlock copied : clipboard.blocks) {\n            origin.getWorld().getBlockAt(origin.getBlockX() + copied.x, origin.getBlockY() + copied.y, origin.getBlockZ() + copied.z).setBlockData(copied.data, false);\n        }\n        player.sendMessage(color(\"&dWorldEdit Schere &8| &aStruktur eingefuegt.\"));\n    }\n\n    private void handleMagnetHoe(PlayerInteractEvent event, Player player) {\n        if (event.getAction() != Action.RIGHT_CLICK_BLOCK && event.getAction() != Action.RIGHT_CLICK_AIR) {\n            return;\n        }\n        event.setCancelled(true);\n        double radius = getConfig().getDouble(\"tools.magnet-hoe.radius\", 8.0);\n        int maxItems = getConfig().getInt(\"tools.magnet-hoe.max-item-stacks\", 32);\n        int moved = 0;\n        for (Entity entity : player.getWorld().getNearbyEntities(player.getLocation(), radius, radius, radius)) {\n            if (!(entity instanceof Item item) || item.isDead()) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            ItemStack stack = item.getItemStack();\n            Map<Integer, ItemStack> leftovers = player.getInventory().addItem(stack);\n            if (leftovers.isEmpty()) {\n                item.remove();\n            } else {\n                ItemStack leftover = leftovers.values().iterator().next();\n                item.setItemStack(leftover);\n            }\n            moved++;\n            if (moved >= maxItems) {\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n        player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.ENTITY_ENDERMAN_TELEPORT, 0.55f, 1.6f);\n        player.sendMessage(color(\"&bMagnetHoe &8| &a\" + moved + \" Item-Stacks angezogen.\"));\n    }\n\n    private void handleSmelterPickaxe(BlockBreakEvent event, ItemStack tool) {\n        ItemStack smelted = smeltedDrop(event.getBlock().getType());\n        if (smelted == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        event.setDropItems(false);\n        event.setExpToDrop(Math.max(event.getExpToDrop(), 1));\n        event.getBlock().getWorld().dropItemNaturally(event.getBlock().getLocation().add(0.5, 0.5, 0.5), smelted);\n        event.getPlayer().playSound(event.getBlock().getLocation(), Sound.BLOCK_FURNACE_FIRE_CRACKLE, 0.55f, 1.4f);\n    }\n\n    private ItemStack smeltedDrop(Material material) {\n        return switch (material) {\n            case IRON_ORE, DEEPSLATE_IRON_ORE, RAW_IRON_BLOCK -> new ItemStack(Material.IRON_INGOT, material == Material.RAW_IRON_BLOCK ? 9 : 1);\n            case GOLD_ORE, DEEPSLATE_GOLD_ORE, NETHER_GOLD_ORE, RAW_GOLD_BLOCK -> new ItemStack(Material.GOLD_INGOT, material == Material.RAW_GOLD_BLOCK ? 9 : 1);\n            case COPPER_ORE, DEEPSLATE_COPPER_ORE, RAW_COPPER_BLOCK -> new ItemStack(Material.COPPER_INGOT, material == Material.RAW_COPPER_BLOCK ? 9 : 1);\n            case ANCIENT_DEBRIS -> new ItemStack(Material.NETHERITE_SCRAP, 1);\n            default -> null;\n        };\n    }\n\n    private void breakVainArea(Player player, Block origin, ItemStack tool) {\n        int limit = getConfig().getInt(\"tools.vain-pickaxe.max-extra-blocks\", 8);\n        int broken = 0;\n        for (int[] offset : miningOffsets(player)) {\n            if (broken >= limit) {\n                return;\n            }\n            Block block = origin.getRelative(offset[0], offset[1], offset[2]);\n            if (block.equals(origin) || !canToolBreak(block)) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            block.breakNaturally(tool);\n            broken++;\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void fellTree(Player player, Block start, ItemStack tool) {\n        if (!isLog(start.getType())) {\n            return;\n        }\n        int maxLogs = getConfig().getInt(\"tools.woodcutter.max-logs\", 160);\n        Set<Block> visited = new HashSet<>();\n        ArrayDeque<Block> queue = new ArrayDeque<>();\n        queue.add(start);\n        while (!queue.isEmpty() && visited.size() < maxLogs) {\n            Block block = queue.removeFirst();\n            if (!visited.add(block) || !isLog(block.getType())) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            for (BlockFace face : BlockFace.values()) {\n                if (face == BlockFace.SELF) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                Block relative = block.getRelative(face);\n                if (isLog(relative.getType()) && !visited.contains(relative)) {\n                    queue.add(relative);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        int broken = 0;\n        this.woodcutterBreaking.add(player.getUniqueId());\n        try {\n            for (Block block : visited) {\n                if (block.equals(start) || !isLog(block.getType())) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                if (player.breakBlock(block)) {\n                    broken++;\n                }\n            }\n        } finally {\n            this.woodcutterBreaking.remove(player.getUniqueId());\n        }\n        if (broken > 0) {\n            player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.BLOCK_WOOD_BREAK, 0.8f, 1.1f);\n            player.sendMessage(color(\"&2Woodcutter &8| &a\" + (broken + 1) + \" Holz-Bloecke abgebaut.\"));\n        }\n    }\n\n    private int[][] miningOffsets(Player player) {\n        List<int[]> offsets = new ArrayList<>();\n        Vector direction = player.getLocation().getDirection();\n        double x = Math.abs(direction.getX());\n        double y = Math.abs(direction.getY());\n        double z = Math.abs(direction.getZ());\n        for (int a = -1; a <= 1; a++) {\n            for (int b = -1; b <= 1; b++) {\n                if (y > x && y > z) {\n                    offsets.add(new int[]{a, 0, b});\n                } else if (x > z) {\n                    offsets.add(new int[]{0, a, b});\n                } else {\n                    offsets.add(new int[]{a, b, 0});\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        return offsets.toArray(new int[0][]);\n    }\n\n    private boolean canToolBreak(Block block) {\n        return !block.getType().isAir() && block.getType().isBlock() && block.getType().getHardness() >= 0.0f;\n    }\n\n    private boolean isLog(Material material) {\n        String name = material.name();\n        return name.endsWith(\"_LOG\")\n                || name.endsWith(\"_WOOD\")\n                || name.endsWith(\"_STEM\")\n                || name.endsWith(\"_HYPHAE\")\n                || name.contains(\"_LOG_\")\n                || name.contains(\"_WOOD_\");\n    }\n\n    private boolean isCustomItem(ItemStack item, String id) {\n        if (item == null || item.getType() == Material.AIR || !item.hasItemMeta()) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        String value = item.getItemMeta().getPersistentDataContainer().get(this.customItemKey, PersistentDataType.STRING);\n        return id.equals(value);\n    }\n\n    private Material materialFromConfig(String path, Material fallback) {\n        String raw = getConfig().getString(path, fallback.name());\n        try {\n            return Material.valueOf(raw.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));\n        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {\n            return fallback;\n        }\n    }\n\n    private String altarKey(Block block) {\n        return block.getWorld().getUID() + \":\" + block.getX() + \":\" + block.getY() + \":\" + block.getZ();\n    }\n\n    private boolean sameBlock(Location first, Location second) {\n        return first.getWorld() == second.getWorld()\n                && first.getBlockX() == second.getBlockX()\n                && first.getBlockY() == second.getBlockY()\n                && first.getBlockZ() == second.getBlockZ();\n    }\n\n    private String color(String text) {\n        return ChatColor.translateAlternateColorCodes('&', text);\n    }\n\n    private boolean hasCompleteSelection(Selection selection) {\n        return selection != null\n                && selection.first != null\n                && selection.second != null\n                && selection.first.getWorld() != null\n                && selection.first.getWorld().equals(selection.second.getWorld());\n    }\n\n    private ItemStack guiItem(Material material, String name, String... loreLines) {\n        ItemStack item = new ItemStack(material);\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta != null) {\n            meta.setDisplayName(color(name));\n            List<String> lore = new ArrayList<>();\n            for (String line : loreLines) {\n                lore.add(color(line));\n            }\n            meta.setLore(lore);\n            item.setItemMeta(meta);\n        }\n        return item;\n    }\n\n    private void giveItem(Player player, ItemStack item) {\n        Map<Integer, ItemStack> leftovers = player.getInventory().addItem(item);\n        for (ItemStack leftover : leftovers.values()) {\n            player.getWorld().dropItemNaturally(player.getLocation(), leftover);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private String prettyMaterial(Material material) {\n        String[] parts = material.name().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).split(\"_\");\n        StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();\n        for (String part : parts) {\n            if (part.isEmpty()) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            if (builder.length() > 0) {\n                builder.append(' ');\n            }\n            builder.append(Character.toUpperCase(part.charAt(0))).append(part.substring(1));\n        }\n        return builder.toString();\n    }\n\n    private boolean isInteger(String text) {\n        try {\n            Integer.parseInt(text);\n            return true;\n        } catch (NumberFormatException ex) {\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    private record Recipe(String resultId, List<Ingredient> ingredients) {\n        private boolean matches(List<Item> items, CustomCraftingAltarPlugin plugin) {\n            for (Ingredient ingredient : this.ingredients) {\n                if (ingredient.count(items, plugin) < ingredient.amount) {\n                    return false;\n                }\n            }\n            return true;\n        }\n\n        private void consume(List<Item> items, CustomCraftingAltarPlugin plugin) {\n            for (Ingredient ingredient : this.ingredients) {\n                ingredient.consume(items, plugin);\n            }\n        }\n\n        private ItemStack createResult(CustomCraftingAltarPlugin plugin) {\n            return plugin.createCustomItem(this.resultId);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static final class Ingredient {\n        private final List<Material> materials;\n        private final String customId;\n        private final int amount;\n\n        private Ingredient(List<Material> materials, String customId, int amount) {\n            this.materials = materials;\n            this.customId = customId;\n            this.amount = amount;\n        }\n\n        private static Ingredient material(Material material, int amount) {\n            return new Ingredient(List.of(material), null, amount);\n        }\n\n        private static Ingredient anyMaterial(List<Material> materials, int amount) {\n            return new Ingredient(materials, null, amount);\n        }\n\n        private static Ingredient custom(String customId, int amount) {\n            return new Ingredient(List.of(), customId, amount);\n        }\n\n        private int count(List<Item> items, CustomCraftingAltarPlugin plugin) {\n            int total = 0;\n            for (Item entity : items) {\n                ItemStack stack = entity.getItemStack();\n                if (matches(stack, plugin)) {\n                    total += stack.getAmount();\n                }\n            }\n            return total;\n        }\n\n        private void consume(List<Item> items, CustomCraftingAltarPlugin plugin) {\n            int remaining = this.amount;\n            for (Item entity : items) {\n                if (remaining <= 0 || entity.isDead()) {\n                    return;\n                }\n                ItemStack stack = entity.getItemStack();\n                if (!matches(stack, plugin)) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                int take = Math.min(remaining, stack.getAmount());\n                remaining -= take;\n                if (stack.getAmount() <= take) {\n                    entity.remove();\n                } else {\n                    stack.setAmount(stack.getAmount() - take);\n                    entity.setItemStack(stack);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        private boolean matches(ItemStack stack, CustomCraftingAltarPlugin plugin) {\n            if (stack == null || stack.getType() == Material.AIR) {\n                return false;\n            }\n            if (this.customId != null) {\n                return plugin.isCustomItem(stack, this.customId);\n            }\n            return this.materials.contains(stack.getType());\n        }\n\n        private String displayName(CustomCraftingAltarPlugin plugin) {\n            if (this.customId != null) {\n                return this.amount + \"x \" + switch (this.customId) {\n                    case \"we_axe\" -> \"WE-Axt\";\n                    case \"vain_pickaxe\" -> \"VainPickaxe\";\n                    case \"walkblocking_boots\" -> \"Walkblocking Boots\";\n                    case \"woodcutter\" -> \"Woodcutter\";\n                    case \"worldedit_shears\" -> \"WorldEdit Schere\";\n                    case \"smelter_pickaxe\" -> \"SmelterPickaxe\";\n                    case \"magnet_hoe\" -> \"MagnetHoe\";\n                    default -> this.customId;\n                };\n            }\n            if (this.materials.size() == 1) {\n                return this.amount + \"x \" + plugin.prettyMaterial(this.materials.get(0));\n            }\n            List<String> names = new ArrayList<>();\n            for (Material material : this.materials) {\n                names.add(plugin.prettyMaterial(material));\n            }\n            return this.amount + \"x \" + String.join(\" oder \", names);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static final class Selection {\n        private Location first;\n        private Location second;\n    }\n\n    private record Clipboard(List<CopiedBlock> blocks) {\n    }\n\n    private record CopiedBlock(int x, int y, int z, BlockData data) {\n    }\n}\n"}, "EPlus": {"file": "EPlusSystemPlugin.java", "code": "package de.smp.eplus;\n\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport org.bukkit.ChatColor;\nimport org.bukkit.Material;\nimport org.bukkit.command.Command;\nimport org.bukkit.command.CommandExecutor;\nimport org.bukkit.command.CommandSender;\nimport org.bukkit.enchantments.Enchantment;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemFlag;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.meta.ItemMeta;\nimport org.bukkit.plugin.java.JavaPlugin;\n\npublic final class EPlusSystemPlugin extends JavaPlugin implements CommandExecutor {\n    @Override\n    public void onEnable() {\n        saveDefaultConfig();\n        if (getCommand(\"eplus\") != null) {\n            getCommand(\"eplus\").setExecutor(this);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean onCommand(CommandSender sender, Command command, String label, String[] args) {\n        if (\"eplus\".equalsIgnoreCase(command.getName())) {\n            return handleEplusCommand(sender, label, args);\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    private boolean handleEplusCommand(CommandSender sender, String label, String[] args) {\n        if (!(sender instanceof org.bukkit.entity.Player)) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cThis command can only be used by players.\"));\n            return true;\n        }\n        if (!sender.hasPermission(\"smp.utility.admin\")) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cNo permission.\"));\n            return true;\n        }\n        org.bukkit.entity.Player player = (org.bukkit.entity.Player)sender;\n        ItemStack item = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();\n        if (item == null || item.getType() == Material.AIR || args.length < 2) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&e/\" + label + \" <enchant/customName> <level>\"));\n            return true;\n        }\n        int level = parsePositiveInt(args[1], 1);\n        Enchantment enchantment = Enchantment.getByName(args[0].toUpperCase());\n        if (enchantment != null) {\n            item.addUnsafeEnchantment(enchantment, level);\n        } else {\n            ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n            if (meta != null) {\n                List<String> lore = meta.hasLore() ? new ArrayList<String>(meta.getLore()) : new ArrayList<String>();\n                lore = insertEnchantLoreAboveSignature(lore, color(\"&7\" + prettifyKey(args[0]) + \" \" + roman(level)));\n                meta.setLore(lore);\n                meta.addItemFlags(ItemFlag.HIDE_ENCHANTS);\n                item.setItemMeta(meta);\n            }\n            Enchantment glintEnchantment = Enchantment.getByName(\"DURABILITY\");\n            if (glintEnchantment == null) {\n                glintEnchantment = Enchantment.getByName(\"UNBREAKING\");\n            }\n            if (glintEnchantment != null) {\n                item.addUnsafeEnchantment(glintEnchantment, Math.max(1, level));\n            }\n        }\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&aEnchant hinzugefuegt.\"));\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    private List<String> insertEnchantLoreAboveSignature(List<String> lore, String lineToInsert) {\n        List<String> result = new ArrayList<String>();\n        boolean inserted = false;\n        for (int i = 0; i < lore.size(); ++i) {\n            String line = lore.get(i);\n            String stripped = ChatColor.stripColor(line);\n            boolean nextLineIsSignature = i + 1 < lore.size() && ChatColor.stripColor(lore.get(i + 1)).startsWith(\"Signiert von \");\n            if (!inserted && (stripped.startsWith(\"Signiert von \") || nextLineIsSignature)) {\n                result.add(lineToInsert);\n                inserted = true;\n            }\n            result.add(line);\n        }\n        if (!inserted) {\n            result.add(lineToInsert);\n        }\n        return result;\n    }\n\n    private String prettifyKey(String text) {\n        String[] parts = text.toLowerCase().replace('-', '_').split(\"_\");\n        StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();\n        for (String part : parts) {\n            if (part.isEmpty()) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            if (builder.length() > 0) {\n                builder.append(' ');\n            }\n            builder.append(Character.toUpperCase(part.charAt(0))).append(part.substring(1));\n        }\n        return builder.length() == 0 ? text : builder.toString();\n    }\n\n    private String roman(int number) {\n        if (number <= 0) {\n            return \"I\";\n        }\n        String[] values = new String[]{\"\", \"I\", \"II\", \"III\", \"IV\", \"V\", \"VI\", \"VII\", \"VIII\", \"IX\", \"X\"};\n        if (number < values.length) {\n            return values[number];\n        }\n        return String.valueOf(number);\n    }\n\n    private int parsePositiveInt(String text, int fallback) {\n        try {\n            int value = Integer.parseInt(text);\n            return value > 0 ? value : fallback;\n        } catch (NumberFormatException exception) {\n            return fallback;\n        }\n    }\n\n    private String prefix() {\n        return getConfig().getString(\"system.prefix\", \"&6&lSMP Test &8| &r\");\n    }\n\n    private String color(String text) {\n        return ChatColor.translateAlternateColorCodes('&', text);\n    }\n}\n"}, "AurumMorph": {"file": "AurumMorphPlugin.java", "code": extends JavaPlugin implements CommandExecutor, TabCompleter, Listener {\n    private static final int MAIN_SIZE_SLOT = 20;\n    private static final int MAIN_GLOW_SLOT = 24;\n    private static final int BACK_SLOT = 45;\n    private static final int CLOSE_SLOT = 49;\n\n    private final Random random = new Random();\n    private File dataFile;\n    private FileConfiguration dataConfig;\n\n    @Override\n    public void onEnable() {\n        saveDefaultConfig();\n        dataFile = new File(getDataFolder(), \"data.yml\");\n        dataConfig = YamlConfiguration.loadConfiguration(dataFile);\n        registerCommand(\"aurummorph\");\n        registerCommand(\"aurummorphadmin\");\n        Bukkit.getPluginManager().registerEvents(this, this);\n        for (Player player : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {\n            applyPlayerState(player);\n        }\n        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimer(this, () -> {\n            for (Player player : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {\n                applyGlow(player);\n            }\n        }, 40L, 40L);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void onDisable() {\n        for (Player player : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {\n            player.setGlowing(false);\n            clearGlowTeams(player);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean onCommand(CommandSender sender, Command command, String label, String[] args) {\n        if (\"aurummorph\".equalsIgnoreCase(command.getName())) {\n            if (!(sender instanceof Player)) {\n                sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDieser Befehl ist nur f\\u00fcr Spieler.\"));\n                return true;\n            }\n            if (!sender.hasPermission(\"goldstube.aurummorph.use\")) {\n                sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDaf\\u00fcr hast du keine Rechte.\"));\n                return true;\n            }\n            openMainGui((Player)sender);\n            return true;\n        }\n        if (\"aurummorphadmin\".equalsIgnoreCase(command.getName())) {\n            if (!(sender instanceof Player)) {\n                sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDie Admin-Werkstatt ist eine GUI.\"));\n                return true;\n            }\n            if (!sender.hasPermission(\"goldstube.aurummorph.admin\")) {\n                sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDaf\\u00fcr hast du keine Rechte.\"));\n                return true;\n            }\n            openAdminGui((Player)sender);\n            return true;\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public List<String> onTabComplete(CommandSender sender, Command command, String alias, String[] args) {\n        return Collections.emptyList();\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler\n    public void onJoin(PlayerJoinEvent event) {\n        applyPlayerState(event.getPlayer());\n        for (Player player : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {\n            if (!player.equals(event.getPlayer())) {\n                applyGlow(player);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler\n    public void onInventoryClick(InventoryClickEvent event) {\n        if (!(event.getWhoClicked() instanceof Player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Player player = (Player)event.getWhoClicked();\n        String title = event.getView().getTitle();\n        int slot = event.getRawSlot();\n        if (mainTitle().equals(title)) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            handleMainClick(player, slot);\n        } else if (sizeTitle().equals(title)) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            handleSizeClick(player, slot);\n        } else if (glowTitle().equals(title)) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            handleGlowClick(player, slot);\n        } else if (adminTitle().equals(title)) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            handleAdminClick(player, slot);\n        } else if (adminSizeTitle().equals(title)) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            handleAdminVoucherClick(player, slot, \"size\");\n        } else if (adminGlowTitle().equals(title)) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            handleAdminVoucherClick(player, slot, \"glow\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler\n    public void onInteract(PlayerInteractEvent event) {\n        Action action = event.getAction();\n        if (action != Action.RIGHT_CLICK_AIR && action != Action.RIGHT_CLICK_BLOCK) {\n            return;\n        }\n        if (claimVoucher(event.getPlayer(), event.getItem())) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void openMainGui(Player player) {\n        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(null, 45, mainTitle());\n        fillBorder(inventory, Material.BLACK_STAINED_GLASS_PANE);\n        inventory.setItem(4, item(Material.NETHER_STAR, \"&6&lAurumMorph\", \"&7Goldstube Styles nur per GUI.\", \"&7W\\u00e4hle Gr\\u00f6sse oder Glow.\"));\n        inventory.setItem(MAIN_SIZE_SLOT, item(Material.GOLDEN_BOOTS, \"&e&lK\\u00f6rpergr\\u00f6sse\", \"&7Aktuell: \" + sizeDisplay(getSelectedSize(player)), \"&aClick: Gr\\u00f6ssen \\u00f6ffnen\"));\n        inventory.setItem(MAIN_GLOW_SLOT, item(Material.GLOWSTONE_DUST, \"&b&lGlowfarben\", \"&7Aktuell: \" + glowDisplay(getSelectedGlow(player)), \"&aClick: Glowfarben \\u00f6ffnen\"));\n        inventory.setItem(40, item(Material.BARRIER, \"&cSchliessen\"));\n        player.openInventory(inventory);\n    }\n\n    private void handleMainClick(Player player, int slot) {\n        if (slot == MAIN_SIZE_SLOT) {\n            openSizeGui(player);\n        } else if (slot == MAIN_GLOW_SLOT) {\n            openGlowGui(player);\n        } else if (slot == 40) {\n            player.closeInventory();\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void openSizeGui(Player player) {\n        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(null, 54, sizeTitle());\n        fillBorder(inventory, Material.YELLOW_STAINED_GLASS_PANE);\n        inventory.setItem(4, item(Material.GOLDEN_BOOTS, \"&e&lK\\u00f6rpergr\\u00f6ssen\", \"&7Freigeschaltete Gr\\u00f6ssen ausw\\u00e4hlen.\"));\n        for (String id : ids(\"sizes\")) {\n            int slot = getConfig().getInt(\"sizes.\" + id + \".slot\", 19);\n            inventory.setItem(slot, styleItem(player, \"size\", id));\n        }\n        inventory.setItem(BACK_SLOT, item(Material.ARROW, \"&eZur\\u00fcck\"));\n        inventory.setItem(CLOSE_SLOT, item(Material.BARRIER, \"&cSchliessen\"));\n        player.openInventory(inventory);\n    }\n\n    private void handleSizeClick(Player player, int slot) {\n        if (slot == BACK_SLOT) {\n            openMainGui(player);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (slot == CLOSE_SLOT) {\n            player.closeInventory();\n            return;\n        }\n        String id = idBySlot(\"sizes\", slot);\n        if (id == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        if (!isUnlocked(player, \"size\", id)) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDiese Gr\\u00f6sse ist nicht freigeschaltet.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        setSelectedSize(player, id);\n        applyScale(player);\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&aGr\\u00f6sse gesetzt: &f\" + plainName(\"sizes\", id)));\n        openSizeGui(player);\n    }\n\n    private void openGlowGui(Player player) {\n        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(null, 54, glowTitle());\n        fillBorder(inventory, Material.LIGHT_BLUE_STAINED_GLASS_PANE);\n        inventory.setItem(4, item(Material.GLOWSTONE_DUST, \"&b&lGlowfarben\", \"&7Freigeschaltete Glowfarbe ausw\\u00e4hlen.\", \"&7Prisma Zufall w\\u00e4hlt zuf\\u00e4llig eine Farbe.\"));\n        for (String id : ids(\"glows\")) {\n            int slot = getConfig().getInt(\"glows.\" + id + \".slot\", 19);\n            inventory.setItem(slot, \"&cSchliessen\"));\n        player.openInventory(inventory);\n    }\n\n    private void handleGlowClick(Player player, int slot) {\n        if (slot == BACK_SLOT) {\n            openMainGui(player);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (slot == CLOSE_SLOT) {\n            player.closeInventory();\n            return;\n        }\n        String id = idBySlot(\"glows\", slot);\n        if (id == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        if (!isUnlocked(player, \"glow\", id)) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDiese Glowfarbe ist nicht freigeschaltet.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        setSelectedGlow(player, id);\n        applyGlow(player);\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&aGlow gesetzt: &f\" + plainName(\"glows\", id)));\n        openGlowGui(player);\n    }\n\n    private void openAdminGui(Player player) {\n        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(null, 45, adminTitle());\n        fillBorder(inventory, Material.RED_STAINED_GLASS_PANE);\n        inventory.setItem(4, item(Material.CHEST, \"&c&lAurumMorph Werkstatt\", \"&7Erstelle Freischalt-Items per Klick.\"));\n        inventory.setItem(MAIN_SIZE_SLOT, item(Material.GOLDEN_BOOTS, \"&e&lGr\\u00f6ssen-Siegel\", \"&aClick: Gr\\u00f6ssen-Items\"));\n        inventory.setItem(MAIN_GLOW_SLOT, item(Material.GLOWSTONE_DUST, \"&b&lGlow-Siegel\", \"&aClick: Glow-Items\"));\n        inventory.setItem(40, item(Material.BARRIER, \"&cSchliessen\"));\n        player.openInventory(inventory);\n    }\n\n    private void handleAdminClick(Player player, int slot) {\n        if (slot == MAIN_SIZE_SLOT) {\n            openAdminVoucherGui(player, \"size\");\n        } else if (slot == MAIN_GLOW_SLOT) {\n            openAdminVoucherGui(player, \"glow\");\n        } else if (slot == 40) {\n            player.closeInventory();\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void openAdminVoucherGui(Player player, String type) {\n        String title = \"size\".equals(type) ? adminSizeTitle() : adminGlowTitle();\n        String section = \"size\".equals(type) ? \"sizes\" : \"glows\";\n        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(null, 54, title);\n        fillBorder(inventory, \"size\".equals(type) ? Material.YELLOW_STAINED_GLASS_PANE : Material.LIGHT_BLUE_STAINED_GLASS_PANE);\n        inventory.setItem(4, item(Material.CHEST, \"&c&lItem-Werkstatt\", \"&7Klicke ein Siegel, um es zu erhalten.\"));\n        for (String id : ids(section)) {\n            int slot = getConfig().getInt(section + \".\" + id + \".slot\", 19);\n            inventory.setItem(slot, voucher(type, id, 1));\n        }\n        inventory.setItem(BACK_SLOT, item(Material.ARROW, \"&eZur\\u00fcck\"));\n        inventory.setItem(CLOSE_SLOT, item(Material.BARRIER, \"&cSchliessen\"));\n        player.openInventory(inventory);\n    }\n\n    private void handleAdminVoucherClick(Player player, int slot, String type) {\n        if (slot == BACK_SLOT) {\n            openAdminGui(player);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (slot == CLOSE_SLOT) {\n            player.closeInventory();\n            return;\n        }\n        String section = \"size\".equals(type) ? \"sizes\" : \"glows\";\n        String id = idBySlot(section, slot);\n        if (id == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        player.getInventory().addItem(voucher(type, id, 1));\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&aSiegel erhalten: &f\" + plainName(section, id)));\n    }\n\n    private ItemStack styleItem(Player player, String type, String id) {\n        String section = item(material, display(section, id),\n                selected ? \"&aAktuell ausgew\\u00e4hlt\" : (unlocked ? \"&aFreigeschaltet\" : \"&cNicht freigeschaltet\"),\n                unlocked ? \"&aClick: ausw\\u00e4hlen\" : \"&7Erh\\u00e4ltlich aus Siegel-Items\");\n        if (selected) {\n            glint(item);\n        }\n        return item;\n    }\n\n    private ItemStack voucher(String type,        ItemStack item = new ItemStack(material, Math.max(1, amount));\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta != null) {\n            meta.setDisplayName(color(getConfig().getString(section + \".\" + id + \".voucher-name\", display(section, id) + \" &7Siegel\")));\n            meta.setLore(colorList(\n                    \"&7Rechtsklick: dauerhaft freischalten.\",\n                    \"&8AurumMorphReward: \" + type + \":\" + id\n            ));\n            item.setItemMeta(meta);\n        }\n        glint(item);\n        return item;\n    }\n\n    private boolean claimVoucher(Player player, ItemStack item) {\n        if (item == null || item.getType() == Material.AIR || !item.hasItemMeta() || !item.getItemMeta().hasLore()) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        String type = null;\n        String id = null;\n        for (String line : item.getItemMeta().getLore()) {\n            String stripped = ChatColor.stripColor(line);\n            if (!stripped.startsWith(\"AurumMorphReward: \")) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            String[] parts = stripped.substring(\"AurumMorphReward: \".length()).split(\":\", 2);\n            if (parts.length == 2) {\n                type = normalize(parts[0]);\n                id = normalize(parts[1]);\n            }\n        }\n        if (type == null || id == null || !exists(type, id)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        dataConfig.set(playerPath(player.getUniqueId()) + \".unlocked.\" + type + \".\" + id, true);\n        saveData();\n        consumeMainHand(player, item);\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&aFreigeschaltet: &f\" + plainName(\"size\".equals(type) ? \"sizes\" : \"glows\", id)));\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    private void consumeMainHand(Player player, ItemStack item) {\n        if (item.getAmount() <= 1) {\n            player.getInventory().setItemInMainHand(new ItemStack(Material.AIR));\n        } else {\n            item.setAmount(item.getAmount() - 1);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void applyPlayerState(Player  attribute.setBaseValue(Math.max(0.1D, Math.min(8.0D, scale)));\n    }\n\n    private void applyGlow(Player player) {\n        clearGlowTeams(player);\n        String id = getSelectedGlow(player);\n        if (\"random\".equalsIgnoreCase(id)) {\n            id = randomGlow(player);\n        }\n        if (\"off\".equalsIgnoreCase(id)) {\n            player.setGlowing(false);\n            return;\n        }\n        player.setGlowing(true);\n        for (Player viewer : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {\n            applyGlowColorForViewer(player, viewer, id);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void player) {\n        String playerName = player.getName();\n        clearGlowTeams(playerName, Bukkit.getScoreboardManager().getMainScoreboard());\n        for (Player viewer : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {\n            clearGlowTeams(playerName, viewer.getScoreboard());\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void clearGlowTeams(String playerName, Scoreboard scoreboard) {\n        if (scoreboard == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        for (Team team : scoreboard.getTeams()) {\n            if (team.getName().startsWith(\"am_\") && team.hasEntry(playerName)) {\n                team.removeEntry(playerName);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private String randomGlow(Player player) {\n        List<String> unlocked = new ArrayList<String>();\n        for (String id : ids(\"glows\")) {\n            if (!\"off\".equalsIgnoreCase(id) && !\"random\".equalsIgnoreCase(id) && isUnlocked(player, \"glow\", id)) {\n                unlocked.add(id);\n            }\n        }\n        if (unlocked.isEmpty()) {\n            return \"off\";\n        }\n        return unlocked.get(random.nextInt(unlocked.size()));\n    }\n\n    private ChatColor teamColor(String id) {\n        String value = getConfig().getString(\"glows.\" + id + \".team-color\", \"WHITE\");\n        try {\n            return ChatColor.valueOf(value.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));\n        } catch (IllegalArgumentException exception) {\n            return ChatColor.WHITE;\n        }\n    }\n\n    private boolean isUnlocked(Player player, String type, String id) {\n        String section = private String getSelectedGlow(Player player) {\n        String id = dataConfig.getString(playerPath(player.getUniqueId()) + \".selected.glow\", \"off\");\n        return return null;\n    }\n\n    private String display(String section, String id) {\n        return getConfig().getString(section + \".\" + id + \".display-name\", \"&f\" + pretty(id));\n    }\n\n meta.setDisplayName(color(name));\n            meta.setLore(colorList(lore));\n            item.setItemMeta(meta);\n        }\n        return item;\n    }\n\n    private List<String> }\n\n    private void fillBorder(Inventory inventory, Material material) {\n        ItemStack filler = item(material, \" \");\n        int rows = inventory.getSize() / 9;\n        for (int slot = 0; slot < inventory.getSize(); slot++) {\n            int row = slot / 9;\n            int column = slot % 9;\n            if (row == 0 || row == rows - 1 || column == 0 || column == 8) {\n                inventory.setItem(slot, filler);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void glint(ItemStack item) {\n        Enchantment enchantment = Enchantment.getByName(\"DURABILITY\");\n        if  }\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta != null) {\n            meta.addItemFlags(ItemFlag.HIDE_ENCHANTS);\n            item.setItemMeta(meta);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private String mainTitle() {\n        return color(getConfig().getString(\"gui.main-title\", \"&6AurumMorph\"));\n    }\n\n    private String sizeTitle() {\n        return color(getConfig().getString(\"gui.size-title\", \"&6AurumMorph - Gr\\u00f6ssen\"));\n    }\n\n    private String glowTitle() {\n        return color(getConfig().getString(\"gui.glow-title\", adminSizeTitle() {\n        return color(getConfig().getString(\"gui.admin-size-title\", \"&6AurumMorph - Gr\\u00f6ssen Items\"));\n    }\n\n    private String adminGlowTitle() {\n        return
