   public void onEnable() {\n        saveDefaultConfig();\n        getConfig().options().copyDefaults(true);\n        saveConfig();\n        database = new SqlDocumentStore(this);\n        database.initialize();\n        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimerAsynchronously(this, database::reconnectIfNeeded, 1200L, 1200L);\n        baitKey = new NamespacedKey(this, \"bait_id\");\n        sellValueKey = new NamespacedKey(this, \"sell_value\");\n        fishingItemKey = new NamespacedKey(this, \"fishing_item\");\n        specialTypeKey = new NamespacedKey(this, \"special_type\");\n        storyKey = new NamespacedKey(this, \"story\");\n        rodTypeKey = new NamespacedKey(this, \"rod_type\");\n        lootboxKey = new NamespacedKey(this, \"lootbox_id\");\n        bossIdKey = new NamespacedKey(this, \"boss_id\");\n        fishSpeciesKey = new NamespacedKey(this, \"fish_species\");\n        fishQualityKey = new NamespacedKey(this, \"fish_quality\");\n        dataFile = new File(getDataFolder(), \"data.yml\");\n        database.restoreIfNewer(\"fishing:data\", dataFile);\n        data = YamlConfiguration.loadConfiguration(dataFile);\n        repairMojibakeConfig();\n        applyBalanceUpdate();\n        reportConfigIssues();\n        Bukkit.getPluginManager().registerEvents(this, this);\n        Objects.requireNonNull(getCommand(\"angeln\"), \"angeln command\").setExecutor(this);\n        Objects.requireNonNull(getCommand(\"angeln\"), \"angeln command\").setTabCompleter(this);\n        Objects.requireNonNull(getCommand(\"angelpass\"), \"angelpass command\").setExecutor(this);\n        getLogger().info(\"FishingPlugin ist aktiv.\");\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void onDisable() {\n        shuttingDown = true;\n        clearFishingReactionHuds();\n        clearReelingGames();\n        flushData();\n        if (database != null) {\n            database.close();\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onFish(PlayerFishEvent event) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.enabled\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        if (event.getState() == PlayerFishEvent.State.BITE) {\n            long biteAt = System.currentTimeMillis();\n            biteTimes.put(event.getPlayer().getUniqueId(), biteAt);\n            startFishingReactionHud(event.getPlayer(), biteAt);\n            return;\n        }\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n        if (event.getState() == PlayerFishEvent.State.FISHING) {\n            stopFishingReactionHud(player.getUniqueId(), false);\n            stopReelingGame(player.getUniqueId(), false);\n            prepareSpecialFishingSession(player, event);\n            String realm = detectSpecialFishingRealm(player, event.getHook() == null ? player.getLocation() : event.getHook().getLocation());\n            if ((\"lava\".equalsIgnoreCase(realm) || \"void\".equalsIgnoreCase(realm)) && blockSpecialRealmCastIfCoolingDown(event, player, realm)) {\n                return;\n            }\n            return;\n        }\n        String realm = fishingRealm(player, event);\n        if (isSpecialFishingReelState(event.getState()) && !\"lava\".equalsIgnoreCase(realm) && !\"void\".equalsIgnoreCase(realm)) {\n            realm = detectSpecialFishingRealm(player, event.getHook() == null ? player.getLocation() : event.getHook().getLocation());\n        }\n        if (isSpecialFishingReelState(event.getState()) && isSpecialRealmManualCatch(player, realm)) {\n            if (getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.permission-required\", false) && !(player.hasPermission(\"chaossmp.fishing.use\") || player.hasPermission(\"goldstube.fishing.use\"))) {\n                return;\n            }\n            if (isBlockedByAntiAfk(player)) {\n                player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cBewege dich etwas, bevor du weitere Angelbelohnungen erhältst.\"));\n                return;\n            }\n            handleSpecialRealmManualCatch(event, player, realm);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (event.getState() != PlayerFishEvent.State.CAUGHT_FISH) {\n            if (isFishingAttemptEndState(event.getState())) {\n                if (biteTimes.remove(player.getUniqueId()) != null) {\n                    resetPrecisionFocus(player, false);\n                }\n                stopFishingReactionHud(player.getUniqueId(), false);\n            }\n            return;\n        }\n        if (getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.permission-required\", false) && !(player.hasPermission(\"chaossmp.fishing.use\") || player.hasPermission(\"goldstube.fishing.use\"))) {\n            return;\n        }\n        if (!canFishInRealm(player, realm)) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (isBlockedByAntiAfk(player)) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cBewege dich etwas, bevor du weitere Angelbelohnungen erhältst.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        if (isBlockedByRealisticFishing(player)) {\n            if (getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.realistic.cancel-catch\", false)) {\n                event.setCancelled(true);\n                player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + getConfig().getString(\"fishing.realistic.block-message\", \"&cDer Anhieb war zu perfekt. Bitte angle manuell und reagiere normal.\")));\n                return;\n            }\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + getConfig().getString(\"fishing.realistic.warning-message\", \"&eUngewöhnlicher Anhieb erkannt. Der Fang wird gezählt, aber markiert.\")));\n        }\n\n        if (shouldStartReelingGame(player, realm)) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            if (event.getCaught() != null && !event.getCaught().isDead()) {\n                event.getCaught().remove();\n            }\n            startReelingGame(player, realm, event.getExpToDrop());\n            return;\n        }\n\n        processFishingReward(player, event.getCaught(), realm, reward -> {\n            if (reward.experience() > 0) {\n                event.setExpToDrop(event.getExpToDrop() + reward.experience());\n            }\n            if (reward.replacement() != null) {\n                replaceCaughtItem(event.getCaught(), reward.replacement());\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    private boolean blockSpecialRealmCastIfCoolingDown(PlayerFishEvent event, Player player, String realm) {\n        long cooldown = getConfig().getLong(\"fishing.realms.\" + realm + \".manual-catch.cooldown-ms\", 5500L);\n        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        long last = specialRealmCooldowns.getOrDefault(player.getUniqueId(), 0L);\n        if (cooldown <= 0L || now - last >= cooldown) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        event.setCancelled(true);\n        cleanupSpecialFishingAttempt(event, player);\n        long remaining = Math.max(1L, (cooldown - (now - last) + 999L) / 1000L);\n        player.spigot().sendMessage(ChatMessageType.ACTION_BAR, TextComponent.fromLegacyText(color(prefix()\n                + getConfig().getString(\"fishing.realms.\" + realm + \".manual-catch.cooldown-message\", \"&cLava/Void-Angeln lädt noch &f{seconds}s&c.\")\n                .replace(\"{seconds}\", String.valueOf(remaining)))));\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler\n    public void onPlayerInteract(PlayerInteractEvent event) {\n        if (event.getHand() != EquipmentSlot.HAND) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Action action = event.getAction();\n        if (action != Action.RIGHT_CLICK_AIR && action != Action.RIGHT_CLICK_BLOCK) {\n            return;\n        }\n        ItemStack item = event.getItem();\n        if (item == null || item.getType().isAir()) {\n            return;\n        }\n        if (handleReelingGamePull(event.getPlayer(), item)) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (handleSpecialCatchItem(event.getPlayer(), item)) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (!isBaitItem(item)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        event.setCancelled(true);\n        activateBait(event.getPlayer(), item);\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onBaitInventoryClick(InventoryClickEvent event) {\n        if (event.getView().getTopInventory().getHolder() instanceof FishingGuiHolder) {\n            return;\n        }\n        if (!(event.getWhoClicked() instanceof Player player) || !event.isRightClick()) {\n            return;\n        }\n        if (event.getClickedInventory() == null || !event.getClickedInventory().equals(player.getInventory())) {\n            return;\n        }\n        ItemStack clicked = event.getCurrentItem();\n        if (clicked == null || clicked.getType().isAir() || !isBaitItem(clicked)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        event.setCancelled(true);\n        activateBait(player, clicked);\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler\n    public void onInventoryClick(InventoryClickEvent event) {\n        if (!(event.getView().getTopInventory().getHolder() instanceof FishingGuiHolder holder)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        event.setCancelled(true);\n        if (!(event.getWhoClicked() instanceof Player player) || event.getClickedInventory() == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        int slot = event.getRawSlot();\n        int topSize = event.getView().getTopInventory().getSize();\n        if (\"market\".equals(holder.page()) && slot >= topSize) {\n            sellClickedInventoryItem(player, event.getCurrentItem());\n            return;\n        }\n        ItemStack clicked = event.getCurrentItem();\n        if (clicked == null || clicked.getType().isAir()) {\n            return;\n        }\n        if (slot < 0 || slot >= topSize) {\n            return;\n        }\n        if (isBackSlot(slot) && !\"main\".equals(holder.page())) {\n            if (\"lootbox-preview\".equals(holder.page())) {\n                openLootboxGui(player);\n                return;\n            }\n            if (\"shop-items\".equals(holder.page())) {\n                openShopGui(player);\n                return;\n            }\n            if (\"drop-items\".equals(holder.page())) {\n                openMainGui(player);\n                return;\n            }\n            openMainGui(player);\n            return;\n        }\n        String action = holder.actions().get(slot);\n        if (action != null && action.startsWith(\"page:\")) {\n            openPagedGui(player, holder, parseInt(action.substring(\"page:\".length()), 0));\n            return;\n        }\n        if (holder.page().equals(\"main\")) {\n            if (slot == 4) {\n                openStatsGui(player);\n            } else if (slot == 10) {\n                openQuestGui(player);\n            } else if (slot == 11) {\n                openCollectionGui(player);\n            } else if (slot == 12) {\n                openPassGui(player);\n            } else if (slot == 14) {\n                openShopGui(player);\n            } else if (slot == 15) {\n                openMarketGui(player);\n            } else if (slot == 16) {\n                openTopGui(player);\n            } else if (slot == 22) {\n                openGuideGui(player);\n            } else if (slot == 24) {\n                openLootboxGui(player);\n            } else if (slot == 28) {\n                openDropsGui(player);\n            } else if (slot == 30) {\n                openAchievementsGui(player);\n            } else if (slot == 32) {\n                openDropsGui(player);\n            } else if (slot == 34) {\n                openSpotsGui(player);\n            } else if (slot == 40) {\n                openSettingsGui(player);\n            }\n        } else if (holder.page().equals(\"settings\")) {\n            if (slot == 20) {\n                cycleFishingMode(player);\n                openSettingsGui(player);\n            } else if (slot == 22) {\n                toggleCatchActionBar(player);\n                openSettingsGui(player);\n            } else if (slot == 24) {\n                sendReelingHelp(player, \"angeln\");\n                player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.UI_BUTTON_CLICK, 0.7f, 1.15f);\n            }\n        } else if (holder.page().equals(\"guide\")) {\n            if (slot == 10) {\n                openShopGui(player);\n            } else if (slot == 11) {\n                openMarketGui(player);\n            } else if (slot == 12) {\n                openQuestGui(player);\n            } else if (slot == 13) {\n                openCollectionGui(player);\n            } else if (slot == 14) {\n                openLootboxGui(player);\n            } else if (slot == 15) {\n                openSpotsGui(player);\n            } else if (slot == 16) {\n                openDropsGui(player);\n            }\n        } else if (holder.page().equals(\"drops\")) {\n            if (\"mystery\".equals(action) || \"overworld\".equals(action) || \"all\".equals(action)) {\n                openDropsCategoryGui(player, action, 0);\n            }\n        } else if (holder.page().equals(\"shop\")) {\n            if (\"rods\".equals(action) || \"bait\".equals(action) || \"special\".equals(action) || \"all\".equals(action)) {\n                openShopCategoryGui(player, action, 0);\n            }\n        } else if (holder.page().equals(\"shop-items\")) {\n            String shopId = action;\n            if (shopId != null) {\n                buyShopItem(player, shopId);\n            }\n        } else if (holder.page().equals(\"lootboxes\")) {\n            String lootboxId = action;\n            if (lootboxId != null) {\n                openLootboxPreviewGui(player, lootboxId);\n            }\n        } else if (holder.page().equals(\"market\")) {\n            if (slot == 22) {\n                sellAllFishingItems(player);\n                openMarketGui(player);\n            }\n        } else if (holder.page().equals(\"pass\") && action != null) {\n            if (action.startsWith(\"claim-pass:\")) {\n                int rewardLevel = parseInt(action.substring(\"claim-pass:\".length()), 0);\n                if (rewardLevel > 0) {\n                    claimFishingPassReward(player, rewardLevel);\n                    openPassGui(player, pageFromFishingPassLevel(rewardLevel));\n                }\n            } else if (action.startsWith(\"claim-pass-all:\")) {\n                int page = parseInt(action.substring(\"claim-pass-all:\".length()), 0);\n                claimAvailableFishingPassRewards(player);\n                openPassGui(player, page);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private int pageFromFishingPassLevel(int level) {\n        ConfigurationSection rewards = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.pass.rewards\");\n        if (rewards == null) {\n            return 0;\n        }\n        List<String> keys = rewards.getKeys(false).stream()\n                .sorted(Comparator.comparingInt(key -> parseInt(key, 0)))\n                .toList();\n        int index = keys.indexOf(String.valueOf(level));\n        return index < 0 ? 0 : index / 7;\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler\n    public void onInventoryDrag(InventoryDragEvent event) {\n        if (event.getView().getTopInventory().getHolder() instanceof FishingGuiHolder) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean onCommand(CommandSender sender, Command command, String label, String[] args) {\n        if args[1], \"status\");\n            return result;\n        }\n        if (args.length == 2 && (\"openbox\".equalsIgnoreCase(args[0]) || \"testbox\".equalsIgnoreCase(args[0])) && getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.bosses.enabled\", false)) {\n            List<String> result = new ArrayList<>();\n            for (BossDefinition boss : bossDefinitions()) {\n                addIfMatches(result, args[1], boss.id());\n            }\n            return result;\n        }\n        if (args.length != 1) {\n            return List.of();\n        }\n        List<String> result = new ArrayList<>();\n        addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"gui\");\n        addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"hilfe\");\n        addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"guide\");\n        addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"spots\");\n        addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"settings\");\n        addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"reeling\");\n        addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"modus\");\n        addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"stats\");\n        addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"top\");\n        addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"drops\");\n        addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"shop\");\n        addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"markt\");\n        addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"collection\");\n        addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"pass\");\n        addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"quests\");\n        addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"achievements\");\n        addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"lootboxen\");\n        if ((sender.hasPermission(\"chaossmp.fishing.admin\") || sender.hasPermission(\"goldstube.fishing.admin\"))) {\n            addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"event\");\n            addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"check\");\n            addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"inspect\");\n            addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"openbox\");\n            addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"simulate\");\n            addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"give\");\n            if (getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.bosses.enabled\", false)) {\n                addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"spawnboss\");\n            }\n        }\n        if ((sender.hasPermission(\"chaossmp.fishing.admin\") || sender.hasPermission(\"goldstube.fishing.admin\"))) {\n            addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"reload\");\n        }\n        return result;\n    }\n\n    private void handleFishingModeCommand(CommandSender sender, String label, String[] args) {\n        if (!(sender instanceof Player player)) {\n            sendUsage(sender, label);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (args.length < 2) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&e/\" + label + \" modus <auto|einfach|classic>\"));\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&7Aktuell: &f\" + fishingModeDisplay(player)));\n            return;\n        }\n        String mode = normalizeFishingMode(args[1]);\n        if (mode.isBlank()) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cUnbekannter Angelmodus. Nutze: &eauto&7, &eeinfach &7oder &eclassic&7.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        data.set(\"players.\" + player.getUniqueId() + \".settings.reeling-mode\", mode);\n        saveData();\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&aAngelmodus gesetzt: &f\" + fishingModeDisplay(player)));\n        player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.UI_BUTTON_CLICK, 0.7f, 1.25f);\n    }\n\n    private String normalizeFishingMode(String input) {\n        String value = input == null ? \"\" : input.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n        if (\"auto\".equals(value) || \"standard\".equals(value) || \"server\".equals(value)) {\n            return \"auto\";\n        }\n        if (\"einfach\".equals(value) || \"easy\".equals(value) || \"kurz\".equals(value)) {\n            return \"easy\";\n        }\n        if (\"classic\".equals(value) || \"klassisch\".equals(value) || \"normal\".equals(value) || \"aus\".equals(value) || \"off\".equals(value)) {\n            return \"classic\";\n        }\n        return \"\";\n    }\n\n    private String fishingMode(Player player) {\n        String mode = data.getString(\"players.\" + player.getUniqueId() + \".settings.reeling-mode\", \"auto\");\n        return switch (mode == null ? \"auto\" : mode.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)) {\n            case \"easy\", \"classic\" -> mode.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n            default -> \"auto\";\n        };\n    }\n\n    private String fishingModeDisplay(Player player) {\n        return switch (fishingMode(player)) {\n            case \"easy\" -> \"Einfach (kurzes Minispiel)\";\n            case \"classic\" -> \"Classic (ohne Minispiel)\";\n            default -> \"Auto (Serverstandard)\";\n        };\n    }\n\n    private void cycleFishingMode(Player player) {\n        String next = switch (fishingMode(player)) {\n            case \"auto\" -> \"easy\";\n            case \"easy\" -> \"classic\";\n            default -> \"auto\";\n        };\n        data.set(\"players.\" + player.getUniqueId() + \".settings.reeling-mode\", next);\n        saveData();\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&aAngelmodus: &f\" + fishingModeDisplay(player)));\n        player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.UI_BUTTON_CLICK, 0.7f, 1.25f);\n    }\n\n    private void handleGiveCommand(CommandSender sender, String label, String[] args) {\n        if (!(sender.hasPermission(\"chaossmp.fishing.admin\") || sender.hasPermission(\"goldstube.fishing.admin\"))) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDazu hast du keine Rechte.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        if (args.length < 3) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&e/\" + label + \" give <Spieler> <Shop-ID|lootbox:ID> [Menge]\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        Player target = Bukkit.getPlayerExact(args[1]);\n        if (target == null) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cSpieler nicht gefunden: &f\" + args[1]));\n            return;\n        }\n        String itemId = args[2];\n        int amount = 1;\n        if (args.length >= 4) {\n            try {\n                amount = Math.max(1, Math.min(64, Integer.parseInt(args[3])));\n            } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) {\n                sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cMenge muss eine Zahl sein.\"));\n                return;\n            }\n        }\n        if (itemId.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).startsWith(\"lootbox:\")) {\n            String lootboxId = itemId.substring(\"lootbox:\".length());\n            ConfigurationSection lootbox = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.lootboxes.\" + lootboxId);\n            if (lootbox == null || !lootbox.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true)) {\n                sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cLootbox-ID nicht gefunden: &f\" + lootboxId));\n                return;\n            }\n            for (int i = 0; i < amount; i++) {\n                giveItem(target, createLootboxItem(lootboxId, lootbox));\n            }\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&a\" + target.getName() + \" hat &f\" + amount + \"x &dLootbox:\" + lootboxId + \" &aerhalten.\"));\n            target.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&aDu hast &f\" + amount + \"x \" + lootbox.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(lootboxId)) + \" &aerhalten.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        String shopId = itemId;\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"shop.items.\" + shopId);\n        if (section == null || !section.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true)) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cShop-ID nicht gefunden: &f\" + shopId + \" &7oder nutze &elootbox:<id>&7.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        for (int i = 0; i < amount; i++) {\n            if (\"bait\".equalsIgnoreCase(section.getString(\"type\", \"item\"))) {\n                giveItem(target, createBaitItem(shopId, section));\n            } else if (\"rod\".equalsIgnoreCase(section.getString(\"type\", \"item\"))) {\n                giveItem(target, createRodItem(shopId, section));\n            } else {\n                Material material = Material.matchMaterial(section.getString(\"material\", \"COD\"));\n                if (material != null && !material.isAir()) {\n                    giveItem(target, new ItemStack(material, Math.max(1, section.getInt(\"amount\", 1))));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&a\" + target.getName() + \" hat &f\" + amount + \"x &e\" + shopId + \" &aerhalten.\"));\n        target.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&aDu hast &f\" + amount + \"x &e\" + section.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(shopId)) + \" &aerhalten.\"));\n    }\n\n    private void handleFishingEventCommand(CommandSender sender, String label, String[] args) {\n        if (!(sender.hasPermission(\"chaossmp.fishing.admin\") || sender.hasPermission(\"goldstube.fishing.admin\"))) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDazu hast du keine Rechte.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        if (args.length < 2 || \"status\".equalsIgnoreCase(args[1])) {\n            if (isFishingEventActive()) {\n                sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&dAngel-Event aktiv: &f\" + remainingFishingEventMinutes() + \" Minuten übrig.\"));\n            } else {\n                sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&7Aktuell ist kein Angel-Event aktiv.\"));\n            }\n            return;\n        }\n        if (\"stop\".equalsIgnoreCase(args[1])) {\n            fishingEventEndsAt = 0L;\n            Bukkit.broadcastMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDas Angel-Event wurde beendet.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        if (\"start\".equalsIgnoreCase(args[1])) {\n            int minutes = getConfig().getInt(\"fishing.event.default-minutes\", 30);\n            if (args.length >= 3) {\n                try {\n                    minutes = Math.max(1, Integer.parseInt(args[2]));\n                } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) {\n                    sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cBitte gib Minuten als Zahl an.\"));\n                    return;\n                }\n            }\n            fishingEventEndsAt = System.currentTimeMillis() + minutes * 60_000L;\n            Bukkit.broadcastMessage(color(prefix() + getConfig().getString(\"fishing.event.start-message\", \"&dAngel-Event gestartet! &7Mehr Mystery, Rare und Jackpot-Chancen für &f{minutes} Minuten&7.\").replace(\"{minutes}\", String.valueOf(minutes))));\n            return;\n        }\n        sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&e/\" + label + \" event start [Minuten]&7, &e/\" + label + \" event stop&7, &e/\" + label + \" event status\"));\n    }\n\n    private void handleConfigCheckCommand(CommandSender sender) {\n        if (!(sender.hasPermission(\"chaossmp.fishing.admin\") || sender.hasPermission(\"goldstube.fishing.admin\"))) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDazu hast du keine Rechte.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        List<String> issues = validateFishingConfig();\n        if (issues.isEmpty()) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&aConfig-Check bestanden. Keine Probleme gefunden.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&eConfig-Check: &c\" + issues.size() + \" Hinweis(e) gefunden.\"));\n        int limit = Math.min(12, issues.size());\n        for (int i = 0; i < limit; i++) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(\"&8- &7\" + issues.get(i)));\n        }\n        if (issues.size() > limit) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&7Weitere Hinweise stehen in der Server-Konsole.\"));\n        }\n        for (String issue : issues) {\n            getLogger().warning(\"[Config-Check] \" + ChatColor.stripColor(color(issue)));\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void handleInspectCommand(CommandSender sender) {\n        if (!(sender.hasPermission(\"chaossmp.fishing.admin\") || sender.hasPermission(\"goldstube.fishing.admin\"))) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDazu hast du keine Rechte.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        if (!(sender instanceof Player player)) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cNur Spieler können ein Item in der Hand prüfen.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        ItemStack item = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();\n        if (item == null || item.getType().isAir()) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cHalte ein Item in der Hand.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&6Item-Inspect: &f\" + item.getType().name() + \" &8x\" + item.getAmount()));\n        if (meta == null) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(\"&8- &7Keine ItemMeta vorhanden.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        PersistentDataContainer container = meta.getPersistentDataContainer();\n        player.sendMessage(color(\"&8- &7Name: &f\" + (meta.hasDisplayName() ? ChatColor.stripColor(meta.getDisplayName()) : \"-\")));\n        player.sendMessage(color(\"&8- &7Fishing Item: &f\" + container.has(fishingItemKey, PersistentDataType.BYTE)));\n        player.sendMessage(color(\"&8- &7Marktwert einzeln: &e\" + marketValue(item, false)));\n        player.sendMessage(color(\"&8- &7Marktwert Bulk: &e\" + marketValue(item, true)));\n        player.sendMessage(color(\"&8- &7Köder: &b\" + valueOrDash(container.get(baitKey, PersistentDataType.STRING))));\n        player.sendMessage(color(\"&8- &7Rod-Typ: &b\" + valueOrDash(container.get(rodTypeKey, PersistentDataType.STRING))));\n        player.sendMessage(color(\"&8- &7Special-Typ: &d\" + valueOrDash(container.get(specialTypeKey, PersistentDataType.STRING))));\n        player.sendMessage(color(\"&8- &7Lootbox-ID: &d\" + valueOrDash(container.get(lootboxKey, PersistentDataType.STRING))));\n        player.sendMessage(color(\"&8- &7Story vorhanden: &f\" + container.has(storyKey, PersistentDataType.STRING)));\n        player.sendMessage(color(\"&8- &7Als Köder erkannt: &f\" + isBaitItem(item)));\n        player.sendMessage(color(\"&8- &7Als Markt-Item erkannt: &f\" + (marketValue(item, false) > 0)));\n    }\n\n    private void handleOpenBoxCommand(CommandSender sender, String label, String[] args) {\n        if (!(sender.hasPermission(\"chaossmp.fishing.admin\") || sender.hasPermission(\"goldstube.fishing.admin\"))) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDazu hast du keine Rechte.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        if (!(sender instanceof Player player)) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cNur Spieler können eine Lootbox testweise öffnen.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        if (args.length < 2) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&e/\" + label + \" openbox <Lootbox-ID>\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        if (openFishingLootbox(player, args[1])) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&aTest-Lootbox geöffnet: &d\" + args[1]));\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void handleSimulateCommand(CommandSender sender, String label, String[] args) {\n        if (!(sender.hasPermission(\"chaossmp.fishing.admin\") || sender.hasPermission(\"goldstube.fishing.admin\"))) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDazu hast du keine Rechte.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        if (!(sender instanceof Player player)) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cSimulation braucht einen Spieler für Realm, Level und aktive Angel.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        int amount = 1000;\n        if (args.length >= 2) {\n            amount = parseInt(args[1], -1);\n            if (amount <= 0) {\n                sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&e/\" + label + \" simulate [Anzahl]\"));\n                return;\n            }\n        }\n        amount = Math.max(1, Math.min(100_000, amount));\n        SimulationResult result = simulateFishing(player, amount);\n        sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&6Simulation: &f\" + amount + \" Fänge &8| &7Realm: &b\" + result.realm() + \" &8| &7Rod: &b\" + result.rodType()));\n        sender.sendMessage(color(\"&8- &7Mystery: &d\" + result.mystery() + \" &8(\" + percent(result.mystery(), amount) + \"&8) &7Lootbox-Pulls: &d\" + result.lootboxes()));\n        sender.sendMessage(color(\"&8- &7Ø $: &e\" + average(result.coins(), amount) + \" &7Ø Rubine: &c\" + average(result.tokens(), amount) + \" &7Ø XP: &a\" + average(result.experience(), amount)));\n        sender.sendMessage(color(\"&8- &7Tiers: &f\" + summarizeCounts(result.tiers(), amount, 5)));\n        sender.sendMessage(color(\"&8- &7Kategorien: &f\" + summarizeCounts(result.categories(), amount, 6)));\n        sender.sendMessage(color(\"&8- &7Top Mystery: &f\" + summarizeCounts(result.mysteryDrops(), Math.max(1, result.mystery()), 6)));\n    }\n\n    private void handleSpawnBossCommand(CommandSender sender, String label, String[] args) {\n        if (!(sender.hasPermission(\"chaossmp.fishing.admin\") || sender.hasPermission(\"goldstube.fishing.admin\"))) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDazu hast du keine Rechte.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.bosses.enabled\", false)) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&7Angelbosse sind deaktiviert. Nutze &e/\" + label + \" drops &7für aktuelle Loot-Tabellen.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        if (!(sender instanceof Player player)) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cNur Spieler können einen Boss testweise spawnen.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        if (args.length < 2) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&e/\" + label + \" spawnboss <Boss-ID>\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        BossDefinition definition = bossDefinition(args[1]);\n        if (definition == null) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cBoss-ID nicht gefunden: &f\" + args[1]));\n            return;\n        }\n        spawnFishingBoss(player, definition.id(), definition.section());\n        sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&aBoss gespawnt: &f\" + definition.id()));\n    }\n\n    private SimulationResult simulateFishing(Player player, int amount) {\n        Map<String, Integer> tiers = new HashMap<>();\n        Map<String, Integer> categories = new HashMap<>();\n        Map<String, Integer> mysteryDrops = new HashMap<>();\n        BaitBonus bait = BaitBonus.none();\n        String realm = fishingRealm(player);\n        String rodType = activeRodType(player);\n        int level = getFishingLevel(player.getUniqueId());\n        int dryStreak = data.getInt(\"players.\" + player.getUniqueId() + \".mystery-dry-streak\", 0);\n        int mystery = 0;\n        int lootboxes = 0;\n        long coins = 0L;\n        long tokens = 0L;\n        long experience = 0L;\n        for (int i = 0; i < amount; i++) {\n            RewardTier tier = rollTier(player, bait);\n            incrementCount(tiers, tier.key());\n            incrementCount(categories, tier.category());\n            double coinMultiplier = (1.0 + Math.min(0.50, level * getConfig().getDouble(\"leveling.coin-bonus-per-level\", 0.01))) * rodCoinMultiplier(rodType);\n            coins += Math.round(randomBetween(tier.coinsMin(), tier.coinsMax()) * coinMultiplier);\n            tokens += Math.round(randomBetween(tier.tokensMin(), tier.tokensMax()) * rodTokenMultiplier(rodType));\n            experience += tier.experience();\n            MysteryDrop drop = rollMysteryDrop(player, 0.0, dryStreak);\n            if (drop == null) {\n                dryStreak++;\n                continue;\n            }\n            dryStreak = 0;\n            mystery++;\n            incrementCount(categories, drop.category());\n            incrementCount(mysteryDrops, drop.name());\n            coins += randomBetween(drop.coinsMin(), drop.coinsMax());\n            tokens += randomBetween(drop.tokensMin(), drop.tokensMax());\n            experience += drop.experience();\n            if (\"lootbox\".equalsIgnoreCase(drop.specialType()) || (drop.lootboxId() != null && !drop.lootboxId().isBlank())) {\n                lootboxes++;\n            }\n        }\n        return new SimulationResult(amount, realm, rodType, tiers, categories, mysteryDrops, mystery, lootboxes, coins, tokens, experience);\n    }\n\n    private CatchReward createBaseReward(Player player, RewardTier tier, BaitBonus bait) {\n        String rodType = activeRodType(player);\n        FishingSpot spot = activeFishingSpot(player);\n        double levelMultiplier = 1.0 + Math.min(0.50, getFishingLevel(player.getUniqueId()) * getConfig().getDouble(\"leveling.coin-bonus-per-level\", 0.01));\n        int coins = (int) Math.round(randomBetween(tier.coinsMin(), tier.coinsMax()) * levelMultiplier * bait.coinMultiplier() * rodCoinMultiplier(rodType) * spot.coinMultiplier());\n        int tokens = (int) Math.round(randomBetween(tier.tokensMin(), tier.tokensMax()) * bait.tokenMultiplier() * rodTokenMultiplier(rodType) * spot.tokenMultiplier());\n        ItemStack rareItem = null;\n        String dropName = randomFishSpecies(spot, tier.key());\n        String category = tier.category();\n        RareItem configuredRareItem = rollRareItem(tier.key());\n        if (configuredRareItem != null) {\n            rareItem = configuredRareItem.createItem();\n            dropName = configuredRareItem.displayName();\n        }\n        boolean specialTier = configuredRareItem != null && !\"normal\".equalsIgnoreCase(category);\n        List<String> commands = configuredRareItem == null ? List.of() : configuredRareItem.commands(player);\n        return new CatchReward(dropName, category, coins, tokens, tier.experience(), rareItem, commands, specialTier, false, false, CatchMoment.none());\n    }\n\n    private FishingSpot activeFishingSpot(Player player) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.spots.enabled\", true)) {\n            return fallbackSpot(\"default\");\n        }\n        String biome = player.getLocation().getBlock().getBiome().name();\n        int level = getFishingLevel(player.getUniqueId());\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.spots.list\");\n        if (section != null) {\n            for (String key : section.getKeys(false)) {\n                ConfigurationSection spot = section.getConfigurationSection(key);\n                if (spot == null || !spot.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true)) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                if (!spot.getStringList(\"biomes\").contains(biome)) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                FishingSpot configured = configuredSpot(key, spot);\n                return level >= configured.minLevel() ? configured : fallbackSpot(\"default\");\n            }\n        }\n        FishingSpot fallback = fallbackSpotByBiome(biome);\n        return level >= fallback.minLevel() ? fallback : fallbackSpot(\"default\");\n    }\n\n    private FishingSpot configuredSpot(String id, ConfigurationSection section) {\n        FishingSpot fallback = fallbackSpot(id);\n        return new FishingSpot(\n                id,\n                section.getString(\"name\", fallback.name()),\n                section.getInt(\"min-level\", fallback.minLevel()),\n                section.getDouble(\"coin-multiplier\", fallback.coinMultiplier()),\n                section.getDouble(\"token-multiplier\", fallback.tokenMultiplier()),\n                section.getDouble(\"rare-multiplier\", fallback.rareMultiplier()),\n                section.getDouble(\"jackpot-multiplier\", fallback.jackpotMultiplier()),\n                section.getDouble(\"mystery-bonus\", fallback.mysteryBonus()),\n                section.getInt(\"size-bonus\", fallback.sizeBonus()),\n                section.getStringList(\"species\").isEmpty() ? fallback.species() : section.getStringList(\"species\")\n        );\n    }\n\n    private FishingSpot fallbackSpotByBiome(String biome) {\n        if (biome.contains(\"RIVER\")) {\n            return fallbackSpot(\"fluss\");\n        }\n        if (biome.contains(\"PEAK\") || biome.contains(\"GROVE\") || biome.contains(\"SLOPE\") || biome.contains(\"MEADOW\")) {\n            return fallbackSpot(\"gebirgssee\");\n        }\n        if (biome.contains(\"WARM_OCEAN\") || biome.contains(\"LUKEWARM_OCEAN\")) {\n            return fallbackSpot(\"tropenmeer\");\n        }\n        if (biome.contains(\"DEEP_\") && biome.contains(\"OCEAN\")) {\n            return fallbackSpot(\"tiefsee\");\n        }\n        return fallbackSpot(\"dorfteich\");\n    }\n\n    private FishingSpot fallbackSpot(String id) {\n        return switch (id.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)) {\n            case \"fluss\" -> new FishingSpot(\"fluss\", \"Fluss\", 3, 1.05, 1.0, 1.10, 1.05, 1.0, 4, List.of(\"Bachforelle\", \"Silberaal\", \"Flussbarsch\", \"Lachs\"));\n            case \"gebirgssee\" -> new FishingSpot(\"gebirgssee\", \"Gebirgssee\", 10, 1.12, 1.05, 1.20, 1.12, 2.0, 10, List.of(\"Kristallforelle\", \"Bergsaibling\", \"Eisrenke\", \"Alpenhecht\"));\n            case \"tropenmeer\" -> new FishingSpot(\"tropenmeer\", \"Tropenmeer\", 18, 1.18, 1.10, 1.25, 1.15, 3.0, 14, List.of(\"Korallenfisch\", \"Goldmakrele\", \"Papageifisch\", \"Sonnenrochen\"));\n            case \"tiefsee\" -> new FishingSpot(\"tiefsee\", \"Tiefsee\", 30, 1.30, 1.20, 1.40, 1.35, 5.0, 25, List.of(\"Abyssaal\", \"Mondangler\", \"Tiefseehai\", \"Schattenmarlin\"));\n            case \"default\" -> new FishingSpot(\"default\", \"Freies Gewässer\", 1, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.0, 0, List.of(\"Karpfen\", \"Rotfeder\", \"Barsch\", \"Forelle\"));\n            default -> new FishingSpot(\"dorfteich\", \"Dorfteich\", 1, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.0, 0, List.of(\"Karpfen\", \"Rotfeder\", \"Teichforelle\", \"Schimmerkarpfen\"));\n        };\n    }\n\n    private String randomFishSpecies(FishingSpot spot, String tier) {\n        List<String> species = spot.species().isEmpty() ? fallbackSpot(\"default\").species() : spot.species();\n        if (\"jackpot\".equalsIgnoreCase(tier) && species.size() > 1) {\n            return species.get(species.size() - 1);\n        }\n        if (\"rare\".equalsIgnoreCase(tier) && species.size() > 2) {\n            return species.get(2 + random.nextInt(species.size() - 2));\n        }\n        return species.get(random.nextInt(species.size()));\n    }\n\n    private double fishingSpotTierMultiplier(Player player, String tier) {\n        FishingSpot spot = activeFishingSpot(player);\n        if (\"rare\".equalsIgnoreCase(tier)) {\n            return spot.rareMultiplier();\n        }\n        if (\"jackpot\".equalsIgnoreCase(tier)) {\n            return spot.jackpotMultiplier();\n        }\n        return 1.0;\n    }\n\n    private double fishingSpotMysteryBonus(Player player) {\n        FishingSpot spot = activeFishingSpot(player);\n        double weatherBonus = player.getWorld().hasStorm() ? getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.spots.weather.rain-mystery-bonus\", 1.5) : 0.0;\n        double nightBonus = player.getWorld().getTime() >= 13000L ? getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.spots.weather.night-mystery-bonus\", 1.0) : 0.0;\n        return spot.mysteryBonus() + weatherBonus + nightBonus;\n    }\n\n    private ItemStack createFishCatchItem(Player player, CatchReward reward) {\n        FishingSpot spot = activeFishingSpot(player);\n        Material material = fishMaterial(reward.category(), reward.name());\n        ItemStack item = new ItemStack(material);\n        if (System.currentTimeMillis() >= 0L) {\n            return enrichFishCatchItem(player, reward, item);\n        }\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta != null) {\n            String quality = reward.moment().name().isBlank() ? \"Normal\" : reward.moment().name();\n            int size = Math.max(12, reward.moment().size());\n            double weight = Math.max(0.1, Math.round((size * size / 2100.0 + random.nextDouble(0.35)) * 10.0) / 10.0);\n            meta.setDisplayName(color(reward.moment().color() + reward.name()));\n            meta.setLore(colorList(List.of(\n                    \"&8ChaosSMP Fang\",\n                    \"&7Spot: &b\" + spot.name(),\n                    \"&7Qualität: \" + reward.moment().color() + quality,\n                    \"&7Länge: &f\" + size + \" cm\",\n                    \"&7Gewicht: &f\" + weight + \" kg\",\n                    \"&7Wert: &e$\" + Math.max(1, reward.coins()),\n                    \"&8Im Angel-Markt verkaufbar.\"\n            )));\n            meta.addItemFlags(ItemFlag.HIDE_ATTRIBUTES);\n            item.setItemMeta(meta);\n        }\n        markFishingMarketItem(item, Math.max(1, reward.coins()));\n        return item;\n    }\n\n    private ItemStack enrichFishCatchItem(Player player, CatchReward reward, ItemStack item) {\n        FishingSpot spot = activeFishingSpot(player);\n        FishProfile profile = fishProfile(reward.name(), spot);\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta != null) {\n            String quality = reward.moment().name().isBlank() ? \"Normal\" : reward.moment().name();\n            int size = fishSize(reward, profile);\n            double weight = fishWeight(size, profile);\n            meta.setDisplayName(color(reward.moment().color() + profile.displayName()));\n            meta.setLore(colorList(List.of(\n                    \"&8ChaosSMP Fisch\",\n                    \"&7Art: &f\" + profile.displayName(),\n                    \"&7Spot: &b\" + spot.name(),\n                    \"&7Lebensraum: &b\" + profile.habitat(),\n                    \"&7Nahrung: &f\" + profile.diet(),\n                    \"&7Qualität: \" + reward.moment().color() + quality,\n                    \"&7Größe: &f\" + size + \" cm\",\n                    \"&7Gewicht: &f\" + weight + \" kg\",\n                    \"&7Seltenheit: \" + fishRarityLabel(reward.category()),\n                    \"&7Textur: &aEigenes Fischmodell\",\n                    \"&7Wert: &e$\" + Math.max(1, reward.coins()),\n                    \"&8Im Angel-Markt verkaufbar.\"\n            )));\n            meta.addItemFlags(ItemFlag.HIDE_ATTRIBUTES);\n            if (profile.modelData() > 0) {\n                meta.setCustomModelData(profile.modelData());\n            }\n            PersistentDataContainer container = meta.getPersistentDataContainer();\n            container.set(fishSpeciesKey, PersistentDataType.STRING, profile.key());\n            container.set(fishQualityKey, PersistentDataType.STRING, quality);\n            item.setItemMeta(meta);\n        }\n        markFishingMarketItem(item, Math.max(1, reward.coins()));\n        return item;\n    }\n\n    private boolean shouldApplyFishProfile(ItemStack item, CatchReward reward) {\n        if (item == null || item.getType().isAir()) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        if (!isFishMaterial(item.getType()) && getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.fish-profiles.\" + normalizeKey(reward.name())) == null) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        String normalized = normalizeKey(reward.name());\n        return !normalized.contains(\"lootbox\")\n                && !normalized.contains(\"kiste\")\n                && !normalized.contains(\"luckyblock\")\n                && !normalized.contains(\"schatzkarte\")\n                && !normalized.contains(\"kompass\");\n    }\n\n    private boolean isFishMaterial(Material material) {\n        return material == Material.COD || material == Material.SALMON || material == Material.TROPICAL_FISH || material == Material.PUFFERFISH;\n    }\n\n    private FishProfile fishProfile(String fishName, FishingSpot spot) {\n        String display = ChatColor.stripColor(color(fishName));\n        String key = normalizeKey(display);\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.fish-profiles.\" + key);\n        String habitat = section == null ? spot.name() : section.getString(\"habitat\", spot.name());\n        String diet = section == null ? defaultFishDiet(display) : section.getString(\"diet\", defaultFishDiet(display));\n        int minSize = section == null ? defaultMinFishSize(display) : section.getInt(\"size-min\", defaultMinFishSize(display));\n        int maxSize = section == null ? defaultMaxFishSize(display) : section.getInt(\"size-max\", defaultMaxFishSize(display));\n        double minWeight = section == null ? defaultMinFishWeight(display) : section.getDouble(\"weight-min\", defaultMinFishWeight(display));\n        double maxWeight = section == null ? defaultMaxFishWeight(display) : section.getDouble(\"weight-max\", defaultMaxFishWeight(display));\n        int modelData = section == null ? generatedFishModelData(key) : section.getInt(\"model-data\", generatedFishModelData(key));\n        return new FishProfile(key, display, habitat, diet, Math.max(1, minSize), Math.max(minSize, maxSize), Math.max(0.1, minWeight), Math.max(minWeight, maxWeight), modelData);\n    }\n\n    private int fishSize(CatchReward reward, FishProfile profile) {\n        int rolled = reward.moment().size() > 0 ? reward.moment().size() : randomBetween(profile.sizeMin(), profile.sizeMax());\n        return Math.max(profile.sizeMin(), Math.min(profile.sizeMax(), rolled));\n    }\n\n    private double fishWeight(int size, FishProfile profile) {\n        double ratio = profile.sizeMax() <= profile.sizeMin() ? 0.5 : (size - profile.sizeMin()) / (double)(profile.sizeMax() - profile.sizeMin());\n        double curved = Math.max(0.0, Math.min(1.0, ratio * ratio));\n        double weight = profile.weightMin() + (profile.weightMax() - profile.weightMin()) * curved + random.nextDouble(0.08);\n        return Math.max(0.1, Math.round(weight * 10.0) / 10.0);\n    }\n\n    private String fishRarityLabel(String category) {\n        return switch (category == null ? \"normal\" : category.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)) {\n            case \"rare\" -> \"&bSelten\";\n            case \"jackpot\" -> \"&6Legendaer\";\n            case \"treasure\" -> \"&eSchatzfang\";\n            case \"junk\" -> \"&7Beifang\";\n            default -> \"&aNormal\";\n        };\n    }\n\n    private int generatedFishModelData(String key) {\n        return 120000 + Math.abs(key.hashCode() % 8000);\n    }\n\n    private String defaultFishDiet(String name) {\n        String normalized = name.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n        if (normalized.contains(\"hecht\") || normalized.contains(\"zander\") || normalized.contains(\"barsch\") || normalized.contains(\"wels\") || normalized.contains(\"lachs\")) {\n            return \"Kleinfische\";\n        }\n        if (normalized.contains(\"karpfen\") || normalized.contains(\"brasse\") || normalized.contains(\"schleie\")) {\n            return \"Pflanzen, Muscheln und Insektenlarven\";\n        }\n        if (normalized.contains(\"forelle\") || normalized.contains(\"dorsch\")) {\n            return \"Insekten, Larven und kleine Fische\";\n        }\n        return \"Plankton, Larven und Kleintiere\";\n    }\n\n    private int defaultMinFishSize(String name) {\n        String normalized = name.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n        if (normalized.contains(\"wels\")) return 80;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"hecht\")) return 45;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"karpfen\")) return 35;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"lachs\")) return 40;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"zander\")) return 35;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"forelle\")) return 22;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"dorsch\")) return 30;\n        return 12;\n    }\n\n    private int defaultMaxFishSize(String name) {\n        String normalized = name.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n        if (normalized.contains(\"wels\")) return 220;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"hecht\")) return 140;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"karpfen\")) return 110;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"lachs\")) return 120;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"zander\")) return 95;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"forelle\")) return 85;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"dorsch\")) return 120;\n        return 45;\n    }\n\n    private double defaultMinFishWeight(String name) {\n        String normalized = name.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n        if (normalized.contains(\"wels\")) return 4.0;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"hecht\")) return 0.8;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"karpfen\")) return 1.2;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"lachs\")) return 1.0;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"zander\")) return 0.7;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"forelle\")) return 0.2;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"dorsch\")) return 0.5;\n        return 0.1;\n    }\n\n    private double defaultMaxFishWeight(String name) {\n        String normalized = name.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n        if (normalized.contains(\"wels\")) return 95.0;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"hecht\")) return 25.0;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"karpfen\")) return 30.0;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"lachs\")) return 24.0;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"zander\")) return 12.0;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"forelle\")) return 8.0;\n        if (normalized.contains(\"dorsch\")) return 22.0;\n        return 2.5;\n    }\n\n    private Material fishMaterial(String category, String name) {\n        String normalized = name.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n        if (\"jackpot\".equalsIgnoreCase(category) || normalized.contains(\"marlin\") || normalized.contains(\"hai\")) {\n            return Material.TROPICAL_FISH;\n        }\n        if (normalized.contains(\"lachs\") || normalized.contains(\"saibling\") || normalized.contains(\"forelle\")) {\n            return Material.SALMON;\n        }\n        if (normalized.contains(\"aal\") || normalized.contains(\"angler\")) {\n            return Material.PUFFERFISH;\n        }\n        return Material.COD;\n    }\n\n    private MysteryDrop rollMysteryDrop(Player player, double bonusChance) {\n        int dryStreak = data.getInt(\"players.\" + player.getUniqueId() + \".mystery-dry-streak\", 0);\n        return rollMysteryDrop(player, bonusChance, dryStreak);\n    }\n\n    private MysteryDrop rollMysteryDrop(Player player, double bonusChance, int dryStreak) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.mystery-drops.enabled\", true)) {\n            return null;\n        }\n        double levelBonus = Math.min(\n                getConfig().getDouble(\"leveling.max-mystery-chance-bonus\", 8.0),\n                getFishingLevel(player.getUniqueId()) * getConfig().getDouble(\"leveling.mystery-chance-bonus-per-level\", 0.08)\n        );\n        double eventBonus = isFishingEventActive() ? getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.event.mystery-bonus\", 8.0) : 0.0;\n        double pityBonus = getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.pity.enabled\", true)\n                ? Math.min(getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.pity.max-bonus\", 20.0), dryStreak * getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.pity.bonus-per-catch\", 0.45))\n                : 0.0;\n        boolean guaranteed = getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.pity.enabled\", true)\n                && dryStreak >= getConfig().getInt(\"fishing.pity.guaranteed-after\", 65);\n        double chance = Math.min(95.0, getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.mystery-drops.chance\", 18.0) + bonusChance + levelBonus + eventBonus + groupMysteryBonus(player) + rodMysteryBonus(activeRodType(player)) + fishingSpotMysteryBonus(player) + pityBonus);\n        if (!guaranteed && random.nextDouble(100.0) >= chance) {\n            return null;\n        }\n        List<MysteryDrop> drops = new ArrayList<>();\n        double total = 0.0;\n        String realm = fishingRealm(player);\n        if (\"overworld\".equalsIgnoreCase(realm) || getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.realm-drops.include-global-in-special-realms\", false)) {\n            total += collectMysteryDrops(\"fishing.mystery-drops.drops\", drops);\n        }\n        total += collectMysteryDrops(\"fishing.realm-drops.\" + realm, drops);\n        if (drops.isEmpty() || total <= 0.0) {\n            return null;\n        }\n        double roll = random.nextDouble(total);\n        double cursor = 0.0;\n        for (MysteryDrop drop : drops) {\n            cursor += drop.weight();\n            if (roll <= cursor) {\n                return drop;\n            }\n        }\n        return drops.get(drops.size() - 1);\n    }\n\n    private double collectMysteryDrops(String path, List<MysteryDrop> drops) {\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(path);\n        if (section == null) {\n            return 0.0;\n        }\n        double total = 0.0;\n        for (String key : section.getKeys(false)) {\n            ConfigurationSection dropSection = section.getConfigurationSection(key);\n            if (dropSection == null || !dropSection.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true)) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            double weight = Math.max(0.0, dropSection.getDouble(\"weight\", 0.0));\n            if (weight <= 0.0) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            MysteryDrop drop = MysteryDrop.fromConfig(key, dropSection);\n            drops.add(drop);\n            total += weight;\n        }\n        return total;\n    }\n\n    private RewardTier rollTier(Player player, BaitBonus bait) {\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.rewards.tiers\");\n        if (section == null) {\n            return new RewardTier(\"normal\", 0.0, \"normal\", 8, 18, 0, 1, 2);\n        }\n        int level = getFishingLevel(player.getUniqueId());\n        List<RewardTier> tiers = new ArrayList<>();\n        double total = 0.0;\n        for (String key : section.getKeys(false)) {\n            ConfigurationSection tierSection = section.getConfigurationSection(key);\n            if (tierSection == null) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            RewardTier tier = new RewardTier(\n                    key,\n                    Math.max(0.0, tierSection.getDouble(\"chance\", 0.0) * bait.tierMultiplier(key) * levelTierMultiplier(level, key) * eventTierMultiplier(key) * groupTierMultiplier(player, key) * rodTierMultiplier(activeRodType(player), key) * fishingSpotTierMultiplier(player, key)),\n                    tierSection.getString(\"category\", key),\n                    tierSection.getInt(\"coins-min\", 0),\n                    tierSection.getInt(\"coins-max\", 0),\n                    tierSection.getInt(\"tokens-min\", 0),\n                    tierSection.getInt(\"tokens-max\", 0),\n                    tierSection.getInt(\"experience\", 0)\n            );\n            tiers.add(tier);\n            total += tier.chance();\n        }\n        if (tiers.isEmpty() || total <= 0.0) {\n            return new RewardTier(\"normal\", 0.0, \"normal\", 8, 18, 0, 1, 2);\n        }\n        double roll = random.nextDouble(total);\n        double cursor = 0.0;\n        for (RewardTier tier : tiers) {\n            cursor += tier.chance();\n            if (roll <= cursor) {\n                return tier;\n            }\n        }\n        return tiers.get(tiers.size() - 1);\n    }\n\n    private double levelTierMultiplier(int level, String tier) {\n        if (\"rare\".equalsIgnoreCase(tier)) {\n            return 1.0 + Math.min(\n                    getConfig().getDouble(\"leveling.max-rare-chance-bonus\", 1.0),\n                    level * getConfig().getDouble(\"leveling.rare-chance-bonus-per-level\", 0.012)\n            );\n        }\n        if (\"jackpot\".equalsIgnoreCase(tier)) {\n            return 1.0 + Math.min(\n                    getConfig().getDouble(\"leveling.max-jackpot-chance-bonus\", 1.5),\n                    level * getConfig().getDouble(\"leveling.jackpot-chance-bonus-per-level\", 0.018)\n            );\n        }\n        return 1.0;\n    }\n\n    private double eventTierMultiplier(String tier) {\n        if (!isFishingEventActive()) {\n            return 1.0;\n        }\n        if (\"rare\".equalsIgnoreCase(tier)) {\n            return getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.event.rare-multiplier\", 1.6);\n        }\n        if (\"jackpot\".equalsIgnoreCase(tier)) {\n            return getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.event.jackpot-multiplier\", 2.0);\n        }\n        return 1.0;\n    }\n\n    private boolean isFishingEventActive() {\n        return fishingEventEndsAt > System.currentTimeMillis();\n    }\n\n    private long remainingFishingEventMinutes() {\n        if (!isFishingEventActive()) {\n            return 0L;\n        }\n        return Math.max(1L, (fishingEventEndsAt - System.currentTimeMillis() + 59_999L) / 60_000L);\n    }\n\n    private RareItem rollRareItem(String tier) {\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.rewards.rare-items\");\n        if (section == null) {\n            return null;\n        }\n        List<RareItem> matching = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (String key : section.getKeys(false)) {\n            ConfigurationSection itemSection = section.getConfigurationSection(key);\n            if (itemSection == null || !tier.equalsIgnoreCase(itemSection.getString(\"tier\", \"\"))) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            Material material = Material.matchMaterial(itemSection.getString(\"material\", \"COD\"));\n            if (material == null || material.isAir()) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            matching.add(new RareItem(\n                    itemSection.getString(\"tier\", tier),\n                    itemSection.getString(\"name\", key),\n                    material,\n                    itemSection.getInt(\"amount-min\", itemSection.getInt(\"amount\", 1)),\n                    itemSection.getInt(\"amount-max\", itemSection.getInt(\"amount\", 1)),\n                    itemSection.getInt(\"sell-price\", 0),\n                    itemSection.getStringList(\"lore\"),\n                    itemSection.getStringList(\"enchantments\"),\n                    itemSection.getInt(\"repair-cost\", 0),\n                    itemSection.getString(\"skull-owner\", \"\"),\n                    itemSection.getString(\"hdb-head-id\", \"\"),\n                    itemSection.getString(\"leather-color\", \"\"),\n                    itemSection.getStringList(\"commands\")\n            ));\n        }\n        if (matching.isEmpty()) {\n            return null;\n        }\n        return matching.get(random.nextInt(matching.size()));\n    }\n\n    private void sendRewardFeedback(Player player, CatchReward reward) {\n        if (reward.mystery()) {\n            String message = getConfig().getString(\"fishing.messages.mystery\", \"&dMystery Drop: &f{name}&7! &a+${coins} &7und &c{tokens} Rubine\");\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + replaceReward(message, reward)));\n            playConfiguredSound(player, \"fishing.sounds.mystery\", Sound.ENTITY_PLAYER_LEVELUP);\n            if (getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.mystery-drops.title.enabled\", true)) {\n                player.sendTitle(color(getConfig().getString(\"fishing.mystery-drops.title.title\", \"&dMystery Drop\")), color(replaceReward(getConfig().getString(\"fishing.mystery-drops.title.subtitle\", \"&f{name}\"), reward)), 5, 35, 10);\n            }\n        } else if (reward.rare()) {\n            String message = getConfig().getString(\"fishing.messages.rare\", \"&6Seltener Fang: &e{name}&7! &a+${coins} &7und &c{tokens} Rubine\");\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + replaceReward(message, reward)));\n            playConfiguredSound(player, \"fishing.sounds.rare\", Sound.ENTITY_EXPERIENCE_ORB_PICKUP);\n        } else {\n            String message = getConfig().getString(\"fishing.messages.normal\", \"&a+&e${coins} &7und &c{tokens} Rubine &7fürs Angeln.\");\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + replaceReward(message, reward)));\n        }\n        if (reward.broadcast()) {\n            String broadcast = replaceReward(getConfig().getString(\"fishing.messages.broadcast\", \"&6{player} &7hat &e{name} &7aus dem Wasser gezogen!\"), reward)\n                    .replace(\"{player}\", player.getName());\n            Bukkit.broadcastMessage(color(prefix() + broadcast));\n        }\n        if (!reward.moment().name().isBlank()) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&7Fang: \" + reward.moment().color() + reward.moment().name() + \" &8(\" + reward.moment().size() + \"cm&8)\"));\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void sendCatchActionBar(Player player, CatchReward reward, BaitBonus bait) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.messages.actionbar.enabled\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        if (!catchActionBarEnabled(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        int level = getFishingLevel(player.getUniqueId());\n        int currentXp = getCurrentLevelXp(player.getUniqueId());\n        int nextXp = xpForNextLevel(level);\n        String baitLine = bait.id().isBlank() ? getConfig().getString(\"fishing.messages.actionbar.no-bait\", \"Kein Köder\") : bait.name() + \" \" + bait.usesRemaining() + \"x\";\n        FishingSpot spot = activeFishingSpot(player);\n        String quality = reward.moment().name().isBlank() ? \"Normal\" : reward.moment().name();\n        String template = getConfig().getString(\"fishing.messages.actionbar.text\", \"&6{name} &8| &b{spot} &8| &f{quality} &7{size}cm &8| &a+${coins} &8| &c+{tokens} Rubine\");\n        String message = replace(template,\n                \"{name}\", reward.name(),\n                \"{spot}\", spot.name(),\n                \"{quality}\", quality,\n                \"{size}\", Math.max(0, reward.moment().size()),\n                \"{coins}\", reward.coins(),\n                \"{tokens}\", reward.tokens(),\n                \"{level}\", level,\n                \"{xp}\", currentXp,\n                \"{next_xp}\", nextXp,\n                \"{bait}\", baitLine);\n        player.spigot().sendMessage(ChatMessageType.ACTION_BAR, TextComponent.fromLegacyText(color(message)));\n    }\n\n    private boolean catchActionBarEnabled(Player player) {\n        return data.getBoolean(\"players.\" + player.getUniqueId() + \".settings.catch-actionbar\", true);\n    }\n\n    private void toggleCatchActionBar(Player player) {\n        boolean enabled = !catchActionBarEnabled(player);\n        data.set(\"players.\" + player.getUniqueId() + \".settings.catch-actionbar\", enabled);\n        saveData();\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + (enabled ? \"&aFang-HUD aktiviert.\" : \"&eFang-HUD deaktiviert.\")));\n        player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.UI_BUTTON_CLICK, 0.7f, enabled ? 1.25f : 0.85f);\n    }\n\n    private void storeRewardStats(Player player, CatchReward reward) {\n        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();\n        int oldLevel = getFishingLevel(uuid);\n        int levelXp = reward.experience() + getConfig().getInt(\"leveling.xp-per-catch\", 4);\n        addStat(uuid, player.getName(), \"catches\", 1);\n        addStat(uuid, player.getName(), \"coins\", reward.coins());\n        addStat(uuid, player.getName(), \"tokens\", reward.tokens());\n        addStat(uuid, player.getName(), \"xp\", reward.experience());\n        addStat(uuid, player.getName(), \"level-xp\", levelXp);\n        addStat(uuid, player.getName(), \"category.\" + normalizeKey(reward.category()), 1);\n        addStat(uuid, player.getName(), \"realm.\" + fishingRealm(player), 1);\n        addStat(uuid, player.getName(), \"spot.\" + activeFishingSpot(player).id(), 1);\n        if (!reward.moment().name().isBlank()) {\n            addStat(uuid, player.getName(), \"quality.\" + normalizeKey(reward.moment().name()), 1);\n        }\n        if (reward.moment().size() > data.getInt(\"players.\" + uuid + \".best-size\")) {\n            data.set(\"players.\" + uuid + \".best-size\", reward.moment().size());\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&bNeuer Größenrekord: &f\" + reward.moment().size() + \"cm&7!\"));\n        }\n        if (reward.rare()) {\n            addStat(uuid, player.getName(), \"rare\", 1);\n        }\n        if (reward.mystery()) {\n            addStat(uuid, player.getName(), \"mystery\", 1);\n            data.set(\"players.\" + uuid + \".last-mystery\", reward.name());\n        }\n        updateMysteryPity(player, reward.mystery());\n        recordCollectionCatch(player, reward);\n        data.set(\"players.\" + uuid + \".last-catch\", reward.name());\n        int newLevel = getFishingLevel(uuid);\n        if (newLevel > oldLevel) {\n            player.sendTitle(color(\"&6Angel-Level \" + newLevel), color(\"&7Du erhältst bessere Fang-Boni.\"), 5, 40, 10);\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&aLevel Up! &7Dein Angel-Level ist jetzt &e\" + newLevel + \"&7.\"));\n            grantFishingPassRewards(player, oldLevel + 1, newLevel);\n        }\n        saveData();\n    }\n\n    private void addFishingXp(Player player, int xp) {\n        if (xp <= 0) {\n            return;\n        }\n        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();\n        int oldLevel = getFishingLevel(uuid);\n        addStat(uuid, player.getName(), \"xp\", xp);\n        addStat(uuid, player.getName(), \"level-xp\", xp);\n        int newLevel = getFishingLevel(uuid);\n        if (newLevel > oldLevel) {\n            player.sendTitle(color(\"&6Angel-Level \" + newLevel), color(\"&7Du erhältst bessere Fang-Boni.\"), 5, 40, 10);\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&aLevel Up! &7Dein Angel-Level ist jetzt &e\" + newLevel + \"&7.\"));\n            grantFishingPassRewards(player, oldLevel + 1, newLevel);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void updateMysteryPity(Player player, boolean mysteryHit) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.pity.enabled\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        String path = \"players.\" + player.getUniqueId() + \".mystery-dry-streak\";\n        if (mysteryHit) {\n            int previous = data.getInt(path, 0);\n            data.set(path, 0);\n            if (previous >= getConfig().getInt(\"fishing.pity.message-after\", 25)) {\n                player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&dPity-System: &7Deine Pechsträhne wurde durch einen Mystery-Fang beendet.\"));\n            }\n            return;\n        }\n        int current = data.getInt(path, 0) + 1;\n        data.set(path, current);\n        int messageEvery = getConfig().getInt(\"fishing.pity.message-every\", 15);\n        if (messageEvery > 0 && current >= getConfig().getInt(\"fishing.pity.message-after\", 25) && current % messageEvery == 0) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&dPity-System: &7Deine Mystery-Chance steigt. Aktuelle Pechsträhne: &f\" + current + \"&7.\"));\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void recordCollectionCatch(Player player, CatchReward reward) {\n        String key = normalizeKey(reward.name());\n        if (key.isBlank()) {\n            return;\n        }\n        String path = \"players.\" + player.getUniqueId() + \".collection.\" + key;\n        if (!data.contains(path + \".first-catch\")) {\n            data.set(path + \".first-catch\", System.currentTimeMillis());\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&bCollection: &f\" + reward.name() + \" &7neu entdeckt!\"));\n        }\n        data.set(path + \".name\", reward.name());\n        data.set(path + \".category\", reward.category());\n        data.set(path + \".rarity\", reward.mystery() ? \"mystery\" : reward.rare() ? \"rare\" : \"normal\");\n        data.set(path + \".market-value\", reward.replacement() == null ? 0 : marketValue(reward.replacement(), false));\n        data.set(path + \".count\", data.getInt(path + \".count\", 0) + 1);\n        if (reward.moment().size() > data.getInt(path + \".record-size\", 0)) {\n            data.set(path + \".record-size\", reward.moment().size());\n        }\n        checkCollectionMilestones(player);\n    }\n\n    private void checkCollectionMilestones(Player player) {\n        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();\n        int discovered = discoveredCollectionCount(uuid);\n        int target = collectionTarget();\n        if (target <= 0) {\n            return;\n        }\n        ConfigurationSection milestones = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.collection.milestones\");\n        if (milestones == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        for (String key : milestones.getKeys(false)) {\n            ConfigurationSection milestone = milestones.getConfigurationSection(key);\n            if (milestone == null || data.getBoolean(\"players.\" + uuid + \".collection-milestones.\" + key, false)) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            int percent = milestone.getInt(\"percent\", parseInt(key.replace(\"%\", \"\"), 0));\n            if (discovered * 100 < target * percent) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            data.set(\"players.\" + uuid + \".collection-milestones.\" + key, true);\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&bCollection-Meilenstein erreicht: &f\" + percent + \"%\"));\n            for (String command : milestone.getStringList(\"commands\")) {\n                Bukkit.dispatchCommand(Bukkit.getConsoleSender(), replaceRandomRanges(command)\n                        .replace(\"{player}\", player.getName())\n                        .replace(\"{uuid}\", uuid.toString())\n                        .replace(\"{percent}\", String.valueOf(percent)));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void grantFishingPassRewards(Player player, int fromLevel, int toLevel) {\n        int unlocked = 0;\n        for (int level = fromLevel; level <= toLevel; level++) {\n            List<String> commands = getConfig().getStringList(\"fishing.pass.rewards.\" + level + \".commands\");\n            if (commands.isEmpty()) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            unlocked++;\n        }\n        if (unlocked > 0) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&6Angelpass: &e\" + unlocked + \" &7neue Belohnung(en) freigeschaltet. &a/angeln pass\"));\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void checkAchievements(Player player) {\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.achievements\");\n        if (section == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();\n        for (String key : section.getKeys(false)) {\n            ConfigurationSection achievement = section.getConfigurationSection(key);\n            if (achievement == null || !achievement.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true) || isRetiredAchievement(key, achievement)) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            String path = \"players.\" + uuid + \".achievements.\" + key;\n            if (data.getBoolean(path, false)) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            String stat = achievement.getString(\"stat\", \"catches\");\n            int needed = achievement.getInt(\"amount\", 1);\n            if (achievementProgress(uuid, stat) < needed) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            data.set(path, true);\n            data.set(\"players.\" + uuid + \".achievement-times.\" + key, System.currentTimeMillis());\n            String name = achievement.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(key));\n            player.sendTitle(color(\"&eErfolg freigeschaltet\"), color(name), 5, 45, 15);\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&eErfolg freigeschaltet: &f\" + name));\n            for (String command : achievement.getStringList(\"commands\")) {\n                Bukkit.dispatchCommand(Bukkit.getConsoleSender(), replaceRandomRanges(command)\n                        .replace(\"{player}\", player.getName())\n                        .replace(\"{uuid}\", uuid.toString())\n                        .replace(\"{achievement}\", key));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void checkQuests(Player player) {\n        ensureActiveQuests(player);\n        boolean changed = false;\n        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();\n        for (String type : List.of(\"daily\", \"weekly\")) {\n            ConfigurationSection active = data.getConfigurationSection(\"players.\" + uuid + \".quests.\" + type + \".active\");\n            if (active == null) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            for (String slot : active.getKeys(false)) {\n                String questId = active.getString(slot + \".id\", \"\");\n                if (questId.isBlank() || active.getBoolean(slot + \".claimed\", false)) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                ConfigurationSection quest = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.quests.\" + type + \".\" + questId);\n                if (quest == null || !quest.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true) || isRetiredQuest(questId, quest)) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                int progress = questProgress(uuid, type, slot, quest);\n                int amount = Math.max(1, quest.getInt(\"amount\", 1));\n                if (progress < amount) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                active.set(slot + \".claimed\", true);\n                payQuestReward(player, type, questId, quest);\n                changed = true;\n            }\n        }\n        if (changed) {\n            saveData();\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void ensureActiveQuests(Player player) {\n        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();\n        ensureQuestSet(uuid, \"daily\", questPeriod(\"daily\"), getConfig().getInt(\"fishing.quests.daily-active\", 3));\n        ensureQuestSet(uuid, \"weekly\", questPeriod(\"weekly\"), getConfig().getInt(\"fishing.quests.weekly-active\", 2));\n    }\n\n    private void ensureQuestSet(UUID uuid, String type, String period, int amount) {\n        String path = \"players.\" + uuid + \".quests.\" + type;\n        if (period.equals(data.getString(path + \".period\", \"\"))) {\n            return;\n        }\n        List<String> ids = configuredQuestIds(type);\n        data.set(path + \".period\", period);\n        data.set(path + \".active\", null);\n        if (ids.isEmpty()) {\n            return;\n        }\n        List<String> shuffled = new ArrayList<>(ids);\n        shuffled.sort(Comparator.comparingInt(id -> Objects.hash(uuid, type, period, id)));\n        int limit = Math.min(Math.max(1, amount), shuffled.size());\n        for (int i = 0; i < limit; i++) {\n            String id = shuffled.get(i);\n            ConfigurationSection quest = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.quests.\" + type + \".\" + id);\n            if (quest == null) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            String slot = \"q\" + i;\n            String stat = quest.getString(\"stat\", \"catches\");\n            data.set(path + \".active.\" + slot + \".id\", id);\n            data.set(path + \".active.\" + slot + \".base\", achievementProgress(uuid, stat));\n            data.set(path + \".active.\" + slot + \".claimed\", false);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private List<String> configuredQuestIds(String type) {\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.quests.\" + type);\n        if (section == null) {\n            return List.of();\n        }\n        return section.getKeys(false).stream()\n                .filter(key -> {\n                    ConfigurationSection quest = section.getConfigurationSection(key);\n                    return quest != null && quest.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true) && !isRetiredQuest(key, quest);\n                })\n                .sorted(String.CASE_INSENSITIVE_ORDER)\n                .toList();\n    }\n\n    private boolean isRetiredQuest(String key, ConfigurationSection quest) {\n        String searchable = (key + \" \"\n                + quest.getString(\"name\", \"\") + \" \"\n                + quest.getString(\"description\", \"\") + \" \"\n                + quest.getString(\"stat\", \"\") + \" \"\n                + String.join(\" \", quest.getStringList(\"rewards.lootboxes\"))).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n        if ((searchable.contains(\"lava\") || searchable.contains(\"nether\")) && !isSpecialRealmEnabled(\"lava\")) {\n            return true;\n        }\n        if ((searchable.contains(\"void\") || searchable.contains(\"end\")) && !isSpecialRealmEnabled(\"void\")) {\n            return true;\n        }\n        return
