searchable.contains(\"boss\") && !getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.bosses.enabled\", false);\n    }\n\n    private boolean isRetiredAchievement(String key, ConfigurationSection achievement) {\n        String searchable = (key + \" \"\n                + achievement.getString(\"name\", \"\") + \" \"\n                + achievement.getString(\"description\", \"\") + \" \"\n                + achievement.getString(\"stat\", \"\") + \" \"\n                + String.join(\" \", achievement.getStringList(\"commands\"))).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n        if ((searchable.contains(\"lava\") || searchable.contains(\"nether\")) && !isSpecialRealmEnabled(\"lava\")) {\n            return true;\n        }\n        if ((searchable.contains(\"void\") || searchable.contains(\"end\")) && !isSpecialRealmEnabled(\"void\")) {\n            return true;\n        }\n        return searchable.contains(\"boss\") && !getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.bosses.enabled\", false);\n    }\n\n    private List<QuestView> activeQuestViews(Player player) {\n        List<QuestView> views = new ArrayList<>();\n        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();\n        for (String type : List.of(\"daily\", \"weekly\")) {\n            ConfigurationSection active = data.getConfigurationSection(\"players.\" + uuid + \".quests.\" + type + \".active\");\n            if (active == null) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            for (String slot : active.getKeys(false)) {\n                String id = active.getString(slot + \".id\", \"\");\n                ConfigurationSection quest = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.quests.\" + type + \".\" + id);\n                if (quest == null || !quest.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true) || isRetiredQuest(id, quest)) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                views.add(new QuestView(\n                        type,\n                        id,\n                        quest.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(id)),\n                        quest.getString(\"description\", \"\"),\n                        questProgress(uuid, type, slot, quest),\n                        Math.max(1, quest.getInt(\"amount\", 1)),\n                        active.getBoolean(slot + \".claimed\", false)\n                ));\n            }\n        }\n        return views;\n    }\n\n    private int questProgress(UUID uuid, String type, String slot, ConfigurationSection quest) {\n        String stat = quest.getString(\"stat\", \"catches\");\n        int current = achievementProgress(uuid, stat);\n        int base = data.getInt(\"players.\" + uuid + \".quests.\" + type + \".active.\" + slot + \".base\", 0);\n        return Math.max(0, current - base);\n    }\n\n    private void payQuestReward(Player player, String type, String questId, ConfigurationSection quest) {\n        int coins = quest.getInt(\"rewards.coins\", 0);\n        int tokens = quest.getInt(\"rewards.tokens\", 0);\n        int xp = quest.getInt(\"rewards.xp\", 0);\n        if (coins > 0) {\n            dispatchRewardCommand(\"coins\", player, coins);\n            addStat(player.getUniqueId(), player.getName(), \"coins\", coins);\n        }\n        if (tokens > 0) {\n            dispatchRewardCommand(\"tokens\", player, tokens);\n            addStat(player.getUniqueId(), player.getName(), \"tokens\", tokens);\n        }\n        if (xp > 0) {\n            addFishingXp(player, xp);\n        }\n        for (String lootboxId : quest.getStringList(\"rewards.lootboxes\")) {\n            ConfigurationSection lootbox = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.lootboxes.\" + lootboxId);\n            if (lootbox != null && lootbox.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true)) {\n                giveItem(player, createLootboxItem(lootboxId, lootbox));\n            }\n        }\n        for (String command : quest.getStringList(\"rewards.commands\")) {\n            Bukkit.dispatchCommand(Bukkit.getConsoleSender(), replaceRandomRanges(command)\n                    .replace(\"{player}\", player.getName())\n                    .replace(\"{uuid}\", player.getUniqueId().toString())\n                    .replace(\"{quest}\", questId)\n                    .replace(\"{type}\", type));\n        }\n        addStat(player.getUniqueId(), player.getName(), \"quests.completed\", 1);\n        addStat(player.getUniqueId(), player.getName(), \"quests.\" + type, 1);\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&aQuest abgeschlossen: &f\" + quest.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(questId))));\n        player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.UI_TOAST_CHALLENGE_COMPLETE, 0.8f, 1.25f);\n    }\n\n    private String questPeriod(String type) {\n        LocalDate now = LocalDate.now();\n        if (\"weekly\".equalsIgnoreCase(type)) {\n            return now.get(IsoFields.WEEK_BASED_YEAR) + \"-W\" + now.get(IsoFields.WEEK_OF_WEEK_BASED_YEAR);\n        }\n        return now.toString();\n    }\n\n    private int achievementProgress(UUID uuid, String stat) {\n        if (\"level\".equalsIgnoreCase(stat)) {\n            return getFishingLevel(uuid);\n        }\n        if (\"collection\".equalsIgnoreCase(stat)) {\n            ConfigurationSection collection = data.getConfigurationSection(\"players.\" + uuid + \".collection\");\n            return collection == null ? 0 : collection.getKeys(false).size();\n        }\n        return data.getInt(\"players.\" + uuid + \".\" + stat, 0);\n    }\n\n    private int configuredAchievementCount() {\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.achievements\");\n        if (section == null) {\n            return 0;\n        }\n        int count = 0;\n        for (String key : section.getKeys(false)) {\n            ConfigurationSection achievement = section.getConfigurationSection(key);\n            if (achievement != null && achievement.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true) && !isRetiredAchievement(key, achievement)) {\n                count++;\n            }\n        }\n        return count;\n    }\n\n    private int completedAchievements(UUID uuid) {\n        ConfigurationSection completed = data.getConfigurationSection(\"players.\" + uuid + \".achievements\");\n        ConfigurationSection configured = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.achievements\");\n        if (completed == null || configured == null) {\n            return 0;\n        }\n        int count = 0;\n        for (String key : completed.getKeys(false)) {\n            ConfigurationSection achievement = configured.getConfigurationSection(key);\n            if (achievement != null && achievement.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true) && !isRetiredAchievement(key, achievement)) {\n                count++;\n            }\n        }\n        return count;\n    }\n\n    private int discoveredCollectionCount(UUID uuid) {\n        ConfigurationSection collection = data.getConfigurationSection(\"players.\" + uuid + \".collection\");\n        return collection == null ? 0 : collection.getKeys(false).size();\n    }\n\n    private int collectionTarget() {\n        int configured = getConfig().getInt(\"fishing.collection.target\", 0);\n        int catalogSize = collectionCatalog().size();\n        return configured > 0 ? Math.max(configured, catalogSize) : catalogSize;\n    }\n\n    private List<CollectionEntry> collectionCatalog() {\n        Map<String, CollectionEntry> entries = new HashMap<>();\n        collectCollectionEntries(entries, \"fishing.rewards.rare-items\", \"rare\");\n        collectCollectionEntries(entries, \"fishing.mystery-drops.drops\", \"mystery\");\n        collectCollectionEntries(entries, \"fishing.realm-drops.overworld\", \"overworld\");\n        ConfigurationSection lootboxes = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.lootboxes\");\n        if (lootboxes != null) {\n            for (String key : lootboxes.getKeys(false)) {\n                ConfigurationSection box = lootboxes.getConfigurationSection(key);\n                if (box != null && box.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true) && !isRetiredFishingLootbox(key, box)) {\n                    String name = ChatColor.stripColor(color(box.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(key))));\n                    addCollectionEntry(entries, new CollectionEntry(normalizeKey(name), name, \"lootbox\", \"special\", Material.ENDER_CHEST, box.getInt(\"sell-price\", 0)));\n                    collectCollectionEntries(entries, \"fishing.lootboxes.\" + key + \".rewards\", \"lootbox\");\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        List<CollectionEntry> list = new ArrayList<>(entries.values());\n        list.sort(Comparator.comparing(CollectionEntry::category).thenComparing(CollectionEntry::name, String.CASE_INSENSITIVE_ORDER));\n        return list;\n    }\n\n    private void collectCollectionEntries(Map<String, CollectionEntry> entries, String path, String rarity) {\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(path);\n        if (section == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        for (String key : section.getKeys(false)) {\n            ConfigurationSection item = section.getConfigurationSection(key);\n            if (item == null || !item.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true)) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            String rawName = item.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(key));\n            String name = ChatColor.stripColor(color(rawName));\n            Material material = Material.matchMaterial(item.getString(\"material\", item.getString(\"icon\", \"COD\")));\n            if (material == null || material.isAir()) {\n                material = Material.COD;\n            }\n            String category = item.getString(\"category\", rarity);\n            int marketValue = item.getInt(\"sell-price\", 0);\n            addCollectionEntry(entries, new CollectionEntry(normalizeKey(name), name, category, rarity, material, marketValue));\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void addCollectionEntry(Map<String, CollectionEntry> entries, CollectionEntry entry) {\n        if (!entry.id().isBlank()) {\n            entries.putIfAbsent(entry.id(), entry);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private String formatDate(long millis) {\n        if (millis <= 0L) {\n            return \"-\";\n        }\n        return new java.text.SimpleDateFormat(\"dd.MM.yyyy\").format(new java.util.Date(millis));\n    }\n\n    private void replaceCaughtItem(Entity caught, ItemStack replacement) {\n        if (caught instanceof Item item) {\n            item.setItemStack(replacement);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (caught != null) {\n            caught.getWorld().dropItemNaturally(caught.getLocation(), replacement);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void dispatchRewardCommand(String type, Player player, int amount) {\n        if (amount <= 0) {\n            return;\n        }\n        String command = getConfig().getString(\"fishing.rewards.commands.\" + type, \"\");\n        if (command == null || command.isBlank()) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Bukkit.dispatchCommand(Bukkit.getConsoleSender(), command\n                .replace(\"{player}\", player.getName())\n                .replace(\"{uuid}\", player.getUniqueId().toString())\n                .replace(\"{amount}\", String.valueOf(amount)));\n    }\n\n    private void dispatchPreparedCommand(Player player, String command, CatchReward reward) {\n        if (command == null || command.isBlank()) {\n            return;\n        }\n        String prepared = replaceRandomRanges(replaceReward(command, reward))\n                .replace(\"{player}\", player.getName())\n                .replace(\"{uuid}\", player.getUniqueId().toString());\n        Bukkit.dispatchCommand(Bukkit.getConsoleSender(), prepared);\n    }\n\n    private String createHeadDatabaseCommand(Player player, String headId, int amount) {\n        String command = getConfig().getString(\"fishing.head-database.give-command\", \"hdb give {player} {id} {amount}\");\n        return replace(command, \"{player}\", player.getName(), \"{uuid}\", player.getUniqueId(), \"{id}\", headId, \"{amount}\", Math.max(1, amount));\n    }\n\n    private boolean isBlockedByAntiAfk(Player player) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.anti-afk.enabled\", true)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        double radius = getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.anti-afk.same-location-radius\", 1.25);\n        int max = getConfig().getInt(\"fishing.anti-afk.max-same-location-catches\", 12);\n        AntiAfkState state = antiAfk.computeIfAbsent(player.getUniqueId(), ignored -> new AntiAfkState(player.getLocation(), 0));\n        if (sameWorldAndNear(state.location(), player.getLocation(), radius)) {\n            state.increment();\n        } else {\n            state.reset(player.getLocation());\n        }\n        return state.catches() > max;\n    }\n\n    private boolean isBlockedByRealisticFishing(Player player) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.realistic.enabled\", true)) {\n            stopFishingReactionHud(player.getUniqueId(), false);\n            return false;\n        }\n        Long biteTime = biteTimes.remove(player.getUniqueId());\n        if (biteTime == null) {\n            stopFishingReactionHud(player.getUniqueId(), false);\n            return false;\n        }\n        long reaction = System.currentTimeMillis() - biteTime;\n        long minimum = getConfig().getLong(\"fishing.realistic.min-reaction-ms\", 250L);\n        long maximum = getConfig().getLong(\"fishing.realistic.max-reaction-ms\", 8000L);\n        if (reaction < minimum) {\n            addStat(player.getUniqueId(), player.getName(), \"anti-auto.too-fast\", 1);\n            finishFishingReactionHud(player, reaction, \"too-fast\", true);\n            return true;\n        }\n        if (reaction > maximum) {\n            addStat(player.getUniqueId(), player.getName(), \"anti-auto.too-late\", 1);\n            boolean blocked = getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.realistic.flag-too-late\", false);\n            finishFishingReactionHud(player, reaction, \"too-late\", blocked);\n            return blocked;\n        }\n        FishingReactionHud hud = fishingReactionHuds.get(player.getUniqueId());\n        String quality = hud != null && reaction >= hud.perfectStartMs() && reaction <= hud.perfectEndMs() ? \"perfect\" : \"good\";\n        addStat(player.getUniqueId(), player.getName(), \"realistic.\" + quality, 1);\n        finishFishingReactionHud(player, reaction, quality, false);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    private void startFishingReactionHud(Player player, long biteAt) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.realistic.enabled\", true)\n                || !getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.realistic.display.enabled\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();\n        stopFishingReactionHud(uuid, false);\n        long minMs = Math.max(0L, getConfig().getLong(\"fishing.realistic.min-reaction-ms\", 250L));\n        long maxMs = Math.max(minMs + 500L, getConfig().getLong(\"fishing.realistic.max-reaction-ms\", 8000L));\n        long perfectStart = clampLong(getConfig().getLong(\"fishing.realistic.display.perfect-start-ms\", 650L), minMs, maxMs);\n        long perfectEnd = clampLong(getConfig().getLong(\"fishing.realistic.display.perfect-end-ms\", 2200L), perfectStart, maxMs);\n        BossBar bar = null;\n        if (getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.realistic.display.bossbar\", true)) {\n            bar = Bukkit.createBossBar(color(getConfig().getString(\"fishing.realistic.display.waiting-title\", \"&eBiss! Beobachte die Anzeige...\")), BarColor.YELLOW, BarStyle.SEGMENTED_10);\n            bar.setProgress(1.0D);\n            bar.addPlayer(player);\n        }\n        BukkitTask task = Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimer(this, () -> updateFishingReactionHud(uuid), 0L, 2L);\n        fishingReactionHuds.put(uuid, new FishingReactionHud(bar, task, biteAt, minMs, perfectStart, perfectEnd, maxMs));\n        playConfiguredSound(player, \"fishing.realistic.display.bite-sound\", Sound.ENTITY_FISHING_BOBBER_SPLASH);\n    }\n\n    private void updateFishingReactionHud(UUID uuid) {\n        FishingReactionHud hud = fishingReactionHuds.get(uuid);\n        Player player = Bukkit.getPlayer(uuid);\n        if (hud == null || player == null || !player.isOnline()) {\n            stopFishingReactionHud(uuid, false);\n            return;\n        }\n        long elapsed = System.currentTimeMillis() - hud.biteAt();\n        if (elapsed > hud.maxMs() + 1200L) {\n            finishFishingReactionHud(player, elapsed, \"too-late\", false);\n            return;\n        }\n        String phase = reactionPhase(elapsed, hud);\n        if (hud.bar() != null) {\n            hud.bar().setTitle(color(reactionTitle(phase)));\n            hud.bar().setColor(reactionBarColor(phase));\n            hud.bar().setProgress(clampDouble(1.0D - ((double) elapsed / Math.max(1.0D, hud.maxMs())), 0.0D, 1.0D));\n        }\n        if (getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.realistic.display.actionbar\", true)) {\n            String message = getConfig().getString(\"fishing.realistic.display.actionbar-format\", \"&6Angeln &8| {meter} &8| {hint}\");\n            message = replace(message,\n                    \"{meter}\", reactionMeter(elapsed, hud),\n                    \"{hint}\", reactionHint(phase),\n                    \"{ms}\", Math.max(0L, elapsed));\n            player.spigot().sendMessage(ChatMessageType.ACTION_BAR, TextComponent.fromLegacyText(color(message)));\n        }\n    }\n\n    private String reactionPhase(long elapsed, FishingReactionHud hud) {\n        if (elapsed < hud.minMs()) {\n            return \"too-fast\";\n        }\n        if (elapsed >= hud.perfectStartMs() && elapsed <= hud.perfectEndMs()) {\n            return \"perfect\";\n        }\n        if (elapsed > hud.maxMs()) {\n            return \"too-late\";\n        }\n        return \"good\";\n    }\n\n    private String reactionTitle(String phase) {\n        return switch (phase) {\n            case \"too-fast\" -> getConfig().getString(\"fishing.realistic.display.early-title\", \"&cNoch nicht ziehen\");\n            case \"perfect\" -> getConfig().getString(\"fishing.realistic.display.perfect-title\", \"&aJetzt einholen!\");\n            case \"too-late\" -> getConfig().getString(\"fishing.realistic.display.late-title\", \"&cZu spät\");\n            default -> getConfig().getString(\"fishing.realistic.display.good-title\", \"&6Bereit - einholen\");\n        };\n    }\n\n    private String reactionHint(String phase) {\n        return switch (phase) {\n            case \"too-fast\" -> getConfig().getString(\"fishing.realistic.display.early-hint\", \"&cWarte kurz\");\n            case \"perfect\" -> getConfig().getString(\"fishing.realistic.display.perfect-hint\", \"&aJetzt Rechtsklick!\");\n            case \"too-late\" -> getConfig().getString(\"fishing.realistic.display.late-hint\", \"&cZu spät\");\n            default -> getConfig().getString(\"fishing.realistic.display.good-hint\", \"&eEinholen möglich\");\n        };\n    }\n\n    private BarColor reactionBarColor(String phase) {\n        return switch (phase) {\n            case \"too-fast\" -> BarColor.RED;\n            case \"perfect\" -> BarColor.GREEN;\n            case \"too-late\" -> BarColor.RED;\n            default -> BarColor.YELLOW;\n        };\n    }\n\n    private String reactionMeter(long elapsed, FishingReactionHud hud) {\n        int segments = Math.max(8, getConfig().getInt(\"fishing.realistic.display.meter-segments\", 18));\n        int marker = (int) clampLong(Math.round(((double) elapsed / Math.max(1.0D, hud.maxMs())) * (segments - 1)), 0L, segments - 1L);\n        int perfectStart = (int) clampLong(Math.round(((double) hud.perfectStartMs() / Math.max(1.0D, hud.maxMs())) * (segments - 1)), 0L, segments - 1L);\n        int perfectEnd = (int) clampLong(Math.round(((double) hud.perfectEndMs() / Math.max(1.0D, hud.maxMs())) * (segments - 1)), perfectStart, segments - 1L);\n        StringBuilder meter = new StringBuilder(\"&8[\");\n        for (int i = 0; i < segments; i++) {\n            if (i == marker) {\n                meter.append(\"&f|\");\n            } else if (i >= perfectStart && i <= perfectEnd) {\n                meter.append(\"&a=\");\n            } else {\n                meter.append(\"&7-\");\n            }\n        }\n        meter.append(\"&8]\");\n        return meter.toString();\n    }\n\n    private void finishFishingReactionHud(Player player, long reaction, String quality, boolean blocked) {\n        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();\n        FishingReactionHud hud = fishingReactionHuds.remove(uuid);\n        if (hud == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        hud.cancel();\n        String result = switch (quality) {\n            case \"too-fast\" -> getConfig().getString(\"fishing.realistic.display.result-too-fast\", \"&cZu früh &8| &7{ms}ms\");\n            case \"too-late\" -> getConfig().getString(\"fishing.realistic.display.result-too-late\", \"&cZu spät &8| &7{ms}ms\");\n            case \"perfect\" -> getConfig().getString(\"fishing.realistic.display.result-perfect\", \"&aPerfekter Anhieb &8| &7{ms}ms\");\n            default -> getConfig().getString(\"fishing.realistic.display.result-good\", \"&eGuter Anhieb &8| &7{ms}ms\");\n        };\n        result = result.replace(\"{ms}\", String.valueOf(Math.max(0L, reaction)));\n        recordPrecisionReaction(player, quality, reaction, blocked);\n        if (hud.bar() != null) {\n            hud.bar().setTitle(color(result));\n            hud.bar().setColor(blocked ? BarColor.RED : (\"perfect\".equals(quality) ? BarColor.GREEN : BarColor.YELLOW));\n            hud.bar().setProgress(\"perfect\".equals(quality) ? 1.0D : 0.45D);\n        }\n        player.spigot().sendMessage(ChatMessageType.ACTION_BAR, TextComponent.fromLegacyText(color(result)));\n        long keepTicks = Math.max(5L, getConfig().getLong(\"fishing.realistic.display.keep-result-ticks\", 30L));\n        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskLater(this, () -> {\n            if (hud.bar() != null) {\n                hud.bar().removeAll();\n            }\n        }, keepTicks);\n    }\n\n    private void stopFishingReactionHud(UUID uuid, boolean keepBarResult) {\n        FishingReactionHud hud = fishingReactionHuds.remove(uuid);\n        if (hud == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        hud.cancel();\n        if (!keepBarResult && hud.bar() != null) {\n            hud.bar().removeAll();\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void clearFishingReactionHuds() {\n        for (FishingReactionHud hud : fishingReactionHuds.values()) {\n            hud.cancel();\n            if (hud.bar() != null) {\n                hud.bar().removeAll();\n            }\n        }\n        fishingReactionHuds.clear();\n    }\n\n    private boolean shouldStartReelingGame(Player player, String realm) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.reeling.enabled\", true)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        if (\"lava\".equalsIgnoreCase(realm) || \"void\".equalsIgnoreCase(realm)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        String mode = fishingMode(player);\n        if (\"classic\".equals(mode)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        double chance = \"easy\".equals(mode)\n                ? getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.reeling.easy-chance\", 30.0)\n                : getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.reeling.chance\", 45.0);\n        return random.nextDouble(100.0) < chance;\n    }\n\n    private void startReelingGame(Player player, String realm, int vanillaExperience) {\n        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();\n        stopReelingGame(uuid, false);\n        FishingSpot spot = activeFishingSpot(player);\n        int level = getFishingLevel(uuid);\n        String rodType = activeRodType(player);\n        double spotDifficulty = clampDouble(1.0D + (spot.minLevel() / 80.0D), 1.0D, 1.45D);\n        double levelEase = clampDouble(1.0D - (level * 0.004D), 0.78D, 1.0D);\n        double rodEase = reelingRodEase(rodType);\n        double difficulty = clampDouble(spotDifficulty * levelEase * rodEase, 0.72D, 1.55D);\n        double distance = getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.reeling.start-distance\", 100.0) * difficulty;\n        double tension = getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.reeling.start-tension\", 28.0) * clampDouble(difficulty, 0.85D, 1.35D);\n        long timeoutAt = System.currentTimeMillis() + getConfig().getLong(\"fishing.reeling.timeout-ms\", 15000L);\n        BossBar bar = null;\n        if (getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.reeling.bossbar\", true)) {\n            bar = Bukkit.createBossBar(color(getConfig().getString(\"fishing.reeling.title\", \"&bFisch am Haken! &7Rechtsklick zum Ziehen\")), BarColor.BLUE, BarStyle.SEGMENTED_10);\n            bar.setProgress(0.0D);\n            bar.addPlayer(player);\n        }\n        ReelingGame game = new ReelingGame(player.getUniqueId(), realm, vanillaExperience, distance, tension, timeoutAt, bar,\n                difficulty,\n                getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.reeling.pull-strength\", 7.5) / clampDouble(difficulty, 0.85D, 1.35D),\n                getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.reeling.pull-tension\", 9.0) * clampDouble(difficulty, 0.85D, 1.25D));\n        BukkitTask task = Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimer(this, () -> updateReelingGame(uuid), 0L, 2L);\n        game.task = task;\n        reelingGames.put(uuid, game);\n        player.sendTitle(color(getConfig().getString(\"fishing.reeling.start-title\", \"&b&lFisch am Haken\")), color(replace(getConfig().getString(\"fishing.reeling.start-subtitle\", \"&7Rechtsklick: ziehen &8| &7Pausen: Spannung senken\"), \"{spot}\", spot.name(), \"{rod}\", rodType)), 5, 35, 8);\n        playConfiguredSound(player, \"fishing.reeling.sounds.start\", Sound.ENTITY_FISHING_BOBBER_SPLASH);\n    }\n\n    private boolean handleReelingGamePull(Player player, ItemStack item) {\n        ReelingGame game = reelingGames.get(player.getUniqueId());\n        if (game == null) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        if (item.getType() != Material.FISHING_ROD) {\n            player.spigot().sendMessage(ChatMessageType.ACTION_BAR, TextComponent.fromLegacyText(color(\"&cHalte deine Angel in der Hand.\")));\n            return true;\n        }\n        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        long cooldown = getConfig().getLong(\"fishing.reeling.pull-cooldown-ms\", 260L);\n        if (now - game.lastPullAt < cooldown) {\n            game.tension = Math.min(100.0D, game.tension + getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.reeling.spam-tension\", 8.0));\n            return true;\n        }\n        game.lastPullAt = now;\n        game.distance -= game.pullStrength;\n        game.tension += game.pullTension;\n        game.combo = Math.min(9, game.combo + 1);\n        player.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.SPLASH, player.getLocation().add(0.0, 1.0, 0.0), 8, 0.35, 0.25, 0.35, 0.02);\n        playConfiguredSound(player, \"fishing.reeling.sounds.pull\", Sound.ENTITY_FISHING_BOBBER_RETRIEVE);\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    private void updateReelingGame(UUID uuid) {\n        ReelingGame game = reelingGames.get(uuid);\n        Player player = Bukkit.getPlayer(uuid);\n        if (game == null || player == null || !player.isOnline()) {\n            stopReelingGame(uuid, false);\n            return;\n        }\n        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        double fight = (getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.reeling.fish-fight\", 0.95) + random.nextDouble(getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.reeling.fish-burst\", 0.85))) * game.difficulty;\n        boolean recentlyPulled = now - game.lastPullAt < getConfig().getLong(\"fishing.reeling.pull-memory-ms\", 700L);\n        if (!recentlyPulled) {\n            game.tension -= getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.reeling.relax-speed\", 3.2);\n            game.combo = 0;\n        } else {\n            fight *= getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.reeling.fight-while-pulling\", 0.45);\n        }\n        game.distance += fight;\n        game.distance = clampDouble(game.distance, 0.0D, getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.reeling.escape-distance\", 135.0));\n        game.tension = clampDouble(game.tension, 0.0D, 120.0D);\n\n        if (game.distance <= 0.0D) {\n            finishReelingGame(player, game, true, getConfig().getString(\"fishing.reeling.success-message\", \"&aFang eingeholt!\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        if (game.tension >= getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.reeling.break-tension\", 100.0)) {\n            finishReelingGame(player, game, false, getConfig().getString(\"fishing.reeling.break-message\", \"&cDie Schnur ist gerissen.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        if (game.distance >= getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.reeling.escape-distance\", 135.0) || now > game.timeoutAt) {\n            finishReelingGame(player, game, false, getConfig().getString(\"fishing.reeling.escape-message\", \"&cDer Fisch ist entkommen.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n\n        double progress = 1.0D - (game.distance / Math.max(1.0D, getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.reeling.start-distance\", 100.0)));\n        if (game.bar != null) {\n            game.bar.setProgress(clampDouble(progress, 0.0D, 1.0D));\n            game.bar.setColor(reelingBarColor(game.tension));\n            game.bar.setTitle(color(replace(getConfig().getString(\"fishing.reeling.bossbar-title\", \"&bEinholen &8| &f{distance}m &8| &eSpannung {tension}%\"),\n                    \"{distance}\", Math.max(0, (int) Math.round(game.distance)),\n                    \"{tension}\", Math.max(0, (int) Math.round(game.tension)))));\n        }\n        String action = replace(getConfig().getString(\"fishing.reeling.actionbar\", \"&bFisch &8{distance} &7{distancebar} &8| &eSpannung &7{tensionbar} &8| &fRechtsklick\"),\n                \"{distance}\", Math.max(0, (int) Math.round(game.distance)) + \"m\",\n                \"{tension}\", Math.max(0, (int) Math.round(game.tension)) + \"%\",\n                \"{distancebar}\", reelingMeter(1.0D - (game.distance / Math.max(1.0D, getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.reeling.escape-distance\", 135.0))), \"&b\"),\n                \"{tensionbar}\", reelingMeter(game.tension / Math.max(1.0D, getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.reeling.break-tension\", 100.0)), reelingTensionColor(game.tension)),\n                \"{combo}\", game.combo);\n        player.spigot().sendMessage(ChatMessageType.ACTION_BAR, TextComponent.fromLegacyText(color(action)));\n    }\n\n    private void finishReelingGame(Player player, ReelingGame game, boolean success, String message) {\n        reelingGames.remove(player.getUniqueId());\n        game.cancel();\n        if (game.bar != null) {\n            game.bar.setProgress(success ? 1.0D : 0.0D);\n            game.bar.setColor(success ? BarColor.GREEN : BarColor.RED);\n            game.bar.setTitle(color(message));\n            Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskLater(this, game.bar::removeAll, 30L);\n        }\n        player.spigot().sendMessage(ChatMessageType.ACTION_BAR, TextComponent.fromLegacyText(color(message)));\n        if (!success) {\n            resetPrecisionFocus(player, false);\n            addStat(player.getUniqueId(), player.getName(), \"reeling.failed\", 1);\n            playConfiguredSound(player, \"fishing.reeling.sounds.fail\", Sound.ENTITY_ITEM_BREAK);\n            return;\n        }\n        addStat(player.getUniqueId(), player.getName(), \"reeling.success\", 1);\n        playConfiguredSound(player, \"fishing.reeling.sounds.success\", Sound.ENTITY_PLAYER_LEVELUP);\n        processFishingReward(player, null, game.realm, reward -> {\n            giveCatchRewardToPlayer(player, reward);\n            if (game.vanillaExperience > 0) {\n                player.giveExp(game.vanillaExperience);\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    private void giveCatchRewardToPlayer(Player player, CatchReward reward) {\n        if (reward.replacement() != null) {\n            HashMap<Integer, ItemStack> leftover = player.getInventory().addItem(reward.replacement());\n            for (ItemStack item : leftover.values()) {\n                player.getWorld().dropItemNaturally(player.getLocation(), item);\n            }\n        }\n        if (reward.experience() > 0) {\n            player.giveExp(reward.experience());\n        }\n    }\n\n    private BarColor reelingBarColor(double tension) {\n        if (tension >= 82.0D) {\n            return BarColor.RED;\n        }\n        if (tension >= 58.0D) {\n            return BarColor.YELLOW;\n        }\n        return BarColor.BLUE;\n    }\n\n    private String reelingTensionColor(double tension) {\n        if (tension >= 82.0D) {\n            return \"&c\";\n        }\n        if (tension >= 58.0D) {\n            return \"&e\";\n        }\n        return \"&a\";\n    }\n\n    private double reelingRodEase(String rodType) {\n        return switch (rodType == null ? \"normal\" : rodType.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)) {\n            case \"tiefsee\", \"mythic\", \"goldstube\" -> 0.82D;\n            case \"glueck\", \"luck\", \"rare\" -> 0.90D;\n            case \"profi\", \"pro\", \"advanced\" -> 0.94D;\n            default -> 1.0D;\n        };\n    }\n\n    private String reelingMeter(double ratio, String filledColor) {\n        int segments = Math.max(8, getConfig().getInt(\"fishing.reeling.meter-segments\", 16));\n        int filled = (int) Math.round(clampDouble(ratio, 0.0D, 1.0D) * segments);\n        StringBuilder meter = new StringBuilder(\"&8[\");\n        for (int i = 0; i < segments; i++) {\n            meter.append(i < filled ? filledColor + \"|\" : \"&7|\");\n        }\n        meter.append(\"&8]\");\n        return meter.toString();\n    }\n\n    private void stopReelingGame(UUID uuid, boolean keepResult) {\n        ReelingGame game = reelingGames.remove(uuid);\n        if (game == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        game.cancel();\n        if (!keepResult && game.bar != null) {\n            game.bar.removeAll();\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void clearReelingGames() {\n        for (ReelingGame game : reelingGames.values()) {\n            game.cancel();\n            if (game.bar != null) {\n                game.bar.removeAll();\n            }\n        }\n        reelingGames.clear();\n    }\n\n    private boolean isFishingAttemptEndState(PlayerFishEvent.State state) {\n        return state == PlayerFishEvent.State.REEL_IN\n                || state == PlayerFishEvent.State.FAILED_ATTEMPT\n                || state == PlayerFishEvent.State.IN_GROUND\n                || state == PlayerFishEvent.State.CAUGHT_ENTITY;\n    }\n\n    private void recordPrecisionReaction(Player player, String quality, long reaction, boolean blocked) {\n        String base = \"players.\" + player.getUniqueId() + \".precision\";\n        data.set(base + \".last-quality\", quality);\n        data.set(base + \".last-reaction-ms\", Math.max(0L, reaction));\n        if (blocked || \"too-fast\".equalsIgnoreCase(quality) || \"too-late\".equalsIgnoreCase(quality)) {\n            resetPrecisionFocus(player, true);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void resetPrecisionFocus(Player player, boolean notify) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.focus.enabled\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        String base = \"players.\" + player.getUniqueId() + \".precision\";\n        int current = data.getInt(base + \".focus\", 0);\n        if (current <= 0) {\n            return;\n        }\n        data.set(base + \".focus\", 0);\n        data.set(base + \".rewarded\", 0);\n        if (notify && getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.focus.reset-message-enabled\", true)) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + getConfig().getString(\"fishing.focus.reset-message\", \"&7Deine Angel-Fokus-Serie wurde zurückgesetzt.\")));\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void grantPrecisionFocusBonus(Player player) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.focus.enabled\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();\n        String base = \"players.\" + uuid + \".precision\";\n        String quality = data.getString(base + \".last-quality\", \"\");\n        if (!\"good\".equalsIgnoreCase(quality) && !\"perfect\".equalsIgnoreCase(quality)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        int add = \"perfect\".equalsIgnoreCase(quality)\n                ? getConfig().getInt(\"fishing.focus.perfect-points\", 2)\n                : getConfig().getInt(\"fishing.focus.good-points\", 1);\n        int max = Math.max(1, getConfig().getInt(\"fishing.focus.max-points\", 100));\n        int current = Math.min(max, data.getInt(base + \".focus\", 0) + Math.max(1, add));\n        data.set(base + \".focus\", current);\n        data.set(base + \".last-quality\", \"\");\n        addStat(uuid, player.getName(), \"precision.points-earned\", Math.max(1, add));\n        if (current > data.getInt(base + \".best\", 0)) {\n            data.set(base + \".best\", current);\n        }\n        ConfigurationSection milestones = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.focus.milestones\");\n        if (milestones == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        int rewarded = data.getInt(base + \".rewarded\", 0);\n        List<Integer> keys = milestones.getKeys(false).stream()\n                .map(key -> parseInt(key, 0))\n                .filter(value -> value > 0)\n                .sorted()\n                .toList();\n        for (int milestone : keys) {\n            if (current < milestone || rewarded >= milestone) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            ConfigurationSection reward = milestones.getConfigurationSection(String.valueOf(milestone));\n            if (reward == null || !reward.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true)) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            grantPrecisionMilestone(player, milestone, reward, current);\n            data.set(base + \".rewarded\", milestone);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void grantPrecisionMilestone(Player player, int milestone, ConfigurationSection reward, int currentFocus) {\n        int coins = reward.getInt(\"coins\", 0);\n        int tokens = reward.getInt(\"tokens\", 0);\n        int xp = reward.getInt(\"xp\", 0);\n        if (coins > 0) {\n            dispatchRewardCommand(\"coins\", player, coins);\n            addStat(player.getUniqueId(), player.getName(), \"coins\", coins);\n        }\n        if (tokens > 0) {\n            dispatchRewardCommand(\"tokens\", player, tokens);\n            addStat(player.getUniqueId(), player.getName(), \"tokens\", tokens);\n        }\n        if (xp > 0) {\n            addFishingXp(player, xp);\n        }\n        for (String command : reward.getStringList(\"commands\")) {\n            Bukkit.dispatchCommand(Bukkit.getConsoleSender(), replaceRandomRanges(command)\n                    .replace(\"{player}\", player.getName())\n                    .replace(\"{uuid}\", player.getUniqueId().toString())\n                    .replace(\"{focus}\", String.valueOf(currentFocus))\n                    .replace(\"{milestone}\", String.valueOf(milestone)));\n        }\n        String message = getConfig().getString(\"fishing.focus.reward-message\", \"&bFokus-Serie &8| &f{focus} &7Punkte erreicht: &a+${coins} &c+{tokens} Rubine\");\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + replace(message,\n                \"{focus}\", currentFocus,\n                \"{milestone}\", milestone,\n                \"{coins}\", coins,\n                \"{tokens}\", tokens,\n                \"{xp}\", xp)));\n        player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.BLOCK_AMETHYST_BLOCK_CHIME, 0.8f, 1.25f);\n    }\n\n    private long clampLong(long value, long min, long max) {\n        return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));\n    }\n\n    private double clampDouble(double value, double min, double max) {\n        return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));\n    }\n\n    private double groupTierMultiplier(Player player, String tier) {\n        int nearby = nearbyFishingPlayers(player);\n        if (nearby <= 1 || (!\"rare\".equalsIgnoreCase(tier) && !\"jackpot\".equalsIgnoreCase(tier))) {\n            return 1.0;\n        }\n        double perPlayer = getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.group-bonus.rare-jackpot-multiplier-per-player\", 0.08);\n        double maximum = getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.group-bonus.max-rare-jackpot-multiplier\", 1.40);\n        return Math.min(maximum, 1.0 + (nearby - 1) * perPlayer);\n    }\n\n    private double groupMysteryBonus(Player player) {\n        int nearby = nearbyFishingPlayers(player);\n        if (nearby <= 1) {\n            return 0.0;\n        }\n        return Math.min(\n                getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.group-bonus.max-mystery-bonus\", 5.0),\n                (nearby - 1) * getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.group-bonus.mystery-bonus-per-player\", 1.0)\n        );\n    }\n\n    private int nearbyFishingPlayers(Player player) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.group-bonus.enabled\", true)) {\n            return 1;\n        }\n        double radius = getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.group-bonus.radius\", 10.0);\n        int count = 1;\n        for (Entity entity : player.getNearbyEntities(radius, radius, radius)) {\n            if (entity instanceof Player nearby && isHoldingFishingRod(nearby)) {\n                count++;\n            }\n        }\n        return count;\n    }\n\n    private boolean isHoldingFishingRod(Player player) {\n        return player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand().getType() == Material.FISHING_ROD\n                || player.getInventory().getItemInOffHand().getType() == Material.FISHING_ROD;\n    }\n\n    private boolean canFishInCurrentRealm(Player player) {\n        return canFishInRealm(player, fishingRealm(player));\n    }\n\n    private boolean canFishInRealm(Player player, String realm) {\n        String required = getConfig().getString(\"fishing.realms.\" + realm + \".required-rod\", \"\");\n        if (required == null || required.isBlank()) {\n            return true;\n        }\n        String active = activeRodType(player);\n        if (required.equalsIgnoreCase(active) || \"goldstube\".equalsIgnoreCase(active)) {\n            return true;\n        }\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + getConfig().getString(\"fishing.realms.\" + realm + \".missing-message\", \"&cDiese Dimension braucht eine spezielle Angel.\")));\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    private void prepareSpecialFishingSession(Player player, PlayerFishEvent event) {\n        specialFishingSessions.remove(player.getUniqueId());\n        specialRealmCastTimes.put(player.getUniqueId(), System.currentTimeMillis());\n        Entity hook = event.getHook();\n        if (hook == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        String instantRealm = detectSpecialFishingRealm(player, hook.getLocation());\n        if (\"lava\".equalsIgnoreCase(instantRealm) || \"void\".equalsIgnoreCase(instantRealm)) {\n            specialFishingSessions.put(player.getUniqueId(), instantRealm);\n        }\n        for (long delay : List.of(4L, 10L, 20L)) {\n            Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskLater(this, () -> {\n            if (!player.isOnline() || hook.isDead()) {\n                return;\n            }\n            String realm = detectSpecialFishingRealm(player, hook.getLocation());\n            if (!\"lava\".equalsIgnoreCase(realm) && !\"void\".equalsIgnoreCase(realm)) {\n                return;\n            }\n            if (!canFishInRealm(player, realm)) {\n                return;\n            }\n            specialFishingSessions.put(player.getUniqueId(), realm);\n            String message = getConfig().getString(\"fishing.realms.\" + realm + \".manual-catch.ready-message\", \"\");\n            if (message != null && !message.isBlank()) {\n                player.spigot().sendMessage(ChatMessageType.ACTION_BAR, TextComponent.fromLegacyText(color(prefix() + message)));\n            }\n            }, delay);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private String detectSpecialFishingRealm(Player player, Location hookLocation) {\n        if (isSpecialRealmEnabled(\"lava\") && hookLocation != null && isNearMaterial(hookLocation, Material.LAVA, 3)) {\n            return \"lava\";\n        }\n        Block target = player.getTargetBlockExact(12);\n        if (isSpecialRealmEnabled(\"lava\") && target != null && target.getType() == Material.LAVA) {\n            return \"lava\";\n        }\n        String rodType = activeRodType(player);\n        if (isSpecialRealmEnabled(\"lava\") && (\"lava\".equalsIgnoreCase(rodType) || \"goldstube\".equalsIgnoreCase(rodType))) {\n            if (player.getWorld().getEnvironment() == World.Environment.NETHER || isNearMaterial(player.getLocation(), Material.LAVA, 5)) {\n                return \"lava\";\n            }\n        }\n        if (isSpecialRealmEnabled(\"void\") && (\"void\".equalsIgnoreCase(rodType) || \"goldstube\".equalsIgnoreCase(rodType))) {\n            if (isVoidFishingLocation(player, hookLocation)) {\n                return \"void\";\n            }\n        }\n        return fishingRealmByWorld(player);\n    }\n\n    private boolean isSpecialRealmEnabled(String realm) {\n        return getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.realms.\" + realm + \".enabled\", false)\n                && getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.realms.\" + realm + \".manual-catch.enabled\", false);\n    }\n\n    private boolean isVoidFishingLocation(Player player, Location hookLocation) {\n        World world = player.getWorld();\n        String worldName = world.getName().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n        if (world.getEnvironment() == World.Environment.THE_END || worldName.contains(\"void\")) {\n            return true;\n        }\n        if (world.getEnvironment() == World.Environment.NETHER) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        Location check = hookLocation == null ? player.getLocation() : hookLocation;\n        double maxY = getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.realms.void.manual-catch.max-y\", 32.0);\n        return check.getY() <= maxY && !hasSolidBlockBelow(check, 18);\n    }\n\n    private boolean hasSolidBlockBelow(Location location, int depth) {\n        if (location == null || location.getWorld() == null) {\n            return true;\n        }\n        int x = location.getBlockX();\n        int z = location.getBlockZ();\n        int minY = Math.max(location.getWorld().getMinHeight(), location.getBlockY() - depth);\n        for (int y = location.getBlockY(); y >= minY; y--) {\n            Material type = location.getWorld().getBlockAt(x, y, z).getType();\n            if (type.isSolid() || type == Material.WATER || type == Material.LAVA) {\n                return true;\n            }\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    private boolean isNearMaterial(Location center, Material material, int radius) {\n        if (center == null || center.getWorld() == null) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        int baseX = center.getBlockX();\n        int baseY = center.getBlockY();\n        int baseZ = center.getBlockZ();\n        for (int x = -radius; x <= radius; x++) {\n            for (int y = -radius; y <= radius; y++) {\n                for (int z = -radius; z <= radius; z++) {\n                    if (center.getWorld().getBlockAt(baseX + x, baseY + y, baseZ + z).getType() == material) {\n                        return true;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    private boolean isSpecialRealmManualCatch(Player player, String realm) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.realms.\" + realm + \".manual-catch.enabled\", true)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        if (!\"lava\".equalsIgnoreCase(realm) && !\"void\".equalsIgnoreCase(realm)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        return canFishInRealm(player, realm);\n    }\n\n    private void handleSpecialRealmManualCatch(PlayerFishEvent event, Player player, String realm) {\n        specialFishingSessions.remove(player.getUniqueId());\n        long cooldown = getConfig().getLong(\"fishing.realms.\" + realm + \".manual-catch.cooldown-ms\", 3200L);\n        long minimumWait = getConfig().getLong(\"fishing.realms.\" + realm + \".manual-catch.min-wait-ms\", 2200L);\n        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        long castAt = specialRealmCastTimes.getOrDefault(player.getUniqueId(), 0L);\n        if (minimumWait > 0L && castAt > 0L && now - castAt < minimumWait) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            specialRealmCooldowns.put(player.getUniqueId(), now);\n            cleanupSpecialFishingAttempt(event, player);\n            long remaining = Math.max(1L, (minimumWait - (now - castAt) + 999L) / 1000L);\n            player.spigot().sendMessage(ChatMessageType.ACTION_BAR, TextComponent.fromLegacyText(color(prefix()\n                    + getConfig().getString(\"fishing.realms.\" + realm + \".manual-catch.too-fast-message\", \"&cWarte noch &f{seconds}s&c, bevor du einholst.\")\n                    .replace(\"{seconds}\", String.valueOf(remaining)))));\n            return;\n        }\n        long last = specialRealmCooldowns.getOrDefault(player.getUniqueId(), 0L);\n        if (cooldown > 0L && now - last < cooldown) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            cleanupSpecialFishingAttempt(event, player);\n            long remaining = Math.max(1L, (cooldown - (now - last) + 999L) / 1000L);\n            player.spigot().sendMessage(ChatMessageType.ACTION_BAR, TextComponent.fromLegacyText(color(prefix()\n                    + getConfig().getString(\"fishing.realms.\" + realm + \".manual-catch.cooldown-message\", \"&cLava/Void-Angeln lädt noch &f{seconds}s&c.\")\n                    .replace(\"{seconds}\", String.valueOf(remaining)))));\n            return;\n        }\n        specialRealmCooldowns.put(player.getUniqueId(), now);\n        cleanupSpecialFishingAttempt(event, player);\n        event.setCancelled(true);\n        processFishingReward(player, null, realm, reward -> {\n            if (reward.replacement() != null) {\n                HashMap<Integer, ItemStack> leftover = player.getInventory().addItem(reward.replacement());\n                for (ItemStack item : leftover.values()) {\n                    player.getWorld().dropItemNaturally(player.getLocation(), item);\n                }\n            }\n            if (reward.experience() > 0) {\n                player.giveExp(reward.experience());\n            }\n        });\n        player.getWorld().spawnParticle(\"lava\".equalsIgnoreCase(realm) ? Particle.LAVA : Particle.PORTAL, player.getLocation().add(0, 1.0, 0), 24, 0.6, 0.8, 0.6, 0.03);\n        playConfiguredSound(player, \"fishing.realms.\" + realm + \".manual-catch.sound\", \"lava\".equalsIgnoreCase(realm) ? Sound.BLOCK_LAVA_POP : Sound.ENTITY_ENDERMAN_TELEPORT);\n    }\n\n    private void cleanupSpecialFishingAttempt(PlayerFishEvent event, Player player) {\n        specialFishingSessions.remove(player.getUniqueId());\n        specialRealmCastTimes.remove(player.getUniqueId());\n        if (event.getHook() != null && !event.getHook().isDead()) {\n            event.getHook().remove();\n        }\n    }\n\n    private boolean isSpecialFishingReelState(PlayerFishEvent.State state) {\n        return state == PlayerFishEvent.State.REEL_IN\n                || state == PlayerFishEvent.State.FAILED_ATTEMPT\n                || state == PlayerFishEvent.State.IN_GROUND;\n    }\n\n    private void processFishingReward(Player player, Entity caught, String realm, Consumer<CatchReward> itemHandler) {\n        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        long minInterval = Math.max(0L, getConfig().getLong(\"fishing.reward-dedupe-ms\", 450L));\n        long previous = lastFishingRewardAt.getOrDefault(player.getUniqueId(), 0L);\n        if (minInterval > 0L && now - previous < minInterval) {\n            getLogger().fine(\"Duplicate fishing reward skipped for \" + player.getName() + \" (\" + (now - previous) + \"ms)\");\n            return;\n        }\n        lastFishingRewardAt.put(player.getUniqueId(), now);\n        activeFishingRealms.put(player.getUniqueId(), realm);\n        try {\n            BaitBonus bait = consumeActiveBait(player);\n            RewardTier tier = rollTier(player, bait);\n            CatchReward reward = createBaseReward(player, tier, bait);\n            MysteryDrop forcedRealmDrop = rollForcedRealmDrop(player, realm);\n            MysteryDrop mysteryDrop = forcedRealmDrop != null ? forcedRealmDrop : rollMysteryDrop(player, bait.mysteryBonus());\n            if (mysteryDrop != null) {\n                reward = reward.combine(mysteryDrop.toReward(player, caught));\n            }\n            CatchMoment moment = rollCatchMoment(player, reward);\n            reward = reward.withMoment(moment);\n            if (reward.replacement() == null) {\n                reward = reward.withReplacement(createFishCatchItem(player, reward));\n            } else if (shouldApplyFishProfile(reward.replacement(), reward)) {\n                reward = reward.withReplacement(enrichFishCatchItem(player, reward, reward.replacement().clone()));\n            }\n            itemHandler.accept(reward);\n            for (String command : reward.commands()) {\n                dispatchPreparedCommand(player, command, reward);\n            }\n            dispatchRewardCommand(\"coins\", player, reward.coins());\n            dispatchRewardCommand(\"tokens\", player, reward.tokens());\n            sendRewardFeedback(player, reward);\n            if (!bait.id().isBlank() && getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.messages.bait-chat-enabled\", false)) {\n                player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&bKöder aktiv: &f\" + bait.name() + \" &8(\" + bait.usesRemaining() + \" Nutzungen übrig)\"));\n            }\n            triggerCatchVariety(player, reward);\n            storeRewardStats(player, reward);\n            grantPrecisionFocusBonus(player);\n            sendCatchActionBar(player, reward, bait);\n            checkAchievements(player);\n            checkQuests(player);\n            rollFishingBoss(player);\n        } finally {\n            activeFishingRealms.remove(player.getUniqueId());\n        }\n    }\n\n    private MysteryDrop rollForcedRealmDrop(Player player, String realm) {\n        if (!\"lava\".equalsIgnoreCase(realm) && !\"void\".equalsIgnoreCase(realm)) {\n            return null;\n        }\n        double chance = getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.realms.\" + realm + \".manual-catch.realm-drop-chance\", 100.0);\n        if (chance <= 0.0 || random.nextDouble(100.0) >= Math.min(100.0, chance)) {\n            return null;\n        }\n        return rollMysteryDropFromPath(\"fishing.realm-drops.\" + realm);\n    }\n\n    private MysteryDrop rollMysteryDropFromPath(String path) {\n        List<MysteryDrop> drops = new ArrayList<>();\n        double total = collectMysteryDrops(path, drops);\n        if (drops.isEmpty() || total <= 0.0) {\n            return null;\n        }\n        double roll = random.nextDouble(total);\n        double cursor = 0.0;\n        for (MysteryDrop drop : drops) {\n            cursor += drop.weight();\n            if (roll <= cursor) {\n                return drop;\n            }\n        }\n        return drops.get(drops.size() - 1);\n    }\n\n    private String activeRodType(Player player) {\n        String main = rodType(player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand());\n        if (main != null) {\n            return main;\n        }\n        String off = rodType(player.getInventory().getItemInOffHand());\n        return off == null ? \"normal\" : off;\n    }\n\n    private String rodType(ItemStack item) {\n        if (item == null || item.getType() != Material.FISHING_ROD) {\n            return null;\n        }\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta == null) {\n            return \"normal\";\n        }\n        String type = meta.getPersistentDataContainer().get(rodTypeKey, PersistentDataType.STRING);\n        if (type != null && !type.isBlank()) {\n            return type;\n        }\n        String searchable = \"\";\n        if (meta.hasDisplayName()) {\n            searchable += ChatColor.stripColor(meta.getDisplayName()).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT) + \" \";\n        }\n        if (meta.hasLore() && meta.getLore() != null) {\n            searchable += ChatColor.stripColor(String.join(\" \", meta.getLore())).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n        }\n        if (searchable.contains(\"lava\")) {\n            return \"lava\";\n        }\n        if (searchable.contains(\"void\")) {\n            return \"void\";\n        }\n        if (searchable.contains(\"goldstube\")) {\n            return \"goldstube\";\n        }\n        return \"normal\";\n    }\n\n    private double rodMysteryBonus(String rodType) {\n        return getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.rods.\" + rodType + \".mystery-bonus\", 0.0);\n    }\n\n    private double rodCoinMultiplier(String rodType) {\n        return getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.rods.\" + rodType + \".coin-multiplier\", 1.0);\n    }\n\n    private double rodTokenMultiplier(String rodType) {\n        return getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.rods.\" + rodType + \".token-multiplier\", 1.0);\n    }\n\n    private double rodTierMultiplier(String rodType, String tier) {\n        if (\"rare\".equalsIgnoreCase(tier)) {\n            return getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.rods.\" + rodType + \".rare-multiplier\", 1.0);\n        }\n        if (\"jackpot\".equalsIgnoreCase(tier)) {\n            return getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.rods.\" + rodType + \".jackpot-multiplier\", 1.0);\n        }\n        return 1.0;\n    }\n\n    private String fishingRealm(Player player) {\n        String active = activeFishingRealms.get(player.getUniqueId());\n        if (active != null && !active.isBlank()) {\n            if ((\"lava\".equalsIgnoreCase(active) || \"void\".equalsIgnoreCase(active)) && !isSpecialRealmEnabled(active)) {\n                return \"overworld\";\n            }\n            return active;\n        }\n        World.Environment environment = player.getWorld().getEnvironment();\n        if (environment == World.Environment.NETHER && isSpecialRealmEnabled(\"lava\")) {\n            return \"lava\";\n        }\n        if (environment == World.Environment.THE_END && isSpecialRealmEnabled(\"void\")) {\n            return \"void\";\n        }\n        return \"overworld\";\n    }\n\n    private String fishingRealm(Player player, PlayerFishEvent event) {\n        String session = specialFishingSessions.get(player.getUniqueId());\n        if (session != null && !session.isBlank()) {\n            if ((\"lava\".equalsIgnoreCase(session) || \"void\".equalsIgnoreCase(session)) && !isSpecialRealmEnabled(session)) {\n                return \"overworld\";\n            }\n            return session;\n        }\n        if (event.getHook() != null) {\n            Location location = event.getHook().getLocation();\n            Material hook = location.getBlock().getType();\n            Material below = location.clone().subtract(0, 1, 0).getBlock().getType();\n            if (isSpecialRealmEnabled(\"lava\") && (hook == Material.LAVA || below == Material.LAVA || isNearMaterial(location, Material.LAVA, 1))) {\n                return \"lava\";\n            }\n        }\n        return fishingRealmByWorld(player);\n    }\n\n    private String fishingRealmByWorld(Player player) {\n        World.Environment environment = player.getWorld().getEnvironment();\n        if (environment == World.Environment.NETHER && isSpecialRealmEnabled(\"lava\")) {\n            return \"lava\";\n        }\n        if (environment == World.Environment.THE_END && isSpecialRealmEnabled(\"void\")) {\n            return \"void\";\n        }\n        return \"overworld\";\n    }\n\n    private List<BossDefinition> bossDefinitions() {\n        List<BossDefinition> definitions = new ArrayList<>();\n        ConfigurationSection realms = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.bosses.realms\");\n        if (realms == null) {\n            return definitions;\n        }\n        for (String realm : realms.getKeys(false)) {\n            ConfigurationSection bosses = realms.getConfigurationSection(realm + \".bosses\");\n            if (bosses == null) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            for (String id : bosses.getKeys(false)) {\n                ConfigurationSection boss = bosses.getConfigurationSection(id);\n                if (boss != null && boss.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true)) {\n                    definitions.add(new BossDefinition(id, realm, boss));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        definitions.sort(Comparator.comparing(BossDefinition::realm).thenComparing(BossDefinition::id, String.CASE_INSENSITIVE_ORDER));\n        return definitions;\n    }\n\n    private BossDefinition bossDefinition(String id) {\n        for (BossDefinition definition : bossDefinitions()) {\n            if (definition.id().equalsIgnoreCase(id)) {\n                return definition;\n            }\n        }\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    private void rollFishingBoss(Player player) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.bosses.enabled\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        String realm = fishingRealm(player);\n        ConfigurationSection realmSection = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.bosses.realms.\" + realm);\n        if (realmSection == null || random.nextDouble(100.0) >= realmSection.getDouble(\"chance\", 0.0)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        ConfigurationSection bosses = realmSection.getConfigurationSection(\"bosses\");\n        if (bosses == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        List<ConfigurationSection> weighted = new ArrayList<>();\n        double total = 0.0;\n        for (String key : bosses.getKeys(false)) {\n            ConfigurationSection boss = bosses.getConfigurationSection(key);\n            if (boss == null || !boss.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true)) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            double weight = Math.max(0.0, boss.getDouble(\"weight\", 0.0));\n            if (weight <= 0.0) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            weighted.add(boss);\n            total += weight;\n        }\n        if (weighted.isEmpty() || total <= 0.0) {\n            return;\n        }\n        double roll = random.nextDouble(total);\n        double cursor = 0.0;\n        ConfigurationSection selected = weighted.get(weighted.size() - 1);\n        for (ConfigurationSection boss : weighted) {\n            cursor += boss.getDouble(\"weight\", 0.0);\n            if (roll <= cursor) {\n                selected = boss;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n        spawnFishingBoss(player, selected.getName(), selected);\n    }\n\n    private void spawnFishingBoss(Player player, String bossId, ConfigurationSection section) {\n        EntityType type;\n        try {\n            type = EntityType.valueOf(section.getString(\"entity\", \"DROWNED\").toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));\n        } catch (IllegalArgumentException exception) {\n            type = EntityType.DROWNED;\n        }\n        Location location = player.getLocation().add(player.getLocation().getDirection().normalize().multiply(4.0));\n        Entity entity = player.getWorld().spawnEntity(location, type);\n        if (!(entity instanceof LivingEntity living)) {\n            entity.remove();\n            return;\n        }\n        String name = color(section.getString(\"name\", \"&4Angelboss\"));\n        double health = Math.max(1.0, section.getDouble(\"health\", 80.0));\n        if (living.getAttribute(Attribute.MAX_HEALTH) != null) {\n            living.getAttribute(Attribute.MAX_HEALTH).setBaseValue(health);\n        }\n        living.setHealth(Math.min(health, living.getAttribute(Attribute.MAX_HEALTH) == null ? health : living.getAttribute(Attribute.MAX_HEALTH).getValue()));\n        living.setCustomName(name);\n        living.setCustomNameVisible(true);\n        living.getPersistentDataContainer().set(bossIdKey, PersistentDataType.STRING, bossId == null || bossId.isBlank() ? normalizeKey(ChatColor.stripColor(name)) : bossId);\n        applyBossVisuals(living, section);\n        BarColor color = BarColor.PURPLE;\n        try {\n            color = BarColor.valueOf(section.getString(\"bar-color\", \"PURPLE\").toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));\n        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) {\n            // Invalid bossbar colors fall back to purple so one config typo cannot break boss spawning.\n        }\n        BossBar bar = Bukkit.createBossBar(name, color, BarStyle.SEGMENTED_10);\n        bar.setProgress(1.0);\n        for (Player online : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {\n            if (online.getWorld().equals(player.getWorld()) && online.getLocation().distanceSquared(location) <= 80 * 80) {\n                bar.addPlayer(online);\n            }\n        }\n        bossBars.put(living.getUniqueId(), bar);\n        bossLootCommands.put(living.getUniqueId(), section.getStringList(\"loot-commands\"));\n        Bukkit.broadcastMessage(color(prefix() + section.getString(\"message\", \"&c{player} hat einen Boss ans Land gezogen!\").replace(\"{player}\", player.getName())));\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onBossDamage(EntityDamageEvent event) {\n        BossBar bar = bossBars.get(event.getEntity().getUniqueId());\n        if (bar == null || !(event.getEntity() instanceof LivingEntity living)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        double max = living.getAttribute(Attribute.MAX_HEALTH) == null ? living.getMaxHealth() : living.getAttribute(Attribute.MAX_HEALTH).getValue();\n        double remaining = Math.max(0.0, living.getHealth() - event.getFinalDamage());\n        bar.setProgress(Math.max(0.0, Math.min(1.0, remaining / max)));\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler\n    public void onBossDeath(EntityDeathEvent event) {\n        UUID uuid = event.getEntity().getUniqueId();\n        BossBar bar = bossBars.remove(uuid);\n        List<String> commands = bossLootCommands.remove(uuid);\n        if (bar == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        bar.removeAll();\n        Player killer = event.getEntity().getKiller();\n        if (killer == null || commands == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        String bossId = event.getEntity().getPersistentDataContainer().get(bossIdKey, PersistentDataType.STRING);\n        if (bossId == null || bossId.isBlank()) {\n            bossId = normalizeKey(ChatColor.stripColor(event.getEntity().getName()));\n        }\n        for (String command : commands) {\n            Bukkit.dispatchCommand(Bukkit.getConsoleSender(), replaceRandomRanges(command)\n                    .replace(\"{player}\", killer.getName())\n                    .replace(\"{uuid}\", killer.getUniqueId().toString())\n                    .replace(\"{boss}\", bossId));\n        }\n        addStat(killer.getUniqueId(), killer.getName(), \"boss-kills\", 1);\n        addStat(killer.getUniqueId(), killer.getName(), \"boss.\" + bossId + \".kills\", 1);\n        checkAchievements(killer);\n        checkQuests(killer);\n        saveData();\n        Bukkit.broadcastMessage(color(prefix() + \"&6\" + killer.getName() + \" &7hat den Angelboss besiegt und Loot erhalten!\"));\n    }\n\n    private boolean sameWorldAndNear(Location first, Location second, double radius) {\n        return first.getWorld() != null && first.getWorld().equals(second.getWorld()) && first.distanceSquared(second) <= radius * radius;\n    }\n\n    private boolean handleSpecialCatchItem(Player player, ItemStack item) {\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta == null) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        PersistentDataContainer container = meta.getPersistentDataContainer();\n        String type = container.get(specialTypeKey, PersistentDataType.STRING);\n        if (type == null || type.isBlank()) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        if (\"bottle_message\".equalsIgnoreCase(type)) {\n            String story = container.get(storyKey, PersistentDataType.STRING);\n            sendBottleStory(player, story == null || story.isBlank() ? \"Die Nachricht ist vom Wasser verwischt.\" : story);\n            consumeOneHeldItem(player, item);\n            return true;\n        }\n        if (\"luckyblock\".equalsIgnoreCase(type)) {\n            player.sendTitle(color(\"&e&lLuckyblock\"), color(\"&7Das Wasser funkelt golden...\"), 5, 35, 10);\n            player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.BLOCK_AMETHYST_BLOCK_CHIME, 0.9f, 1.25f);\n            player.getWorld().strikeLightningEffect(player.getLocation());\n            for (String command : getConfig().getStringList(\"fishing.special-items.luckyblock.commands\")) {\n                Bukkit.dispatchCommand(Bukkit.getConsoleSender(), replaceRandomRanges(command).replace(\"{player}\", player.getName()).replace(\"{uuid}\", player.getUniqueId().toString()));\n            }\n            consumeOneHeldItem(player, item);\n            return true;\n        }\n        if (\"goldstuben_jackpot\".equalsIgnoreCase(type)) {\n            player.sendTitle(color(\"&6&lChaos Jackpot\"), color(\"&fDie Belohnung wird ausgelost!\"), 5, 45, 15);\n            player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.UI_TOAST_CHALLENGE_COMPLETE, 1.0f, 1.0f);\n            for (String command : getConfig().getStringList(\"fishing.special-items.goldstuben-jackpot.commands\")) {\n                Bukkit.dispatchCommand(Bukkit.getConsoleSender(), replaceRandomRanges(command).replace(\"{player}\", player.getName()).replace(\"{uuid}\", player.getUniqueId().toString()));\n            }\n            Bukkit.broadcastMessage(color(prefix() + \"&6\" + player.getName() + \" &7hat einen &eChaos Jackpot &7eingeloest!\"));\n            consumeOneHeldItem(player, item);\n            return true;\n        }\n        if (\"lootbox\".equalsIgnoreCase(type)) {\n            long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n            long lastOpen = lootboxCooldowns.getOrDefault(player.getUniqueId(), 0L);\n            long cooldown = getConfig().getLong(\"fishing.lootboxes.open-cooldown-ms\", 1200L);\n            if (now - lastOpen < cooldown) {\n                player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cBitte warte kurz, bevor du die nächste Lootbox öffnest.\"));\n                return true;\n            }\n            lootboxCooldowns.put(player.getUniqueId(), now);\n            String lootboxId = container.get(lootboxKey, PersistentDataType.STRING);\n            if (openFishingLootbox(player, lootboxId == null || lootboxId.isBlank() ? \"common\" : lootboxId)) {\n                consumeOneHeldItem(player, item);\n            }\n            return true;\n        }\n        if (\"fishing_net\".equalsIgnoreCase(type)) {\n            useFishingNet(player, item);\n            return true;\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    private void useFishingNet(Player player, ItemStack item) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.special-items.fishing-net.enabled\", true)) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cFischernetze sind aktuell deaktiviert.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        if (!isNearMaterial(player.getLocation(), Material.WATER, getConfig().getInt(\"fishing.special-items.fishing-net.water-radius\", 5))) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + getConfig().getString(\"fishing.special-items.fishing-net.need-water\", \"&cDu musst am Wasser stehen, um das Fischernetz auszuwerfen.\")));\n            playConfiguredSound(player, \"fishing.special-items.fishing-net.sounds.fail\", Sound.BLOCK_NOTE_BLOCK_BASS);\n            return;\n        }\n        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        long cooldown = getConfig().getLong(\"fishing.special-items.fishing-net.cooldown-ms\", 6000L);\n        long previous = fishingNetCooldowns.getOrDefault(player.getUniqueId(), 0L);\n        if (cooldown > 0L && now - previous < cooldown) {\n            long seconds = Math.max(1L, (cooldown - (now - previous) + 999L) / 1000L);\n            player.spigot().sendMessage(ChatMessageType.ACTION_BAR, TextComponent.fromLegacyText(color(prefix()\n                    + getConfig().getString(\"fishing.special-items.fishing-net.cooldown-message\", \"&cDein Fischernetz trocknet noch &f{seconds}s&c.\")\n                    .replace(\"{seconds}\", String.valueOf(seconds)))));\n            return;\n        }\n        fishingNetCooldowns.put(player.getUniqueId(), now);\n        int pulls = Math.max(1, getConfig().getInt(\"fishing.special-items.fishing-net.pulls\", 5));\n        double mysteryBonus = getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.special-items.fishing-net.mystery-bonus\", 5.0);\n        player.sendTitle(color(getConfig().getString(\"fishing.special-items.fishing-net.title\", \"&b&lFischernetz\")),\n                color(getConfig().getString(\"fishing.special-items.fishing-net.subtitle\", \"&7{pulls} Fänge werden eingeholt...\").replace(\"{pulls}\", String.valueOf(pulls))),\n                5, 35, 10);\n        playConfiguredSound(player, \"fishing.special-items.fishing-net.sounds.cast\", Sound.ENTITY_FISHING_BOBBER_SPLASH);\n        player.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.SPLASH, player.getLocation().add(0.0, 0.35, 0.0), 45, 1.25, 0.25, 1.25, 0.03);\n\n        NetSummary summary = new NetSummary();\n        BaitBonus noBait = BaitBonus.none();\n        for (int i = 0; i < pulls; i++) {\n            CatchReward reward = createNetReward(player, noBait, mysteryBonus);\n            giveCatchRewardToPlayer(player, reward);\n            for (String command : reward.commands()) {\n                dispatchPreparedCommand(player, command, reward);\n            }\n            dispatchRewardCommand(\"coins\", player, reward.coins());\n            dispatchRewardCommand(\"tokens\", player, reward.tokens());\n            triggerCatchVariety(player, reward);\n            storeRewardStats(player, reward);\n            summary = summary.add(reward);\n        }\n        addStat(player.getUniqueId(), player.getName(), \"special.fishing-net.used\", 1);\n        checkAchievements(player);\n        checkQuests(player);\n        consumeOneHeldItem(player, item);\n        saveData();\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + getConfig().getString(\"fishing.special-items.fishing-net.summary\",\n                        \"&bFischernetz: &f{pulls} Fänge &8| &a+${coins} &8| &c+{tokens} Rubine &8| &d{mystery} Mystery\")\n                .replace(\"{pulls}\", String.valueOf(summary.pulls()))\n                .replace(\"{coins}\", String.valueOf(summary.coins()))\n                .replace(\"{tokens}\", String.valueOf(summary.tokens()))\n                .replace(\"{mystery}\", String.valueOf(summary.mystery()))));\n        player.spigot().sendMessage(ChatMessageType.ACTION_BAR, TextComponent.fromLegacyText(color(\"&bFischernetz &8| &f\" + summary.pulls() + \" Fänge &8| &a+$\" + summary.coins() + \" &8| &c+\" + summary.tokens() + \" Rubine\")));\n        playConfiguredSound(player, \"fishing.special-items.fishing-net.sounds.success\", Sound.ENTITY_PLAYER_LEVELUP);\n    }\n\n    private CatchReward createNetReward(Player player, BaitBonus bait, double mysteryBonus) {\n        RewardTier tier = rollTier(player, bait);\n        CatchReward reward = createBaseReward(player, tier, bait);\n        MysteryDrop drop = rollMysteryDrop(player, mysteryBonus);\n        if (drop != null) {\n            reward = reward.combine(drop.toReward(player, player));\n        }\n        reward = reward.withMoment(rollCatchMoment(player, reward));\n        if (reward.replacement() == null) {\n            reward = reward.withReplacement(createFishCatchItem(player, reward));\n        } else if (shouldApplyFishProfile(reward.replacement(), reward)) {\n            reward = reward.withReplacement(enrichFishCatchItem(player, reward, reward.replacement().clone()));\n        }\n        return reward;\n    }\n\n    private boolean openFishingLootbox(Player player, String lootboxId) {\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.lootboxes.\" + lootboxId);\n        if (section == null || !section.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true)) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDiese Lootbox ist nicht konfiguriert.\"));\n            return false;\n        }\n        String name = section.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(lootboxId));\n        player.sendTitle(color(name), color(section.getString(\"subtitle\", \"&7Die Belohnung wird gezogen...\")), 5, 40, 15);\n        player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.UI_TOAST_CHALLENGE_COMPLETE, 1.0f, 1.15f);\n        player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.BLOCK_NOTE_BLOCK_CHIME, 0.8f, 1.65f);\n        String particleName = section.getString(\"particle\", \"END_ROD\");\n        try {\n            player.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.valueOf(particleName.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT)), player.getLocation().add(0, 1.1, 0), section.getInt(\"particle-count\", 80), 0.8, 0.8, 0.8, 0.04);\n        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) {\n            player.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.END_ROD, player.getLocation().add(0, 1.1, 0), 80, 0.8, 0.8, 0.8, 0.04);\n        }\n        List<ConfigurationSection> rewards = new ArrayList<>();\n        double total = 0.0;\n        ConfigurationSection rewardSection = section.getConfigurationSection(\"rewards\");\n        if (rewardSection == null) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDiese Lootbox hat keine Rewards konfiguriert.\"));\n            return false;\n        }\n        for (String key : rewardSection.getKeys(false)) {\n            ConfigurationSection reward = rewardSection.getConfigurationSection(key);\n            if (reward == null || !reward.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true)) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            double weight = Math.max(0.0, reward.getDouble(\"weight\", 0.0));\n            if (weight <= 0.0) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            rewards.add(reward);\n            total += weight;\n        }\n        if (rewards.isEmpty() || total <= 0.0) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDiese Lootbox hat keine aktiven Rewards mit Gewicht.\"));\n            return false;\n        }\n        double roll = random.nextDouble(total);\n        double cursor = 0.0;\n        ConfigurationSection selected = rewards.get(rewards.size() - 1);\n        for (ConfigurationSection reward : rewards) {\n            cursor += reward.getDouble(\"weight\", 0.0);\n            if (roll <= cursor) {\n                selected = reward;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n        giveLootboxReward(player, selected);\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    private void giveLootboxReward(Player player, ConfigurationSection reward) {\n        int coins = randomBetween(reward.getInt(\"coins-min\", reward.getInt(\"coins\", 0)), reward.getInt(\"coins-max\", reward.getInt(\"coins\", 0)));\n        int tokens = randomBetween(reward.getInt(\"tokens-min\", reward.getInt(\"tokens\", 0)), reward.getInt(\"tokens-max\", reward.getInt(\"tokens\", 0)));\n        if (coins > 0) {\n            dispatchRewardCommand(\"coins\", player, coins);\n            addStat(player.getUniqueId(), player.getName(), \"coins\", coins);\n        }\n        if (tokens > 0) {\n            dispatchRewardCommand(\"tokens\", player, tokens);\n            addStat(player.getUniqueId(), player.getName(), \"tokens\", tokens);\n        }\n        Material material = Material.matchMaterial(reward.getString(\"material\", \"\"));\n        if (material != null
