&& !material.isAir()) {\n            ItemStack rewardItem = new ItemStack(material, Math.max(1, randomBetween(reward.getInt(\"amount-min\", reward.getInt(\"amount\", 1)), reward.getInt(\"amount-max\", reward.getInt(\"amount\", 1)))));\n            ItemMeta meta = rewardItem.getItemMeta();\n            if (meta != null) {\n                meta.setDisplayName(color(reward.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(material.name()))));\n                meta.setLore(colorList(reward.getStringList(\"lore\")));\n                meta.addItemFlags(ItemFlag.HIDE_ATTRIBUTES);\n                rewardItem.setItemMeta(meta);\n            }\n            applyEnchantments(rewardItem, reward.getStringList(\"enchantments\"));\n            applyRepairCost(rewardItem, reward.getInt(\"repair-cost\", 0));\n            markFishingMarketItem(rewardItem, reward.getInt(\"sell-price\", 0));\n            giveItem(player, rewardItem);\n        }\n        for (String command : reward.getStringList(\"commands\")) {\n            Bukkit.dispatchCommand(Bukkit.getConsoleSender(), replaceRandomRanges(command).replace(\"{player}\", player.getName()).replace(\"{uuid}\", player.getUniqueId().toString()));\n        }\n        String rewardName = reward.getString(\"name\", \"Belohnung\");\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&dLootbox: &f\" + rewardName + \" &7gezogen!\"));\n        addStat(player.getUniqueId(), player.getName(), \"lootboxes-opened\", 1);\n        checkAchievements(player);\n        checkQuests(player);\n        if (reward.getBoolean(\"broadcast\", false)) {\n            Bukkit.broadcastMessage(color(prefix() + \"&6\" + player.getName() + \" &7hat aus einer Lootbox &e\" + rewardName + \" &7gezogen!\"));\n        }\n        saveData();\n    }\n\n    private List<String> wrapStory(String story) {\n        List<String> lines = new ArrayList<>();\n        if (story == null || story.isBlank()) {\n            return List.of(\"Die Nachricht ist verblasst.\");\n        }\n        StringBuilder line = new StringBuilder();\n        for (String word : story.split(\" \")) {\n            if (line.length() + word.length() > 48) {\n                if (!line.isEmpty()) {\n                    lines.add(line.toString());\n                }\n                line = new StringBuilder();\n            }\n            if (!line.isEmpty()) {\n                line.append(' ');\n            }\n            line.append(word);\n        }\n        if (!line.isEmpty()) {\n            lines.add(line.toString());\n        }\n        return lines;\n    }\n\n    private void sendBottleStory(Player player, String story) {\n        player.sendMessage(color(\"&8&m--------------------------------\"));\n        player.sendMessage(color(\"&b&lFlaschenpost &8| &7Eine alte Nachricht\"));\n        player.sendMessage(color(\"&8&m--------------------------------\"));\n        for (String line : wrapStory(story)) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(\"&f\" + line));\n        }\n        player.sendMessage(color(\"&8&m--------------------------------\"));\n        player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.ITEM_BOOK_PAGE_TURN, 0.8f, 1.1f);\n    }\n\n    private void consumeOneHeldItem(Player player, ItemStack clicked) {\n        ItemStack hand = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();\n        if (hand.isSimilar(clicked)) {\n            hand.setAmount(hand.getAmount() - 1);\n            return;\n        }\n        ItemStack offhand = player.getInventory().getItemInOffHand();\n        if (offhand.isSimilar(clicked)) {\n            offhand.setAmount(offhand.getAmount() - 1);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private String activeBaitLine(UUID uuid) {\n        String baitId = data.getString(\"players.\" + uuid + \".active-bait.id\", \"\");\n        int uses = getActiveBaitUses(uuid);\n        if (baitId == null || baitId.isBlank() || uses <= 0) {\n            return \"keiner\";\n        }\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"shop.items.\" + baitId);\n        String name = section == null ? prettifyKey(baitId) : ChatColor.stripColor(color(section.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(baitId))));\n        return name + \" (\" + uses + \" Fänge)\";\n    }\n\n    private int getActiveBaitUses(UUID uuid) {\n        return Math.max(0, data.getInt(\"players.\" + uuid + \".active-bait.uses\", 0));\n    }\n\n    private void openMainGui(Player player) {\n        sendGuideHintOnce(player);\n        Inventory Material.HEART_OF_THE_SEA : unlocked ? spotIcon(spot.id()) : Material.GRAY_DYE;\n            List<String> lore = new ArrayList<>();\n            lore.add(current ? \"&aAktuell aktiv\" : unlocked ? multiplierPercent(spot.jackpotMultiplier()));\n            lore.add(\"&7Mystery: &d+\" + spot.mysteryBonus() + \"%\");\n            lore.add(\"&7Größenbonus: &b+\" + spot.sizeBonus() + \"cm\");\n            lore.add(\"&8Fischarten:\");\n            for (String species : spot.species()) {\n                lore.add(\"&7- &f\" + species);\n            }\n            inventory.setItem(slots[i], item(icon, (current ? \"&a&l\" : unlocked ? \"&b\" : \"&7\") + spot.name(), lore.toArray(String[]::new)));\n        }\n        inventory.setItem(40, item(Material.CLOCK, \"&eZeit & Wetter\",\n                player.getWorld().hasStorm() ? \"&7Wetter: &bRegenbonus aktiv\" : \"&7Wetter: &fNormal\",\n                player.getWorld().getTime() >= 13000L ? \"&7Zeit: &dNachtbonus aktiv\" : \"&7Zeit: &eTag\",\n                \"&7Diese Boni wirken auf Mystery-Drops.\"));\n        inventory.setItem(49, item(Material.ARROW, \"&eZurück\"));\n        player.openInventory(inventory);\n    }\n\n    private List<FishingSpot> configuredFishingSpots() {\n        List<FishingSpot> spots = new ArrayList<>();\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.spots.list\");\n        if (section != null) {\n            for (String key : section.getKeys(false)) {\n                ConfigurationSection spot = section.getConfigurationSection(key);\n                if (spot != null && spot.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true)) {\n                    spots.add(configuredSpot(key, spot));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        if (spots.isEmpty()) {\n            spots.add(fallbackSpot(\"dorfteich\"));\n            spots.add(fallbackSpot(\"fluss\"));\n            spots.add(fallbackSpot(\"gebirgssee\"));\n            spots.add(fallbackSpot(\"tropenmeer\"));\n            spots.add(fallbackSpot(\"tiefsee\"));\n        }\n        return spots;\n    }\n\n    private Material spotIcon(String id) {\n        return switch (id.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)) {\n            case \"fluss\" -> Material.SALMON;\n            case \"gebirgssee\" -> Material.PRISMARINE_CRYSTALS;\n            case \"tropenmeer\" -> Material.TROPICAL_FISH;\n            case \"tiefsee\" -> Material.HEART_OF_THE_SEA;\n            default -> Material.COD;\n        };\n    }\n\n    private String multiplierPercent(double multiplier) {\n        int percent = (int) Math.round(multiplier * 100.0);\n        return percent + \"%\";\n    }\n\n    private void sendGuideHintOnce(Player player) {\n        String path = \"players.\" + player.getUniqueId() + \".seen-guide-hint\";\n        if (data.getBoolean(path, false)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        data.set(path, true);\n        saveData();\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&eNeu beim Angeln? &7Öffne &f/angeln guide &7oder klicke im Menü auf &eHilfe & Guide&7.\"));\n    }\n\n   inventory.setItem(22, item(Material.AMETHYST_SHARD, \"&cRubine\", \"&f\" + stat(player, \"tokens\")));\n        inventory.setItem(23, item(Material.EXPERIENCE_BOTTLE, \"&aAngel-Level\", \"&7Level: getLevelXp(player.getUniqueId()), \"&7Fang-XP: &f\" + stat(player, \"xp\")));\n        inventory.setItem(24, item(Material.NAUTILUS_SHELL, \"&dMystery Drops\", \"&f\" + stat(player, \"mystery\")));\n        inventory.setItem(29, item(Material.HEART_OF_THE_SEA, \"&6Seltene Fänge\", \"&f\" + stat(player, \"rare\")));\n        inventory.setItem(30, item(Material.LEATHER_BOOTS, \"&7Schrott\", \"&f\" + stat(player, \"category.junk\")));\n        inventory.setItem(31, item(Material.CHEST, \"&6Schätze\", \"&f\" + stat(player, \"category.treasure\")));\n        inventory.setItem(32, item(Material.NETHER_STAR, \"&dJackpots\", \"&f\" + stat(player, \"category.jackpot\")));\n        inventory.setItem(33, item(Material.PRISMARINE_SHARD, \"&3Begegnungen\", \"&f\" + stat(player, \"category.encounter\")));\n        inventory.setItem(40, item(Material.NAME_TAG, \"&dBesondere Momente\", \"&7Rekordgröße: &b\" + stat(player, \"best-size\") + \"cm\", \"&7Legendär: &6\" + stat(player, \"quality.legendär\"), \"&7Gigantisch: &d\" + stat(player, \"quality.gigantisch\"), \"&7Streak: &a\" + stat(player, \"streak.current\")));\n        inventory.setItem(42, item(Material.ENDER_CHEST, \"&dLootbox & Pity\", \"&7Lootboxen geöffnet: &f\" + stat(player, \"lootboxes-opened\"), \"&7Mystery-Pechsträhne: &f\" + data.getInt(\"players.\" + player.getUniqueId() + \".mystery-dry-streak\", 0), \"&7Garantiert nach: &e\" + getConfig().getInt(\"fishing.pity.guaranteed-after\", 65)));\n        inventory.setItem(49, item(Material.ARROW, \"&eZurück\"));\n        inventory.setItem(41, item(Material.AMETHYST_SHARD, \"&bPraezisions-Fokus\",\n                \"&7Aktuell: &b\" + precisionFocus(player) + \"&8/&b\" + getConfig().getInt(\"fishing.focus.max-points\", 100),\n                \"&7Bestwert: &f\" + data.getInt(\"players.\" + player.getUniqueId() + \".precision.best\", 0),\n                \"&7Perfekte Anhiebe: &a\" + stat(player, \"realistic.perfect\"),\n                \"&7Gute Anhiebe: &e\" + stat(player, \"realistic.good\"),\n                \"&8Perfekte Anhiebe geben mehr Fokus.\"));\n        player.openInventory(inventory);\n    }\n\n    private void openCollectionGui(Player player) {\n        openCollectionGui(player, 0);\n    }\n\n    private void openCollectionGui(Player player, int page) {\n        Map<Integer, String> actions = new HashMap<>();\n        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(new FishingGuiHolder(\"collection\", actions), 54, color(getConfig().getString(\"gui.titles.collection\", \"&bCollection\")));\n        fillBorder(inventory);\n        ConfigurationSection collection = data.getConfigurationSection(\"players.\" + player.getUniqueId() + \".collection\");\n        List<CollectionEntry> entries = collectionCatalog();\n        int discovered = discoveredCollectionCount(player.getUniqueId());\n        int target = collectionTarget();\n        inventory.setItem(4, item(Material.BOOK, \"&b&lCollection\", \"&7Entdeckt: &e\" + discovered + \"&8/&e\" + target, \"&7Fortschritt: &a\" + percent(discovered, Math.max(1, target)), \"&7Unentdeckte Items erscheinen als &f???&7.\"));\n        List<Integer> slots = contentSlots();\n        int totalPages = totalPages(entries.size());\n        int currentPage = clampPage(page, totalPages);\n        int start = currentPage * slots.size();\n        for (int i = 0; i < slots.size() && start + i < entries.size(); i++) {\n            CollectionEntry catalog = entries.get(start + i);\n            ConfigurationSection entry = collection == null ? null : collection.getConfigurationSection(catalog.id());\n            boolean found = entry != null;\n            if (found) {\n                inventory.setItem(slots.get(i), item(catalog.icon(),\n                        \"&a\" + entry.getString(\"name\", catalog.name()),\n                        \"&7Kategorie: &e\" + entry.getString(\"category\", catalog.category()),\n                        \"&7Seltenheit: &d\" + entry.getString(\"rarity\", catalog.rarity()),\n                        \"&7Gefangen: &b\" + entry.getInt(\"count\", 0),\n                        \"&7Erstfang: &f\" + formatDate(entry.getLong(\"first-catch\", 0L)),\n                        \"&7Rekord: &a\" + entry.getInt(\"record-size\", 0) + \"cm\",\n                        \"&7Marktwert: &e\" + entry.getInt(\"market-value\", catalog.marketValue())));\n            } else {\n                inventory.setItem(slots.get(i), item(Material.GRAY_DYE,\n                        \"&7???\",\n                        \"&7Kategorie: &e\" + catalog.category(),\n                        \"&7Seltenheit: &d\" + catalog.rarity(),\n                        \"&8Noch nicht entdeckt.\"));\n            }\n        }\n        addPageControls(inventory, actions, currentPage, totalPages);\n        player.openInventory(inventory);\n    }\n\n    private void openPassGui(Player player) {\n        openPassGui(player, 0);\n    }\n\n    private void openPassGui(Player player, int page) {\n        Map<Integer, String> actions = new HashMap<>();\n        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(new FishingGuiHolder(\"pass\", actions), 54, color(getConfig().getString(\"gui.titles.pass\", \"&6Angelpass\")));\n        fillBorder(inventory, Material.CYAN_STAINED_GLASS_PANE);\n        int level = getFishingLevel(player.getUniqueId());\n        ConfigurationSection rewards = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.pass.rewards\");\n        List<String> keys = rewards == null ? List.of() : rewards.getKeys(false).stream()\n                .sorted(Comparator.comparingInt(key -> parseInt(key, 0)))\n                .toList();\n        int[] slots = {10, 11, 12, 13, 14, 15, 16, 28, 29, 30, 31, 32, 33, 34};\n        int[] trackSlots = {19, 20, 21, 22, 23, 24, 25};\n        int totalPages = Math.max(1, (int) Math.ceil(keys.size() / (double) slots.length));\n        int currentPage = clampPage(page, totalPages);\n        int start = currentPage * slots.length;\n        inventory.setItem(4, item(Material.EXPERIENCE_BOTTLE, \"&6&lAngelpass\",\n                \"&7Angel-Level: &e\" + level,\n                \"&7XP: &b\" + getCurrentLevelXp(player.getUniqueId()) + \"&8/&b\" + xpForNextLevel(level),\n                \"&7Offene Rewards: &a\" + availableFishingPassRewards(player),\n                \"&7Seite: &f\" + (currentPage + 1) + \"&8/&f\" + totalPages,\n                \"&8Free Track, einmal pro Level.\",\n                \"&8Grün = abgeholt, Glanz = bereit.\"));\n        for (int i = 0; i < trackSlots.length; i++) {\n            int visualLevel = start + i < keys.size() ? parseInt(keys.get(start + i), 0) : -1;\n            Material track = visualLevel > 0 && level >= visualLevel ? Material.LIME_STAINED_GLASS_PANE : Material.GRAY_STAINED_GLASS_PANE;\n            inventory.setItem(trackSlots[i], item(track, \" \"));\n        }\n        if (rewards != null) {\n            for (int i = 0; i < slots.length && start + i < keys.size(); i++) {\n                String key = keys.get(start + i);\n                int rewardLevel = parseInt(key, 0);\n                boolean claimed = data.getBoolean(\"players.\" + player.getUniqueId() + \".pass.claimed.\" + key, false);\n                boolean unlocked = level >= rewardLevel;\n                Material icon = claimed ? Material.LIME_CONCRETE : unlocked ? passRewardIcon(rewards.getConfigurationSection(key)) : Material.GRAY_DYE;\n                ItemStack rewardItem = item(icon, (claimed ? \"&a\" : unlocked ? \"&6\" : \"&7\") + \"Angelpass &8| &fLevel \" + key,\n                        fishingPassRewardDescription(rewards.getConfigurationSection(key)),\n                        claimed ? \"&aBereits abgeholt\" : unlocked ? \"&eClick: Belohnung abholen\" : \"&7Gesperrt bis Level \" + key,\n                        \"&8Free Track\");\n                if (unlocked && !claimed) {\n                    addGlow(rewardItem);\n                }\n                inventory.setItem(slots[i], rewardItem);\n                actions.put(slots[i], \"claim-pass:\" + key);\n            }\n        }\n        inventory.setItem(40, item(Material.WRITABLE_BOOK, \"&bSo funktioniert der Angelpass\",\n                \"&7Fange Fische und seltene Drops,\",\n                \"&7um Angel-XP und Level zu erhalten.\",\n                \"&7Jede Belohnung wird nur einmal vergeben.\"));\n        inventory.setItem(42, item(Material.CHEST_MINECART, \"&aAlle offenen abholen\",\n                \"&7Holt alle freigeschalteten\",\n                \"&7und noch offenen Rewards.\",\n                \"&7Offen: &a\" + availableFishingPassRewards(player),\n                \"&aClick\"));\n        actions.put(42, \"claim-pass-all:\" + currentPage);\n        if (currentPage > 0) {\n            inventory.setItem(48, item(Material.ARROW, \"&eVorherige Seite\"));\n            actions.put(48, \"page:\" + (currentPage - 1));\n        }\n        inventory.setItem(49, item(Material.OAK_DOOR, \"&eZurück\"));\n        if (currentPage + 1 < totalPages) {\n            inventory.setItem(50, item(Material.ARROW, \"&eNächste Seite\"));\n            actions.put(50, \"page:\" + (currentPage + 1));\n        }\n        player.openInventory(inventory);\n    }\n\n    private int availableFishingPassRewards(Player player) {\n        int level = getFishingLevel(player.getUniqueId());\n        ConfigurationSection rewards = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.pass.rewards\");\n        if (rewards == null) {\n            return 0;\n        }\n        int count = 0;\n        for (String key : rewards.getKeys(false)) {\n            int rewardLevel = parseInt(key, 0);\n            boolean claimed = data.getBoolean(\"players.\" + player.getUniqueId() + \".pass.claimed.\" + key, false);\n            if (rewardLevel > 0 && level >= rewardLevel && !claimed && !getConfig().getStringList(\"fishing.pass.rewards.\" + key + \".commands\").isEmpty()) {\n                count++;\n            }\n        }\n        return count;\n    }\n\n    private Material passRewardIcon(ConfigurationSection reward) {\n        if (reward == null) {\n            return Material.GOLD_INGOT;\n        }\n        String joined = String.join(\" \", reward.getStringList(\"commands\")).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n        if (joined.contains(\"rubies\")) {\n            return Material.AMETHYST_SHARD;\n        }\n        if (joined.contains(\"spawner\")) {\n            return Material.SPAWNER;\n        }\n        if (joined.contains(\"beacon\")) {\n            return Material.BEACON;\n        }\n        if (joined.contains(\"experience_bottle\")) {\n            return Material.EXPERIENCE_BOTTLE;\n        }\n        if (joined.contains(\"chest\")) {\n            return Material.CHEST;\n        }\n        return Material.GOLD_INGOT;\n    }\n\n    private String fishingPassRewardDescription(ConfigurationSection reward) {\n        if (reward == null) {\n            return \"&7Keine Belohnung konfiguriert.\";\n        }\n        List<String> descriptions = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (String command : reward.getStringList(\"commands\")) {\n            String[] parts = command.trim().split(\"\\\\s+\");\n            if (parts.length >= 4 && \"smpeconomy\".equalsIgnoreCase(parts[0]) && \"give\".equalsIgnoreCase(parts[1])) {\n                descriptions.add(\"&7Reward: &a\" + parts[3] + \"$\");\n            } else if (parts.length >= 4 && \"rubies\".equalsIgnoreCase(parts[0]) && \"give\".equalsIgnoreCase(parts[1])) {\n                descriptions.add(\"&7Reward: &c\" + parts[3] + \" Rubine\");\n            } else if (parts.length >= 4 && \"give\".equalsIgnoreCase(parts[0])) {\n                descriptions.add(\"&7Reward: &f\" + parts[3] + \"x \" + prettifyKey(parts[2]));\n            }\n        }\n        return descriptions.isEmpty() ? \"&7Konfigurierte Spezialbelohnung\" : String.join(\" &8+ \", descriptions);\n    }\n\n    private void claimFishingPassReward(Player player, int level) {\n        if (getFishingLevel(player.getUniqueId()) < level) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDiese Angelpass-Belohnung ist noch gesperrt.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        String path = \"players.\" + player.getUniqueId() + \".pass.claimed.\" + level;\n        if (data.getBoolean(path, false)) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&eDiese Angelpass-Belohnung wurde bereits abgeholt.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        List<String> commands = getConfig().getStringList(\"fishing.pass.rewards.\" + level + \".commands\");\n        if (commands.isEmpty()) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cFür dieses Level ist keine Belohnung hinterlegt.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        for (String command : commands) {\n            Bukkit.dispatchCommand(Bukkit.getConsoleSender(), replaceRandomRanges(command)\n                    .replace(\"{player}\", player.getName())\n                    .replace(\"{uuid}\", player.getUniqueId().toString())\n                    .replace(\"{level}\", String.valueOf(level)));\n        }\n        data.set(path, true);\n        saveData();\n        player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.UI_TOAST_CHALLENGE_COMPLETE, 0.9f, 1.1f);\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&aAngelpass-Belohnung für Level &e\" + level + \" &aerhalten.\"));\n    }\n\n    private void claimAvailableFishingPassRewards(Player player) {\n        ConfigurationSection rewards = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.pass.rewards\");\n        if (rewards == null) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDer Angelpass hat aktuell keine Belohnungen.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        int level = getFishingLevel(player.getUniqueId());\n        int claimedCount = 0;\n        List<String> keys = rewards.getKeys(false).stream()\n                .sorted(Comparator.comparingInt(key -> parseInt(key, 0)))\n                .toList();\n        for (String key : keys) {\n            int rewardLevel = parseInt(key, 0);\n            String path = \"players.\" + player.getUniqueId() + \".pass.claimed.\" + key;\n            List<String> commands = getConfig().getStringList(\"fishing.pass.rewards.\" + key + \".commands\");\n            if (rewardLevel <= 0 || level < rewardLevel || data.getBoolean(path, false) || commands.isEmpty()) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            for (String command : commands) {\n                Bukkit.dispatchCommand(Bukkit.getConsoleSender(), replaceRandomRanges(command)\n                        .replace(\"{player}\", player.getName())\n                        .replace(\"{uuid}\", player.getUniqueId().toString())\n                        .replace(\"{level}\", String.valueOf(rewardLevel)));\n            }\n            data.set(path, true);\n            claimedCount++;\n        }\n        if (claimedCount <= 0) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&eKeine offenen Angelpass-Belohnungen verfügbar.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        saveData();\n        player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.UI_TOAST_CHALLENGE_COMPLETE, 0.9f, 1.25f);\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&a\" + claimedCount + \" Angelpass-Belohnung(en) abgeholt.\"));\n    }\n\n    private void openQuestGui(Player player) {\n        openQuestGui(player, 0);\n    }\n\n    private void openQuestGui(Player player, int page) {\n        ensureActiveQuests(player);\n        Map<Integer, String> actions = new HashMap<>();\n        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(new FishingGuiHolder(\"quests\", actions), 54, color(getConfig().getString(\"gui.titles.quests\", \"&eAngelquests\")));\n        fillBorder(inventory);\n        inventory.setItem(4, item(Material.WRITABLE_BOOK, \"&e&lAngelquests\", \"&7Daily: &f\" + questPeriod(\"daily\"), \"&7Weekly: &f\" + questPeriod(\"weekly\"), \"&7Belohnungen werden automatisch\", \"&7bei Abschluss ausgezahlt.\"));\n        List<QuestView> quests = activeQuestViews(player);\n        List<Integer> slots = contentSlots();\n        int totalPages = totalPages(quests.size());\n        int currentPage = clampPage(page, totalPages);\n        int start = currentPage * slots.size();\n        for (int i = 0; i < slots.size() && start + i < quests.size(); i++) {\n            QuestView quest = quests.get(start + i);\n            Material icon = quest.claimed() ? Material.LIME_DYE : quest.progress() >= quest.amount() ? Material.GOLD_INGOT : (\"weekly\".equals(quest.type()) ? Material.MAP : Material.PAPER);\n            inventory.setItem(slots.get(i), item(icon,\n                    (\"weekly\".equals(quest.type()) ? \"&6Weekly &8| \" : \"&eDaily &8| \") + quest.name(),\n                    \"&7Fortschritt: &f\" + Math.min(quest.progress(), quest.amount()) + \"&8/&f\" + quest.amount(),\n                    quest.claimed() ? \"&aAbgeschlossen\" : quest.progress() >= quest.amount() ? \"&6Wird ausgezahlt...\" : \"&7Noch offen\",\n                    \"&8\" + quest.description()));\n        }\n        addPageControls(inventory, actions, currentPage, totalPages);\n        player.openInventory(inventory);\n    }\n\n    private void openLootboxGui(Player player) {\n        Map<Integer, String> actions = new HashMap<>();\n        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(new FishingGuiHolder(\"lootboxes\", actions), 54, color(getConfig().getString(\"gui.titles.lootboxes\", \"&dFishing Lootboxen\")));\n        fillBorder(inventory);\n        inventory.setItem(4, item(Material.ENDER_CHEST, \"&d&lFishing Lootboxen\", \"&7Übersicht aller eigenen\", \"&7Fishing-Lootboxen und Rewards.\", \"&7Geöffnet: &f\" + stat(player, \"lootboxes-opened\")));\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.lootboxes\");\n        int slot = 10;\n        if (section != null) {\n            for (String key : section.getKeys(false)) {\n                ConfigurationSection lootbox = section.getConfigurationSection(key);\n                if (lootbox == null || !lootbox.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true) || isRetiredFishingLootbox(key, lootbox)) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                ConfigurationSection rewards = lootbox.getConfigurationSection(\"rewards\");\n                int rewardCount = rewards == null ? 0 : rewards.getKeys(false).size();\n                inventory.setItem(slot, item(Material.CHEST, lootbox.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(key)),\n                        \"&7ID: &f\" + key,\n                        \"&7Rewards: &e\" + rewardCount,\n                        \"&7Partikel: &f\" + lootbox.getString(\"particle\", \"END_ROD\"),\n                        \"&aClick: Vorschau\"));\n                actions.put(slot, key);\n                slot = nextContentSlot(slot);\n                if (slot >= 44) {\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        inventory.setItem(49, item(Material.ARROW, \"&eZurück\"));\n        player.openInventory(inventory);\n    }\n\n    private void openLootboxPreviewGui(Player player, String lootboxId) {\n        openLootboxPreviewGui(player, lootboxId, 0);\n    }\n\n    private void openLootboxPreviewGui(Player player, String lootboxId, int page) {\n        ConfigurationSection lootbox = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.lootboxes.\" + lootboxId);\n        if (lootbox == null) {\n            openLootboxGui(player);\n            return;\n        }\n        Map<Integer, String> actions = new HashMap<>();\n        actions.put(-1, lootboxId);\n        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(new FishingGuiHolder(\"lootbox-preview\", actions), 54, color(lootbox.getString(\"name\", \"&dLootbox\")));\n        fillBorder(inventory);\n        inventory.setItem(4, item(Material.ENDER_CHEST, lootbox.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(lootboxId)), \"&7ID: &f\" + lootboxId, \"&7Rechtsklick auf eine gezogene Box\", \"&7öffnet sie mit Animation.\"));\n        ConfigurationSection rewards = lootbox.getConfigurationSection(\"rewards\");\n        List<String> keys = rewards == null ? List.of() : rewards.getKeys(false).stream().sorted(String.CASE_INSENSITIVE_ORDER).toList();\n        List<Integer> slots = contentSlots();\n        int totalPages = totalPages(keys.size());\n        int currentPage = clampPage(page, totalPages);\n        int start = currentPage * slots.size();\n        if (rewards != null) {\n            for (int i = 0; i < slots.size() && start + i < keys.size(); i++) {\n                String key = keys.get(start + i);\n                ConfigurationSection reward = rewards.getConfigurationSection(key);\n                if (reward == null || !reward.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true)) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                Material material = Material.matchMaterial(reward.getString(\"material\", \"GOLD_INGOT\"));\n                if (material == null || material.isAir()) {\n                    material = reward.getInt(\"tokens-max\", 0) > 0 ? Material.AMETHYST_SHARD : Material.GOLD_INGOT;\n                }\n                List<String> lore = new ArrayList<>();\n                lore.add(\"&7Gewicht: &f\" + reward.getDouble(\"weight\", 0.0));\n                if (reward.getInt(\"coins-max\", reward.getInt(\"coins\", 0)) > 0) {\n                    lore.add(\"&7$: &e\" + range(reward, \"coins\"));\n                }\n                if (reward.getInt(\"tokens-max\", reward.getInt(\"tokens\", 0)) > 0) {\n                    lore.add(\"&7Rubine: &c\" + range(reward, \"tokens\"));\n                }\n                if (!reward.getStringList(\"enchantments\").isEmpty()) {\n                    lore.add(\"&dVerzaubert\");\n                }\n                if (reward.getBoolean(\"broadcast\", false)) {\n                    lore.add(\"&6Broadcast\");\n                }\n                inventory.setItem(slots.get(i), item(material, reward.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(key)), lore.toArray(String[]::new)));\n            }\n        }\n        addPageControls(inventory, actions, currentPage, totalPages);\n        player.openInventory(inventory);\n    }\n\n    private void openBossGui(Player player) {\n        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(new FishingGuiHolder(\"bosses\", Map.of()), 54, color(getConfig().getString(\"gui.titles.bosses\", \"&4Angelbosse\")));\n        fillBorder(inventory);\n        List<BossDefinition> bosses = bossDefinitions();\n        inventory.setItem(4, item(Material.DRAGON_HEAD, \"&4&lAngelbosse\", \"&7Besiegt gesamt: &f\" + stat(player, \"boss-kills\"), \"&7Konfigurierte Bosse: &e\" + bosses.size()));\n        int[] slots = {10, 11, 12, 13, 14, 15, 16, 19, 20, 21, 22, 23, 24, 25, 28, 29, 30, 31, 32, 33, 34};\n        for (int i = 0; i < Math.min(slots.length, bosses.size()); i++) {\n            BossDefinition boss = bosses.get(i);\n            ConfigurationSection section = boss.section();\n            Material icon = Material.matchMaterial(section.getString(\"icon\", \"DRAGON_HEAD\"));\n            if (icon == null || icon.isAir()) {\n                icon = Material.DRAGON_HEAD;\n            }\n            inventory.setItem(slots[i], item(icon,\n                    section.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(boss.id())),\n                    \"&7ID: &f\" + boss.id(),\n                    \"&7Realm: &b\" + boss.realm(),\n                    \"&7Entity: &f\" + section.getString(\"entity\", \"DROWNED\"),\n                    \"&7HP: &c\" + section.getInt(\"health\", 80),\n                    \"&7Gewicht: &e\" + section.getDouble(\"weight\", 0.0),\n                    \"&7Besiegt: &a\" + stat(player, \"boss.\" + boss.id() + \".kills\"),\n                    \"&8Admin: /angeln spawnboss \" + boss.id()));\n        }\n        inventory.setItem(49, item(Material.ARROW, \"&eZurück\"));\n        player.openInventory(inventory);\n    }\n\n    private void openAchievementsGui(Player player) {\n        openAchievementsGui(player, 0);\n    }\n\n    private void openAchievementsGui(Player player, int page) {\n        Map<Integer, String> actions = new HashMap<>();\n        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(new FishingGuiHolder(\"achievements\", actions), 54, color(getConfig().getString(\"gui.titles.achievements\", \"&eAngelerfolge\")));\n        fillBorder(inventory);\n        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();\n        inventory.setItem(4, item(Material.TOTEM_OF_UNDYING, \"&e&lAngelerfolge\", \"&7Abgeschlossen: &a\" + completedAchievements(uuid) + \"&8/&f\" + configuredAchievementCount()));\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.achievements\");\n        List<String> keys = section == null ? List.of() : section.getKeys(false).stream()\n                .filter(key -> {\n                    ConfigurationSection achievement = section.getConfigurationSection(key);\n                    return achievement != null && achievement.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true) && !isRetiredAchievement(key, achievement);\n                })\n                .sorted(String.CASE_INSENSITIVE_ORDER)\n                .toList();\n        List<Integer> slots = contentSlots();\n        int totalPages = totalPages(keys.size());\n        int currentPage = clampPage(page, totalPages);\n        int start = currentPage * slots.size();\n        if (section != null) {\n            for (int i = 0; i < slots.size() && start + i < keys.size(); i++) {\n                String key = keys.get(start + i);\n                ConfigurationSection achievement = section.getConfigurationSection(key);\n                if (achievement == null || !achievement.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true) || isRetiredAchievement(key, achievement)) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                boolean done = data.getBoolean(\"players.\" + uuid + \".achievements.\" + key, false);\n                String stat = achievement.getString(\"stat\", \"catches\");\n                int needed = achievement.getInt(\"amount\", 1);\n                int current = achievementProgress(uuid, stat);\n                Material icon = Material.matchMaterial(achievement.getString(\"icon\", done ? \"LIME_DYE\" : \"GRAY_DYE\"));\n                if (icon == null || icon.isAir()) {\n                    icon = done ? Material.LIME_DYE : Material.GRAY_DYE;\n                }\n                inventory.setItem(slots.get(i), item(icon,\n                        (done ? \"&a\" : \"&e\") + achievement.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(key)),\n                        \"&7Fortschritt: &f\" + Math.min(current, needed) + \"&8/&f\" + needed,\n                        done ? \"&aAbgeschlossen\" : \"&7Noch offen\",\n                        \"&8\" + achievement.getString(\"description\", \"\")));\n            }\n        }\n        addPageControls(inventory, actions, currentPage, totalPages);\n        player.openInventory(inventory);\n    }\n\n    private void openDropsGui(Player player) {\n        openDropsCategoryGui(player, \"all\", 0);\n    }\n\n    private void openDropsGui(Player player, int page) {\n        openDropsCategoryGui(player, \"all\", page);\n    }\n\n    private void openDropsCategoryGui(Player player, String category, int page) {\n        Map<Integer, String> actions = new HashMap<>();\n        actions.put(-1, category);\n        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(new FishingGuiHolder(\"drop-items\", actions), 54, color(getConfig().getString(\"gui.titles.drops\", \"&dMystery Drops\") + color(\" &8| \" + dropCategoryName(category))));\n        fillBorder(inventory, Material.BLACK_STAINED_GLASS_PANE);\n        inventory.setItem(4, item(dropCategoryIcon(category), \"&d&l\" + dropCategoryName(category), \"&7Chance pro Fang: &f\" + getConfig().getDouble(\"fishing.mystery-drops.chance\", 18.0) + \"%\", \"&7Kategorie: &f\" + dropCategoryName(category)));\n        List<DropView> drops = dropViews(category);\n        List<Integer> slots = contentSlots();\n        int totalPages = totalPages(drops.size());\n        int currentPage = clampPage(page, totalPages);\n        int start = currentPage * slots.size();\n        for (int i = 0; i < slots.size() && start + i < drops.size(); i++) {\n            DropView view = drops.get(start + i);\n            ConfigurationSection drop = view.section();\n            Material material = Material.matchMaterial(drop.getString(\"material\", drop.getString(\"icon\", \"CHEST\")));\n            if (material == null || material.isAir()) {\n                material = Material.CHEST;\n            }\n            inventory.setItem(slots.get(i), item(material,\n                    drop.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(view.key())),\n                    \"&7Welt: &b\" + dropCategoryName(view.category()),\n                    \"&7Kategorie: &f\" + drop.getString(\"category\", \"mystery\"),\n                    \"&7Gewicht: &f\" + drop.getDouble(\"weight\", 0.0),\n                    \"&7$: &e\" + range(drop, \"coins\"),\n                    \"&7Rubine: &c\" + range(drop, \"tokens\"),\n                    drop.getBoolean(\"broadcast\", false) ? \"&6Broadcast\" : \"&8ID: \" + view.key()));\n        }\n        addPageControls(inventory, actions, currentPage, totalPages);\n        inventory.setItem(49, item(Material.ARROW, \"&eZurück\"));\n        player.openInventory(inventory);\n    }\n\n    private void openTopGui(Player player) {\n        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(new FishingGuiHolder(\"top\", Map.of()), 54, color(getConfig().getString(\"gui.titles.top\", \"&6Angelrangliste\")));\n        fillBorder(inventory);\n        List<PlayerStat> stats = sortedStats();\n        inventory.setItem(4, item(Material.GOLD_BLOCK, \"&6&lTop Angler\", \"&7Sortiert nach Fängen\"));\n        int[] slots = {19, 20, 21, 22, 23, 24, 25, 28, 29, 30};\n        for (int i = 0; i < Math.min(slots.length, stats.size()); i++) {\n            PlayerStat stat = stats.get(i);\n            inventory.setItem(slots[i], item(i == 0 ? Material.NETHER_STAR : Material.FISHING_ROD, \"&e#\" + (i + 1) + \" &f\" + stat.name(), \"&7Fänge: &b\" + stat.catches(), \"&7Mystery: &d\" + stat.mystery(), \"&7Rubine: &c\" + stat.tokens()));\n        }\n        inventory.setItem(49, item(Material.ARROW, \"&eZurück\"));\n        player.openInventory(inventory);\n    }\n\n    private boolean isDropCategory(String category) {\n        return \"mystery\".equalsIgnoreCase(category)\n                || \"overworld\".equalsIgnoreCase(category)\n                || \"all\".equalsIgnoreCase(category);\n    }\n\n    private List<DropView> dropViews(String category) {\n        List<DropView> result = new ArrayList<>();\n        if (\"all\".equalsIgnoreCase(category) || \"mystery\".equalsIgnoreCase(category)) {\n            collectDropViews(result, \"mystery\", \"fishing.mystery-drops.drops\");\n        }\n        if (\"all\".equalsIgnoreCase(category) || \"overworld\".equalsIgnoreCase(category)) {\n            collectDropViews(result, \"overworld\", \"fishing.realm-drops.overworld\");\n        }\n        result.sort(Comparator.comparing(DropView::category, String.CASE_INSENSITIVE_ORDER)\n                .thenComparing((DropView view) -> view.section().getString(\"category\", \"mystery\"), String.CASE_INSENSITIVE_ORDER)\n                .thenComparing(DropView::key, String.CASE_INSENSITIVE_ORDER));\n        return result;\n    }\n\n    private void collectDropViews(List<DropView> result, String category, String path) {\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(path);\n        if (section == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        for (String key : section.getKeys(false)) {\n            ConfigurationSection drop = section.getConfigurationSection(key);\n            if (drop != null && drop.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true)) {\n                result.add(new DropView(category, key, drop));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private String dropCategoryName(String category) {\n        if (\"mystery\".equalsIgnoreCase(category)) {\n            return \"Allgemein\";\n        }\n        if (\"overworld\".equalsIgnoreCase(category)) {\n            return \"Overworld\";\n        }\n        if (\"lava\".equalsIgnoreCase(category)) {\n            return \"Lava\";\n        }\n        if (\"void\".equalsIgnoreCase(category)) {\n            return \"Void\";\n        }\n        return \"Alle Drops\";\n    }\n\n    private Material dropCategoryIcon(String category) {\n        if (\"mystery\".equalsIgnoreCase(category)) {\n            return Material.NAUTILUS_SHELL;\n        }\n        if (\"overworld\".equalsIgnoreCase(category)) {\n            return Material.GRASS_BLOCK;\n        }\n        if (\"lava\".equalsIgnoreCase(category)) {\n            return Material.LAVA_BUCKET;\n        }\n        if (\"void\".equalsIgnoreCase(category)) {\n            return Material.ENDER_EYE;\n        }\n        return Material.BOOK;\n    }\n\n    private void openShopGui(Player player) {\n        Map<Integer, String> actions = new HashMap<>();\n        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(new FishingGuiHolder(\"shop\", actions), 54, color(getConfig().getString(\"gui.titles.shop\", \"&aAngel-Shop\")));\n        fillBorder(inventory, Material.BLACK_STAINED_GLASS_PANE);\n        inventory.setItem(4, item(Material.EMERALD, \"&a&lAngel-Shop\", \"&7Käufe laufen ausschließlich\", \"&7über $.\", \"&7Nur aktive Wasser-Angelitems\", \"&7werden hier angezeigt.\"));\n        inventory.setItem(20, item(Material.FISHING_ROD, \"&6Angeln\", \"&7Upgrade-Ruten für\", \"&7normales Wasserangeln.\", \"&aClick\"));\n        actions.put(20, \"rods\");\n        inventory.setItem(22, item(Material.SLIME_BALL, \"&aKöder\", \"&7Normale Köder für bessere\", \"&7Fangchancen und Lootboxen.\", \"&aClick\"));\n        actions.put(22, \"bait\");\n        inventory.setItem(24, item(Material.NETHER_STAR, \"&dUtility\", \"&7Fischernetze, Lootboxen\", \"&7und besondere Angelitems.\", \"&aClick\"));\n        actions.put(24, \"special\");\n        inventory.setItem(31, item(Material.CHEST, \"&eAlles anzeigen\", \"&7Alle aktiven Shop-Items\", \"&7sauber sortiert nach Kategorie.\", \"&aClick\"));\n        actions.put(31, \"all\");\n        inventory.setItem(49, item(Material.ARROW, \"&eZurück\"));\n        player.openInventory(inventory);\n    }\n\n    private void openShopGui(Player player, int page) {\n        openShopCategoryGui(player, \"all\", page);\n    }\n\n    private void openShopCategoryGui(Player player, String category, int page) {\n        Map<Integer, String> actions = new HashMap<>();\n        actions.put(-1, category);\n        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(new FishingGuiHolder(\"shop-items\", actions), 54, color(getConfig().getString(\"gui.titles.shop\", \"&aAngel-Shop\") + color(\" &8| \" + shopCategoryName(category))));\n        fillBorder(inventory, Material.BLACK_STAINED_GLASS_PANE);\n        inventory.setItem(4, item(shopCategoryIcon(category), \"&a&l\" + shopCategoryName(category), \"&7Käufe werden ausschließlich\", \"&7über $ bezahlt.\", \"&7Kategorie: &f\" + shopCategoryName(category)));\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"shop.items\");\n        List<String> keys = section == null ? List.of() : section.getKeys(false).stream()\n                .filter(key -> {\n                    ConfigurationSection shopItem = section.getConfigurationSection(key);\n                    return shopItem != null && shopItem.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true) && !isRetiredSpecialFishingItem(key, shopItem) && matchesShopCategory(shopItem, category);\n                })\n                .sorted(Comparator.comparingInt((String key) -> shopSortWeight(section.getConfigurationSection(key))).thenComparing(String.CASE_INSENSITIVE_ORDER))\n                .toList();\n        List<Integer> slots = contentSlots();\n        int totalPages = totalPages(keys.size());\n        int currentPage = clampPage(page, totalPages);\n        int start = currentPage * slots.size();\n        if (section != null) {\n            for (int i = 0; i < slots.size() && start + i < keys.size(); i++) {\n                String key = keys.get(start + i);\n                ConfigurationSection shopItem = section.getConfigurationSection(key);\n                if (shopItem == null || !shopItem.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true)) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                Material material = Material.matchMaterial(shopItem.getString(\"material\", \"COD\"));\n                if (material == null || material.isAir()) {\n                    material = Material.COD;\n                }\n                int price = shopItem.getInt(\"price\", 0);\n                boolean bait = \"bait\".equalsIgnoreCase(shopItem.getString(\"type\", \"item\"));\n                int amount = bait ? 1 : shopItem.getInt(\"amount\", 1);\n                int slot = slots.get(i);\n                inventory.setItem(slot, item(material, shopItem.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(key)), \"&7Preis: &e$\" + price, bait ? \"&7Kauf: &f1 Köder\" : \"&7Menge: &f\" + amount, shopItem.getInt(\"uses\", 0) > 0 ? \"&7Nutzungen: &b\" + shopItem.getInt(\"uses\", 1) : \"&7Typ: &f\" + shopItem.getString(\"type\", \"item\"), \"&7Kategorie: &f\" + shopCategoryName(shopCategory(shopItem)), \"&aClick: kaufen\"));\n                actions.put(slot, key);\n            }\n        }\n        addPageControls(inventory, actions, currentPage, totalPages);\n        inventory.setItem(49, item(Material.ARROW, \"&eZurück\"));\n        player.openInventory(inventory);\n    }\n\n    private void openMarketGui(Player player) {\n        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(new FishingGuiHolder(\"market\", Map.of()), 54, color(getConfig().getString(\"gui.titles.market\", \"&6Angel-Markt\")));\n        fillBorder(inventory);\n        inventory.setItem(4, item(Material.GOLD_INGOT, \"&6&lAngel-Markt\", \"&7Klicke unten in deinem Inventar\", \"&7auf geangelte Items zum Verkaufen.\", \"&7Auszahlung: &e$\"));\n        inventory.setItem(22, item(Material.HOPPER, \"&aAlles Verkaufbare verkaufen\", \"&7Verkauft alle erkannten\", \"&7Angel-Items in deinem Inventar.\", \"&aClick\"));\n        inventory.setItem(29, item(Material.COD, \"&bNormale Fänge\", \"&7Fische, Muscheln, Schuppen\", \"&7und kleinere Funde haben\", \"&7Materialpreise aus der Config.\"));\n        inventory.setItem(31, item(Material.NETHER_STAR, \"&dMystery & Jackpot\", \"&7Custom Fishing Items tragen\", \"&7einen versteckten Marktwert.\"));\n        inventory.setItem(33, item(Material.BARREL, \"&eTipp\", \"&7Lege Items ins Inventar\", \"&7und klicke sie unten an.\"));\n        inventory.setItem(49, item(Material.ARROW, \"&eZurück\"));\n        player.openInventory(inventory);\n    }\n\n    private void buyShopItem(Player player, String shopId) {\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"shop.items.\" + shopId);\n        if (section == null || !section.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        long cooldown = getConfig().getLong(\"shop.purchase-cooldown-ms\", 750L);\n        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        long lastBuy = shopCooldowns.getOrDefault(player.getUniqueId(), 0L);\n        if (cooldown > 0L && now - lastBuy < cooldown) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&eBitte warte kurz, bevor du erneut kaufst.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        int price = Math.max(0, section.getInt(\"price\", 0));\n        if (price > 0 && getSmpMoney(player) < price) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDu hast nicht genug $. &7Benötigt: &e$\" + price));\n            return;\n        }\n        shopCooldowns.put(player.getUniqueId(), now);\n        String withdrawCommand = getConfig().getString(\"shop.withdraw-command\", \"\");\n        if (price > 0 && withdrawCommand != null && !withdrawCommand.isBlank()) {\n            Bukkit.dispatchCommand(Bukkit.getConsoleSender(), withdrawCommand\n                    .replace(\"{player}\", player.getName())\n                    .replace(\"{uuid}\", player.getUniqueId().toString())\n                    .replace(\"{price}\", String.valueOf(price)));\n        }\n        if (\"bait\".equalsIgnoreCase(section.getString(\"type\", \"item\"))) {\n            giveItem(player, createBaitItem(shopId, section));\n        } else if (\"rod\".equalsIgnoreCase(section.getString(\"type\", \"item\"))) {\n            giveItem(player, createRodItem(shopId, section));\n        } else {\n            Material material = Material.matchMaterial(section.getString(\"material\", \"COD\"));\n            if (material != null && !material.isAir()) {\n                giveItem(player, new ItemStack(material, Math.max(1, section.getInt(\"amount\", 1))));\n            }\n        }\n        for (String command : section.getStringList(\"commands\")) {\n            Bukkit.dispatchCommand(Bukkit.getConsoleSender(), replaceRandomRanges(command)\n                    .replace(\"{player}\", player.getName())\n                    .replace(\"{uuid}\", player.getUniqueId().toString())\n                    .replace(\"{price}\", String.valueOf(price)));\n        }\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&aGekauft: &f\" + ChatColor.stripColor(color(section.getString(\"name\", shopId))) + \" &7für &e$\" + price + \"&7.\"));\n        openShopGui(player);\n    }\n\n    private int getSmpMoney(Player player) {\n        File pluginsFolder = getDataFolder().getParentFile();\n        if (pluginsFolder == null) {\n            return 0;\n        }\n        File smpDataFile = new File(new File(pluginsFolder, \"SMPAddonSystem\"), \"data.yml\");\n        if (!smpDataFile.exists()) {\n            return 0;\n        }\n        FileConfiguration smpData = YamlConfiguration.loadConfiguration(smpDataFile);\n        return Math.max(0, smpData.getInt(\"players.\" + player.getUniqueId() + \".coins\", 0));\n    }\n\n    private boolean matchesShopCategory(ConfigurationSection item, String category) {\n        if (\"all\".equalsIgnoreCase(category)) {\n            return true;\n        }\n        return shopCategory(item).equalsIgnoreCase(category);\n    }\n\n    private boolean isRetiredSpecialFishingItem(String key, ConfigurationSection item) {\n        String searchable = (key + \" \"\n                + item.getString(\"name\", \"\") + \" \"\n                + item.getString(\"type\", \"\") + \" \"\n                + item.getString(\"category\", \"\") + \" \"\n                + item.getString(\"rod-type\", \"\")).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n        return searchable.contains(\"lava\") || searchable.contains(\"void\") || searchable.contains(\"boss\");\n    }\n\n    private boolean isRetiredFishingLootbox(String key, ConfigurationSection box) {\n        String searchable = (key + \" \"\n                + box.getString(\"name\", \"\") + \" \"\n                + String.join(\" \", box.getStringList(\"item-lore\"))).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n        return searchable.contains(\"lava\") || searchable.contains(\"void\") || searchable.contains(\"boss\");\n    }\n\n    private String shopCategory(ConfigurationSection item) {\n        String configured = item.getString(\"category\", \"\");\n        if (configured != null && !configured.isBlank()) {\n            return configured.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n        }\n        String type = item.getString(\"type\", \"item\");\n        if (\"rod\".equalsIgnoreCase(type)) {\n            return \"rods\";\n        }\n        if (\"bait\".equalsIgnoreCase(type)) {\n            return \"bait\";\n        }\n        return \"special\";\n    }\n\n    private String shopCategoryName(String category) {\n        if (\"rods\".equalsIgnoreCase(category)) {\n            return \"Angeln\";\n        }\n        if (\"bait\".equalsIgnoreCase(category)) {\n            return \"Köder\";\n        }\n        if (\"special\".equalsIgnoreCase(category)) {\n            return \"Spezial\";\n        }\n        return \"Alle Items\";\n    }\n\n    private Material shopCategoryIcon(String category) {\n        if (\"rods\".equalsIgnoreCase(category)) {\n            return Material.FISHING_ROD;\n        }\n        if (\"bait\".equalsIgnoreCase(category)) {\n            return Material.SLIME_BALL;\n        }\n        if (\"special\".equalsIgnoreCase(category)) {\n            return Material.NETHER_STAR;\n        }\n        return Material.CHEST;\n    }\n\n    private int shopSortWeight(ConfigurationSection item) {\n        if (item == null) {\n            return 999;\n        }\n        String category = shopCategory(item);\n        if (\"rods\".equals(category)) {\n            return 10 + item.getInt(\"price\", 0) / 1000;\n        }\n        if (\"bait\".equals(category)) {\n            return 100 + item.getInt(\"price\", 0) / 100;\n        }\n        return 300 + item.getInt(\"price\", 0) / 1000;\n    }\n\n    private CatchMoment rollCatchMoment(Player player, CatchReward reward) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"variety.enabled\", true)) {\n            return CatchMoment.none();\n        }\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"variety.qualities\");\n        if (section == null) {\n            return CatchMoment.none();\n        }\n        List<CatchMoment> moments = new ArrayList<>();\n        double total = 0.0;\n        for (String key : section.getKeys(false)) {\n            ConfigurationSection quality = section.getConfigurationSection(key);\n            if (quality == null || !quality.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true)) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            double weight = Math.max(0.0, quality.getDouble(\"weight\", 0.0));\n            if (weight <= 0.0) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            int minSize = quality.getInt(\"size-min\", 10);\n            int maxSize = quality.getInt(\"size-max\", minSize);\n            moments.add(new CatchMoment(\n                    quality.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(key)),\n                    quality.getString(\"color\", \"&7\"),\n                    randomBetween(minSize, maxSize) + activeFishingSpot(player).sizeBonus(),\n                    weight,\n                    Math.max(0.0, quality.getDouble(\"coin-multiplier\", 1.0)),\n                    Math.max(0.0, quality.getDouble(\"token-multiplier\", 1.0)),\n                    quality.getInt(\"bonus-xp\", 0),\n                    quality.getStringList(\"messages\")\n            ));\n            total += weight;\n        }\n        if (moments.isEmpty() || total <= 0.0) {\n            return CatchMoment.none();\n        }\n        double roll = random.nextDouble(total);\n        double cursor = 0.0;\n        for (CatchMoment moment : moments) {\n            cursor += moment.weight();\n            if (roll <= cursor) {\n                return moment;\n            }\n        }\n        return moments.get(moments.size() - 1);\n    }\n\n    private void triggerCatchVariety(Player player, CatchReward reward) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"variety.enabled\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        sendRandomMomentMessage(player, reward);\n        handleCatchStreak(player, reward);\n        rollMiniEvent(player, reward);\n    }\n\n    private void sendRandomMomentMessage(Player player, CatchReward reward) {\n        List<String> messages = new ArrayList<>(reward.moment().messages());\n        if (messages.isEmpty()) {\n            messages.addAll(getConfig().getStringList(\"variety.random-messages\"));\n        }\n        if (!messages.isEmpty() && random.nextDouble(100.0) < getConfig().getDouble(\"variety.message-chance\", 35.0)) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + replaceReward(messages.get(random.nextInt(messages.size())), reward)));\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void handleCatchStreak(Player player, CatchReward reward) {\n        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();\n        int streak = data.getInt(\"players.\" + uuid + \".streak.current\") + 1;\n        data.set(\"players.\" + uuid + \".streak.current\", streak);\n        if (streak > data.getInt(\"players.\" + uuid + \".streak.best\")) {\n            data.set(\"players.\" + uuid + \".streak.best\", streak);\n        }\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"variety.streak-rewards\");\n        if (section == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        ConfigurationSection rewardSection = section.getConfigurationSection(String.valueOf(streak));\n        if (rewardSection == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        int coins = rewardSection.getInt(\"coins\", 0);\n        int tokens = rewardSection.getInt(\"tokens\", 0);\n        if (coins > 0) {\n            dispatchRewardCommand(\"coins\", player, coins);\n            addStat(uuid, player.getName(), \"coins\", coins);\n        }\n        if (tokens > 0) {\n            dispatchRewardCommand(\"tokens\", player, tokens);\n            addStat(uuid, player.getName(), \"tokens\", tokens);\n        }\n        for (String command : rewardSection.getStringList(\"commands\")) {\n            dispatchPreparedCommand(player, command, reward);\n        }\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + replace(getConfig().getString(\"variety.streak-message\", \"&aAngel-Serie: &e{streak}&7! Bonus erhalten.\"), \"{streak}\", streak)));\n    }\n\n    private void rollMiniEvent(Player player, CatchReward reward) {\n        if (random.nextDouble(100.0) >= getConfig().getDouble(\"variety.mini-events.chance\", 4.0)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"variety.mini-events.events\");\n        if (section == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        List<MiniEvent> events = new ArrayList<>();\n        double total = 0.0;\n        for (String key : section.getKeys(false)) {\n            ConfigurationSection eventSection = section.getConfigurationSection(key);\n            if (eventSection == null || !eventSection.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true)) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            double weight = Math.max(0.0, eventSection.getDouble(\"weight\", 0.0));\n            if (weight <= 0.0) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            events.add(new MiniEvent(\n                    eventSection.getString(\"message\", \"\"),\n                    eventSection.getString(\"sound\", \"\"),\n                    eventSection.getStringList(\"commands\"),\n                    weight,\n                    eventSection.getBoolean(\"broadcast\", false),\n                    eventSection.getString(\"entity\", \"\"),\n                    eventSection.getBoolean(\"lightning\", false),\n                    eventSection.getDouble(\"velocity-y\", 0.0),\n                    eventSection.getString(\"particle\", \"\"),\n                    eventSection.getInt(\"particle-count\", 25)\n            ));\n            total += weight;\n        }\n        if (events.isEmpty() || total <= 0.0) {\n            return;\n        }\n        double roll = random.nextDouble(total);\n        double cursor = 0.0;\n        for (MiniEvent event : events) {\n            cursor += event.weight();\n            if (roll <= cursor) {\n                runMiniEvent(player, reward, event);\n                return;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void runMiniEvent(Player player, CatchReward reward, MiniEvent event) {\n        String message = replaceReward(event.message(), reward).replace(\"{player}\", player.getName());\n        if (!message.isBlank()) {\n            if (event.broadcast()) {\n                Bukkit.broadcastMessage(color(prefix() + message));\n            } else {\n                player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + message));\n            }\n        }\n        if (!event.sound().isBlank()) {\n            try {\n                player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.valueOf(event.sound().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT)), 0.8f, 1.0f);\n            } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) {\n                player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.ENTITY_EXPERIENCE_ORB_PICKUP, 0.8f, 1.0f);\n            }\n        }\n        for (String command : event.commands()) {\n            dispatchPreparedCommand(player, command, reward);\n        }\n        if (!event.entityType().isBlank()) {\n            try {\n                EntityType type = EntityType.valueOf(event.entityType().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));\n                player.getWorld().spawnEntity(player.getLocation().add(0, 0.5, 0), type);\n            } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) {\n                // Invalid configured event entities are ignored.\n            }\n        }\n        if (event.lightning()) {\n            player.getWorld().strikeLightningEffect(player.getLocation());\n        }\n        if (event.velocityY() > 0.0) {\n            player.setVelocity(player.getVelocity().add(new Vector(0.0, event.velocityY(), 0.0)));\n        }\n        if (!event.particle().isBlank()) {\n            try {\n                player.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.valueOf(event.particle().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT)), player.getLocation().add(0, 1.0, 0), event.particleCount(), 0.7, 0.7, 0.7, 0.02);\n            } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) {\n                player.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.SPLASH, player.getLocation().add(0, 1.0, 0), 20, 0.6, 0.6, 0.6, 0.02);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private ItemStack createBaitItem(String shopId, ConfigurationSection section) {\n        Material material = Material.matchMaterial(section.getString(\"material\", \"COD\"));\n        if (material == null || material.isAir()) {\n            material = Material.COD;\n        }\n        ItemStack item = new ItemStack(material, 1);\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta != null) {\n            meta.setDisplayName(color(section.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(shopId))));\n            List<String> lore = new ArrayList<>(section.getStringList(\"lore\"));\n            lore.add(\"&8Angel-Köder: \" + shopId);\n            meta.setLore(colorList(lore));\n            meta.addItemFlags(ItemFlag.HIDE_ATTRIBUTES);\n            meta.getPersistentDataContainer().set(baitKey, PersistentDataType.STRING, shopId);\n            item.setItemMeta(meta);\n        }\n        return item;\n    }\n\n    private ItemStack createRodItem(String shopId, ConfigurationSection section) {\n        ItemStack item = new ItemStack(Material.FISHING_ROD, 1);\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta != null) {\n            meta.setDisplayName(color(section.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(shopId))));\n            List<String> lore = new ArrayList<>(section.getStringList(\"lore\"));\n            lore.add(\"&8Rod-Typ: \" + section.getString(\"rod-type\", shopId));\n            meta.setLore(colorList(lore));\n            meta.getPersistentDataContainer().set(rodTypeKey, PersistentDataType.STRING, section.getString(\"rod-type\", shopId));\n            meta.addItemFlags(ItemFlag.HIDE_ATTRIBUTES, ItemFlag.HIDE_ENCHANTS);\n            item.setItemMeta(meta);\n        }\n        applyEnchantments(item, section.getStringList(\"enchantments\"));\n        applyRepairCost(item, section.getInt(\"repair-cost\", 0));\n        return item;\n    }\n\n    private ItemStack createLootboxItem(String lootboxId, ConfigurationSection section) {\n        Material material = Material.PLAYER_HEAD;\n        ItemStack item = new ItemStack(material, 1);\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta != null) {\n            meta.setDisplayName(color(section.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(lootboxId))));\n            List<String> lore = new ArrayList<>();\n            for (String line : section.getStringList(\"item-lore\")) {\n                lore.add(fixLegacyText(line));\n            }\n            if (lore.isEmpty()) {\n                lore.add(\"&7Rechtsklick zum Öffnen.\");\n            }\n            lore.replaceAll(this::fixLegacyText);\n            lore.add(\"\");\n            lore.add(\"&8Signatur: &dChaosSMP Fishing\");\n            meta.setLore(colorList(lore));\n            meta.addItemFlags(ItemFlag.HIDE_ATTRIBUTES, ItemFlag.HIDE_ENCHANTS);\n            item.setItemMeta(meta);\n        }\n        applySkullOwner(item, section.getString(\"skull-owner\", \"MHF_Chest\"));\n        applySpecialItemData(item, \"lootbox\", lootboxId, List.of());\n        markFishingMarketItem(item, section.getInt(\"sell-price\", 0));\n        return item;\n    }\n\n    private String fixLegacyText(String value) {\n        if (value == null) {\n            return \"\";\n        }\n        return value\n                .replace(\"Ö\", \"Ö\")\n                .replace(\"ö\", \"ö\")\n                .replace(\"Ä\", \"Ä\")\n                .replace(\"ä\", \"ä\")\n                .replace(\"Ü\", \"Ü\")\n                .replace(\"ü\", \"ü\")\n                .replace(\"ß\", \"ß\");\n    }\n\n    private void sellClickedInventoryItem(Player player, ItemStack item) {\n        if (item == null || item.getType().isAir()) {\n            return;\n        }\n        if (isBaitItem(item)) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cKöder können nicht verkauft werden.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        int value = marketValue(item, false);\n        if (value <= 0) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDieses Item nimmt der Angel-Markt nicht an.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        int amount = item.getAmount();\n        item.setAmount(0);\n        payoutMarketSale(player, value, amount);\n    }\n\n    private void sellAllFishingItems(Player player) {\n        int total = 0;\n        int soldItems = 0;\n        for (ItemStack item : player.getInventory().getStorageContents()) {\n            if (item == null || item.getType().isAir()) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            if (isBaitItem(item)) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            int value = marketValue(item, true);\n            if (value <= 0) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            soldItems += item.getAmount();\n            total += value;\n            item.setAmount(0);\n        }\n        if (total <= 0) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDu hast keine verkaufbaren Angel-Items im Inventar.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        payoutMarketSale(player, total, soldItems);\n    }\n\n    private void payoutMarketSale(Player player, int coins, int soldItems) {\n        dispatchRewardCommand(\"coins\", player, coins);\n        addStat(player.getUniqueId(), player.getName(), \"market.coins\", coins);\n        addStat(player.getUniqueId(), player.getName(), \"market.items\", soldItems);\n        checkQuests(player);\n        saveData();\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&aAngel-Markt: &e+$\" + coins + \" &7für &f\" + soldItems + \" Item(s)&7.\"));\n        player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.ENTITY_EXPERIENCE_ORB_PICKUP, 0.8f, 1.2f);\n    }\n\n    private int marketValue(ItemStack item, boolean bulkSale) {\n        if (isBaitItem(item)) {\n            return 0;\n        }\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta != null) {\n            Integer storedValue = meta.getPersistentDataContainer().get(sellValueKey, PersistentDataType.INTEGER);\n            if (storedValue != null && storedValue > 0) {\n                return storedValue * item.getAmount();\n            }\n        }\n        if (!isSafeMaterialMarketItem(item, bulkSale)) {\n            return 0;\n        }\n        return Math.max(0, getConfig().getInt(\"market.material-prices.\" + item.getType().name(), 0)) * item.getAmount();\n    }\n\n    private boolean isSafeMaterialMarketItem(ItemStack item, boolean bulkSale) {\n        if (item == null || item.getType().isAir()) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        if (item.hasItemMeta()) {\n            ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n            if (meta != null && (meta.hasDisplayName() || meta.hasEnchants() || meta.hasLore())) {\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n        if (!bulkSale && getConfig().getBoolean(\"market.allow-manual-vanilla-sales\", true)) {\n            return getConfig().isInt(\"market.material-prices.\" + item.getType().name());\n        }\n        return getConfig().getStringList(\"market.bulk-safe-materials\").contains(item.getType().name());\n    }\n\n    private void markFishingMarketItem(ItemStack item, int sellValue) {\n        if (item == null || item.getType().isAir() || sellValue <= 0) {\n            return;\n        }\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        meta.getPersistentDataContainer().set(fishingItemKey, PersistentDataType.BYTE, (byte) 1);\n        meta.getPersistentDataContainer().set(sellValueKey, PersistentDataType.INTEGER, sellValue);\n        item.setItemMeta(meta);\n    }\n\n    private boolean isBaitItem(ItemStack item) {\n        return findBaitId(item) != null;\n    }\n\n    private String findBaitId(ItemStack item) {\n        if (item == null || item.getType().isAir()) {\n            return null;\n        }\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta == null) {\n            return null;\n        }\n        String baitId = meta.getPersistentDataContainer().get(baitKey, PersistentDataType.STRING);\n        if (baitId != null && !baitId.isBlank()) {\n            return baitId;\n        }\n        ConfigurationSection items = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"shop.items\");\n        if (items == null) {\n            return null;\n        }\n        String displayName = meta.hasDisplayName() ? ChatColor.stripColor(meta.getDisplayName()) : \"\";\n        for (String key : items.getKeys(false)) {\n            ConfigurationSection section = items.getConfigurationSection(key);\n            if (section == null || !\"bait\".equalsIgnoreCase(section.getString(\"type\", \"item\"))) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            Material material = Material.matchMaterial(section.getString(\"material\", \"\"));\n            if (material != null && item.getType() != material) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            String configuredName = ChatColor.stripColor(color(section.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(key))));\n            if (!displayName.isBlank() && displayName.equals(configuredName)) {\n                return key;\n            }\n            if (meta.hasLore() && meta.getLore() != null) {\n                String marker = \"Angel-Köder: \" + key;\n                for (String line : meta.getLore()) {\n                    if (ChatColor.stripColor(line).contains(marker)) {\n                        return key;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    private void activateBait(Player player, ItemStack item) {\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        String baitId = findBaitId(item);\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"shop.items.\" + baitId);\n        if (baitId == null || baitId.isBlank() || section == null || !\"bait\".equalsIgnoreCase(section.getString(\"type\", \"item\"))) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDieser Köder ist nicht mehr gültig.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        meta.getPersistentDataContainer().set(baitKey, PersistentDataType.STRING, baitId);\n        item.setItemMeta(meta);\n        int uses = Math.max(1, section.getInt(\"uses\", 1));\n        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();\n        String activeId = data.getString(\"players.\" + uuid + \".active-bait.id\", \"\");\n        int activeUses = data.getInt(\"players.\" + uuid + \".active-bait.uses\", 0);\n        boolean extendsActiveBait = baitId.equals(activeId) && activeUses > 0;\n        if (activeId != null && !activeId.isBlank() && activeUses > 0 && !extendsActiveBait && !player.isSneaking()) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cEs ist bereits ein anderer Köder aktiv. &7Sneake beim Rechtsklick, um ihn zu ersetzen.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        int newUses = extendsActiveBait ? activeUses + uses : uses;\n        item.setAmount(item.getAmount() - 1);\n        data.set(\"players.\" + uuid + \".active-bait.id\", baitId);\n        data.set(\"players.\" + uuid + \".active-bait.uses\", newUses);\n        saveData();\n        String name = ChatColor.stripColor(color(section.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(baitId))));\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + (extendsActiveBait ? \"&aKöder verlängert: &f\" : \"&aKöder aktiviert: &f\") + name + \" &8(\" + newUses + \" Fänge)&7.\"));\n        player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.ITEM_BUNDLE_INSERT, 0.8f, 1.2f);\n    }\n\n    private BaitBonus consumeActiveBait(Player player) {\n        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();\n        String baitId = data.getString(\"players.\" + uuid + \".active-bait.id\", \"\");\n        int uses = data.getInt(\"players.\" + uuid + \".active-bait.uses\", 0);\n        if (baitId == null || baitId.isBlank() || uses <= 0) {\n            clearActiveBait(uuid);\n            return BaitBonus.none();\n        }\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"shop.items.\" + baitId);\n        if (section == null || !\"bait\".equalsIgnoreCase(section.getString(\"type\", \"item\"))) {\n            clearActiveBait(uuid);\n            saveData();\n            return BaitBonus.none();\n        }\n        int remaining = Math.max(0, uses - 1);\n        if (remaining == 0) {\n            clearActiveBait(uuid);\n        } else {\n            data.set(\"players.\" + uuid + \".active-bait.uses\", remaining);\n        }\n        saveData();\n        return new BaitBonus(\n                baitId,\n                ChatColor.stripColor(color(section.getString(\"name\", prettifyKey(baitId)))),\n                section.getDouble(\"modifiers.mystery-bonus\", 0.0),\n                Math.max(0.0, section.getDouble(\"modifiers.coin-multiplier\", 1.0)),\n                Math.max(0.0, section.getDouble(\"modifiers.token-multiplier\", 1.0)),\n                Math.max(0.0, section.getDouble(\"modifiers.rare-multiplier\", 1.0)),\n                Math.max(0.0, section.getDouble(\"modifiers.jackpot-multiplier\", 1.0)),\n                remaining\n        );\n    }\n\n    private void clearActiveBait(UUID uuid) {\n        data.set(\"players.\" + uuid + \".active-bait\", null);\n    }\n\n    private void giveItem(Player player, ItemStack item) {\n        Map<Integer, ItemStack> leftovers = player.getInventory().addItem(item);\n        for (ItemStack leftover : leftovers.values()) {\n            player.getWorld().dropItemNaturally(player.getLocation(), leftover);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private int getLevelXp(UUID uuid) {\n        return data.getInt(\"players.\" + uuid + \".level-xp\");\n    }\n\n    private int getFishingLevel(UUID uuid) {\n        int xp = getLevelXp(uuid);\n        int level = 1;\n        while (xp >= xpForNextLevel(level) && level < getConfig().getInt(\"leveling.max-level\", 100)) {\n            xp -= xpForNextLevel(level);\n            level++;\n        }\n        return level;\n    }\n\n    private int getCurrentLevelXp(UUID uuid) {\n        int xp = getLevelXp(uuid);\n        int level = 1;\n        while (xp >= xpForNextLevel(level) && level < getConfig().getInt(\"leveling.max-level\", 100)) {\n            xp -= xpForNextLevel(level);\n            level++;\n        }\n        return xp;\n    }\n\n    private int xpForNextLevel(int level) {\n        int base = getConfig().getInt(\"leveling.base-xp\", 100);\n        int step = getConfig().getInt(\"leveling.xp-step\", 35);\n        return Math.max(1, base + Math.max(0, level - 1) * step);\n    }\n\n    private void sendTop(CommandSender sender) {\n        List<PlayerStat> stats = sortedStats();\n        if (stats.isEmpty()) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&eNoch keine Angelstats vorhanden.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        int size = Math.min(getConfig().getInt(\"leaderboard.size\", 10), stats.size());\n        sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&6Angel Top \" + size));\n        for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {\n            PlayerStat stat = stats.get(i);\n            sender.sendMessage(color(\"&e#\" + (i + 1) + \" &f\" + stat.name() + \" &8- &b\" + stat.catches() + \" Fänge &8| &d\" + stat.mystery() + \" Mystery\"));\n        }\n    }\n\n    private List<PlayerStat> sortedStats() {\n        ConfigurationSection players = data.getConfigurationSection(\"players\");\n        if (players == null) {\n            return List.of();\n        }\n        List<PlayerStat> stats = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (String uuidText : players.getKeys(false)) {\n            String path = \"players.\" + uuidText;\n            stats.add(new PlayerStat(data.getString(path + \".name\", uuidText), data.getInt(path + \".catches\"), data.getInt(path + \".mystery\"), data.getInt(path + \".tokens\")));\n        }\n        stats.sort(Comparator.comparingInt(PlayerStat::catches).reversed());\n        return stats;\n    }\n\n    private void fillBorder(Inventory inventory) {\n        fillBorder(inventory, Material.GRAY_STAINED_GLASS_PANE);\n    }\n\n    private void fillBorder(Inventory inventory, Material material) {\n        ItemStack filler = item(Material.GRAY_STAINED_GLASS_PANE, \" \");\n        if (material != null && !material.isAir()) {\n            filler = item(material, \" \");\n        }\n        for (int i = 0; i < inventory.getSize(); i++) {\n            int row = i / 9;\n            int col = i % 9;\n            if (row == 0 || row == inventory.getSize() / 9 - 1 || col == 0 || col == 8) {\n                inventory.setItem(i, filler);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private int nextContentSlot(int slot) {\n        int next = slot + 1;\n        if (next % 9 == 8) {\n            next += 2;\n        }\n        return next;\n    }\n\n    private boolean isBackSlot(int slot) {\n        return slot == 45 || slot == 49;\n    }\n\n    private List<Integer> contentSlots() {\n        List<Integer> slots = new ArrayList<>();\n        int slot = 10;\n        while (slot < 44) {\n            slots.add(slot);\n            slot = nextContentSlot(slot);\n        }\n        return slots;\n    }\n\n    private int pageSize() {\n        return contentSlots().size();\n    }\n\n    private int totalPages(int entries) {\n        return Math.max(1, (int) Math.ceil(entries / (double) pageSize()));\n    }\n\n    private int clampPage(int page, int totalPages) {\n        return Math.max(0, Math.min(page, Math.max(0, totalPages - 1)));\n    }\n\n    private void addPageControls(Inventory inventory, Map<Integer, String> actions, int page, int totalPages) {\n        int current = clampPage(page, totalPages);\n        inventory.setItem(49, item(Material.ARROW, \"&eZurück\"));\n        if (current > 0) {\n            inventory.setItem(48, item(Material.ARROW, \"&eVorherige Seite\", \"&7Seite &f\" + current + \"&8/&f\" + totalPages));\n            actions.put(48, \"page:\" + (current - 1));\n        }\n        if (current + 1 < totalPages) {\n            inventory.setItem(50, item(Material.ARROW, \"&eNächste Seite\", \"&7Seite &f\" + (current + 2) + \"&8/&f\" + totalPages));\n            actions.put(50, \"page:\" + (current + 1));\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void openPagedGui(Player player, FishingGuiHolder holder, int page) {\n        if (\"shop\".equals(holder.page())) {\n            openShopGui(player, page);\n        } else if (\"shop-items\".equals(holder.page())) {\n            openShopCategoryGui(player, holder.actions().getOrDefault(-1, \"all\"), page);\n        } else if (\"drops\".equals(holder.page())) {\n            openDropsGui(player, page);\n        } else if (\"drop-items\".equals(holder.page())) {\n            openDropsCategoryGui(player, holder.actions().getOrDefault(-1, \"all\"), page);\n
