    } else if (\"collection\".equals(holder.page())) {\n            openCollectionGui(player, page);\n        } else if (\"pass\".equals(holder.page())) {\n            openPassGui(player, page);\n        } else if (\"quests\".equals(holder.page())) {\n            openQuestGui(player, page);\n        } else if (\"achievements\".equals(holder.page())) {\n            openAchievementsGui(player, page);\n        } else if (\"lootbox-preview\".equals(holder.page())) {\n            String lootboxId = holder.actions().getOrDefault(-1, \"common\");\n            openLootboxPreviewGui(player, lootboxId, page);\n        } else if (\"bosses\".equals(holder.page())) {\n            openBossGui(player);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private ItemStack item(Material material, String name, String... lore) {\n        ItemStack item = new ItemStack(material == null ? Material.STONE : material);\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta != null) {\n            meta.setDisplayName(color(name));\n            meta.setLore(colorList(List.of(lore)));\n            meta.addItemFlags(ItemFlag.HIDE_ATTRIBUTES);\n            item.setItemMeta(meta);\n        }\n        return item;\n    }\n\n    private void addGlow(ItemStack item) {\n        if (item == null || item.getType().isAir()) {\n            return;\n        }\n        item.addUnsafeEnchantment(Enchantment.UNBREAKING, 1);\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta != null) {\n            meta.addItemFlags(ItemFlag.HIDE_ENCHANTS);\n            item.setItemMeta(meta);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void applyEnchantments(ItemStack item, List<String> enchantments) {\n        for (String line : enchantments) {\n            String[] parts = line.split(\":\");\n            if (parts.length == 0) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            String key = parts[0].trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n            int level = 1;\n            if (parts.length > 1) {\n                try {\n                    level = Math.max(1, Integer.parseInt(parts[1].trim()));\n                } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) {\n                    level = 1;\n                }\n            }\n            Enchantment enchantment = Enchantment.getByKey(NamespacedKey.minecraft(key));\n            if (enchantment != null) {\n                item.addUnsafeEnchantment(enchantment, level);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void applyRepairCost(ItemStack item, int repairCost) {\n        if (repairCost <= 0) {\n            return;\n        }\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta instanceof Repairable repairable) {\n            repairable.setRepairCost(repairCost);\n            item.setItemMeta(meta);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void applyBossVisuals(LivingEntity living, ConfigurationSection section) {\n        EntityEquipment equipment = living.getEquipment();\n        if (equipment == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        ConfigurationSection visual = section.getConfigurationSection(\"visual\");\n        if (visual == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        equipment.setHelmet(createBossVisualItem(visual.getConfigurationSection(\"helmet\")));\n        equipment.setChestplate(createBossVisualItem(visual.getConfigurationSection(\"chestplate\")));\n        equipment.setLeggings(createBossVisualItem(visual.getConfigurationSection(\"leggings\")));\n        equipment.setBoots(createBossVisualItem(visual.getConfigurationSection(\"boots\")));\n        equipment.setItemInMainHand(createBossVisualItem(visual.getConfigurationSection(\"main-hand\")));\n        equipment.setItemInOffHand(createBossVisualItem(visual.getConfigurationSection(\"off-hand\")));\n        equipment.setHelmetDropChance(0.0f);\n        equipment.setChestplateDropChance(0.0f);\n        equipment.setLeggingsDropChance(0.0f);\n        equipment.setBootsDropChance(0.0f);\n        equipment.setItemInMainHandDropChance(0.0f);\n        equipment.setItemInOffHandDropChance(0.0f);\n    }\n\n    private ItemStack createBossVisualItem(ConfigurationSection section) {\n        if (section == null || !section.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true)) {\n            return null;\n        }\n        Material material = Material.matchMaterial(section.getString(\"material\", \"AIR\"));\n        if (material == null || material.isAir()) {\n            return null;\n        }\n        ItemStack item = new ItemStack(material, 1);\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta != null) {\n            if (section.contains(\"name\")) {\n                meta.setDisplayName(color(section.getString(\"name\", \"\")));\n            }\n            List<String> lore = section.getStringList(\"lore\");\n            if (!lore.isEmpty()) {\n                meta.setLore(colorList(lore));\n            }\n            int customModelData = section.getInt(\"custom-model-data\", 0);\n            if (customModelData > 0) {\n                meta.setCustomModelData(customModelData);\n            }\n            meta.addItemFlags(ItemFlag.HIDE_ATTRIBUTES, ItemFlag.HIDE_ENCHANTS);\n            item.setItemMeta(meta);\n        }\n        applySkullOwner(item, section.getString(\"skull-owner\", \"\"));\n        applyLeatherColor(item, section.getString(\"leather-color\", \"\"));\n        applyEnchantments(item, section.getStringList(\"enchantments\"));\n        return item;\n    }\n\n    private void applySpecialItemData(ItemStack item, String specialType, String lootboxId, List<String> stories) {\n        if (specialType == null || specialType.isBlank()) {\n            return;\n        }\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        PersistentDataContainer container = meta.getPersistentDataContainer();\n        container.set(specialTypeKey, PersistentDataType.STRING, specialType);\n        if (lootboxId != null && !lootboxId.isBlank()) {\n            container.set(lootboxKey, PersistentDataType.STRING, lootboxId);\n        }\n        if (stories != null && !stories.isEmpty()) {\n            container.set(storyKey, PersistentDataType.STRING, stories.get(random.nextInt(stories.size())));\n        }\n        item.setItemMeta(meta);\n    }\n\n    private void applyLeatherColor(ItemStack item, String colorValue) {\n        if (colorValue == null || colorValue.isBlank()) {\n            return;\n        }\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (!(meta instanceof LeatherArmorMeta leatherMeta)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        String normalized = colorValue.trim().replace(\"#\", \"\");\n        if (normalized.length() != 6) {\n            return;\n        }\n        try {\n            int rgb = Integer.parseInt(normalized, 16);\n            leatherMeta.setColor(Color.fromRGB(rgb));\n            item.setItemMeta(leatherMeta);\n        } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) {\n            // Invalid configured leather colors are ignored.\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void applySkullOwner(ItemStack item, String owner) {\n        if (owner == null || owner.isBlank() || item.getType() != Material.PLAYER_HEAD) {\n            return;\n        }\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta instanceof SkullMeta skullMeta) {\n            skullMeta.setOwningPlayer(Bukkit.getOfflinePlayer(owner));\n            item.setItemMeta(skullMeta);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void addStat(UUID uuid, String name, String key, int amount) {\n        String path = \"players.\" + uuid;\n        data.set(path + \".name\", name);\n        data.set(path + \".\" + key, Math.max(0, data.getInt(path + \".\" + key) + amount));\n    }\n\n    private int stat(Player player, String key) {\n        return data.getInt(\"players.\" + player.getUniqueId() + \".\" + key);\n    }\n\n    private int precisionFocus(Player player) {\n        return Math.max(0, data.getInt(\"players.\" + player.getUniqueId() + \".precision.focus\", 0));\n    }\n\n    private void saveData() {\n        if (shuttingDown || pendingDataSaveTask != null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        pendingDataSaveTask = Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskLater(this, () -> {\n            pendingDataSaveTask = null;\n            writeDataNow();\n        }, 20L);\n    }\n\n    private void flushData() {\n        if (pendingDataSaveTask != null) {\n            pendingDataSaveTask.cancel();\n            pendingDataSaveTask = null;\n        }\n        writeDataNow();\n    }\n\n    private synchronized void writeDataNow() {\n        if (data == null || dataFile == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        File temporaryFile = new File(dataFile.getParentFile(), dataFile.getName() + \".tmp\");\n        try {\n            data.save(temporaryFile);\n            try {\n                Files.move(temporaryFile.toPath(), dataFile.toPath(), StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);\n            } catch (AtomicMoveNotSupportedException exception) {\n                Files.move(temporaryFile.toPath(), dataFile.toPath(), StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);\n            }\n            if (database != null) {\n                database.queueSnapshot(\"fishing:data\", dataFile);\n            }\n        } catch (IOException exception) {\n            getLogger().warning(\"Konnte data.yml nicht speichern: \" + exception.getMessage());\n            if (temporaryFile.exists() && !temporaryFile.delete()) {\n                getLogger().fine(\"Temporäre Angel-Datendatei konnte nicht entfernt werden: \" + temporaryFile.getName());\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void repairMojibakeConfig() {\n        boolean changed = false;\n        for (String path : getConfig().getKeys(true)) {\n            Object value = getConfig().get(path);\n            if (value instanceof String text) {\n                String fixed = repairMojibake(text);\n                if (!fixed.equals(text)) {\n                    getConfig().set(path, fixed);\n                    changed = true;\n                }\n            } else if (value instanceof List<?> list) {\n                List<String> fixedList = new ArrayList<>();\n                boolean listChanged = false;\n                for (Object entry : list) {\n                    if (entry instanceof String text) {\n                        String fixed = repairMojibake(text);\n                        fixedList.add(fixed);\n                        listChanged |= !fixed.equals(text);\n                    } else {\n                        fixedList.add(String.valueOf(entry));\n                    }\n                }\n                if (listChanged) {\n                    getConfig().set(path, fixedList);\n                    changed = true;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        if (changed) {\n            saveConfig();\n            getLogger().info(\"Config-Umlaute wurden automatisch repariert.\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void applyBalanceUpdate() {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"fishing.balance.auto-update\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        boolean changed = false;\n aktualisiert.\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    private boolean setConfigDefault(String path, Object value) {\n        if (getConfig().contains(path)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        getConfig().set(path, value);\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    private boolean setConfigMinimum(String path, double minimum) {\n        if (getConfig().getDouble(path, 0.0) >= minimum) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        getConfig().set(path, minimum);\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    private void reportConfigIssues() {\n        List<String> issues = validateFishingConfig();\n        if (issues.isEmpty()) {\n            getLogger().info(\"Config-Check bestanden.\");\n            return;\n        }\n        getLogger().warning(\"Config-Check hat \" + issues.size() + \" Hinweis(e) gefunden. Nutze /angeln check für Details.\");\n        for (int i = 0; i < Math.min(8, issues.size()); i++) {\n            getLogger().warning(\"[Config-Check] \" + issues.get(i));\n        }\n    }\n\n    private List<String> validateFishingConfig() {\n        List<String> issues = new ArrayList<>();\n        validateConfigSection(\"\", getConfig(), issues);\n        validateWeightedSection(\"fishing.rewards.tiers\", issues);\n        validateWeightedSection(\"fishing.mystery-drops.drops\", issues);\n        validateWeightedSection(\"fishing.lootboxes.common.rewards\", issues);\n        validateWeightedSection(\"fishing.lootboxes.chaossmp.rewards\", issues);\n        validateWeightedSection(\"fishing.lootboxes.lava.rewards\", issues);\n        validateWeightedSection(\"fishing.lootboxes.void.rewards\", issues);\n        validateWeightedSection(\"fishing.lootboxes.mythic.rewards\", issues);\n        validateWeightedSection(\"variety.mini-events.events\", issues);\n        return issues;\n    }\n\n    private void validateConfigSection(String path, ConfigurationSection section, List<String> issues) {\n        for (String key : section.getKeys(false)) {\n            String childPath = path.isBlank() ? key : path + \".\" + key;\n            if (section.isConfigurationSection(key)) {\n                ConfigurationSection child = section.getConfigurationSection(key);\n                if (child != null) {\n                    validateTypedConfigValues(childPath, child, issues);\n                    validateConfigSection(childPath, child, issues);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void validateTypedConfigValues(String path, ConfigurationSection section, List<String> issues) {\n        validateMaterial(path, \"material\", section.getString(\"material\", \"\"), issues);\n        validateMaterial(path, \"icon\", section.getString(\"icon\", \"\"), issues);\n        validateEnum(path, \"entity\", section.getString(\"entity\", \"\"), EntityType.class, issues);\n        validateEnum(path, \"particle\", section.getString(\"particle\", \"\"), Particle.class, issues);\n        validateSound(path, section.getString(\"sound\", \"\"), issues);\n        String lootboxId = section.getString(\"lootbox\", \"\");\n        if (lootboxId != null && !lootboxId.isBlank() && getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"fishing.lootboxes.\" + lootboxId) == null) {\n            issues.add(path + \".lootbox verweist auf unbekannte Lootbox '\" + lootboxId + \"'.\");\n        }\n        for (String enchantmentLine : section.getStringList(\"enchantments\")) {\n            String enchantmentKey = enchantmentLine.split(\":\", 2)[0].trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n            if (!enchantmentKey.isBlank() && Enchantment.getByKey(NamespacedKey.minecraft(enchantmentKey)) == null) {\n                issues.add(path + \".enchantments enthält unbekannte Verzauberung '\" + enchantmentKey + \"'.\");\n            }\n        }\n        if (section.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true) && section.contains(\"weight\") && section.getDouble(\"weight\", 0.0) <= 0.0) {\n            issues.add(path + \".weight ist 0 oder kleiner, obwohl der Eintrag aktiv ist.\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void validateMaterial(String path, String key, String value, List<String> issues) {\n        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Material material = Material.matchMaterial(value);\n        if (material == null || material.isAir()) {\n            issues.add(path + \".\" + key + \" nutzt unbekanntes Material '\" + value + \"'.\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    private <T extends Enum<T>> void validateEnum(String path, String key, String value, Class<T> enumType, List<String> issues) {\n        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {\n            return;\n        }\n        try {\n            Enum.valueOf(enumType, value.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));\n        } catch (IllegalArgumentException exception) {\n            issues.add(path + \".\" + key + \" nutzt unbekannten Wert '\" + value + \"'.\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void validateSound(String path, String value, List<String> issues) {\n        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {\n            return;\n        }\n        try {\n            Sound.valueOf(value.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));\n        } catch (IllegalArgumentException exception) {\n            issues.add(path + \".sound nutzt unbekannten Wert '\" + value + \"'.\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void validateWeightedSection(String path, List<String> issues) {\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(path);\n        if (section == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        double total = 0.0;\n        int enabled = 0;\n        for (String key : section.getKeys(false)) {\n            ConfigurationSection child = section.getConfigurationSection(key);\n            if (child == null || !child.getBoolean(\"enabled\", true)) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            enabled++;\n            total += Math.max(0.0, child.getDouble(\"weight\", child.getDouble(\"chance\", 0.0)));\n        }\n        if (enabled > 0 && total <= 0.0) {\n            issues.add(path + \" hat aktive Einträge, aber kein positives Gesamtgewicht.\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    private String repairMojibake(String text) {\n        if (text == null || (text.indexOf('\\u00C3') < 0 && text.indexOf('\\u00C2') < 0 && text.indexOf('\\u00E2') < 0)) {\n            return text == null ? \"\" : text;\n        }\n        return new String(text.getBytes(StandardCharsets.ISO_8859_1), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n    }\n\n    private int randomBetween(int min, int max) {\n        int lower = Math.min(min, max);\n        int upper = Math.max(min, max);\n        return lower + random.nextInt(upper - lower + 1);\n    }\n\n    private int randomRange(ConfigurationSection section, String key) {\n        return randomBetween(section.getInt(key + \"-min\", section.getInt(key, 0)), section.getInt(key + \"-max\", section.getInt(key, section.getInt(key + \"-min\", 0))));\n    }\n\n    private String range(ConfigurationSection section, String key) {\n        int min = section.getInt(key + \"-min\", section.getInt(key, 0));\n        int max = section.getInt(key + \"-max\", min);\n        return min == max ? String.valueOf(min) : min + \"-\" + max;\n    }\n\n    private int parseInt(String text, int fallback) {\n        try {\n            return Integer.parseInt(text);\n        } catch (NumberFormatException exception) {\n            return fallback;\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void incrementCount(Map<String, Integer> counts, String key) {\n        counts.merge(key == null || key.isBlank() ? \"-\" : ChatColor.stripColor(color(key)), 1, Integer::sum);\n    }\n\n    private String percent(int value, int total) {\n        if (total <= 0) {\n            return \"0.00%\";\n        }\n        return String.format(Locale.US, \"%.2f%%\", value * 100.0 / total);\n    }\n\n    private String average(long value, int total) {\n        if (total <= 0) {\n            return \"0.00\";\n        }\n        return String.format(Locale.US, \"%.2f\", value / (double) total);\n    }\n\n    private String summarizeCounts(Map<String, Integer> counts, int total, int limit) {\n        if (counts.isEmpty()) {\n            return \"-\";\n        }\n        List<Map.Entry<String, Integer>> entries = new ArrayList<>(counts.entrySet());\n        entries.sort(Map.Entry.<String, Integer>comparingByValue().reversed());\n        List<String> parts = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (int i = 0; i < Math.min(limit, entries.size()); i++) {\n            Map.Entry<String, Integer> entry = entries.get(i);\n            parts.add(entry.getKey() + \" \" + entry.getValue() + \" (\" + percent(entry.getValue(), total) + \")\");\n        }\n        return String.join(\", \", parts);\n    }\n\n    private void sendUsage(CommandSender sender, String label) {\n        sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&e/\" + label + \" &7- Angel GUI\"));\n        sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() (value.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).startsWith(input.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT))) {\n            result.add(value);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void playConfiguredSound(Player player, String path, Sound fallback) {\n        String soundName = getConfig().getString(path, fallback.name());\n        try {\n            player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.valueOf(soundName.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT)), 0.8f, 1.15f);\n        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) {\n            player.playSound(player.getLocation(), fallback, 0.8f, 1.15f);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private String prefix() {\n        return getConfig().getString(\"system.prefix\", \"&6&lChaosSMP &8| &r\");\n    }\n\n    private String valueOrDash(String value) {\n        return value == null || value.isBlank() ? \"-\" : value;\n    }\n\n    private String replaceReward(String text, CatchReward reward) {\n        return replace(text, \"{name}\", reward.name(), \"{category}\", reward.category(), \"{coins}\", reward.coins(), \"{tokens}\", reward.tokens(), \"{experience}\", reward.experience(), \"{quality}\", reward.moment().name(), \"{size}\", reward.moment().size());\n    }\n\n    private String replaceRandomRanges(String text) {\n        if (text == null || text.isBlank()) {\n            return \"\";\n        }\n        Matcher matcher = Pattern.compile(\"\\\\{random:(\\\\d+)-(\\\\d+)\\\\}\").matcher(text);\n        StringBuffer buffer = new StringBuffer();\n        while (matcher.find()) {\n            int min = Integer.parseInt(matcher.group(1));\n            int max = Integer.parseInt(matcher.group(2));\n            matcher.appendReplacement(buffer, String.valueOf(randomBetween(min, max)));\n        }\n        matcher.appendTail(buffer);\n        return buffer.toString();\n    }\n\n    private String replace(String text, Object... replacements) {\n        String result = text == null ? \"\" : text;\n        for (int i = 0; i + 1 < replacements.length; i += 2) {\n            result = result.replace(String.valueOf(replacements[i]), String.valueOf(replacements[i + 1]));\n        }\n        return result;\n    }\n\n    private String normalizeKey(String input) {\n        return input.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).replaceAll(\"[^a-z0-9_.-]\", \"\");\n    }\n\n    private String prettifyKey(String key) {\n        String[] parts = key.replace('-', ' ').replace('_', ' ').split(\" \");\n        StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();\n        for (String part : parts) {\n            if (part.isBlank()) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            if (!builder.isEmpty()) {\n                builder.append(' ');\n            }\n            builder.append(part.substring(0, 1).toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT)).append(part.substring(1).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT));\n        }\n        return builder.toString();\n    }\n\n    private String color(String text) {\n        return ChatColor.translateAlternateColorCodes('&', normalizeGermanTypography(text));\n    }\n\n    private static String normalizeGermanTypography(String text) {\n        if (text == null) {\n            return \"\";\n        }\n        return text\n            .replace(\"Öffnen\", \"Öffnen\").replace(\"öffnen\", \"öffnen\")\n            .replace(\"Auswählen\", \"Auswählen\").replace(\"auswählen\", \"auswählen\")\n            .replace(\"Ausgewählt\", \"Ausgewählt\").replace(\"ausgewählt\", \"ausgewählt\")\n            .replace(\"Kaeufe\", \"Käufe\").replace(\"kaeufe\", \"käufe\")\n            .replace(\"Benoetigt\", \"Benötigt\").replace(\"benoetigt\", \"benötigt\")\n            .replace(\"Wähle\", \"Wähle\").replace(\"waehle\", \"wähle\")\n            .replace(\"Haelt\", \"Hält\").replace(\"haelt\", \"hält\")\n            .replace(\"Zaeh\", \"Zäh\").replace(\"zaeh\", \"zäh\")\n            .replace(\"Glaenzen\", \"Glänzen\").replace(\"glaenzen\", \"glänzen\")\n            .replace(\"Gewaesser\", \"Gewässer\").replace(\"gewaesser\", \"gewässer\")\n            .replace(\"Gewaessern\", \"Gewässern\").replace(\"gewaessern\", \"gewässern\")\n            .replace(\"Legendaer\", \"Legendär\").replace(\"legendaer\", \"legendär\")\n            .replace(\"Gefaehrlich\", \"Gefährlich\").replace(\"gefaehrlich\", \"gefährlich\")\n            .replace(\"Wunderschoen\", \"Wunderschön\").replace(\"wunderschoen\", \"wunderschön\")\n            .replace(\"Ploetze\", \"Plötze\").replace(\"ploetze\", \"plötze\")\n            .replace(\"Barschkoenig\", \"Barschkönig\").replace(\"barschkoenig\", \"barschkönig\")\n            .replace(\"Koenigslachs\", \"Königslachs\").replace(\"koenigslachs\", \"königslachs\")\n            .replace(\"Urzeitstoer\", \"Urzeitstör\").replace(\"urzeitstoer\", \"urzeitstör\")\n            .replace(\"Daemonenfisch\", \"Dämonenfisch\").replace(\"daemonenfisch\", \"dämonenfisch\")\n            .replace(\"Köder\", \"Köder\").replace(\"köder\", \"köder\")\n            .replace(\"Ausschliesslich\", \"Ausschließlich\").replace(\"ausschliesslich\", \"ausschließlich\")\n            .replace(\"Zurück\", \"Zurück\").replace(\"zurück\", \"zurück\")\n            .replace(\"Verfuegbar\", \"Verfügbar\").replace(\"verfügbar\", \"verfügbar\")\n            .replace(\"Ungültig\", \"Ungültig\").replace(\"ungültig\", \"ungültig\")\n            .replace(\"Enthaelt\", \"Enthält\").replace(\"enthaelt\", \"enthält\")\n            .replace(\"Ueber\", \"Über\").replace(\"ueber\", \"über\")\n            .replace(\"Für\", \"Für\").replace(\"für\", \"für\");\n    }\n\n    private List<String> colorList(List<String> lines) {\n        List<String> colored = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (String line : lines) {\n            colored.add(color(line));\n        }\n        return colored;\n    }\n\n    private record RewardTier(String key, double chance, String category, int coinsMin, int coinsMax, int tokensMin, int tokensMax, int experience) {\n    }\n\n    private record CatchReward(String name, String category, int coins, int tokens, int experience, ItemStack replacement, List<String> commands, boolean rare, boolean mystery, boolean broadcast, CatchMoment moment) {\n        private CatchReward combine(CatchReward other) {\n            ItemStack newReplacement = other.replacement() != null ? other.replacement() : replacement;\n            List<String> newCommands = new ArrayList<>(commands);\n            newCommands.addAll(other.commands());\n            return new CatchReward(other.name(), other.category(), coins + other.coins(), tokens + other.tokens(), experience + other.experience(), newReplacement, newCommands, rare || other.rare(), mystery || other.mystery(), broadcast || other.broadcast(), moment);\n        }\n\n        private CatchReward withMoment(CatchMoment newMoment) {\n            int newCoins = (int) Math.round(coins * newMoment.coinMultiplier());\n            int newTokens = (int) Math.round(tokens * newMoment.tokenMultiplier());\n            return new CatchReward(name, category, newCoins, newTokens, experience + newMoment.bonusXp(), replacement, commands, rare, mystery, broadcast, newMoment);\n        }\n\n        private CatchReward withReplacement(ItemStack newReplacement) {\n            return new CatchReward(name, category, coins, tokens, experience, newReplacement, commands, rare, mystery, broadcast, moment);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private record NetSummary(int pulls, int coins, int tokens, int mystery) {\n        private NetSummary() {\n            this(0, 0, 0, 0);\n        }\n\n        private NetSummary add(CatchReward reward) {\n            return new NetSummary(\n                    pulls + 1,\n                    coins + Math.max(0, reward.coins()),\n                    tokens + Math.max(0, reward.tokens()),\n                    mystery + (reward.mystery() ? 1 : 0)\n            );\n        }\n    }\n\n    private record CatchMoment(String name, String color, int size, double weight, double coinMultiplier, double tokenMultiplier, int bonusXp, List<String> messages) {\n        private static CatchMoment none() {\n            return new CatchMoment(\"\", \"&7\", 0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0, List.of());\n        }\n    }\n\n    private record FishingSpot(String id, String name, int minLevel, double coinMultiplier, double tokenMultiplier, double rareMultiplier, double jackpotMultiplier, double mysteryBonus, int sizeBonus, List<String> species) {\n    }\n\n    private record FishProfile(String key, String displayName, String habitat, String diet, int sizeMin, int sizeMax, double weightMin, double weightMax, int modelData) {\n    }\n\n    private record MiniEvent(String message, String sound, List<String> commands, double weight, boolean broadcast, String entityType, boolean lightning, double velocityY, String particle, int particleCount) {\n    }\n\n    private record DropView(String category, String key, ConfigurationSection section) {\n    }\n\n    private record MysteryDrop(String key, double weight, String name, String category, Material material, int amountMin, int amountMax, int sellPrice, int coinsMin, int coinsMax, int tokensMin, int tokensMax, int experience, String entityType, String skullOwner, String hdbHeadId, String leatherColor, String specialType, String lootboxId, List<String> stories, List<String> lore, List<String> enchantments, int repairCost, List<String> commands, boolean replaceCatch, boolean broadcast) {\n        private static MysteryDrop fromConfig(String key, ConfigurationSection section) {\n            Material material = Material.matchMaterial(section.getString(\"material\", section.getString(\"icon\", \"CHEST\")));\n            return new MysteryDrop(\n                    key,\n                    section.getDouble(\"weight\", 0.0),\n                    section.getString(\"name\", key),\n                    section.getString(\"category\", \"mystery\"),\n                    material == null ? Material.CHEST : material,\n                    section.getInt(\"amount-min\", section.getInt(\"amount\", 1)),\n                    section.getInt(\"amount-max\", section.getInt(\"amount\", 1)),\n                    section.getInt(\"sell-price\", 0),\n                    section.getInt(\"coins-min\", section.getInt(\"coins\", 0)),\n                    section.getInt(\"coins-max\", section.getInt(\"coins\", 0)),\n                    section.getInt(\"tokens-min\", section.getInt(\"tokens\", 0)),\n                    section.getInt(\"tokens-max\", section.getInt(\"tokens\", 0)),\n                    section.getInt(\"experience\", 0),\n                    section.getString(\"entity\", \"\"),\n                    section.getString(\"skull-owner\", \"\"),\n                    section.getString(\"hdb-head-id\", \"\"),\n                    section.getString(\"leather-color\", \"\"),\n                    section.getString(\"special-type\", \"\"),\n                    section.getString(\"lootbox\", \"\"),\n                    section.getStringList(\"stories\"),\n                    section.getStringList(\"lore\"),\n                    section.getStringList(\"enchantments\"),\n                    section.getInt(\"repair-cost\", 0),\n                    section.getStringList(\"commands\"),\n                    section.getBoolean(\"replace-catch\", true),\n                    section.getBoolean(\"broadcast\", false)\n            );\n        }\n\n        private CatchReward toReward(Player player, Entity caught) {\n            ItemStack item = null;\n            int amount = Math.max(1, randomAmount(amountMin, amountMax));\n            boolean usesHeadDatabase = hdbHeadId != null && !hdbHeadId.isBlank();\n            if (!usesHeadDatabase && replaceCatch && material != null && !material.isAir()) {\n                item = new ItemStack(material, amount);\n                ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n                if (meta != null) {\n                    meta.setDisplayName(ChatColor.translateAlternateColorCodes('&', name));\n                    meta.setLore(lore.stream().map(line -> ChatColor.translateAlternateColorCodes('&', line)).toList());\n                    meta.addItemFlags(ItemFlag.HIDE_ATTRIBUTES);\n                    item.setItemMeta(meta);\n                }\n                FishingPlugin plugin = JavaPlugin.getPlugin(FishingPlugin.class);\n                plugin.applySkullOwner(item, skullOwner);\n                plugin.applyLeatherColor(item, leatherColor);\n                plugin.applyEnchantments(item, enchantments);\n                plugin.applyRepairCost(item, repairCost);\n                plugin.applySpecialItemData(item, specialType, lootboxId, stories);\n                plugin.markFishingMarketItem(item, marketPrice());\n            }\n            if (entityType != null && !entityType.isBlank()) {\n                try {\n                    EntityType type = EntityType.valueOf(entityType.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));\n                    Location location = caught != null ? caught.getLocation() : player.getLocation();\n                    location.getWorld().spawnEntity(location, type);\n                } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) {\n                    // Invalid configured entity names are skipped so one typo cannot break fishing.\n                }\n            }\n            int coins = randomAmount(coinsMin, coinsMax);\n            int tokens = randomAmount(tokensMin, tokensMax);\n            FishingPlugin plugin = JavaPlugin.getPlugin(FishingPlugin.class);\n            List<String> rewardCommands = new ArrayList<>(commands);\n            if (usesHeadDatabase) {\n                rewardCommands.add(plugin.createHeadDatabaseCommand(player, hdbHeadId, amount));\n            }\n            return new CatchReward(ChatColor.stripColor(ChatColor.translateAlternateColorCodes('&', name)), category, coins, tokens, experience, item, rewardCommands, true, true, broadcast, CatchMoment.none());\n        }\n\n        private static int randomAmount(int min, int max) {\n            int lower = Math.min(min, max);\n            int upper = Math.max(min, max);\n            return lower + new Random().nextInt(upper - lower + 1);\n        }\n\n        private int marketPrice() {\n            if (sellPrice > 0) {\n                return sellPrice;\n            }\n            return Math.max(1, ((coinsMin + coinsMax) / 4) + (tokensMin + tokensMax) * 3 + experience);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private record RareItem(String tier, String name, Material material, int amountMin, int amountMax, int sellPrice, List<String> lore, List<String> enchantments, int repairCost, String skullOwner, String hdbHeadId, String leatherColor, List<String> commands) {\n        private ItemStack createItem() {\n            if (hdbHeadId != null && !hdbHeadId.isBlank()) {\n                return null;\n            }\n            int lower = Math.min(amountMin, amountMax);\n            int upper = Math.max(amountMin, amountMax);\n            int amount = Math.max(1, lower + new Random().nextInt(upper - lower + 1));\n            ItemStack item = new ItemStack(material, amount);\n            ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n            if (meta != null) {\n                meta.setDisplayName(ChatColor.translateAlternateColorCodes('&', name));\n                meta.setLore(lore.stream().map(line -> ChatColor.translateAlternateColorCodes('&', line)).toList());\n                meta.addItemFlags(ItemFlag.HIDE_ATTRIBUTES);\n                item.setItemMeta(meta);\n            }\n            FishingPlugin plugin = JavaPlugin.getPlugin(FishingPlugin.class);\n            plugin.applySkullOwner(item, skullOwner);\n            plugin.applyLeatherColor(item, leatherColor);\n            plugin.applyEnchantments(item, enchantments);\n            plugin.applyRepairCost(item, repairCost);\n            plugin.markFishingMarketItem(item, marketPrice());\n            return item;\n        }\n\n        private String displayName() {\n            return ChatColor.stripColor(ChatColor.translateAlternateColorCodes('&', name));\n        }\n\n        private int marketPrice() {\n            if (sellPrice > 0) {\n                return sellPrice;\n            }\n            if (\"jackpot\".equalsIgnoreCase(tier)) {\n                return material == Material.PLAYER_HEAD ? 700 : 450;\n            }\n            if (\"rare\".equalsIgnoreCase(tier)) {\n                return material == Material.PLAYER_HEAD ? 250 : 120;\n            }\n            if (\"good\".equalsIgnoreCase(tier)) {\n                return 35;\n            }\n            return 10;\n        }\n\n        private List<String> commands(Player player) {\n            List<String> rewardCommands = new ArrayList<>(commands);\n            if (hdbHeadId != null && !hdbHeadId.isBlank()) {\n                FishingPlugin plugin = JavaPlugin.getPlugin(FishingPlugin.class);\n                rewardCommands.add(plugin.createHeadDatabaseCommand(player, hdbHeadId, 1));\n            }\n            return rewardCommands;\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static final class AntiAfkState {\n        private Location location;\n        private int catches;\n\n        private AntiAfkState(Location location, int catches) {\n            this.location = location;\n            this.catches = catches;\n        }\n\n        private Location location() {\n            return location;\n        }\n\n        private int catches() {\n            return catches;\n        }\n\n        private void increment() {\n            catches++;\n        }\n\n        private void reset(Location newLocation) {\n            location = newLocation;\n            catches = 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    private record PlayerStat(String name, int catches, int mystery, int tokens) {\n    }\n\n    private record SimulationResult(int amount, String realm, String rodType, Map<String, Integer> tiers, Map<String, Integer> categories, Map<String, Integer> mysteryDrops, int mystery, int lootboxes, long coins, long tokens, long experience) {\n    }\n\n    private record QuestView(String type, String id, String name, String description, int progress, int amount, boolean claimed) {\n tierMultiplier(String tier) {\n            if (\"rare\".equalsIgnoreCase(tier)) {\n                return rareMultiplier;\n            }\n            if (\"jackpot\".equalsIgnoreCase(tier)) {\n                return jackpotMultiplier;\n            }\n            return 1.0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    private record FishingReactionHud(BossBar bar, BukkitTask task, long biteAt, long minMs, long event.setCancelled(true);\n            }\n            return;\n        }\n        event.setCancelled(true);\n        ItemStack clicked = event.getCurrentItem();\n        if (clicked == null || clicked.getType().isAir()) {\n            return;\n        }\n        if (event.getRawSlot() == 40) {\n            player.closeInventory();\n            return;\n        }\n        if (event.getRawSlot() == 31) {\n            openCosmetics(player);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (event.getRawSlot() == 30) {\n            openCosmetics(player);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (event.getRawSlot() == 24) {\n            startParkour(player);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (event.getRawSlot() == 32) {\n            claimDaily(player);\n            openNavigator(player);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (event.getRawSlot() == 20) {\n            openProfile(player);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (event.getRawSlot() == 13) String server = getConfig().getString(\"warps.\" + warp + \".server\", \"\");\n        if (server != null && !server.isBlank()) {\n            player.closeInventory();\n            connectToServer(player, server);\n            return;\n        }\n        Location location = readLocation(\"warps.\" + warp);\n        if (location == null) {\n            player.sendMessage(prefix() ByteArrayOutputStream();\n            DataOutputStream dataOutputStream = new DataOutputStream(byteArrayOutputStream);\n            dataOutputStream.writeUTF(\"Connect\");\n            dataOutputStream.writeUTF(server);\n            player.sendPluginMessage(this, \"BungeeCord\", byteArrayOutputStream.toByteArray());\n            player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.ENTITY_ENDERMAN_TELEPORT, 0.7f, 1.15f);\n            player.sendMessage(prefix() + color(\"&aVerbinde mit &f\" + server + \"&a...\"));\n        } catch (IOException exception) {\n            player.sendMessage(prefix() + color(\"&cVerbindung fehlgeschlagen: &f\" + exception.getMessage()));\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void reloadLobbyNpcs() {\n        removeLobbyNpcs();\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"npcs.enabled\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"npcs.list\");\n        if (section == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Location spawn = readLocation(\"spawn\");\n        if (spawn == null || spawn.getWorld() == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        for (String id : section.getKeys(false)) {\n            spawnLobbyNpc(id, spawn);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void removeLobbyNpcs() {\n        if (npcActionKey == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        for (World world : Bukkit.getWorlds()) {\n            for (Entity entity : world.getEntities()) {\n                if (entity.getPersistentDataContainer().has(npcActionKey, PersistentDataType.STRING) || isLegacyLobbyNpc(entity)) {\n                    entity.remove();\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private boolean isLegacyLobbyNpc(Entity entity) {\n        String name = entity.getCustomName();\n        if (name == null) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        String plain = ChatColor.stripColor(name).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n        return plain.contains(\"survival smp\")\n                || plain.contains(\"chaossmp\")\n                || plain.contains(\"parkour\")\n                || plain.contains(\"cosmetics\")\n                || plain.contains(\"navigator\")\n                || plain.contains(\"guide\");\n    }\n\n    private void spawnLobbyNpc(String id, Location spawn) {\n        String path = \"npcs.list.\" + id;\n        EntityType type = parseEntityType(getConfig().getString(path + \".type\", \"VILLAGER\"));\n        Location location = spawn.clone().add(\n                getConfig().getDouble(path + \".offset-x\", 0.0),\n                getConfig().getDouble(path + \".offset-y\", 0.0),\n                getConfig().getDouble(path + \".offset-z\", 0.0)\n        );\n        location.setYaw((float) getConfig().getDouble(path + \".yaw\", spawn.getYaw()));\n        Entity entity = spawn.getWorld().spawnEntity(location, type);\n        entity.getPersistentDataContainer().set(npcActionKey, PersistentDataType.STRING, getConfig().getString(path + \".action\", \"navigator\"));\n        entity.setCustomName(color(getConfig().getString(path + \".name\", \"&bNPC\")));\n        entity.setCustomNameVisible(true);\n        entity.setInvulnerable(true);\n        entity.setSilent(true);\n        if (entity instanceof LivingEntity living) {\n            living.setAI(false);\n            living.setCollidable(false);\n            living.setRemoveWhenFarAway(false);\n        }\n        if (entity instanceof Villager villager) {\n            villager.setProfession(parseVillagerProfession(getConfig().getString(path + \".profession\", \"NITWIT\")));\n            villager.setVillagerLevel(5);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private EntityType parseEntityType(String raw) {\n        try {\n            EntityType type = EntityType.valueOf(raw == null ? \"VILLAGER\" :  }\n    }\n\n    private Villager.Profession parseVillagerProfession(String raw) {\n        try {\n            return Villager.Profession.valueOf(raw == null ? \"NITWIT\" : player, String action) {\n        if (action.startsWith(\"server:\")) {\n            connectToServer(player, action.substring(\"server:\".length()));\n            return;\n        }\n        if (\"cosmetics\".equalsIgnoreCase(action)) {\n            openCosmetics(player);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (\"spawn\".equalsIgnoreCase(action)) {\n            teleportToSpawn(player);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (\"parkour\".equalsIgnoreCase(action)) {\n            startParkour(player);\n            return;\n        }\n        openNavigator(player);\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler\n    public void onMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n        if (parkourStartTimes.containsKey(player.getUniqueId()) && handleParkourFall(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Location spawn = readLocation(\"spawn\");\n        double voidY = getConfig().getDouble(\"settings.void-teleport-y\", 0.0);\n        if (spawn != null && spawn.getWorld() != null && spawn.getWorld().equals(player.getWorld())) {\n            voidY = Math.max(voidY, spawn.getY() - getConfig().getDouble(\"settings.void-fall-distance\", 10.0));\n        }\n        if (player.getLocation().getY() <= voidY) {\n            teleportToSpawn(player);\n            return;\n        }\n        handleParkourMove(player);\n        if (isSmpPortalPad(player, spawn)) {\n            long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n            long readyAt = portalCooldowns.getOrDefault(player.getUniqueId(), 0L);\n            if (readyAt <= now) {\n                portalCooldowns.put(player.getUniqueId(), now + 3_000L);\n                player.sendTitle(color(\"&d&lCHAOS&f&lSMP\"), color(\"&7Verbinde mit dem Survival\"), 0, 30, 10);\n                player.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.PORTAL, player.getLocation().add(0.0, 1.0, 0.0), 36, 0.5, 0.8, 0.5, 0.08);\n                connectToServer(player, getConfig().getString(\"network.smp-server\", \"smp\"));\n            }\n            return;\n        }\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"settings.launchpads\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Material pad = material(\"settings.launchpad-material\", Material.HEAVY_WEIGHTED_PRESSURE_PLATE);\n        if (player.getLocation().getBlock().getType() != pad) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Vector direction = player.getLocation().getDirection().setY(0).normalize();\n        double forward = getConfig().getDouble(\"settings.launchpad-forward\", 1.9);\n        double up = getConfig().getDouble(\"settings.launchpad-up\", 0.95);\n        player.setVelocity(direction.multiply(forward).setY(up));\n        player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.ENTITY_FIREWORK_ROCKET_LAUNCH, 0.75f, 1.35f);\n    }\n\n    private boolean isSmpPortalPad(Player player, Location spawn) {\n        if (spawn == null || spawn.getWorld() == null || !spawn.getWorld().equals(player.getWorld())) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        Location location = player.getLocation();\n        double relativeX = location.getX() - spawn.getX();\n        double relativeZ = location.getZ() - spawn.getZ();\n        return Math.abs(relativeX) <= 3.5 && relativeZ <= -8.0 && relativeZ >= -10.5\n                && location.getBlock().getType() == Material.HEAVY_WEIGHTED_PRESSURE_PLATE;\n    }\n\n    private void useBooster(Player player) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"settings.booster-gadget\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        long readyAt = boosterCooldowns.getOrDefault(player.getUniqueId(), 0L);\n        if (readyAt > now) {\n            long seconds = Math.max(1L, (readyAt - now + 999L) / 1000L);\n            player.sendMessage(prefix() + message(\"messages.booster-cooldown\").replace(\"%seconds%\", String.valueOf(seconds)));\n            return;\n        }\n        boosterCooldowns.put(player.getUniqueId(), now + 12_000L);\n        Vector velocity = player.getLocation().getDirection().multiply(1.45).setY(0.72);\n        player.setVelocity(velocity);\n        player.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.FIREWORK, player.getLocation().add(0.0, 0.8, 0.0), 22, 0.25, 0.35, 0.25, 0.04);\n        player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.ENTITY_FIREWORK_ROCKET_LAUNCH, 0.8f, 1.4f);\n        addLobbyXp(player, getConfig().getInt(\"rewards.gadget-xp\", 5));\n    }\n\n    private void startParkour(Player player) {\n        Location spawn = readLocation(\"spawn\");\n        if (spawn == null || spawn.getWorld() == null) {\n            player.sendMessage(prefix() + color(\"&cParkour ist noch nicht bereit.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        clearParkourBlocks(player.getUniqueId());\n        Location startBlock = spawn.clone().add(0.0, getConfig().getDouble(\"settings.parkour-start-height\", 22.0), 0.0);\n        startBlock.setX(Math.floor(startBlock.getX()) + 0.0);\n        startBlock.setY(Math.floor(startBlock.getY()));\n        startBlock.setZ(Math.floor(startBlock.getZ()) + 0.0);\n        List<Location> blocks = new ArrayList<>();\n        blocks.add(startBlock);\n        parkourBlocks.put(player.getUniqueId(), blocks);\n        placeParkourBlock(startBlock, Material.GOLD_BLOCK);\n        createNextParkourBlock(player.getUniqueId(), 1);\n        parkourStartTimes.put(player.getUniqueId(), System.currentTimeMillis());\n        parkourStages.put(player.getUniqueId(), 0);\n        player.setAllowFlight(false);\n        player.setFlying(false);\n        Location start = startBlock.clone().add(0.5, 1.1, 0.5);\n        start.setYaw(spawn.getYaw());\n        start.setPitch(0.0f);\n        player.teleport(start);\n        player.playSound(start, Sound.BLOCK_NOTE_BLOCK_PLING, 0.8f, 1.6f);\n        player.sendMessage(prefix() + color(\"&aParkour gestartet. &7Springe zu Checkpoint &f1&7.\"));\n    }\n\n    private void handleParkourMove(Player player) {\n        Location belowLocation = player.getLocation().clone().subtract(0.0, 1.0, 0.0);\n        Material below = belowLocation.getBlock().getType();\n        if (below == Material.GOLD_BLOCK && !parkourStartTimes.containsKey(player.getUniqueId())) {\n            parkourStartTimes.put(player.getUniqueId(), System.currentTimeMillis());\n            parkourStages.put(player.getUniqueId(), 0);\n            player.sendMessage(prefix() + color(\"&aParkour Timer gestartet.\"));\n            player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.BLOCK_NOTE_BLOCK_PLING, 0.7f, 1.4f);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (!parkourStartTimes.containsKey(player.getUniqueId())) {\n            return;\n        }\n        int currentStage = parkourStages.getOrDefault(player.getUniqueId(), 0);\n        int landedStage = parkourStageAt(player.getUniqueId(), belowLocation);\n        if (landedStage <= currentStage) {\n            return;\n        }\n        parkourStages.put(player.getUniqueId(), landedStage);\n        int maxStage = getConfig().getInt(\"settings.parkour-stages\", 12);\n        if (landedStage < maxStage) {\n            createNextParkourBlock(player.getUniqueId(), landedStage + 1);\n            trimParkourBlocks(player.getUniqueId(), landedStage);\n            player.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.END_ROD, player.getLocation().add(0.0, 0.8, 0.0), 14, 0.25, 0.35, 0.25, 0.02);\n            player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.BLOCK_NOTE_BLOCK_PLING, 0.7f, 1.2f + landedStage * 0.05f);\n            player.sendMessage(prefix() + color(\"&aSprung geschafft! &7Naechster Checkpoint: &f\" + (landedStage + 1) + \"&7.\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        long millis = System.currentTimeMillis() - parkourStartTimes.remove(player.getUniqueId());\n        parkourStages.remove(player.getUniqueId());\n        if (getConfig().getBoolean(\"settings.double-jump\", true)) {\n            player.setAllowFlight(true);\n        }\n        clearParkourBlocks(player.getUniqueId());\n        double seconds = millis / 1000.0;\n        String bestPath = \"parkour.best.\" + player.getUniqueId();\n        double best = getConfig().getDouble(bestPath, 0.0);\n        boolean record = best <= 0.0 || seconds < best;\n        if (record) {\n            getConfig().set(bestPath, Math.round(seconds * 100.0) / 100.0);\n            saveConfig();\n        }\n        addLobbyXp(player, getConfig().getInt(\"rewards.parkour-finish-xp\", 75));\n        player.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.FIREWORK, player.getLocation().add(0.0, 1.0, 0.0), 28, 0.35, 0.5, 0.35, 0.04);\n        player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.UI_TOAST_CHALLENGE_COMPLETE, 0.9f, 1.1f);\n        player.sendMessage(prefix() + color(\"&dParkour geschafft: &f\" + String.format(Locale.US, \"%.2fs\", seconds) + (record ? \" &aNEUER REKORD!\" : \"\")));\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler\n    public void onToggleFlight(PlayerToggleFlightEvent event) {\n        Player player = event.getPlayer();\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"settings.double-jump\", true) || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE || player.getGameMode() == GameMode.SPECTATOR) {\n            return;\n        }\n        event.setCancelled(true);\n        if (parkourStartTimes.containsKey(player.getUniqueId())) {\n            player.setAllowFlight(false);\n            return;\n        }\n        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        long lastJump = jumpCooldowns.getOrDefault(player.getUniqueId(), 0L);\n        if (now - lastJump < 900L) {\n            return;\n        }\n        jumpCooldowns.put(player.getUniqueId(), now);\n        player.setAllowFlight(false);\n        player.setFlying(false);\n        player.setVelocity(player.getLocation().getDirection().multiply(1.25).setY(0.85));\n        player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.ENTITY_BREEZE_JUMP, 0.7f, 1.6f);\n        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskLater(this, () -> {\n            if (player.isOnline()) {\n                player.setAllowFlight(true);\n            }\n        }, 20L);\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onBlockBreak(BlockBreakEvent event) {\n        protect(event.getPlayer(), event);\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onBlockPlace(BlockPlaceEvent event) {\n        protect(event.getPlayer(), event);\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onDamage(EntityDamageEvent event) {\n        Entity entity = event.getEntity();\n        if (entity.getPersistentDataContainer().has(npcActionKey, PersistentDataType.STRING)) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n            return;\n        }\n        if (entity instanceof Player && protectionEnabled()) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onFood(FoodLevelChangeEvent event) {\n        if (event.getEntity() instanceof Player && protectionEnabled()) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onDrop(PlayerDropItemEvent event) {\n        if (protectionEnabled() && !canBuild(event.getPlayer())) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)\n    public void onSwap(PlayerSwapHandItemsEvent event) {\n        if (protectionEnabled() && !canBuild(event.getPlayer())) {\n            event.setCancelled(true);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void protect(Player player, org.bukkit.event.Cancellable event) {\n        if (!protectionEnabled() || canBuild(player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        event.setCancelled(true);\n        player.sendMessage(prefix() + message(\"messages.build-denied\"));\n    }\n\n    private void openNavigator(Player player) {\n        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(null, 45, MENU_TITLE);\n        ItemStack filler = item(Material.BLACK_STAINED_GLASS_PANE, \"&8\", List.of());\n        ItemStack accent = item(Material.PURPLE_STAINED_GLASS_PANE, \"&5\", List.of());\n        for (int slot = 0; slot < inventory.getSize(); slot++) {\n            inventory.setItem(slot, filler);\n        }\n        for (int slot : new int[]{0, 1, 2, 6, 7, 8, 9, 17, 27, 35, 36, 37, 38, 42, 43, 44}) {\n            inventory.setItem(slot, accent);\n        }\n        inventory.setItem(4, item(Material.NETHER_STAR, \"&d&lCHAOS&f&lSMP\", List.of(\"&7Wähle deinen nächsten Schritt.\", \"&8Lobby Navigator\")));\n        inventory.setItem(20, item(Material.PLAYER_HEAD, \"&b&lDein Profil\", List.of(\"&7Level: &f\" + lobbyLevel(player), \"&7XP: &f\" + lobbyXp(player) + \"&8/&f\" + nextLevelXp(player), \"&eKlicken zum Öffnen\")));\n        inventory.setItem(24, item(Material.LIGHT_WEIGHTED_PRESSURE_PLATE, \"&e&lSky Parkour\", List.of(\"&7Persoenlicher dynamischer Parcours.\", \"&7Bestzeit: &f\" + parkourBestText(player), \"&eKlicken zum Starten\")));\n        inventory.setItem(30, item(Material.AMETHYST_SHARD, \"&d&lCosmetics\", List.of(\"&7Partikel, Auren und Trails.\", \"&7Aktiv: &f\" + selectedCosmetic.getOrDefault(player.getUniqueId(), \"none\"), \"&eKlicken zum Öffnen\")));\n        inventory.setItem(32, item(dailyClaimedToday(player) ? Material.MINECART : Material.CHEST_MINECART, \"&6&lDaily Reward\", List.of(\"&7Status: \" + (dailyClaimedToday(player) ? \"&cHeute abgeholt\" : \"&aBereit\"), \"&7Belohnung: &d+\" + getConfig().getInt(\"rewards.daily-xp\", 120) + \" Lobby-XP\", dailyClaimedToday(player) ? \"&8Morgen wieder verfügbar\" : \"&eKlicken zum Abholen\")));\n        ConfigurationSection warps = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"warps\");\n        if (warps != null) {\n            for (String key : warps.getKeys(false)) {\n                String path = \"warps.\" + key;\n                int slot = Math.max(0, Math.min(44, getConfig().getInt(path + \".slot\", 22)));\n                Material icon = material(path + \".material\", Material.ENDER_PEARL);\n                String display = getConfig().getString(path + \".display\", \"&b\" + key);\n                List<String> lore = getConfig().getStringList(path + \".lore\");\n                inventory.setItem(slot, item(icon, display, lore));\n            }\n        }\n        inventory.setItem(40, item(Material.BARRIER, \"&cSchliessen\", List.of(\"&7Zurück ins Spiel.\")));\n        player.openInventory(inventory);\n    }\n\n    private String parkourBestText(Player player) {\n        double best = getConfig().getDouble(\"parkour.best.\" + player.getUniqueId(), 0.0);\n        return best <= 0.0 ? \"Noch keine Zeit\" : String.format(Locale.US, \"%.2fs\", best);\n    }\n\n    private void giveJoinItems(Player player) {\n        if (canBuild(player) && !getConfig().getBoolean(\"settings.clear-inventory-on-join\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        giveConfiguredItem(player, \"join-items.navigator\");\n        giveConfiguredItem(player, \"join-items.profile\");\n        giveConfiguredItem(player, \"join-items.cosmetics\");\n        giveVisibilityItem(player);\n    }\n\n    private void giveConfiguredItem(Player player, String path) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(path + \".enabled\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        int slot = Math.max(0, Math.min(8, getConfig().getInt(path + \".slot\", 4)));\n        Material material = material(path + \".material\", Material.COMPASS);\n        String name = getConfig().getString(path + \".name\", \"&bItem\");\n        List<String> lore = getConfig().getStringList(path + \".lore\");\n        player.getInventory().setItem(slot, item(material, name, lore));\n    }\n\n    private void giveVisibilityItem(Player player) {\n        String path = \"join-items.visibility\";\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(path + \".enabled\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        boolean hidden = playersHidden.getOrDefault(player.getUniqueId(), false);\n        int slot = Math.max(0, Math.min(8, getConfig().getInt(path + \".slot\", 8)));\n        Material material = hidden ? material(path + \".hidden-material\", Material.GRAY_DYE) : material(path + \".material\", Material.LIME_DYE);\n        String name = hidden ? getConfig().getString(path + \".hidden-name\", \"&cSpieler versteckt\") : getConfig().getString(path + \".name\", \"&aSpieler sichtbar\");\n        player.getInventory().setItem(slot, item(material, name, getConfig().getStringList(path + \".lore\")));\n    }\n\n    private boolean handleParkourFall(Player player) {\n        List<Location> blocks = parkourBlocks.get(player.getUniqueId());\n        Location reference = blocks == null || blocks.isEmpty() ? player.getLocation() : blocks.get(Math.max(0, Math.min(blocks.size() - 1, parkourStages.getOrDefault(player.getUniqueId(), 0))));\n        if (player.getLocation().getY() >= reference.getY() - getConfig().getDouble(\"settings.parkour-fail-distance\", 8.0)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        clearParkourBlocks(player.getUniqueId());\n        parkourStartTimes.remove(player.getUniqueId());\n        parkourStages.remove(player.getUniqueId());\n        if (getConfig().getBoolean(\"settings.double-jump\", true)) {\n            player.setAllowFlight(true);\n        }\n        teleportToSpawn(player);\n        player.sendMessage(prefix() + color(\"&cParkour reset. &7Du bist heruntergefallen.\"));\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    private int parkourStageAt(UUID uuid, Location blockLocation) {\n        List<Location> blocks = parkourBlocks.get(uuid);\n        if (blocks == null || blockLocation == null || blockLocation.getWorld() == null) {\n            return -1;\n        }\n        for (int i = 0; i < blocks.size(); i++) {\n            Location location = blocks.get(i);\n            if (location.getWorld() != null\n                    && location.getWorld().equals(blockLocation.getWorld())\n                    && location.getBlockX() == blockLocation.getBlockX()\n                    && location.getBlockY() == blockLocation.getBlockY()\n                    && location.getBlockZ() == blockLocation.getBlockZ()) {\n                return i;\n            }\n        }\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    private void createNextParkourBlock(UUID uuid, int stage) {\n        List<Location> blocks = parkourBlocks.get(uuid);\n        if (blocks == null || blocks.isEmpty() || blocks.size() > stage) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Location previous = blocks.get(blocks.size() - 1);\n        int[][] offsets = new int[][]{{3, 0, 1}, {3, 1, -1}, {2, 0, 3}, {3, -1, 0}, {2, 1, -3}, {4, 0, 0}, {3, 1, 2}, {2, -1, -2}};\n        int[] offset = offsets[Math.floorMod(stage - 1, offsets.length)];\n        Location next = previous.clone().add(offset[0], offset[1], offset[2]);\n        next.setY(Math.max(previous.getWorld().getMinHeight() + 8, Math.min(previous.getWorld().getMaxHeight() - 6, next.getY())));\n        blocks.add(next);\n        Material material = stage >= getConfig().getInt(\"settings.parkour-stages\", 12) ? Material.EMERALD_BLOCK : (stage % 3 == 0 ? Material.PRISMARINE_BRICKS : Material.SMOOTH_QUARTZ);\n        placeParkourBlock(next, material);\n        next.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.END_ROD, next.clone().add(0.5, 1.1, 0.5), 14, 0.2, 0.25, 0.2, 0.01);\n    }\n\n    private void placeParkourBlock(Location location, Material material) {\n        if (location == null || location.getWorld() == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        location.getBlock().setType(material, false);\n        location.clone().add(0, 1, 0).getBlock().setType(Material.AIR, false);\n        location.clone().add(0, 2, 0).getBlock().setType(Material.AIR, false);\n    }\n\n    private void trimParkourBlocks(UUID uuid, int landedStage) {\n        List<Location> blocks = parkourBlocks.get(uuid);\n        if (blocks == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        for (int i = 0; i < landedStage - 1 && i < blocks.size(); i++) {\n            Location location = blocks.get(i);\n            if (location != null && location.getWorld() != null) {\n                location.getBlock().setType(Material.AIR, false);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void clearParkourBlocks(UUID uuid) {\n        List<Location> blocks = parkourBlocks.remove(uuid);\n        if (blocks == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        for (Location location : blocks) {\n            if (location != null && location.getWorld() != null) {\n                location.getBlock().setType(Material.AIR, false);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private ItemStack item(Material material, String name, List<String> lore) {\n        ItemStack stack = new ItemStack(material);\n        ItemMeta meta = stack.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta != null) {\n            meta.setDisplayName(color(name));\n            List<String> coloredLore = new ArrayList<>();\n            for (String line : lore) {\n                coloredLore.add(color(line));\n            }\n            meta.setLore(coloredLore);\n            meta.addItemFlags(ItemFlag.values());\n            stack.setItemMeta(meta);\n        }\n        return stack;\n    }\n\n    private boolean isLobbyItem(ItemStack stack) {\n        if (stack == null || !stack.hasItemMeta()) {\n            return false;\n        }\n        String name = plainName(stack);\n        return name.contains(NAVIGATOR_KEY) || name.contains(SPAWN_ITEM_KEY) || name.contains(SPEED_ITEM_KEY) || name.contains(VISIBLE_ITEM_KEY) || name.contains(HIDDEN_ITEM_KEY) || name.contains(COSMETICS_KEY) || name.contains(PROFILE_KEY) || name.contains(BOOSTER_KEY);\n    }\n\n    private void openProfile(Player player) {\n        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(null, 45, PROFILE_TITLE);\n        ItemStack filler = item(Material.BLACK_STAINED_GLASS_PANE, \"&8\", List.of());\n        ItemStack accent = item(Material.BLUE_STAINED_GLASS_PANE, \"&9\", List.of());\n        for (int slot = 0; slot < inventory.getSize(); slot++) {\n            inventory.setItem(slot, filler);\n        }\n        for (int slot : new int[]{0, 1, 7, 8, 9, 17, 27, 35, 36, 37, 43, 44}) {\n            inventory.setItem(slot, accent);\n        }\n        double best = getConfig().getDouble(\"parkour.best.\" + player.getUniqueId(), 0.0);\n        String bestText = best <= 0.0 ? \"-\" : String.format(Locale.US, \"%.2fs\", best);\n        String dailyText = dailyClaimedToday(player) ? \"&cHeute abgeholt\" : \"&aBereit\";\n        inventory.setItem(4, item(Material.PLAYER_HEAD, \"&b&l\" + player.getName(), List.of(\n                \"&7Lobby-Level: &d\" + lobbyLevel(player),\n                \"&7XP: &f\" + lobbyXp(player) + \"&8/&f\" + nextLevelXp(player),\n                \"&7Parkour-Rekord: &e\" + bestText,\n                \"&7Cosmetic: &f\" + selectedCosmetic.getOrDefault(player.getUniqueId(), \"none\")\n        )));\n        inventory.setItem(20, item(Material.CHEST_MINECART, \"&6&lDaily Reward\", List.of(\"&7Status: \" + dailyText, \"&7Belohnung: &d+\" + getConfig().getInt(\"rewards.daily-xp\", 120) + \" XP\", \"&eClick: Abholen\")));\n        inventory.setItem(22, item(Material.GOLD_BLOCK, \"&e&lParkour\", List.of(\"&7Bestzeit: &f\" + bestText, \"&eClick: Starten\")));\n        inventory.setItem(24, item(Material.AMETHYST_SHARD, \"&d&lCosmetics\", List.of(\"&7Aktuell: &f\" + selectedCosmetic.getOrDefault(player.getUniqueId(), \"none\"), \"&eClick: Öffnen\")));\n        inventory.setItem(40, item(Material.ARROW, \"&eZurück\", List.of(\"&7Zurück zum Navigator.\")));\n        player.openInventory(inventory);\n    }\n\n    private void openCosmetics(Player player) {\n        Inventory inventory = Bukkit.createInventory(null, 45, COSMETICS_TITLE);\n        ItemStack filler inventory.getSize(); slot++) {\n            inventory.setItem(slot, filler);\n        }\n        for (int slot : new int[]{0, 1, 7, 8, 9, 17, 27, 35, 36, 37, 43, 44}) {\n            inventory.setItem(slot, accent);\n        }\n        inventory.setItem(4, item(Material.AMETHYST_SHARD, \"&d&lLobby Cosmetics\", List.of(\"&7Wähle deinen Lobby-Effekt.\")));\n        inventory.setItem(20, cosmeticItem(player, \"hearts\", Material.RED_DYE, \"&c&lHerz Aura\", \"&7Sanfte Herz-Partikel.\"));\n        inventory.setItem(21, cosmeticItem(player, \"flame\",
