\"&3&lSoul Orbit\", \"&7Kalte Seelenflammen kreisen um dich.\"));\n        inventory.setItem(30, cosmeticItem(player, \"cherry\", Material.CHERRY_LEAVES, \"&d&lCherry Bloom\", \"&7Sanfte Blüten für den Spawn.\"));\n        inventory.setItem(32, cosmeticItem(player, \"void\", Material.ECHO_SHARD, \"&5&lVoid Pulse\", \"&7Dunkle End-Partikel mit Premium-Gefühl.\"));\n        inventory.setItem(33, cosmeticItem(player, \"royal\", Material.AMETHYST_CLUSTER, \"&d&lRoyal Spark\", \"&7Glitzernde Amethyst-Sparks.\"));\n        inventory.setItem(31, cosmeticItem(player, \"none\", Material.BARRIER, \"&cCosmetic deaktivieren\", \"&7Entfernt deinen Effekt.\"));\n        inventory.setItem(40, item(Material.ARROW, \"&eZurück\", List.of(\"&7Zurück zum Navigator.\")));\n        player.openInventory(inventory);\n    }\n\n    private ItemStack cosmeticItem(Player player, String id, Material material, String name, String lore) {\n        boolean selected = selectedCosmetic.getOrDefault(player.getUniqueId(), \"none\").equals(id);\n        List<String> lines = new ArrayList<>();\n        lines.add(lore);\n        lines.add(\"&8\");\n        lines.add(selected ? \"&aAktuell ausgewählt\" : \"&eClick: Auswählen\");\n        ItemStack stack = item(material, name, lines);\n        if (selected && stack.hasItemMeta()) {\n            ItemMeta meta = stack.getItemMeta();\n            meta.addEnchant(org.bukkit.enchantments.Enchantment.LUCK_OF_THE_SEA, 1, true);\n            meta.addItemFlags(ItemFlag.HIDE_ENCHANTS);\n            stack.setItemMeta(meta);\n        }\n        return stack;\n    }\n\n    private void handleCosmeticsClick(Player player, int slot) {\n        if (slot == 40) {\n            openNavigator(player);\n            return;\n        }\n        String id = switch (slot) {\n            case 20 -> \"hearts\";\n            case 21 -> \"flame\";\n            case 22 -> \"cloud\";\n            case 23 -> \"magic\";\n            case 24 -> \"crit\";\n            case 29 -> \"soul\";\n            case 30 -> \"cherry\";\n            case 32 -> \"void\";\n            case 33 -> \"royal\";\n            case 31 -> \"none\";\n            default -> null;\n        };\n        if (id == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        selectedCosmetic.put(player.getUniqueId(), id);\n        getConfig().set(\"cosmetics.selected.\" + player.getUniqueId(), id);\n        saveConfig();\n        player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.BLOCK_AMETHYST_BLOCK_CHIME, 0.8f, 1.2f);\n        player.sendMessage(prefix() + color(id.equals(\"none\") ? \"&7Cosmetic deaktiviert.\" : \"&aCosmetic ausgewählt: &f\" + id));\n        openCosmetics(player);\n    }\n\n    private void claimDaily(Player player) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"settings.daily-rewards\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        if (dailyClaimedToday(player)) {\n            player.sendMessage(prefix() + message(\"messages.daily-already-claimed\"));\n            player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.BLOCK_NOTE_BLOCK_BASS, 0.7f, 0.8f);\n            return;\n        }\n        int xp = getConfig().getInt(\"rewards.daily-xp\", 120);\n        getConfig().set(playerPath(player) + \".daily-last\", LocalDate.now().toString());\n        addLobbyXp(player, xp);\n        saveConfig();\n        player.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.HAPPY_VILLAGER, player.getLocation().add(0.0, 1.0, 0.0), 24, 0.35, 0.45, 0.35, 0.02);\n        player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.UI_TOAST_CHALLENGE_COMPLETE, 0.8f, 1.35f);\n        player.sendMessage(prefix() + message(\"messages.daily-claimed\").replace(\"%xp%\", String.valueOf(xp)));\n    }\n\n    private boolean dailyClaimedToday(Player player) {\n        return LocalDate.now().toString().equals(getConfig().getString(playerPath(player) + \".daily-last\", \"\"));\n    }\n\n    private void addLobbyXp(Player player, int amount) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"settings.lobby-levels\", true) || amount <= 0) {\n            return;\n        }\n        String path = playerPath(player);\n        int beforeLevel = lobbyLevel(player);\n        int xp = lobbyXp(player) + amount;\n        getConfig().set(path + \".xp\", xp);\n        int afterLevel = lobbyLevelForXp(xp);\n        getConfig().set(path + \".level\", afterLevel);\n        saveConfig();\n        if (afterLevel > beforeLevel) {\n            player.sendTitle(color(\"&dLevel Up\"), color(\"&7Lobby-Level &f\" + afterLevel), 5, 35, 10);\n            player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.ENTITY_PLAYER_LEVELUP, 0.8f, 1.25f);\n            player.sendMessage(prefix() + message(\"messages.level-up\").replace(\"%level%\", String.valueOf(afterLevel)));\n        }\n    }\n\n    private int lobbyXp(Player player) {\n        return Math.max(0, getConfig().getInt(playerPath(player) + \".xp\", 0));\n    }\n\n    private int lobbyLevel(Player player) {\n        int stored = getConfig().getInt(playerPath(player) + \".level\", 1);\n        return Math.max(stored, lobbyLevelForXp(lobbyXp(player)));\n    }\n\n    private int lobbyLevelForXp(int xp) {\n        int base = Math.max(50, getConfig().getInt(\"rewards.level-base-xp\", 250));\n        int level = 1;\n        int remaining = Math.max(0, xp);\n        while (remaining >= base * level) {\n            remaining -= base * level;\n            level++;\n        }\n        return level;\n    }\n\n    private int nextLevelXp(Player player) {\n        int base = Math.max(50, getConfig().getInt(\"rewards.level-base-xp\", 250));\n        int level = lobbyLevel(player);\n        int totalForLevelStart = 0;\n        for (int i = 1; i < level; i++) {\n            totalForLevelStart += base * i;\n        }\n        return totalForLevelStart + base * level;\n    }\n\n    private String playerPath(Player player) {\n        return \"players.\" + player.getUniqueId();\n    }\n\n    private void teleportToSpawn(Player player) {\n        Location spawn = readLocation(\"spawn\");\n        if (spawn == null) {\n            player.sendMessage(prefix() + message(\"messages.spawn-missing\"));\n            return;\n        }\n        parkourStartTimes.remove(player.getUniqueId());\n        parkourStages.remove(player.getUniqueId());\n        clearParkourBlocks(player.getUniqueId());\n        if (getConfig().getBoolean(\"settings.double-jump\", true)) {\n            player.setAllowFlight(true);\n        }\n        player.teleport(spawn);\n        player.playSound(spawn, Sound.ENTITY_ENDERMAN_TELEPORT, 0.8f, 1.25f);\n        player.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.END_ROD, spawn.clone().add(0.0, 1.2, 0.0), 24, 0.35, 0.6, 0.35, 0.02);\n        player.sendMessage(prefix() + message(\"messages.teleported\"));\n    }\n\n    private void ensureLobbySpawn() {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"settings.auto-build-spawn\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        World world = Bukkit.getWorlds().isEmpty() ? null : Bukkit.getWorlds().get(0);\n        if (world == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        int y = getConfig().getInt(\"settings.spawn-platform-y\", 99);\n        int radius = 14;\n        world.setStorm(false);\n        world.setThundering(false);\n        world.setTime(6000L);\n\n        for (int x = -radius - 4; x <= radius + 4; x++) {\n            for (int z = -radius - 4; z <= radius + 4; z++) {\n                for (int clearY = y - 6; clearY <= y + 12; clearY++) {\n                    Block block = world.getBlockAt(x, clearY, z);\n                    if (isAutoLobbyBlock(block.getType())) {\n                        block.setType(Material.AIR, false);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (int x = -radius; x <= radius; x++) {\n            for (int z = -radius; z <= radius; z++) {\n                double distance = Math.sqrt((double) x * x + (double) z * z);\n                if (distance > radius + 0.2) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                Material floor = Material.POLISHED_ANDESITE;\n                if (distance > radius - 1.2) floor = Material.POLISHED_BLACKSTONE_BRICKS;\n                else if (Math.abs(x) <= 1 || Math.abs(z) <= 1) floor = Material.SMOOTH_QUARTZ;\n                else if (distance > 8.0 && distance < 9.4) floor = Material.OXIDIZED_CUT_COPPER;\n                else if (distance < 4.2) floor = Material.TUFF_BRICKS;\n                world.getBlockAt(x, y, z).setType(floor, false);\n                if (distance <= radius - 1.0) world.getBlockAt(x, y - 1, z).setType(Material.TUFF_BRICKS, false);\n                if (distance <= radius - 3.0) world.getBlockAt(x, y - 2, z).setType(Material.DEEPSLATE_BRICKS, false);\n                for (int clearY = y + 1; clearY <= y + 7; clearY++) {\n                    world.getBlockAt(x, clearY, z).setType(Material.AIR, false);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (int[] light : new int[][]{{0, 4}, {0, -4}, {4, 0}, {-4, 0}}) {\n            world.getBlockAt(light[0], y, light[1]).setType(Material.SEA_LANTERN, false);\n        }\n        for (int[] pylon : new int[][]{{-9, -9}, {-9, 9}, {9, -9}, {9, 9}}) {\n            world.getBlockAt(pylon[0], y + 1, pylon[1]).setType(Material.POLISHED_BLACKSTONE_BRICKS, false);\n            world.getBlockAt(pylon[0], y + 2, pylon[1]).setType(Material.WEATHERED_CUT_COPPER, false);\n            world.getBlockAt(pylon[0], y + 3, pylon[1]).setType(Material.SEA_LANTERN, false);\n            world.getBlockAt(pylon[0], y + 4, pylon[1]).setType(Material.END_ROD, false);\n        }\n\n        buildLobbySidePad(world, y, -10, 2, Material.CHERRY_PLANKS, Material.PINK_GLAZED_TERRACOTTA);\n        buildLobbySidePad(world, y, 10, 2, Material.PRISMARINE_BRICKS, Material.LIGHT_BLUE_GLAZED_TERRACOTTA);\n        buildSmpPortal(world, y);\n        clearStaticParkour(world, y + 2);\n\n        getConfig().set(\"spawn.world\", world.getName());\n        getConfig().set(\"spawn.x\", 0.5);\n        getConfig().set(\"spawn.y\", y + 1.0);\n        getConfig().set(\"spawn.z\", 0.5);\n        getConfig().set(\"spawn.yaw\", 180.0);\n        getConfig().set(\"spawn.pitch\", 0.0);\n        world.setSpawnLocation(0, y + 1, 0);\n        saveConfig();\n    }\n\n    private void buildLobbySidePad(World world, int y, int centerX, int centerZ, Material floor, Material center) {\n        for (int x = centerX - 2; x <= centerX + 2; x++) {\n            for (int z = centerZ - 2; z <= centerZ + 2; z++) {\n                if (Math.abs(x - centerX) + Math.abs(z - centerZ) > 3) continue;\n                world.getBlockAt(x, y + 1, z).setType(floor, false);\n            }\n        }\n        world.getBlockAt(centerX, y + 1, centerZ).setType(center, false);\n        world.getBlockAt(centerX, y + 2, centerZ).setType(Material.END_ROD, false);\n    }\n\n    private void buildSmpPortal(World world, int y) {\n        int portalZ = -13;\n        for (int x = -4; x <= 4; x++) {\n            for (int portalY = y + 1; portalY <= y + 6; portalY++) {\n                boolean frame = x == -4 || x == 4 || portalY == y + 1 || portalY == y + 6;\n                world.getBlockAt(x, portalY, portalZ).setType(frame ? (x % 2 == 0 ? Material.CRYING_OBSIDIAN : Material.OBSIDIAN) : Material.PURPLE_STAINED_GLASS, false);\n            }\n        }\n        for (int z = -12; z <= -8; z++) {\n            for (int x = -3; x <= 3; x++) {\n                world.getBlockAt(x, y, z).setType(Math.abs(x) <= 1 ? Material.SMOOTH_QUARTZ : Material.POLISHED_BLACKSTONE_BRICKS, false);\n            }\n        }\n        for (int x = -3; x <= 3; x++) {\n            world.getBlockAt(x, y + 1, -9).setType(Material.HEAVY_WEIGHTED_PRESSURE_PLATE, false);\n        }\n        world.getBlockAt(-5, y + 1, portalZ).setType(Material.SOUL_LANTERN, false);\n        world.getBlockAt(5, y + 1, portalZ).setType(Material.SOUL_LANTERN, false);\n    }\n\n    private void ensureLobbySpawnLegacy() {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"settings.auto-build-spawn\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        World world = Bukkit.getWorlds().isEmpty() ? null : Bukkit.getWorlds().get(0);\n        if (world == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        int y = getConfig().getInt(\"settings.spawn-platform-y\", 99);\n        int radius = 9;\n        for (int x = -radius - 4; x <= radius + 4; x++) {\n            for (int z = -radius - 4; z <= radius + 4; z++) {\n                for (int clearY = y - 18; clearY <= y + 28; clearY++) {\n                    Block block = world.getBlockAt(x, clearY, z);\n                    if (isAutoLobbyBlock(block.getType())) {\n                        block.setType(Material.AIR, false);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        for (int x = -radius; x <= radius; x++) {\n            for (int z = -radius; z <= radius; z++) {\n                double dist = Math.sqrt((double) x * x + (double) z * z);\n                if (dist > radius + 0.35) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                Material material = dist > radius - 1.2 ? Material.DEEPSLATE_TILES : (Math.abs(x) <= 1 || Math.abs(z) <= 1 ? Material.SMOOTH_QUARTZ : Material.POLISHED_ANDESITE);\n                world.getBlockAt(x, y, z).setType(material, false);\n                for (int clearY = y + 1; clearY <= y + 5; clearY++) {\n                    world.getBlockAt(x, clearY, z).setType(Material.AIR, false);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        for (int[] pos : new int[][]{{-7, -7}, {-7, 7}, {7, -7}, {7, 7}}) {\n            world.getBlockAt(pos[0], y + 1, pos[1]).setType(Material.POLISHED_BLACKSTONE, false);\n            world.getBlockAt(pos[0], y + 2, pos[1]).setType(Material.SEA_LANTERN, false);\n            world.getBlockAt(pos[0], y + 3, pos[1]).setType(Material.IRON_BARS, false);\n        }\n        for (int[] pos : new int[][]{{0, -10}, {-10, 0}, {10, 0}}) {\n            world.getBlockAt(pos[0], y + 1, pos[1]).setType(Material.DEEPSLATE_BRICKS, false);\n            world.getBlockAt(pos[0], y + 2, pos[1]).setType(Material.END_ROD, false);\n        }\n        for (int z = -11; z <= -9; z++) {\n            for (int x = -2; x <= 2; x++) {\n                world.getBlockAt(x, y, z).setType(Material.SMOOTH_QUARTZ, false);\n            }\n        }\n        for (int x : new int[]{-2, 2}) {\n            world.getBlockAt(x, y + 1, -10).setType(Material.POLISHED_BLACKSTONE, false);\n            world.getBlockAt(x, y + 2, -10).setType(Material.POLISHED_BLACKSTONE, false);\n            world.getBlockAt(x, y + 3, -10).setType(Material.SEA_LANTERN, false);\n        }\n        for (int x = -3; x <= 3; x++) {\n            world.getBlockAt(x, y + 1, -8).setType(Material.HEAVY_WEIGHTED_PRESSURE_PLATE, false);\n        }\n        clearStaticParkour(world, y + 2);\n        getConfig().set(\"spawn.world\", world.getName());\n        getConfig().set(\"spawn.x\", 0.5);\n        getConfig().set(\"spawn.y\", y + 2.0);\n        getConfig().set(\"spawn.z\", 0.5);\n        getConfig().set(\"spawn.yaw\", 180.0);\n        getConfig().set(\"spawn.pitch\", 0.0);\n        saveConfig();\n    }\n\n    private boolean isAutoLobbyBlock(Material material) {\n        return material == Material.DEEPSLATE_TILES\n                || material == Material.SMOOTH_QUARTZ\n                || material == Material.POLISHED_ANDESITE\n                || material == Material.POLISHED_BLACKSTONE\n                || material == Material.SEA_LANTERN\n                || material == Material.IRON_BARS\n                || material == Material.DEEPSLATE_BRICKS\n                || material == Material.END_ROD\n                || material == Material.HEAVY_WEIGHTED_PRESSURE_PLATE\n                || material == Material.GOLD_BLOCK\n                || material == Material.EMERALD_BLOCK\n                || material == Material.PRISMARINE_BRICKS\n                || material == Material.POLISHED_BLACKSTONE_BRICKS\n                || material == Material.TUFF_BRICKS\n                || material == Material.OXIDIZED_CUT_COPPER\n                || material == Material.WEATHERED_CUT_COPPER\n                || material == Material.CHERRY_PLANKS\n                || material == Material.PINK_GLAZED_TERRACOTTA\n                || material == Material.LIGHT_BLUE_GLAZED_TERRACOTTA\n                || material == Material.CRYING_OBSIDIAN\n                || material == Material.OBSIDIAN\n                || material == Material.PURPLE_STAINED_GLASS\n                || material == Material.SOUL_LANTERN;\n    }\n\n    private void clearStaticParkour(World world, int baseY) {\n        int[][] path = parkourPath();\n        for (int[] point : path) {\n            int x = point[0];\n            int y = baseY + point[1];\n            int z = point[2];\n            for (int dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {\n                for (int dz = -1; dz <= 1; dz++) {\n                    for (int dy = 0; dy <= 2; dy++) {\n                        world.getBlockAt(x + dx, y + dy, z + dz).setType(Material.AIR, false);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void buildParkour(World world, int baseY) {\n        int[][] path = parkourPath();\n        for (int i = 0; i < path.length; i++) {\n            int x = path[i][0];\n            int y = baseY + path[i][1];\n            int z = path[i][2];\n            Material material = i == 0 ? Material.GOLD_BLOCK : i == path.length - 1 ? Material.EMERALD_BLOCK : (i % 2 == 0 ? Material.SMOOTH_QUARTZ : Material.PRISMARINE_BRICKS);\n            world.getBlockAt(x, y, z).setType(material, false);\n            world.getBlockAt(x, y + 1, z).setType(Material.AIR, false);\n            if (i == 0 || i == path.length - 1) {\n                for (int dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {\n                    for (int dz = -1; dz <= 1; dz++) {\n                        world.getBlockAt(x + dx, y, z + dz).setType(material, false);\n                        world.getBlockAt(x + dx, y + 1, z + dz).setType(Material.AIR, false);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            world.getBlockAt(x, y + 2, z).setType(i == path.length - 1 ? Material.END_ROD : Material.AIR, false);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private Location parkourStartLocation() {\n        return parkourStageLocation(0);\n    }\n\n    private int[][] parkourPath() {\n        return new int[][]{\n                {12, 0, 0}, {15, 0, 0}, {18, 1, 2}, {21, 1, 2}, {24, 2, 0},\n                {27, 2, -2}, {30, 3, -2}, {33, 3, 0}, {36, 4, 2}, {39, 4, 0}\n        };\n    }\n\n    private Location parkourStageLocation(int stage) {\n        Location spawn = readLocation(\"spawn\");\n        if (spawn == null || spawn.getWorld() == null) {\n            return null;\n        }\n        int[][] path = parkourPath();\n        int index = Math.max(0, Math.min(path.length - 1, stage));\n        int[] point = path[index];\n        return new Location(spawn.getWorld(), point[0] + 0.5, spawn.getY() + point[1] + 1.0, point[2] + 0.5, -90.0f, 0.0f);\n    }\n\n    private void teleportToParkourStage(Player player, int stage) {\n        Location location = parkourStageLocation(stage);\n        if (location == null) {\n            teleportToSpawn(player);\n            return;\n        }\n        player.teleport(location);\n        player.playSound(location, Sound.ENTITY_ENDERMAN_TELEPORT, 0.5f, 1.35f);\n    }\n\n    private int parkourStageAt(Location blockLocation) {\n        Location spawn = readLocation(\"spawn\");\n        if (spawn == null || spawn.getWorld() == null || blockLocation == null || blockLocation.getWorld() == null || !spawn.getWorld().equals(blockLocation.getWorld())) {\n            return -1;\n        }\n        int[][] path = parkourPath();\n        for (int i = 0; i < path.length; i++) {\n            int x = path[i][0];\n            int y = spawn.getBlockY() + path[i][1];\n            int z = path[i][2];\n            if (blockLocation.getBlockX() == x && blockLocation.getBlockY() == y && blockLocation.getBlockZ() == z) {\n                return i;\n            }\n            if ((i == 0 || i == path.length - 1) && Math.abs(blockLocation.getBlockX() - x) <= 1 && blockLocation.getBlockY() == y && Math.abs(blockLocation.getBlockZ() - z) <= 1) {\n                return i;\n            }\n        }\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    private void toggleSpeed(Player player) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"settings.speed-toggle\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        boolean enabled = !speedEnabled.getOrDefault(player.getUniqueId(), false);\n        speedEnabled.put(player.getUniqueId(), enabled);\n        if (enabled) {\n            player.addPotionEffect(new PotionEffect(PotionEffectType.SPEED, Integer.MAX_VALUE, 1, true, false, false));\n            player.sendMessage(prefix() + message(\"messages.speed-enabled\"));\n            player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.BLOCK_NOTE_BLOCK_PLING, 0.7f, 1.5f);\n        } else {\n            player.removePotionEffect(PotionEffectType.SPEED);\n            player.sendMessage(prefix() + message(\"messages.speed-disabled\"));\n            player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.BLOCK_NOTE_BLOCK_BASS, 0.7f, 0.8f);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void toggleVisibility(Player player) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"settings.player-hider\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        boolean hidden = !playersHidden.getOrDefault(player.getUniqueId(), false);\n        playersHidden.put(player.getUniqueId(), hidden);\n        applyVisibility(player);\n        giveVisibilityItem(player);\n        player.sendMessage(prefix() + message(hidden ? \"messages.players-hidden\" : \"messages.players-shown\"));\n        player.playSound(player.getLocation(), Sound.UI_BUTTON_CLICK, 0.8f, hidden ? 0.85f : 1.25f);\n    }\n\n    private void startCosmeticTask() {\n        if (cosmeticTask != null) {\n            cosmeticTask.cancel();\n        }\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"cosmetics.enabled\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        cosmeticTask = Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimer(this, () -> {\n            for (Player player : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {\n                playCosmetic(player);\n            }\n            if (++ambientTicks % 5 == 0) {\n                playLobbyAmbient();\n            }\n        }, 20L, 8L);\n    }\n\n    private void playLobbyAmbient() {\n        Location spawn = readLocation(\"spawn\");\n        if (spawn == null || spawn.getWorld() == null || spawn.getWorld().getPlayers().isEmpty()) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Location portal = spawn.clone().add(0.0, 2.0, -13.5);\n        spawn.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.PORTAL, portal, 10, 2.4, 2.1, 0.15, 0.035);\n        if (ambientTicks % 20 == 0) {\n            spawn.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.END_ROD, spawn.clone().add(0.0, 1.2, 0.0), 5, 3.0, 0.3, 3.0, 0.01);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void playCosmetic(Player player) {\n        String id = selectedCosmetic.getOrDefault(player.getUniqueId(), \"none\");\n        if (\"none\".equals(id)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Location location = player.getLocation().add(0.0, 0.15, 0.0);\n        switch (id) {\n            case \"hearts\" -> player.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.HEART, location.clone().add(0.0, 1.2, 0.0), 1, 0.35, 0.25, 0.35, 0.0);\n            case \"flame\" -> player.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.FLAME, location, 4, 0.25, 0.05, 0.25, 0.01);\n            case \"cloud\" -> player.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.CLOUD, location, 3, 0.22, 0.03, 0.22, 0.01);\n            case \"magic\" -> player.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.PORTAL, location.clone().add(0.0, 0.8, 0.0), 8, 0.28, 0.55, 0.28, 0.02);\n            case \"crit\" -> player.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.CRIT, location.clone().add(0.0, 1.0, 0.0), 6, 0.3, 0.45, 0.3, 0.02);\n            case \"soul\" -> player.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.SOUL_FIRE_FLAME, location.clone().add(0.0, 0.35, 0.0), 4, 0.28, 0.22, 0.28, 0.01);\n            case \"cherry\" -> player.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.CHERRY_LEAVES, location.clone().add(0.0, 1.1, 0.0), 5, 0.35, 0.3, 0.35, 0.01);\n            case \"void\" -> player.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.REVERSE_PORTAL, location.clone().add(0.0, 0.75, 0.0), 10, 0.25, 0.55, 0.25, 0.02);\n            case \"royal\" -> player.getWorld().spawnParticle(Particle.END_ROD, location.clone().add(0.0, 0.9, 0.0), 5, 0.32, 0.45, 0.32, 0.015);\n            default -> {\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void applyVisibility(Player viewer) {\n        boolean hidden = playersHidden.getOrDefault(viewer.getUniqueId(), false);\n        for (Player target : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {\n            if getConfig().set(path + \".y\", round(location.getY()));\n        getConfig().set(path + \".z\", round(location.getZ()));\n        getConfig().set(path + \".yaw\", round(location.getYaw()));\n  }\n        boardTask = Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimer(this, () -> {\n            for (Player player : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {\n                updateScoreboard(player);\n                sendActionbar(player);\n            }\n        }, 20L, 60L);\n    }\n\n    private void updateScoreboard(Player player) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"scoreboard.enabled\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        Scoreboard scoreboard = Objects.requireNonNull(Bukkit.getScoreboardManager()).getNewScoreboard();\n        Objective objective = lines = getConfig().getStringList(\"scoreboard.lines\");\n        int score = lines.size();\n        for (String raw : lines) {\n            String line = placeholders(player, raw);\n            objective.getScore(uniqueLine(line, score)).setScore(score);\n            score--;\n        }\n        player.setScoreboard(scoreboard);\n    }\n\n    private String uniqueLine(String line, int score)   private void sendActionbar(Player player) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"settings.actionbar\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        if player.sendActionBar(color(text));\n    }\n\n    private String findWarpBySlot(int slot) {\n        ConfigurationSection warps = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"warps\");\n        if (warps == key;\n            }\n        }\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    private int firstFreeWarpSlot() {\n        boolean[] used = new boolean[27];\n        ConfigurationSection warps = \".slot\", -1);\n                if (slot >= 0 && slot < used.length) {\n                    used[slot] = true;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        for (int slot : List.of(10, 11, if (player.hasPermission(\"lobbysystem.admin\")) {\n            return true;\n        }\n        player.sendMessage(prefix() + message(\"messages.no-permission\"));\n        return false;\n    }\n\n addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"give\");\n            addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"list\");\n            addIfMatches(result, args[0], \"reload\");\n        } else if (args.length == 2 &&  return result;\n    }\n\n    private boolean handlePrefixCommand(CommandSender sender) {\n        if (!(sender instanceof Player)) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cThis command sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cSpieler nicht gefunden.\"));\n                return true;\n            }\n            int amount = args.length > amountArgIndex ? target.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&aDu hast einen Prefix-Gutschein erhalten: &e\" + id));\n            }\n            return true;\n        }\n        sendPrefixAdminUsage(sender, label);\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler\n    public void onInventoryClick(InventoryClickEvent event) {\n        if (!(event.getWhoClicked() instanceof Player)) {\n            return;\n        }\n event.setCancelled(true);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler\n    public void onPlayerJoin(PlayerJoinEvent event) {\n        applyPlayerNames(event.getPlayer());\n    }\n\n    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)\n    public void onPaperAsyncChat(AsyncChatEvent event) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"chat.enabled\", true) ||           return;\n        }\n        event.renderer(ChatRenderer.viewerUnaware((source, sourceDisplayName, message) -> {\n            String legacyMessage = .replace(\"{rank}\", rankPrefix(source))\n                    .replace(\"{player}\", styledPlayerName(source))\n                    .replace(\"{message}\", legacyMessage);\n            return if (existingFormat.contains(\"%1$s\")) {\n                event.setFormat(existingFormat.replace(\"%1$s\", formattedPlayer));\n            } else {\n player.getInventory().addItem(createPrefixVoucher(id, 1));\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&aPrefix-Gutschein erhalten: &e\" + id));\n    }\n\n    private ItemStack        }\n        return item;\n    }\n\n    private ItemStack createPrefixVoucher(String id, int amount) {\n        Material material = materialFromConfig(\"prefix-items.\" + id +     meta.setDisplayName(color(getConfig().getString(\"prefix-items.\" + id + \".voucher-name\", getPrefixDisplayName(id) + \" &7Gutschein\")));\n            List<String> lore = lore.add(\"&8PrefixReward: {id}:{color}\");\n            }\n            List<String> coloredLore = new ArrayList<String>();\n            for (String line : lore) {\n                coloredLore.add(color(line.replace(\"{id}\", id).replace(\"{color}\", getPrefixColor(id))));\n            }\n            meta.setLore(coloredLore);\n            item.setItemMeta(meta);\n        }\n (stripped.isEmpty()) {\n            return \"\";\n        }\n        String prefixText = team.getPrefix();\n        if (stripped.endsWith(\"|\")) {\n            return prefixText + \" \";\n        }\n        return prefixText + \" &8| \";\n    }\n\n    private void applyPlayerNames(Player player) {\n        if (!getConfig().getBoolean(\"chat.update-display-name\", true)) {\n            return;\n        }\n        String styledName = color(styledPlayerName(player));\n        player.setDisplayName(styledName);\n        if (getConfig().getBoolean(\"chat.update-tab-name\", false)) {\n            try {\n                player.setPlayerListName(styledName);\n            } catch (IllegalArgumentException exception) {\n                getLogger().fine(\"Could not set tab name for \" + player.getName() + \": \" + exception.getMessage());\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private String prefixIdByGuiSlot(int slot) {\n        for (String id : prefixIds()) {\n            if (getConfig().getInt(\"prefix-items.\" + id + \".slot\", -1) == slot) {\n                return id;\n            }\n        }\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    private Set<String> prefixIds() {\n        ConfigurationSection section = getConfig().getConfigurationSection(\"prefix-items\");\n        if (section == null) {\n            return Collections.emptySet();\n        }\n        return section.getKeys(false);\n    }\n\n    private boolean prefixExists(String id) {\n        return getConfig().isConfigurationSection(\"prefix-items.\" + id);\n    }\n\n    private String getPrefixDisplayName(String id) {\n        return color(getConfig().getString(\"prefix-items.\" + id + \".display-name\", \"&f\" + prettifyKey(id)));\n    }\n\n    private String getPrefixColor(String id) {\n        return getConfig().getString(\"prefix-items.\" + id + \".chat-color\", \"&f\");\n    }\n\n    private String getPrefixStyle(Player player) {\n        return dataConfig.getString(playerPath(player.getUniqueId()) + \".prefix-style\", \"classic\");\n    }\n\n    private boolean isGradientEnabled(String id) {\n        return getConfig().getBoolean(\"prefix-items.\" + id + \".gradient.enabled\", false);\n    }\n\n    private String getGradientStart(String id) {\n        return getConfig().getString(\"prefix-items.\" + id + \".gradient.start\", \"FFFFFF\");\n    }\n\n    private String getGradientEnd(String id) {\n        return getConfig().getString(\"prefix-items.\" + id + \".gradient.end\", \"FFFFFF\");\n    }\n\n    private Material materialFromConfig(String path, Material fallback) {\n        Material material = Material.matchMaterial(getConfig().getString(path, fallback.name()));\n        return material == null ? fallback : material;\n    }\n\n    private ItemStack createPlayerHead(UUID uuid, String name, String... lore) {\n        ItemStack item = new ItemStack(Material.PLAYER_HEAD);\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta instanceof SkullMeta) {\n            SkullMeta skullMeta = (SkullMeta)meta;\n            skullMeta.setOwningPlayer(Bukkit.getOfflinePlayer(uuid));\n            skullMeta.setDisplayName(color(name));\n            skullMeta.setLore(colorList(lore));\n            item.setItemMeta(skullMeta);\n        } else if (meta != null) {\n            meta.setDisplayName(color(name));\n            meta.setLore(colorList(lore));\n            item.setItemMeta(meta);\n        }\n        return item;\n    }\n\n    private ItemStack createGuiItem(Material material, String name, String... lore) {\n        ItemStack item = new ItemStack(material);\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta != null) {\n            meta.setDisplayName(color(name));\n            meta.setLore(colorList(lore));\n            item.setItemMeta(meta);\n        }\n        return item;\n    }\n\n    private List<String> colorList(String... lore) {\n        List<String> result = new ArrayList<String>();\n        for (String line : lore) {\n            result.add(color(line));\n        }\n        return result;\n    }\n\n    private void fillBorder(Inventory inventory, Material material, String name) {\n        ItemStack border = createGuiItem(material, name);\n        int size = inventory.getSize();\n        for (int i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n            int row = i / 9;\n            int col = i % 9;\n            if (row == 0 || row == size / 9 - 1 || col == 0 || col == 8) {\n                inventory.setItem(i, border);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void addGuiGlint(ItemStack item) {\n        Enchantment enchantment = Enchantment.getByName(\"DURABILITY\");\n        if (enchantment == null) {\n            enchantment = Enchantment.getByName(\"UNBREAKING\");\n        }\n        if (enchantment != null) {\n            item.addUnsafeEnchantment(enchantment, 1);\n        }\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta != null) {\n            meta.addItemFlags(ItemFlag.HIDE_ENCHANTS);\n            item.setItemMeta(meta);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private String gradientName(String text, String startHex, String endHex) {\n        return gradientName(text, startHex, endHex, false);\n    }\n\n    private String gradientName(String text, String startHex, String endHex, boolean bold) {\n        int start = parseHexColor(startHex, 0xFFFFFF);\n        int end = parseHexColor(endHex, 0xFFFFFF);\n        if (text.length() <= 1) {\n            return hexColor(start) + (bold ? ChatColor.BOLD : \"\") + text;\n        }\n        StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();\n        for (int i = 0; i < text.length(); ++i) {\n            double ratio = (double)i / (double)(text.length() - 1);\n            int r = interpolate((start >> 16) & 0xFF, (end >> 16) & 0xFF, ratio);\n            int g = interpolate((start >> 8) & 0xFF, (end >> 8) & 0xFF, ratio);\n            int b = interpolate(start & 0xFF, end & 0xFF, ratio);\n            builder.append(hexColor((r << 16) | (g << 8) | b));\n            if (bold) {\n                builder.append(ChatColor.BOLD);\n            }\n            builder.append(text.charAt(i));\n        }\n        return builder.toString();\n    }\n\n    private String hexColor(int rgb) {\n        String hex = String.format(\"%06X\", rgb);\n        StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder(\"\\u00A7x\");\n        for (int i = 0; i < hex.length(); ++i) {\n            builder.append('\\u00A7').append(hex.charAt(i));\n        }\n        return builder.toString();\n    }\n\n    private int parseHexColor(String text, int fallback) {\n        try {\n            return Integer.parseInt(text.replace(\"#\", \"\"), 16);\n        } catch (NumberFormatException exception) {\n            return fallback;\n        }\n    }\n\n    private int interpolate(int start, int end, double ratio) {\n        return (int)Math.round(start + (end - start) * ratio);\n    }\n\n    private String prefixGuiTitle() {\n        return color(getConfig().getString(\"gui.title\", \"&dPrefix Farben\"));\n    }\n\n    private String prefixAdminGuiTitle() {\n        return color(getConfig().getString(\"gui.admin-title\", \"&dPrefix Admin\"));\n    }\n\n    private String prefix() {\n        return getConfig().getString(\"system.prefix\", \"&6&lSMP Test &8| &r\");\n    }\n\n    private String playerPath(UUID uuid) {\n        return \"players.\" + uuid.toString();\n    }\n\n    private String normalizeKey(String text) {\n        return text.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).replaceAll(\"[^a-z0-9_-]\", \"\");\n    }\n\n    private String prettifyKey(String text) {\n        String[] parts = text.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).replace('-', '_').split(\"_\");\n        StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();\n        for (String part : parts) {\n            if (part.isEmpty()) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            if (builder.length() > 0) {\n                builder.append(' ');\n            }\n            builder.append(Character.toUpperCase(part.charAt(0))).append(part.substring(1));\n        }\n        return builder.length() == 0 ? text : builder.toString();\n    }\n\n    private int parsePositiveInt(String text, int fallback) {\n        try {\n            int value = Integer.parseInt(text);\n            return value > 0 ? value : fallback;\n        } catch (NumberFormatException exception) {\n            return fallback;\n        }\n    }\n\n    private String color(String text) {\n        return ChatColor.translateAlternateColorCodes('&', text);\n    }\n\n    private Component legacy(String text) {\n        return LegacyComponentSerializer.legacySection().deserialize(color(text));\n    }\n\n    private void addIfMatches(List<String> list, String typed, String value) {\n        if (value.startsWith(typed.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT))) {\n            list.add(value);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void sendPrefixAdminUsage(CommandSender sender, String label) {\n        sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&e/\" + label + \" gui &7- Gutschein-GUI oeffnen\"));\n        sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&e/\" + label + \" give <id> [spieler] [anzahl]\"));\n        sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&e/\" + label + \" list &7- Prefixe anzeigen\"));\n        sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&e/\" + label + \" reload &7- Config neu laden\"));\n    }\n\n    private void saveDataFile() {\n        try {\n            dataConfig.save(dataFile);\n        } catch (IOException exception) {\n            getLogger().warning(\"Could not save data.yml: \" + exception.getMessage());\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void registerCommand(String name) {\n        if (getCommand(name) != null) {\n            getCommand(name).setExecutor(this);\n            getCommand(name).setTabCompleter(this);\n        }\n    }\n}\n"}, "Signierungs System": {"file": "SignSystemPlugin.java", "code": "package de.smp.sign;\n\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport org.bukkit.Material;\nimport org.bukkit.NamespacedKey;\nimport org.bukkit.ChatColor;\nimport org.bukkit.command.Command;\nimport org.bukkit.command.CommandExecutor;\nimport org.bukkit.command.CommandSender;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;\nimport org.bukkit.inventory.meta.ItemMeta;\nimport org.bukkit.persistence.PersistentDataType;\nimport org.bukkit.plugin.Plugin;\nimport org.bukkit.plugin.java.JavaPlugin;\n\npublic final class SignSystemPlugin extends JavaPlugin implements CommandExecutor {\n    private NamespacedKey signerKey;\n\n    @Override\n    public void onEnable() {\n        saveDefaultConfig();\n        signerKey = new NamespacedKey((Plugin)this, \"signer_uuid\");\n        if (getCommand(\"sign\") != null) {\n            getCommand(\"sign\").setExecutor(this);\n        }\n        if (getCommand(\"unsign\") != null) {\n            getCommand(\"unsign\").setExecutor(this);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean onCommand(CommandSender sender, Command command, String label, String[] args) {\n        if (\"sign\".equalsIgnoreCase(command.getName())) {\n            return handleSignCommand(sender, args);\n        }\n        if (\"unsign\".equalsIgnoreCase(command.getName())) {\n            return handleUnsignCommand(sender);\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    private boolean handleSignCommand(CommandSender sender, String[] args) {\n        if (!(sender instanceof org.bukkit.entity.Player)) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cThis command can only be used by players.\"));\n            return true;\n        }\n        org.bukkit.entity.Player player = (org.bukkit.entity.Player)sender;\n        ItemStack item = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();\n        if (item == null || item.getType() == Material.AIR) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cHalte ein Item in der Hand.\"));\n            return true;\n        }\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        if (meta != null) {\n            if (meta.getPersistentDataContainer().has(signerKey, PersistentDataType.STRING)) {\n                player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDieses Item ist bereits signiert.\"));\n                return true;\n            }\n            List<String> lore = meta.hasLore() ? new ArrayList<String>(meta.getLore()) : new ArrayList<String>();\n            for (String line : lore) {\n                if (ChatColor.stripColor(line).startsWith(\"Signiert von \")) {\n                    player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cDieses Item ist bereits signiert.\"));\n                    return true;\n                }\n            }\n            if (args.length > 0) {\n                lore.add(color(\"&7\\\"\" + joinArgs(args, 0) + \"\\\"\"));\n            }\n            lore.add(color(\"&8Signiert von &6\" + player.getName()));\n            meta.setLore(lore);\n            meta.getPersistentDataContainer().set(signerKey, PersistentDataType.STRING, player.getUniqueId().toString());\n            item.setItemMeta(meta);\n        }\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&aItem signiert.\"));\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    private boolean handleUnsignCommand(CommandSender sender) {\n        if (!(sender instanceof org.bukkit.entity.Player)) {\n            sender.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cThis command can only be used by players.\"));\n            return true;\n        }\n        org.bukkit.entity.Player player = (org.bukkit.entity.Player)sender;\n        ItemStack item = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();\n        if (item == null || item.getType() == Material.AIR || !item.hasItemMeta() || !item.getItemMeta().hasLore()) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cHalte ein signiertes Item in der Hand.\"));\n            return true;\n        }\n        ItemMeta meta = item.getItemMeta();\n        List<String> lore = new ArrayList<String>(meta.getLore());\n        String signer = meta.getPersistentDataContainer().get(signerKey, PersistentDataType.STRING);\n        boolean signedByPlayer = player.getUniqueId().toString().equals(signer);\n        if (!signedByPlayer) {\n            player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&cNur der Signierer kann dieses Item unsignen.\"));\n            return true;\n        }\n        List<String> newLore = new ArrayList<String>();\n        for (int i = 0; i < lore.size(); ++i) {\n            String line = lore.get(i);\n            String stripped = ChatColor.stripColor(line);\n            boolean nextLineIsSignature = i + 1 < lore.size() && ChatColor.stripColor(lore.get(i + 1)).startsWith(\"Signiert von \");\n            if (stripped.startsWith(\"Signiert von \") || nextLineIsSignature) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            newLore.add(line);\n        }\n        meta.setLore(newLore.isEmpty() ? null : newLore);\n        meta.getPersistentDataContainer().remove(signerKey);\n        item.setItemMeta(meta);\n        player.sendMessage(color(prefix() + \"&aSignatur entfernt.\"));\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    private String prefix() {\n        return getConfig().getString(\"system.prefix\", \"&6&lSMP Test &8| &r\");\n    }\n\n    private String color(String text) {\n        return org.bukkit.ChatColor.translateAlternateColorCodes('&', text);\n    }\n\n    private String joinArgs(String[] args, int start) {\n        StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();\n        for (int i = start; i < args.length; ++i) {\n            if (i > start) {\n                builder.append(' ');\n            }\n            builder.append(args[i]);\n        }\n        return builder.toString();\n    }\n}\n"}}</script>
<script>
// ---- Systeme: hier eintragen ----
var SYSTEMS=[
  {name:"ChaosGuard",desc:"Ban- und AntiCheat-System mit über 25 Prüfungen, etwa KillAura, Reach, Speed, Flight, Scaffold und XRay. Meldet Verstöße an das Team und kickt oder bannt automatisch.",url:"#"},
  {name:"CustomCraftingAltar",desc:"Crafting-Altar mit Beacon als Kern, an dem Spieler mit seltenen Zutaten besondere Items herstellen. Enthält den Colorfloor für die Walkblocking Boots.",url:"#"},
  {name:"EPlus",desc:"Admin-Befehl, der dem Item in der Hand Verzauberungen hinzufügt, auch über das normale Level hinaus und als eigene Verzauberung mit Lore-Text.",url:"#"},
  {name:"AurumMorph",desc:"Menü für Körpergröße und Glowfarben. Freigeschaltet wird über Siegel-Items, die man in einer Werkstatt erstellt.",url:"#"},
  {name:"FishingSystem",desc:"Angelsystem mit Level und XP, Ködern, Daily- und Weekly-Quests, Fang-Collection, Angelpass, Lootboxen und Events.",url:"#"},
  {name:"Lobby System",desc:"Lobby mit Navigator-Menü, Profil mit Level und XP, Sky Parkour mit Bestzeit, Cosmetics, täglicher Belohnung und NPCs.",url:"#"},
  {name:"Prefix System",desc:"Menü für freischaltbare Chat-Prefixes. Freigeschaltet wird über Gutschein-Items, der gewählte Prefix erscheint im Chat.",url:"#"},
  {name:"Signierungs System",desc:"Mit /sign signierst du ein Item mit deinem Namen und einem Spruch. /unsign entfernt die Signatur, aber nur für den Signierer.",url:"#"}
];
var LANGS=["Java","SQL","YAML","HTML & CSS","JavaScript","Python"];
var lg=document.getElementById("langs");
LANGS.forEach(function(l){var c=document.createElement("span");c.className="chip";c.textContent=l;lg.appendChild(c)});
var list=document.getElementById("systemList");
var CODES=JSON.parse(document.getElementById("codes").textContent);
var KW=/\b(abstract|boolean|break|case|catch|class|continue|default|else|enum|extends|final|finally|for|if|implements|import|instanceof|int|long|double|float|new|null|package|private|protected|public|return|static|super|switch|this|throw|throws|true|false|try|void|while|var|record)\b/;
var TOK=new RegExp("(\\/\\/.*|\\/\\*[\\s\\S]*?\\*\\/)|(\"(?:\\\\.|[^\"\\\\\\n])*\")|"+KW.source,"g");
function esc(t){return t.replace(/&/g,"&amp;").replace(/</g,"&lt;").replace(/>/g,"&gt;")}
function hl(src){
  var out="",last=0,m;TOK.lastIndex=0;
  while((m=TOK.exec(src))){
    out+=esc(src.slice(last,m.index));
    var cls=m[1]?"c":m[2]?"s":"k";
    out+='<span class="'+cls+'">'+esc(m[0])+'</span>';
    last=TOK.lastIndex;
  }
  return out+esc(src.slice(last));
}
SYSTEMS.forEach(function(s){
  var wrap=document.createElement("div");wrap.className="sys";
  var a=document.createElement("a");a.className="row";a.href=s.url;
  var b=document.createElement("b");b.textContent=s.name;
  var d=document.createElement("span");d.textContent=s.desc;
  var e=document.createElement("em");e.textContent="Öffnen";
  a.appendChild(b);a.appendChild(d);a.appendChild(e);wrap.appendChild(a);
  var c=CODES[s.name];
  if(c){
    var det=document.createElement("details"),sm=document.createElement("summary"),pre=document.createElement("pre");
    sm.textContent="Code anzeigen ("+c.file+", "+c.code.replace(/\n$/,"").split("\n").length+" Zeilen)";
    pre.className="code";pre.tabIndex=0;pre.setAttribute("aria-label","Quellcode "+c.file);
    det.appendChild(sm);det.appendChild(pre);
    det.addEventListener("toggle",function(){if(det.open&&!pre.dataset.done){pre.innerHTML=hl(c.code);pre.dataset.done="1"}});
    wrap.appendChild(det);
  }
  list.appendChild(wrap);
});
document.getElementById("yr").textContent=new Date().getFullYear();

// ---- Hinweis-Toast ----
var toast=document.getElementById("toast"),tt;
function say(m){toast.textContent=m;toast.classList.add("on");clearTimeout(tt);tt=setTimeout(function(){toast.classList.remove("on")},2200)}

// ---- Kopierschutz ----
function okTarget(n){n=n&&(n.nodeType===3?n.parentNode:n);return !!(n&&n.closest&&n.closest(".allow"))}
["contextmenu","dragstart","selectstart"].forEach(function(t){
  document.addEventListener(t,function(e){if(t==="selectstart"&&okTarget(e.target))return;e.preventDefault();if(t==="contextmenu")say("Rechtsklick ist deaktiviert.")})
});
["copy","cut"].forEach(function(t){
  document.addEventListener(t,function(e){var s=getSelection();if(s&&okTarget(s.anchorNode))return;e.preventDefault();if(e.clipboardData)e.clipboardData.setData("text/plain","Kopieren ist auf dieser Seite nicht erlaubt.");say("Kopieren ist nicht erlaubt.")})
});
document.addEventListener("keydown",function(e){
  var k=(e.key||"").toLowerCase(),c=e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey;
  var block=e.key==="F12"||(c&&["u","s","p","a","c","x"].indexOf(k)>-1&&!(["c","a"].indexOf(k)>-1&&okTarget(getSelection().anchorNode)))||(c&&e.shiftKey&&["i","j","c"].indexOf(k)>-1);
  if(block){e.preventDefault();say("Diese Aktion ist deaktiviert.")}
});
document.querySelectorAll("[data-copy]").forEach(function(b){
  b.addEventListener("click",function(){
    var v=b.getAttribute("data-copy");
    try{navigator.clipboard.writeText(v).then(function(){say("Kopiert: "+v)},function(){say("Bitte tippe: "+v)})}catch(x){say("Bitte tippe: "+v)}
  });
});

// ---- Hero: leuchtendes Blockraster ----
(function(){
  var cv=document.getElementById("grid"),ctx=cv.getContext("2d"),S=36,cells={},run=false,W,H,dpr=Math.min(devicePixelRatio||1,2);
  function size(){var r=cv.getBoundingClientRect();W=r.width;H=r.height;cv.width=W*dpr;cv.height=H*dpr;ctx.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);draw()}
  function col(n){return getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(n).trim()||"#d9412f"}
  function draw(){
    ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);
    ctx.strokeStyle=col("--line");ctx.globalAlpha=.55;ctx.lineWidth=1;
    ctx.beginPath();for(var x=0;x<=W;x+=S){ctx.moveTo(x+.5,0);ctx.lineTo(x+.5,H)}for(var y=0;y<=H;y+=S){ctx.moveTo(0,y+.5);ctx.lineTo(W,y+.5)}ctx.stroke();
    var cs=[col("--accent"),col("--accent2"),col("--gold")],any=false;
    for(var k in cells){var c=cells[k];if(c.a<=.02){delete cells[k];continue}any=true;
      ctx.globalAlpha=c.a*.75;ctx.fillStyle=cs[c.c];ctx.fillRect(c.x*S+1,c.y*S+1,S-1,S-1);c.a*=.94}
    ctx.globalAlpha=1;
    if(any){requestAnimationFrame(draw)}else run=false;
  }
  function hit(px,py){
    var r=cv.getBoundingClientRect(),x=Math.floor((px-r.left)/S),y=Math.floor((py-r.top)/S);
    if(y<0||x<0||px>r.right||py>r.bottom)return;
    cells[x+"_"+y]={x:x,y:y,a:1,c:(x+y)%3};
    if(!run){run=true;requestAnimationFrame(draw)}
  }
  var reduce=matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce)").matches;
  if(!reduce){window.addEventListener("pointermove",function(e){hit(e.clientX,e.clientY)})}
  addEventListener("resize",size);size();
  if(!reduce){for(var i=0;i<14;i++)(function(i){setTimeout(function(){hit(W*.55+Math.random()*W*.4,20+Math.random()*H*.6)},300+i*120)})(i)}
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
